Ctrl+V reads the clipboard in this order: the page's own blocks, or text the page copied in one block (its inline HTML, see "Copying text"), or cells copied with a table's tools (one cell its text, as its words copied in it are, where its HTML, a table of one cell, read as another program's came in as its words with a divider after them, the rule for a table of one column, `import/html.mjs`; more a table of them as they were, not as another program's table comes in, made over with a header row), then text of this page the browser copied (one line holding the page's chips, `core.mjs` `holdsAtoms`: its text again, each chip a copy of the one the page shows, so a formula, a link shown with its icon and one left empty paste as themselves), else another program's content (`import/read.mjs`): HTML with rich formatting (`containsRichFormatting`: spans, divs and paragraphs alone paste as text; WordPress's always), several lines or a table of it as blocks, one line as formatted text; without HTML, several lines as Markdown (`import/markdown.mjs`), one line as inline Markdown (bold, code, links, bare addresses, formulas) when it has any; a whole `$$ … $$` a formula block; several lines from VS Code / Cursor code in its language, except Markdown: its source text goes through the existing Markdown reader before the syntax-colored HTML or code fallback, including a single heading. An unspecified IDE language (`Plain Text`, including unknown language ids) takes the same route when that reader finds a block structure or inline formatting; it need not recognize every line or require blank lines between blocks. An explicit programming language retains its code behavior even for Markdown-like comments. A single Markdown paragraph keeps the usual inline paste behavior; pasting into existing code still inserts literal text. Picture files become images (a file too big for its block makes none, the block's words said: `refusesFile`, the same for a file dropped in) unless they come with Office's or a table's HTML (Excel puts a picture of its cells beside them), with the caret in a table's cell pictures of the cell, in at the caret ("Pictures in a cell"); the block a file makes goes right after the block the caret is in (or the last block selected), whole, its text not split (the new block a sibling of that block), where a block pasted there goes: in the first box out that takes it, a list or quote that does not splitting right after the block (`placeFor`, `placeAfter`; in a paragraph an item holds it stays in the item; the caret in a code block's code, after the code block, and in a media block's caption after that block, a new one's code and a caption made on the page standing for their block: `unitElement`, the part's own block, where a caption once stood for the block around its figure and the picture went after a whole section or list, or nowhere for a figure straight in the body); an empty paragraph the caret is in (a unit that says `replacedByFile`, not a node's title: `removableEmpty`, not `isFirstLine`) is taken away by the same step, so the block stands in its place (the user's of 2026-11-09: the block went after it and the empty paragraph stayed; an empty item, heading or quote paragraph is no such block and stays before it); one undo step, the picture or file filling its placeholder part of it (`media.paste` hands `{ pasted: true }` to the fill, `finishStep` in `history.mjs`, as a picture pasted with its address always was: one Ctrl+Z took the paste back to what stood there, where the first undid the picture and left the placeholder; scenarios `pasteFileAtCaret`, `pasteFileReplacesEmpty`; it once stood straight in the list or quote, which the file does not take, and was held as a conflict, and in a new code block's `<pre>`, never written). The HTML conversion (`import/html.mjs`: a cleaning as DOMPurify's, a normalizing, then element by element; Word's, WPS's and PowerPoint's HTML cleaned first by `import/office.mjs`, "Pasting from Word") produces block specs, which `import/to-blocks.mjs` makes the page's blocks (made to fit, not checked as the clipboard's own data is: no limit of 500, nothing refused for another block's sake; a tabs area in a disclosure is each tab's name and blocks, a row of columns in a column its columns' blocks, an address with a space is written as an address is): headings 1–4, paragraphs, lists (an item holds its blocks after its text, nested by their indent; a numbered list starts where its `<ol start>` says: `html.mjs` gives its items their numbers, `itemNumber`, and the first one's is the list's start), to-dos, quotes, a callout a highlight block with its emoji, a toggle a disclosure, code (its language where the kit knows it), a formula block, a table (spans spread into empty cells, a header row and column), an image of a web or data: address (downloaded into the document's folder once on the page), an https page an embed, columns and tabs; and, besides these, the kit's own flowchart (`ol.doc-flow`, copied from a reader's page or an exported report: `import/html.mjs` `flowchartBlock`, `to-blocks.mjs` `stepOf`) a flowchart again, each step a node with its emoji icon, its title (its own words, written the short way, made one, bold), its content apart and its color and direction (the weight Chrome writes a step's own words at, 700 now and 600 in a page exported before, any from 500 up, makes no bold of all it holds), where a box takes none its nodes' blocks. `tests/unit/paste.mjs` holds the conversion to its expected results for 21 samples (web pages, Google Docs and Sheets, Excel, Word, WPS, GitHub, WeChat…). Besides these: an AI answer's cards, highlight blocks and columns ("Cards of an AI answer"); formulas AI answers copy (`import/math.mjs`, `import/inline.mjs`): KaTeX, Gemini's `data-math`, MathML, MathJax 2 drawn on a page, and `$…$`, `$$…$$`, `\(…\)`, `\[…\]` and GPT's escaped `\\(…\\)` in text, made the page's formulas (KaTeX is loaded first); colors (`import/colors.mjs`): from Word, Google Docs and Notion a style's color becomes the nearest of the page's nine (CIEDE2000), from any other page only one of the page's own (a block's fill or the words' deeper one). Where it goes: with the caret in a text block, one line goes in at the caret with the format bar's marks the block takes, its own alone: the marks around the caret split there (`marks.mjs` `outOfMarks`) and a mark at its ends one with the same mark beside it (`joinMarks`: bold pasted in bold is one bold; plain text takes the caret's, as typing does), and the spaces at its ends, every character of it kept (`pastedLines` `edges`: text the page copied in a block with the spaces it was copied with, the user's of 2026-11-11, words copied with a space beside them having pasted without it, each line's ends trimmed as another program's HTML is; another program's formatted line those its plain text begins or ends with, a space, a tab or a non-breaking one, never its markup's line breaks and indentation; plain text as it is), in a block that cannot split too; a space at an end of what is pasted that the line would not show, at its edge (a paragraph's start or end, by a line break) or beside another space, a non-breaking one on the page, as the browser types a space there (`showEdgeSpaces`), so that words pasted at a paragraph's end with a space after them stand a space from what is typed next, and an ordinary space in the file as any (`core.mjs` `spaced`; scenario `pasteKeepsSpaces`), a detail card's entry pasted in the page of its card still its entry (`core.mjs` `markOf`, `cardNamed`) (an address alone a link, `www.` one as `http://`: pasted, in any block that takes links, in a line of Markdown, and typed into the link card alike; the user chose `http://`, the scheme that works everywhere, which a browser upgrades by itself where the site has it. `links/address.mjs` is the one place that reads an address); blocks split the block at the caret: the first, a paragraph, joins the text before it, the others follow in the first box out that takes them (a list or quote that does not splits there, its blocks after the caret going on after them in one of its own; side by side lists of a kind join; a list item holding blocks is such a box, so what is pasted by a block it holds stays in the item; a container made on the page and not written yet, a list just made of "- ", is the block around what it holds as the file's are), list items copied on their own going in as a list of their kind where the box does not take items (`asLists`: into a disclosure's content, a highlight block, a column, a tab, a section; in a list of their kind they are its items, in a list of another kind it splits around them, an item keeping its type), the text after the caret joins the last pasted text block, the last of a basic block (`lastLine`: a list's, a quote's, a flowchart's or a node's, itself for a text block) whose text may be any text (else a paragraph of its own, in the first box out that takes one: a formula block and code have no text it can join, what a container or a card holds stays its own, and nothing is left with no block for it: a box that takes no paragraph, a list or a flowchart, takes only blocks with a text block of their own, `tests/unit/clipboard-kinds.mjs`); where they go out of a box that stays whole with blocks in it after the caret, a section in a highlight block, a node at a title with content below it, the text stays before those blocks, a block of its own, as Enter makes one (`place.stays`, `splitBelow`), and an empty block gives them its place (with nothing after the caret, a block that holds no text is selected, no caret in it); in code, the text with its lines; in a flowchart node's title, what lands in it looks as what is typed there (`titled`: bold where bold is armed, its empty title, as plain text, a formatted line, a paragraph copied and the first of several lines alike, the rest its content; plain text bold in a title bold throughout; the title never given up for the blocks pasted, which go after it); in a block that cannot split (a cell, a caption, a card's title), the lines joined, with the marks the block takes (cells pasted into a table's cells are cells: "Tables"): the text of the blocks `read()` made, the page's own as another program's (a list's markers go, its bold stays; Word's and Google Docs' colors as for one line; a block copied as itself its data's lines, else the words of its markup, `joinedHtml`); in a field of plain text (`plain` in its caps: the page's title, a code block's title) the words alone, on one line, decided as soon as the clipboard is read, before anything that is for blocks: a copy with no unit on this page is refused nowhere there, and nothing waits for KaTeX (of the page's own blocks their lines' words, `plainOf`, not the Markdown other programs get). A page's title takes no block. With blocks selected, the blocks go after them (items as above) and are selected; so they do with the caret on a formula block (a right click puts it there), which has no text a paste goes into, its TeX changing in its editor alone (selected, it is what a paste goes after; scenario `pasteOnFormulaBlock`). One undo step (scenario `pasteIntoContainers`). A table and a formula block are made by a paste as by the "/" menu (表格, 公式块: a table is written by `components/table/file.mjs`, a formula block as any text block, its TeX checked by `texSource` in `components/math/index.mjs`); the kit's `math.js` has KaTeX's mhchem (`\ce`, `\pu`) and the commands of LaTeX packages AI answers use (`\abs`, `\dv`, `\mathbbm`, `\SI`…, `DocMath.macros`), which the editor's formulas use too.
- **Pasting from Word.** `clipboard/import/office.mjs`, asked by `read.mjs` for HTML of Office's namespaces that `isWordContent` takes for a document's or slides' (Word, WPS Writer, Outlook, PowerPoint: a test of the text in one pass over its start tags, each read up to its `>` or the next `<`, for the program in a meta, Word.Document, Microsoft Word, WPS or PowerPoint, Word's namespace, an `Mso…` class, an `mso-` style or an `<o:p>`; never a spreadsheet's, Excel's carrying `mso-` styles too, whose cells keep their own way as tables). `filterWordContent(html, { colors })` parses Word's whole document into one of its own and returns its body's content cleaned, a string whose first element is the first block (a copy of cells starts with its `<table>`); it never throws, and on an error gives the HTML back as it came. In order: Office's self-closing tags (`<o:lock …/>`) are opened and closed before parsing, or the parser would take the text after them inside (one walk over the text, quoted values stepped over); conditional comments: `<!--[if …]>…<![endif]-->` (settings, VML pictures, equations, field codes) goes, what `<![if !X]>…<![endif]>` shows older programs stays (a picture's fallback `<img>`, an equation's picture, a footnote's number, a list's marker) but the row of empty cells for misaligned columns, the second `<br>` at a paragraph's end, a review comment's anchor and empty bookmarks (an `[if !X]` with another `[if …]` before its `[endif]` goes alone, so each sibling is looked at once), then every comment goes; what is not content goes with all it holds (style sheets, not applied, so class styles give no color or font; `<xml>`; VML `v:*`, OMML `m:*` and Office's other `o:*` elements but `o:p` and `o:wrapblock`; hidden text, `display:none`, `visibility:hidden`, `mso-hide`; review comments, their marks, anchors and list; tracked deletions, a `<del>` with `cite` or `datetime`, so the text reads with the changes accepted); Word's layout divs (WordSection1, borders around paragraphs, the footnote list, frames) are unwrapped, so a list over a section's edge stays one and no bare nested divs reach the cards' rule, after each stretch of a div's inline content that holds a word or a picture is made a paragraph (a div that is only that becomes the paragraph, its style kept for the steps after: PowerPoint writes a div for each paragraph, its text straight inside; a stretch of only spaces and line breaks stays); a tab (`mso-tab-count`) is one space and the source's whitespace (Word wraps it at about 80 characters) what a browser shows, read across a stretch's text nodes: a run one space, none at a block's edges, beside a `<br>` or between blocks, none for a line break between two Chinese or Japanese characters (one between Korean words), U+00A0 never touched, `<pre>` as it is; Title (`MsoTitle`) becomes a heading 1, an outline level (`mso-outline-level`) the heading of that level, Quote and Intense Quote one quote of paragraphs, Subtitle a paragraph, and a numbered heading keeps its number as text ("1. 引言"); list paragraphs (`mso-list:l0 level2 lfo1`, their marker drawn as text in a span styled `mso-list:Ignore`, or inside `<![if !supportLists]>`; and PowerPoint's paragraphs holding its bullet, a span styled `mso-special-format:bullet`, their level the rank of their left indent among the run's) become `<ul>`/`<ol>` of `<li><p>…</p></li>` (the `<p>` keeps underlines and line breaks in the item's text), nested by level from the first of each run of them side by side (one level deeper at most; a run copied from level 2 starts at the top; another list, `l` or `lfo`, or a change of kind at the same depth a new list beside the last, so a numbered item's bullets nest in it), bulleted or numbered (`numberingOf`) as the list's own definition says where the clipboard carries it (Word's style sheet, read before it goes, one walk over its text: `@list l0:level1 {mso-level-number-format:…}` for the paragraph's list and level, `bullet` or `image` a bullet, any other format a number, none written Word's default, decimal; PowerPoint's `mso-special-format`, `bullet` or `numbullet`), else as the marker's text reads, whatever follows it (WPS writes its number with nothing after it, and Word does for a list set to follow its number with nothing): a number (1. 1) (1) （1） 1、 1.1. a. A) i. IV. 一、 （一） 十二、 ① 第一章) not in a symbol font (Symbol, Wingdings, Webdings, Courier New: Word draws bullets as their letters) numbered, anything else (• o § - * ■) bulleted; a numbered list starts at its first item's number, `<ol start>` (5. 5, 三、 3, ⑤ 5, iv. 4; letters and roman numerals read by the definition's format, else a run of i, v and x roman and any other letter a letter; a marker of no number, Word's ordinal words, the definition's `mso-level-start-at`), which `html.mjs` gives the list's items as their numbers and `to-blocks.mjs` makes the list's start (the page writes a list's numbers in its own style for the depth, so a, i or 一 come in as the numbers they are); only list paragraphs are lists, so a paragraph that begins "2026. " with no `mso-list` stays a paragraph; the marker leaves one ordinary space where it was when spacing followed it, none for WPS's or PowerPoint's; formatting written as style becomes tags (bold from 500, italic, underline but a link's, strikethrough, superscript, subscript; "normal" undoes nothing), fonts and font sizes going (24pt would make a heading in mid-paragraph); with `colors` (always, from `read.mjs`) an inline element's text color and highlight (`color`, `<font color>`, `background-color`, `background`, `mso-highlight`; not a link's, nor a paragraph's or a cell's shading) are handed over as written as `data-paste-color` / `data-paste-background`, which `markColors` reads before any style and matches to the page's nine (black, `windowtext` and `auto` no color, any other unsaturated text color gray, a white or nearly white highlight none), but a white or nearly white text color (every channel 240 or more, over white) is not handed over: Word writes it for words on shading, which is not carried, and the importer would make it gray; page, section and column breaks go, and a `<br>` between blocks; table cells keep their spans and paragraphs, Word's placeholder cell stays empty, cells of a `<thead>` become `<th>`; spans and fonts without a color, bookmarks (`<a name>`), `<ins>` and Office's elements left (`o:p`, `w:sdt`, `st1:*`) are unwrapped; every attribute goes but a link's `href` (as written: `#_ftn1` stays relative, footnotes kept as links and paragraphs after a divider; a `file:` address with a place, which Word writes at times for a place in a saved document, reduced to the place, `#_ftn1`), a picture's `src`, `width`, `height` and `alt`, a cell's `colspan` and `rowspan`, a numbered list's `start`, and the colors; formatting left empty goes; a paragraph of nothing but spaces is Word's empty paragraph, written as one `&nbsp;` (the importer makes no block of it, and a table copied with one after it is still a table alone). The importer reads the result as any page's. Not read yet: Word's pictures (`file:` addresses in its temporary folder: `to-blocks.mjs` takes web and `data:` pictures only), equations (OMML, inside a comment). Word's HTML of plain paragraphs only, no formatting, no list, is pasted as its plain text (`read.mjs`, as any HTML without rich formatting), which is read as Markdown, so there two lines one of which begins "2026. " make a numbered list; a list read from text starts at its first line's number (`markdown.mjs` keeps each item's, `to-blocks.mjs` starts the list at the first), so that line reads "2026. …" still. Two numbered lists side by side with nothing between them (Word's, or a page's `<ol>` after an `<ol>`) come in as one list from the first one's number: `to-blocks.mjs` joins numbered items in a row. Clipboard HTML may come from any page, so nothing in the filter takes more than a pass over its input. In Chrome it takes about 90 ms a megabyte of Word's HTML, linear (measured 2026-11-21: 1 MB 92 ms, 6 MB 546 ms), and a megabyte built against it (unclosed tags, attributes, quotes or `[if]` comments, a background of unclosed parentheses, a margin of a million digits) under 90 ms. In jsdom, moving or removing one of many siblings takes time in their number, so the list step and a paragraph of thousands of spans or comments grow faster than linearly there (not in a browser), which only a test of megabytes would meet. `tests/unit/paste.mjs`: the Word and WPS samples' results (WPS's list numbered), the samples through the filter giving no cards, the filter rule by rule on small bodies of Word's HTML, the markers of each kind numbered or bulleted with nothing after them, a list's start, list definitions, a paragraph beginning with a number left one, PowerPoint's text (written by hand from its format) pasted whole, and a time limit on input built to stall the string tests (the style sheet's walk and a marker's number among them).
- **A whole Markdown document.** The import (`src/transfer/import.mjs`, and `gallery import`) reads a `.md` file with the paste's reader in a mode of its own, `readMarkdownDocument` (`import/markdown.mjs`, its header lists the rules): as a Markdown viewer shows the file (CommonMark and GFM: a paragraph's lines joined, tables, task lists, footnotes, link definitions, `$…$` formulas by Pandoc's rule and a display formula closed before an empty line, fenced code with its title, quotes of paragraphs and GitHub's alerts, HTML blocks kept as written, a mermaid chain of steps a flow) where a paste reads Markdown its own way, and says what it leaves out, each at its line; it returns the formulas it read and the links to addresses that are no web ones, at their lines, for the import to say which KaTeX cannot draw (`renderTex`, the kit's macros, `MATH_MACROS` in `src/shared/components/math.mjs`) and which a document does not follow. With `yuque` it reads Yuque's Markdown, a paste's ("Pasting from Yuque"). `to-blocks.mjs` takes its sections, its HTML blocks (a marker the import writes the HTML in place of) and, with `held`, list items holding all their blocks.
- **Pasting from Yuque.** `clipboard/import/yuque.mjs` (the user's of 2026-11-09, with samples of one document of every kind of block, copied twice in macOS Chrome: `tests/unit/fixtures/paste/yuque/`), asked first by `readExternal` and answering only for a clipboard of Yuque's; anything else is read as before. A copy in a Yuque document (the samples': ⌘A ⌘C in its reader; a smaller selection is taken to copy the same way, and one of a single line goes in as that line) puts the document's nodes there three times: `text/ne-inode` (JSON), an `<ne-clipboard data="…">` heading its `text/html` (the same JSON, URL-encoded; the HTML after it is the page's look of them, where a formula is its TeX as text, a 提示 a styled div and a board nothing) and `text/lake` (Yuque's own HTML: `data-lake-id`, `<card name value="data:…">` of URL-encoded JSON, lists flat with `data-lake-indent`). They are read richest first, the JSON, then lake made the same nodes (`lakeTree`; HTML in lake's form with no `<ne-clipboard>`, an older copy's, too), and all of them read alike. Each kind one rule (`yuqueSpecs`, block specs, `to-blocks.mjs` making the page's blocks): a paragraph a paragraph (an image in it a block of its own between its words; one formula alone in it a formula block, Yuque having no other display formula); headings 1–4 their levels, 5 and 6 heading 4 (as Markdown's are); Yuque's flat list items nested by their levels (a numbered one's place its number, a to-do's check); a quote a quote; a 提示 (its alert: info, tips, success, warning, danger) the page's callout (提示块, `report_callout` in `to-blocks.mjs`, its blocks), info the callout's own blue (no class), tips yellow, success green, warning orange, danger red, any other kind blue; a 折叠块 a disclosure, its summary the title; a table a table with its merged cells (a row's `spans` naming its cells; spans that tile no table spread into empty cells), each cell's paragraphs its lines, a column's alignment its cells' look, a header row and column as any table from elsewhere gets them (一键优化); its cards: a divider, code in its mode's language (named as a fence names one, a language the kit does not know kept by that name; its name the code block's title), a formula an inline formula (`\(…\)`), a picture of its address (downloaded into the document's folder once on the page, as any pasted picture's; its caption Yuque's title where Yuque shows it, never its alt, which is a file's name), a board (画板) a gray line saying it was not pasted, named by its mind map's first node, any other card a link where it holds a web address and else a gray line naming it. Inline: bold, italic, underline, strike, sup, sub, code, links, and colors as the page's nearest (`colors.mjs`: Yuque's styles are meant, as Word's are); a soft line break a line break. One line copied (a paragraph's words, a heading's, an item's, its plain text one line) goes in at the caret with its marks, as one line of a page's HTML does. The toolbar's 复制为 Markdown puts Markdown with some HTML on the clipboard and, as HTML, its words in one span (no rich formatting, so a paste would read it by the HTML conversion's rules, each line a paragraph): it is Yuque's when its text holds a mark of Yuque's own (`:::info`, an `ne-` or `lake-` class, `<font style>`) or its HTML is that span (Yuque's font, white space kept, no block element: `yuqueMarkdownCopy`), and is then read by the whole-document reader with `yuque` ("A whole Markdown document": a paragraph's lines joined, two spaces at a line's end a line break) and what Yuque writes besides (`markdown.mjs` and `inline.mjs` say it): `:::kind … :::` a 提示, every HTML block read as the paste reads HTML (`<img class="ne-image">` a picture, a `<details class="lake-collapse">`'s paragraphs), a row of more cells than its header's its cells past the header's last joined by `|` (Yuque writes a `|` in a cell as it is), the delimiter row's alignments the cells' looks, `$ … $` with spaces inside a formula, a `<font style>`'s colors the nearest, a ```` ```mermaid ```` fence code. So it makes the blocks ⌘A ⌘C makes but what its Markdown has not (a board, merged cells: empty cells) and where Yuque writes it ambiguously (`**起点 **` before code, a backtick in inline code), which only ⌘A ⌘C keeps (unit `paste-yuque.mjs`, scenario `yuquePaste`).
- **Cards of an AI answer.** `clipboard/import/cards.mjs` (the user's of 2026-11-20, with two copies of ChatGPT answers: they came in as plain paragraphs, every div unwrapped). ChatGPT draws some of an answer as cards (a diagram of layers and arrows, a row of small boxes, scenes beside their pictures) and its copy button puts them on the clipboard as bare nested `<div>`s of `<p>`s and spans, nothing saying what they were. A paste reads them from their structure before `html.mjs` normalizes the HTML (`markCards`; `htmlToBlocks` and `containsRichFormatting` with `cards`, which only `readExternal` and `externalContent` pass: an import and a Markdown document's HTML blocks never), and only in bare HTML: no element outside code and formulas with an attribute but the content's (`bareHtml`: `href`, `src`, `alt`, a cell's spans…), and never Office's (its Word filter leaves it bare). So a copy of a web page (Chrome writes each element's style), Word, Google Docs, Notion, Feishu or Yuque reads as before, as every sample of the two corpora in `tests/unit/paste.mjs` does. A div is a line (a `<p>`, or words straight in it, a text editor's line), a card (two parts or more, one a `<p>` or a list of its own; or divs alone, one a card and the rest lines: a picture's link beside a scene's words), a grid (divs alone, all cards, or all labels: in each a `<p>` of at most 40 characters) or a wrapper (anything holding a heading, a table, a quote, code, a picture, a rule, a disclosure or a formula block, and anything else), whose divs are read in turn; the answer's own wrappers, from the clipboard's root down while a div holds one part, are no card (a grid copied alone is its rows). A card is a highlight block (高亮块, `div.doc-panel`) with no icon (spec `box`, `to-blocks.mjs`) of its lines as paragraphs (bold and links kept), its lists and its grids, a card in it giving its lines; a grid is rows of columns (spec `column_list`, 分栏), at most 3 cells a row and the rows as even as they go (4: 2 + 2, 6: 3 + 3), a cell a highlight block of its lines (in a highlight block its lines alone: no box in a box; in a column a grid is its lines: no row of columns in a column), and cells of more than 80 characters go one under another. An empty div (ChatGPT's arrows and rules) is nothing, a line its paragraph; list items holding divs, tables and headings come in as before. One undo step; the file holds the page's blocks as the menu makes them, nothing of the copy's (unit `paste.mjs` on a sample written after the user's, `tests/unit/fixtures/paste/ai-cards.html`, and counterexamples: styled, classed and `data-` divs, a text editor's lines, an answer of paragraphs, items holding divs, a section, paragraphs a page wraps in divs, Office's; scenario `aiCardsPaste`).
- **Resizing cards, tables and code blocks.** `resize/`: with the pointer within a few pixels of the right edge of a card or table wrapper (or its bottom edge, for a card holding a chart, a table or a code block), the cursor changes (`html[data-ge-resize]`) and the read-only hint stays away; pressing there drags that edge. A 2px gray line shows where the edge goes (`--ge-guide-hot`, darker where it snaps: `--ge-guide-snap`, `look.mjs`; never a bright blue, the user's of 2026-11-07), two mirrored ones for a centered card (which grows on both sides; a table placed past the text, 宽表格, is centred on its line too: its width dragged puts its right end at the pointer, twice the pointer's way from the line's middle or from the page's area's left edge, the less of the two, and a column's strip dragged, `table/widths.mjs` `follow`, keeps the column's edge under the pointer while the table grows on both sides, render.md "Table layout internals"), and once the pointer has moved the bottom notice says the size it makes ("图形宽度：半宽", "代码块高度：显示全部代码"; no label beside the line), said again only when it changes and taken off when the drag ends, the result's notice ("已调整…") following; nothing changes until the pointer is released, and releasing where the drag started or pressing Esc changes nothing. Every snap below takes the edge within 6px of its place (`SNAP`; 14px until 2026-11-07, which the user found too strong), a code block's or table's height back where it started within 3px. Widths are shares of the width the block stands in: the reading width, or its column's. A chart card's height is its chart's (the kit keeps no room under a drawn chart: `chart.css` reserves 280px only before it is drawn), and its bottom edge snaps level with the bottom of a card beside it, alone in another column of its row ("与旁边对齐", `besideBottoms`), so two cards side by side stand one height (scenario `cardHeights`). They snap at the middle ("半宽", or "到中线" for a card not at the start) and at the end ("全宽" / "占满本行"); a table goes by how far the pointer moves: its own right end moves that much, also when the table is wider than its frame and scrolls in it (its whole width is the wrapper's scroll width; scrolled to the end, that end is at the frame's edge, and a table scrolled to its end stays so after the change). It snaps back where it started and to the reading width ("铺满正文", from a narrower table widened and from a wider one, scrolling in its frame, narrowed: scenario `tableEdgeSnap`), and past that the table becomes that much wider and scrolls in a frame that stays at the reading width (labelled "超宽表" with the table's width). A table's width does not snap to the middle, and never goes below the columns' readable floors (`tables.js` leaves their sum, with the wrapper's borders, on the wrapper as `data-doc-min-width`), so a table narrower than the text does not scroll; its share is rounded up, never leaving the frame narrower than where it was let go. A wrapper with its own width is rarely a whole number of pixels, so `tables.js` gives a table that fills it, or would overhang it by up to 4px, `width: 100%` instead of a pixel width; otherwise it could scroll by a hair. A card's chart height (its bottom edge is there only for a card holding a chart: the svg its host draws, straight in it or beside a part's own controls) is set in the SVG's own units (scaled down with the drawing in a narrow card), never below 160px. A table's scroll height stops at all its rows (the default when that fits, `none` otherwise); a table of 12 body rows or fewer has no bottom edge to drag (a table the table tools manage has its own strip along its bottom edge: see "Tables"). A code block's bottom edge (from the file or made on the page; not while folded) sets `--doc-code-height` on the block, the height of its frame (the `pre`'s border box; its header is not counted, and the `pre` takes the block's value): shorter than its code, the code scrolls in it, the line numbers with it; at least 40px, at most all its code, and near that it snaps to it ("显示全部代码"), which removes the height; one of three lines or fewer has no edge unless it has a height. Its block menu then offers "显示全部代码". A code block made or converted on the page carries its height in its markup (`height` in its node or its `replace`). The block menu of a card offers "居中显示" (the compact class, no span) and "全宽显示" (no span), of a table "铺满正文宽度" ("铺满这一栏" in a column; span 1), and "恢复默认大小" once a size is set. Every change is one undo step of the editor's look step (classes and sizes). `SIZES` in `components/registry.mjs` lists the sizes each unit kind takes (each component's description has its own) and `sizeValue()` their allowed values (a share 0.15–1 for cards, up to 3 for tables; heights in px, `none` for tables, 40–4000px for code); `captureLayout()` records them, `collectEdits()` sends changed ones as `units: [{ id, size, value }]` (null removes one). `applyEdits()` rewrites only the named declarations of the unit's `style` attribute, splitting it at the semicolons outside quotes, parentheses and entities, so every other byte stays; it adds the attribute when there is none and drops it when it empties, and refuses a size the kind does not take (`invalid`).
- **Block colors.** A block takes the families of colors its unit says (`colors` in its description, the user's rule; `COLOR_FAMILIES` in `registry.mjs` is the one list): body text (小字 too), headings and list items a text color and a background, one of each at once (`["text", "background"]`); a highlight block, a callout, a disclosure and a code block a background only (`["background"]`); every other block none (a quote, a divider, a table, a formula block, an image, a file, a bookmark, an embed, a section, a row of columns and its columns, a list as a whole (its items take them), a chart card, a detail card, the page's icon, tabs and a document's own widget). `TOGGLES` gets each family's nine classes from it, and the block menu, the server's checks, copy and paste, conversions (a block turned into a kind without a family loses that color) and the kit's stylesheets all go by it (unit test in `tests/unit/components.mjs`). A block menu has 颜色 › right below its types with the families its blocks all take (`block-menu/colors.mjs`): 上次使用 (the color given last on this page, when its family is offered), 文本颜色 and 背景颜色, each 默认 and the nine colors, as swatches drawn in the kit's own classes (a background's with the block's fill, `--doc-fill`, not the words' deeper one the kit gives a span); each family's color is ringed apart (默认 when the block has none of it), and a pick changes its own family only (`colorLook`), 默认 taking that family's away (scenarios `blockColorFamilies`, `colorKinds`). A kind that shows one of the nine with none of a family says so (`colorDefaults` in its description, `COLOR_DEFAULTS`: a callout's background is blue): for blocks all of that kind the family has no 默认, that color is ringed for none and picking it takes the class away (scenario `calloutLook`). A color is a class `doc-text-<color>` or `doc-highlight-<color>` (`components/colors.mjs`; the nine colors' names and values (a text color, a block's fill and the deeper fill of words: `text`, `fill`, `mark`) are written once in `src/shared/colors.mjs`, which the pickers, the paste's nearest-color matching and a copy's styles read, and `tests/unit/clipboard.mjs` holds to the kit's CSS), at most one of each family (a table cell one of the eighteen: `colorLook(…, { only: true })`), on the block itself: its whole text in that color, or a background with room around its text (the kit's base.css); a list item's text color is its marker's too and its background lies behind its marker and the items it holds (flow.css), a disclosure's background takes the place of its panel's and its text color is its title's and its blocks' (details.css); a callout's background is its fill and its line that color's deep shade (`--doc-fill-deep`, the color's text color), the palette's blue when it has none, as one given blue, and a text color colors its words only (callout.css); a code block's background is `--doc-code-background`, its text color the color of the code the highlighter leaves plain (code.css); a formula block's background has room at its sides (math.css); a file's and a bookmark's fill is the figure's, round as the row or card in it (file.css, bookmark.css). The kit's stylesheets write the nine's class lists as `@text-colors` and `@highlight-colors`, which `kit-build.mjs` expands from `src/shared/colors.mjs`. Blocks of these kinds take them as class switches (`toggles`), one undo step (`lookStep`, which takes several elements: several selected blocks colored at once undo together), and the server adds the class attribute to a block that has none and takes it away with its last class (`toggleUnit`). A block made on the page, split off another or turned into another type carries its color (`classes` on a new node, a part or a conversion, checked against the kind's switches: `validClasses`, `validPart`; `standIn`), a container made on the page too (a highlight block made of a colored node or pasted with its background, a disclosure pasted with its own: `nodeOf`, written after the kit's class), and an item holding blocks as one holding none (`components/list/file.mjs`). The menu's rows may open such a submenu, with a search field at its top when the row gives one (在下方新建: the "/" menu's blocks, found as "/" finds them, `typeItems` with the words typed) (`menu.mjs`; any card: a list that goes on past its edge fades out over its last 34px there (`.fade-b` and `.fade-t`), drawn by a sticky strip of the card's own color so the card keeps its shadow, under a search field at the top; the wheel past its end does not scroll the page, `overscroll-behavior: contain`, which closed it; a row is deeper gray pressed, a row that takes something away for good red on a pale red while highlighted, `danger`: a block's 删除 in the block menu (one block or several), a table's rows' or columns' 删除, a tab's 删除, a card's on a custom page, and one that cannot be done now gray as any other row (the table's cell bar's 删除所在行 and 删除所在列 in its 更多 list the same, `.ge-more-item[data-danger]`); a titled group of rows has no separator before its title (its title parts it), and a card of rows with no icons (`icons: false`) has its rows' words 9px in (the finish of 2026-11-17, "Look" above: the card 14px round, 5px round its flush rows, each 9px round and the row gray 7% under the pointer or the keys; the block menu's grid in its middle, so its icons stand 12px from the card's side as its rows' do); a note under a row and 没有结果 in the secondary gray; a row with a check is a `menuitemcheckbox` or `menuitemradio`, and a search field names the highlighted row, `aria-activedescendant`; `showCurrent` opens a card with its choice made in the middle, a code block's languages): as wide as its rows, 180px at least and 320px at most, past which a row's words are cut by an ellipsis (`FIT`: any card opened with no width given, so 一键优化 › and a table's 对齐 › are 180px; every card took 265px whatever it held until the user found 一键优化 › too wide, 2026-11-07; a note under a row wraps at the card's width and never widens it), unless the row gives its own width (`width`: 颜色 › its swatches', 在下方新建 › the block menu's, a bookmark's 显示为 › 168px; 分区与分栏 › is as wide as its rows since 2026-11-17, its English row whole), as a top-level card with rows that change as it is used does (the block menu as wide as its grid, the "/" menu and 链接到页面 240px, a code block's languages 220px; one with a search field and no width given 240px, `SEARCHING`), so it never jumps as its rows come and go; a card of a few fixed rows asks for a floor of its own and grows past it with its rows (`least`: a table's row and column menus the block menu's 188px, a list's number menu 136px; fixed widths cut their English rows by an ellipsis until 2026-11-17); overlapping the menu by 4px, at most 70% of the window high; opened 120ms after the pointer rests on its row (as the workbench's; 80ms, 200ms while another was open, until 2026-11-17), a click, Enter or →; closed 100ms after the pointer leaves its row unless it goes into it (250ms until then), by ← or with the menu; while opened from the keys, ↑/↓ and Enter work in it. A row may both run and open a submenu, a split row (an item with both `run` and `submenu`: a table's 一键优化 ›, the user's of 2026-11-07): its words (`.ge-pop-main`) and its › (`.ge-pop-more`, 32px, the › where another submenu row has its own) stand side by side 2px apart, each grey on its own with no line between them, the words while the row is highlighted and the pointer is not on the ›, the › under the pointer and while its submenu is open (the row itself takes no grey); a click on its words or Enter runs the row, resting on its words opens nothing (and closes its open submenu after 250ms, as resting on another row does), resting on its › or a click on it opens the submenu, → opens it and ← leaves it as any submenu's. The menu tells the two apart by the element under the pointer: the › part, marked `data-more` on the row while the pointer is on it.
- **Alignment and indent.** `align.mjs`, `components/align.mjs` (the user's, 2026-11-09; 缩进和对齐 › in the block menu). A paragraph (小字 too) and a heading take an alignment, centred or at the right (`doc-align-center`, `doc-align-right`; the left is neither, the kit's own), and an indent of 1 to 8 levels (`doc-indent-1` … `doc-indent-8`): class switches of their components (`toggles: ALIGN_TOGGLES`, the kit's list `TEXT_ALIGN_CLASSES` of `src/shared/components/paragraph.mjs`), so the server writes them, a conversion between the two keeps them (`convert.mjs` `standIn`), and the kit draws them in the page, for a reader and in an export (`styles/flow.css`: `text-align`, and a margin of two body characters a level, 32px whatever the block's size, its background moving with its words). Their block menu has 对齐与缩进 › right above 颜色 (several selected that all take it too, one step for them all), its submenu 左对齐, 居中对齐 and 右对齐 (the one they share checked, drawn with the table's alignment icons), a line, then 增加缩进 and 减少缩进 (a level for every block, disabled where none can go further; their icons the menus' lines with the middle two moved in, an arrow in or out at their left; the row's the same lines, no arrow); each one undo step (a look step of the classes, which selects the blocks again on undo). Enter carries them to the paragraph it makes, the half after the caret or the empty line before it (`core.mjs` `newBlock`: `carry`), as a word processor's new line keeps its paragraph's. Backspace at the start of such a block takes them off first, one at a time, before anything else (`typing/` `backspace`: `unalign`): a centred or right-aligned block goes to the left, then its indent a level less each press, then it joins the block above or goes as Backspace always does; each one undo step, the caret staying at its start. A paragraph left in a block's place by Backspace after it (no text following the block) takes the alignment the block showed ("The caret after a block"), so the same chain runs from there. A list item takes neither (its indent is its list's). A table takes the alignment alone, by the same row (the user's, 2026-11-09: "the same as a text block's"): its frame (`div.doc-table-wrap`; a bare `table` of a class of its own too) placed on the page at the left (the kit's own), centred or at the right where it is narrower than the text, the same two classes on the frame (`toggles: ALIGN_PLACES` of the table unit, drawn by auto margins in `styles/table.css`); `alignRows` builds the row for any kind that takes an alignment, named 对齐 with the three alone where one of the blocks takes no indent (`indentable`: a table, so also several selected with a table among them), above the block's own rows with the same icons, one undo step; Backspace after a centred or right-aligned table leaves a paragraph centred or at the right, as for a picture. A cell's alignment is its own, the column menu's ("Tables"): a first reading of the user's word gave 对齐 › every cell of the table one, and was taken out. Gallery's own copy and paste carries them with a block's colors: `colorOf` (`clipboard/own/describe.mjs`) puts a paragraph's or a heading's alignment and indent classes into the block's `color` field (`BLOCK_LOOKS`, which `checkBlocks` allows), and `colored` puts on those the kind's `toggles` take, so a centred paragraph pastes centred and, with a field of its own to say so, is not joined into the paragraph at the caret (`first.color`); a cell's classes are no paragraph's (`TEXT_BLOCKS`: only `p` and `h1`–`h4`). An Agent's `style="text-align: center"` or `right` on those is made the class by `naturalize()` ("Natural HTML").
- **New blocks and conversions.** Where a block type shows is its description's own (`types` in `components/<name>/index.mjs`: `turn` its place among the types a text block is turned into, `fresh` among those made as an empty block standing for an empty one (a divider, the media blocks, a Sheet area, a formula block, a table), `menu` its group and place in the "/" menu); `registry.mjs` derives `TURN_INTO`, `FRESH` and `MENU_GROUPS` from them (and refuses two types in one place, or a made type with no menu place), `convert.mjs` builds `belowTargets` and `typedTargets` from them, and the "/" menu groups read the same. A type that is no text block is made by its definition's `create` (a divider, the media blocks) or its page object's where that needs the page (a code block, a formula block, a Sheet area, a table), all with one set of arguments; any other type is a text block, made by `standIn`. What follows a new block is its component's `onCreated` (a media block's picture or file chosen, a formula block's formula typed), else the caret goes in it, and a block turned from its menu with text in it is selected as any block turned; what comes before that, when a block with text is turned, is what the type's `preload` brings (`type-grid.mjs` `preloading`, KaTeX for a formula block, a group turned before it came drawn again by `redraw`). `convert.mjs` turns a text block into another type (`TURN_INTO`: text, small text (小字, `p.doc-note`), the four heading levels `h1`–`h4` (the fourth body-sized), an item of a bulleted or numbered list, a disclosure (折叠列表: the block goes into its content as it is, its title left empty with the caret in it, the reader's rule, as a highlight block takes it; from the "/" menu or 在下方新建 an empty block, with nothing in it, is not carried in: the new disclosure or highlight block stands for it with an empty paragraph of its own, `convertBlock`'s `fresh`), a highlight panel (高亮块, `div.doc-panel`, the opening summary's look: a container whose blocks keep their types, so a block turned into one goes in as it is, and there is none inside another), a callout (提示块, `div.doc-callout`: the same kind of container, "Callouts"), a quote paragraph, a code block (the text as its code) or a formula block (the text as its TeX; a new block in its place, as the server takes no text block turned into one in place; KaTeX preloaded with the grid so that it is drawn as it is made, and an empty block's formula typed next, as from "/"); in the block menu's grid in that order, five on a line (文本 and the headings; the lists, 折叠列表, 步骤流程图; 高亮块, 提示块, 引用块, 代码块, 公式块: the user's order, 2026-10-30), no divider, which "/" and 在下方新建 make), a whole list into the other kind of list or into paragraphs, and a quote, a highlight panel or a disclosure into plain blocks (a disclosure's title becomes a paragraph, its content follows) or into one another, what it holds carried along (`containerTargets()` / `convertContainer()`: a disclosure's title the first paragraph of the quote or highlight block; a quote or highlight block turned into a disclosure has every block of it in its content and the title empty, as a group's blocks go (the reader's rule); a new highlight block with 💡; from the block menu a new disclosure or highlight block is selected, as one a single block was turned into is; offered where the box takes the new container and the new container every block it holds, so a quote only for text and never a highlight block in a highlight block). In all: text blocks (文本, 小字, 标题 1–4, the list kinds, 待办) turn into one another and into a quote, a highlight block, a disclosure, a code block or a formula block; a whole list into another kind of list or paragraphs; a disclosure, quote or highlight block into another of them or into plain blocks; a divider into an empty block of any type. The type names, icons and search words are each type's in its component's description (`TYPE_INFO` in `components/registry.mjs`). A text block may be converted when it is a unit of its own in a box (or the item or paragraph of a list or quote that is a box), made on the page or in the file, and the document's script does not refer to it (it may count on its element; `convertible()`, checked again by the server). The conversion drops its class, which tells its type, and carries every other attribute, an id among them (on the page `standIn()`, in the file each unit's `carried` attributes as written), so links and ARIA still find it; a block a link or ARIA names converts and keeps its id, but still does not go. A divider line converts too (`dividerTargets()`, `convertDivider()`): into an empty block of the type in its place, written at once, the line going; `targetsOf()` lists the types its box `accepts`; a code block keeps its type (only undo turns one made by a conversion back). A block whose tag stays (a quote's paragraph becoming a paragraph, an item going to the other kind of list) is moved as it is; otherwise a new element stands for it with the original's identity (`data-ge-id`, see "Sync state"), so it is that unit (`data-ge-unit`), page order, handles and moves treat it as such, and the save converts it in place (`data-ge-convert`, written while the element's type differs from the type the unit showed as when last committed). A block turned into a list item, quote paragraph or panel paragraph joins the list, quote or panel of that kind right before its place, else right after it, else a new one (`newContainer()`: `data-ge-new`, a pure box of its own with `data-ge-box-kind`, going once empty); taking one out of a list, quote or panel splits it there, the blocks after it moving into a new container of the same kind. A new disclosure (`newDetails()`) is open while edited, with its title (`data-ge-kind="label"`) and a content region that is a box holding an empty paragraph; a block turned into one is that content (`holderAt()` in `convert.mjs`: an item or a quote's paragraph in a new list or quote of its kind there), the caret in the empty title (the block menu selects the disclosure). A new code block (`newCode()`) is a `div.doc-code` (the block, framed like the file's code blocks) holding its title (an empty `codeTitle` text block) and a `pre` holding a `code` text block, with no language of its own until the reader picks one (the kit highlights it in the language it looks like, below); a divider (`newDivider()`) replaces an empty block and is followed by an empty paragraph. The text of a new section's or disclosure's title and a new code block's code stand for their block (`unitElement()`). Every conversion is one structural undo step; the containers it made are recorded too (`result.created`). Every unit and box carries its kind on the page (`data-ge-unit-kind`, `data-ge-box-kind`, set by `captureLayout()`), so new and converted blocks read the same way. Triggers: the block handle's "+" opens the "/" menu with no "/" in the empty block it makes (`add`: what is typed there filters it; see "Deleting blocks"); "/" (or "、", "／") typed in a text block opens it — typed only: `markdown.typed` passes the input's `data` (or a composition's end) and the menu opens only when that ends with the character, as the "@" menu's, so a deletion, a cut, an undo or redo, a paste, the arrow keys or a click that leaves the caret after a "/" or "、" ("文字、文字、文字" with the middle 文字 deleted) opens nothing (scenario `slashOnlyTyped`, which also drives a key, an input method's text and a composition committed alone and with a phrase, all of which still open it) (`slash-menu/`; in text no block can come of but that takes links, as "@" goes by, a table's cell, a caption, a card's title, with 公式, 链接 and 页面 alone, the keys the menu's before the cell's: `typedKey`; a tab's name and a disclosure's title take no chip and open nothing): at the block's start, after a space, a whole in the text or a character no Latin word or address has ("and/or", "https:/" open nothing; "、" anywhere, but closing at once when nothing matches, so "甲、乙" stays a comma; it follows the pinyin a Chinese input method is composing, looking for its letters without the syllables' separators, "g's" as "gs", so it stays open, Enter putting the letters in and a second Enter choosing: scenario `slashMenuIme`), 324px wide, at most min(448px, 40vh) high and scrolling inside, below the caret or above it where there is more room (never over it: `menu.mjs` place); its card stands under the "/" itself, 12px below its line (8px under the "+"'s empty line), measured anew each time it is placed, so it no longer slides along as the reader types (the caret's place, 4px before it, until 2026-11-17); over the "/…" typed lies the "@" menu's pale field (`typed-field.mjs`: drawn over the text, never in it, so nothing of it reaches the block's words, a commit or undo, and no fuzz is asked; 0.5px corners and no outline (a 5.5px one, which Chrome rounds to 6px, covered the characters beside the "/" and reached into the lines above and below — the user's of 2026-11-20), so it is the text's own box, a box for each line, as wide as the typed text and no higher than the text's line-height: scenario `slashField`, light and dark), with 输入以搜索 after a lone "/" where nothing follows it and no input method composes; unfiltered it shows 建议 (the four rows chosen last, kept in localStorage) and the blocks by group (基本区块, 高级区块: media, code, tabs, formula blocks; 内联), filtered by what follows (name, pinyin, initials, English; while composing too) one list, best match first (in a block holding nothing else, the blocks before what goes in the text: "/gs" is 公式块 there, 公式 in text) (`matchScore()`: the name itself, a word itself, a word's start, inside one: "gl" is 高亮块 before 折叠列表), with ↑/↓, Enter or Tab and Esc while it is open; it closes when the "/" goes, the caret leaves for another block or goes before the "/", what follows starts with a space or ends with two, or three more characters come after nothing matched, none of which an input method's letters count for: what they turn into is looked for once they do, while they compose a row shown or 没有结果. In a block holding nothing but the "/…", the block chosen is what the block turns into; in one with text, a block of the type goes below it (`insertBelow()` in `editor.mjs`: the block split at its end as Enter would, the new block turned, one undo step; the spaces before a "/…" at the block's end go with it), and 公式 (the format bar's √x, named 公式: a formula block of its own would be 公式块) or 链接 put a formula (its field open) or a link (a gray 链接 there until its address is given, then its views offered) where the "/" was; Markdown at a block's start followed by a space (a type's `markdown`, tried in one loop by `markdownShortcut()` in `markdown/rules.mjs`, applied by `markdown/`: `#`, `##`, `###`, `####`, `-`/`*`/`+`, `1.`, `>`, `[]` and their full-width forms) converts at once, and so does a block holding nothing but `---` (a divider) or ```` ``` ```` (a code block: `alone`), what was typed being removed in the same step; `**text**` or `` `text` `` closed at the caret (`inlineMark()`) becomes bold or code through `formatRange()`, and the characters typed next go after the new element (typed text directly, an input method's text into a text node padded with a zero-width space while composing). Enter at the very start of a flow block or disclosure title with text adds an empty paragraph before it and the caret stays (`paragraphBefore()`; before a section's title the paragraph ends the section before), Enter on an empty nested item that is the last of what the item above it holds takes it out a level (as Shift+Tab), on one with more after it under that item (an item after it, a block after its list) turns it into body text where it stands, the paragraph held by that item and its list split around it (a block Enter carries the type of goes out a level only as its parent's last child, else turns into text; the user's of 2026-11-12, where Shift+Tab took the items after it under it), on an empty item of a top-level list turns it into body text where it stands (as Backspace there does), at the very start of an item with text puts an empty item above it, on an empty quote paragraph turns it into a paragraph after its quote, Enter on a panel's empty last paragraph moves it after the panel (`leaveBox()`: `typing/leave.mjs` is the one rule of where Enter and Backspace leave a container, read from what the components declare: a box that says `leaveByEnter` gives up its empty last block, a block of a type that says `resettable` (a quote's paragraph, a list item) turns into body text, Backspace at the start of a quote's first paragraph too, and a unit that says `removedEmpty` goes whole on Backspace when nothing is written in it), Enter in a disclosure's title puts the caret into its content (opened, with a new paragraph when it has none), Enter in a section's title (an `h2` heading a section, directly or in its header row) opens a paragraph at the start of the section. Nested list items: Tab puts an item under the one before it (`indentItem()`: at the end of that item's last list, or in a new list of the item's kind put at the end of that item; its own lists come along), Shift+Tab takes it back out right after the item it hung from (`outdentItem()`: the items after it in its list go to the end of its own last list, and the list it leaves goes when empty); Backspace at the start of a nested item takes it out too, and the first item of a list does not indent; selected items indent and outdent together (`nestBlocks()`, Tab and Shift+Tab with blocks selected: outdenting from the last, one undo step, the items staying selected). At the start of an item of a top-level list, Backspace turns it into body text where it stands: before or after the list for the first or last item, in between the list split around it (`takeOut()`; a numbered list's second part counts on, `data-continue`); an item holding lists becomes a paragraph followed by its lists (`toBodyText()`: the item goes, its lists move out as lists of the flow). Enter in the text of an item holding blocks splits it: the new item goes right after it in its list and holds all the item held (its lists, the paragraphs and pictures under it), with no text of its own over them when Enter was at the end of the text (`splitBlock()` with `carry`, `keepLine`; one undo step; the user's of 2026-11-12, where the words after the caret had become the first item of the item's first list), while the "/" menu's block below (`insertBelow`) still opens the item's first list; the line above an item may be deep inside the item before it (`previousLine()`, which merging joins); Delete at the end of an item joins the next one to it, its lists (if any) going under it (`mergeWithPrevious()`, into an item that may hold lists); an item holding lists does not change type, and emptied it is written so, the blocks it holds under it. Delete in such an item emptied takes nothing in and says how to delete it with what it holds (its handle, then Delete), and Backspace that cannot join it to the line above says so, each in the words of what it holds: its items (子项) where the blocks it holds are lists of items, its content (内容) where any is another block, a paragraph, an image or a task board (`holdsSays`, `mergeSays` in `components/list/index.mjs`; scenario `listDeleteHolds`). The kit draws the markers by depth ("Flows" under Design defaults), so nothing on the page tells the levels apart but the nesting. Detail cards: the format bar's card button makes the selected text a link to a new card (`dialogLink()`, `a.doc-dialog-trigger[href="#dialog-N"]`, the first id the page does not use, `freshDialogId()`) and puts the card (`newDialog()`: `dialog.doc-dialog` with a content region that is a box holding a heading 3 of the selected text and an empty paragraph) at the end of `main`, in one undo step; the card opens (`DocDialogs.open`) with the caret in that paragraph. "在下方新建一块" in a block's menu (`insertBelow()` in `editor.mjs`, which the "/" menu's blocks use too) makes the block of the type picked below it: a text block that splits splits at its end, as Enter there would, and the new block turns into the type; after any other block an empty paragraph of its box does (a block where no paragraph can go hands on to the block around it). The block menu (`block-menu/` on the card of `menu.mjs`, with no search: a card as wide as its grid of types (188px, `gridWidth()`, for every block, so no room is left beside the grid; a row with a hint, its current choice or its key, keeps its label's words and cuts the hint short with an ellipsis where the two do not fit, as an English row's choice may not: `menu.mjs`) that starts with the types the block can be turned into as a grid of icons, five to a line, with the format bar's buttons and spacing (32×28px, 4px apart) (`type-grid.mjs`, shared with the format bar; the icons are the types' own: thin lines, a serif T, a light H with its level small beside it; 小字, seldom used, has no cell of its own but is the gray triangle in the bottom-right corner of 文本's (a split button, shown only with the pointer or the arrow keys on 文本's cell, or when the block is 小字, not for a block that is 文本: a light gray, its tips a little rounded, its right angle rounded off by the cell's corner; darker under the pointer, blue when the block is 小字, not on the arrow keys' path), so 文本 and the four headings fill the first line; its own type marked as a pressed button of the format bar is, its icon's lines in the bar's blue (`--ge-accent`) with no fill of its own (the user's of 2026-11-07: a blue fill under the blue icon looked heavy), the pointer's gray filling it as any other cell, each icon's name and Markdown shortcut in a tooltip, a click on its own type just closes the menu); then the block's commands with icons (nothing highlighted when it opens; the type or command under the pointer is highlighted until the pointer leaves it, as the format bar's buttons; one the keys move to stays, and ↑ from nothing reaches the last; the "/" menu instead always keeps one row highlighted, the one Enter runs); a block that turns into nothing has its name as the title instead) has "在下方新建" (resting on it opens the "/" menu's blocks, `typeItems()`, one of which goes below the block through `insertBelow()`; not for a Sheet tab) and "在下方新建分区" among chapters. It keeps clear of the block (`around`): left of the handle when there is room, else below the block or above it (the larger side, scrolling inside when the menu does not fit), and over the block only when the block leaves neither; a block taller than a line of text (`TALL`, 34px: a table, a formula, a highlight, an image, a paragraph of two lines) is the exception, the menu beside its handle even where that is over the block, left of it first and right of it where there is no room left, centered on the handle (pushed in at the window's edges) and never over the handle (the user's, 2026-11-09; scenario `handleMenuBeside`). The page is in a frame in the workbench and draws nothing outside it, so the left room is the frame's own: no room there, the menu goes right of the handle and does not cover the sidebar. An empty convertible block with the caret shows "输入 / 选择类型" (`data-ge-placeholder`, a `::before` out of the flow), an empty heading its level ("标题 1"–"标题 4", fainter), a new section's empty title "分区标题", a new disclosure's "折叠列表标题", a new card's "弹窗标题". Code blocks: on entering edit mode a code block keeps the lines `code.js` highlighted and is only marked (`data-ge-code`, its baseline from its source, `codeBaseline()`); a click into it opens it (`openCode()`: its source text in a `code` text block, the caret at the character clicked), as do the arrow keys reaching it from the blocks beside it (Enter on it selected puts a paragraph after it). In code, Enter breaks the line keeping the indentation, Tab indents two spaces, a paste keeps its lines; the text block ends with a line break of its own (shown as the last line, not code, `codeText()`). An open code block is highlighted again as it is typed (`highlightCode()`: `DocCode.View` renders the text off the page, its lines' spans replace the block's content and the caret goes back to its offset; not while an input method composes), and its line numbers are a column of their own before the code (`syncGutter()`: `span.ge-code-gutter`, `contenteditable=false`, `data-doc-dev`, none with `data-line-numbers="false"`), so neither is ever saved: the code saved is `codeText()`. On saving, `convertedUnits()` sends `{ id, replace: { type, html } }` for a converted text block (an emptied one is deleted instead) and `{ id, retag }` for a list; `placedUnits()` goes through every box as for moves and sends each new block that is not a part of a text block as `{ id: "n<k>", insert, place }` next to the unit that stayed before it (or after it, or into the box), in page order. `nodeOf()` gives `{ type, html }` for a text block (a code block's code, with its `language`; a list item with the lists it holds as `children`), `{ type }` for a divider, `{ type, id, title, children }` for a detail card (only while a link on the page opens it), `{ type, children }` for a container (a disclosure with its `title`, its children being its content) and `{ unit }` for an original unit inside a container made on the page; a unit inside such a container that was deleted is deleted too. The server (`applyEdits()`): `replace` rewrites the unit's element (the comments above it stay) (a change the file made to it meanwhile is a conflict of the commit, and `force` keeps the reader's version), checks the type (`LEAVES`), the inline markup (`validLeaf()`; none in code) and, where the unit stays, that its box takes the new kind; a moved or placed converted unit is checked with its new kind (with its own kind when the file refuses the conversion: never a paragraph moved into a list for the item it was to be). A bare attribute its kind sets (a to-do item's `data-checked`) the file wrote with a value (`data-checked="true"`, which the kit reads as there) goes bare when the page writes the item again (`switched()` in `core.mjs`: an item turned back by undo), as the file takes it. `retag` rewrites the two tag names only. `insert` checks its place like a move (`accepts`, an anchor that stays), its node recursively (types, markup, what each container holds, each original unit once and never one deleted or moved, nor a tab), takes the original units it holds from their places like moves (entries with `at: null`, written by the node) and writes the node in the kit's plain markup at the anchor's indentation on a line of its own, a new section set apart like the chapters around it (`separator()`), a container's children one indentation step of the file deeper, a section as `<section class="doc-section">` with its blocks, a disclosure as `<details class="doc-details">` with its summary label and content region (closed), a panel as `<div class="doc-panel">` and a callout as `<div class="doc-callout">` (each holding its blocks), a code block as `<div class="doc-code" data-language="…"><div class="doc-code-title">…</div><pre><code>` with its lines as they are (a language is a name, a title plain text on one line, both checked), a divider as `<hr>`, small text as `<p class="doc-note">`, an item with its lists as `<li>text` followed by them, a list going into an item that sits on one line (`<li>text</li>`) on lines of its own below the text (`itemIndent()`), and a detail card as the kit writes one (`<dialog class="doc-dialog" id>`, its panel and `.doc-dialog-content` with its blocks), its id refused when the file uses it already. A list item's text is written in place (only its text range), and items split off it are inserted right before and after it, so the lists inside it can change in the same save. Placements at the same place keep the order of the operations (`seq`), and `compose()` refuses a region that would end up inside itself. *Several blocks at once* (`convert.mjs` `groupTargets()` and `convertGroup()`, `editor.mjs` `convertAll()`): the block menu of several selected blocks (and the format bar over text across blocks) offers, below "已选中 N 块", the types they can all be turned into, in the grid of one block's menu (five to a line, the same keys), only when every one of them can be turned (all or nothing: a text block with types, a list item, a whole list or quote; with a card, a table, a code block, a divider, a media block, a disclosure, a highlight block or any other whole block among them there is none), their type marked only when they all share it (a mix marks none), no divider (that is for one empty block). A pick turns them in document order as one undo step, a block already of the type left as it is: a text block or an item as one block's conversion turns it (`convertBlock`, so a turned item joins the list of its new kind right before or after it, and a list it leaves splits), a whole list into another kind of list or paragraphs as its own menu does (`convertContainer`) and into any other type item by item (each item a block of its own), a whole quote paragraph by paragraph (its paragraphs are text blocks of their own, as text across them gives them: a box over one turns as a drag over its text does). A block inside another of the group goes with it (`membersOf`: text across an item and the list it holds turns the item, its content staying as it is). Every item of one list in the group (a box over a list gives its items) turned into another kind of list is that list turned (`retagList`: its nesting, first number and direction kept, the items the same elements); into or out of 步骤流程图 the list is made a flowchart node by item, and back (`flowchartFromList()`, `listFromFlowchart()`: each item a node, its words its bold title, what it holds its content; "Flowcharts"), so a numbered list's items become one flowchart, and back, and the nodes of a flowchart given in part turn one by one into items between its parts (`convertStep()`); a flowchart's title turns into nothing, so text across titles is offered no type. An item holding blocks stays an item (the file keeps such an item's type): it goes to another kind of list (into 步骤流程图 a node with its content) or into a highlight block or a disclosure, and is offered nothing else (nothing it holds is moved out after it to make way for a type that cannot hold it). One such item's block menu and the format bar over its words offer the same (`turnTargets()`, which the group's `memberTargets` asks too; it offered nothing before), wherever it stands in its list: from the middle, `convertBlock` takes it out as any item (the list split around it: a numbered list's items after it from 1 again, a flowchart's two parts keeping its direction, a to-do's too), the element moved with what it holds (only the type changes, what it holds kept), one undo step that selects it (scenario `turnMiddleItems`, as for middle items of a box). Lists the group turned into the type that then stand side by side are one (`joins()` of `components/list/join.mjs`: free, the second with no start of its own, flowcharts laid out alike), so two bulleted lists and the paragraph between them turned into 编号列表 are one numbered list, consecutive items being one run; two it found side by side and left as they were (already of the type) stay two, as `join.mjs` keeps a pair written apart. Into 高亮块, and into 折叠列表 (`BOXED` in `convert.mjs`; the reader's rule for a disclosure, whose title is left empty): one new highlight block (disclosure) at the first block's place (an item's list split there), every block moved into it (its content) keeping its type (for one block as for several: one item or quote paragraph turned into 高亮块 goes in as it is too, in a new list or quote of its kind, `convertBlock`), an item or a quote's paragraph in a list or quote of its kind there (the one before it when that is of its kind), what a block of the group holds going with it; a container they all leave (a section, a column) goes, as when they are deleted or dragged away (`removalTarget`; `groupParents` names the boxes holding it, so undo puts it back). Afterwards what the blocks are now is selected (a list turned whole the items again, those of a list joined to another among them, a highlight block or disclosure itself); undo selects what they were and redo what they are (`selectAfter` of a `struct` step, `history.mjs`) (scenarios `groupTurnInto`, `turnIntoDisclosure`; `tests/unit/editor.mjs`).
- **Empty blocks.** Every block left empty stays (a reader places blocks first and fills them later): a paragraph, heading or list item is written empty (`<p></p>`); a code block too: by its type's `empty` (`describeNode`, `keepsWhenEmpty` (an emptied block of such a type stays, written empty) and its counterpart `dropsWhenEmpty` (one that may go and is not kept: it goes with its save), the server's `emptyLeaf`: no other list says it; an emptied one stays, one made on the page too, since a block the page makes has its kind's caps, `capsFor`); a callout as a highlight block (its paragraph empty, "Callouts"); a formula block as `\[\]` (its placeholder 添加一个 TeX 公式 on the page; emptied, it stays); a disclosure with no title and no blocks; the media blocks (图片, 文件, 网页书签, 嵌入) as an empty figure (`<figure class="doc-image"></figure>`: each `file.mjs`'s `empty` node, the shared description's `empty`, which the component checks pass), which the kit shows to no reader while the page edited shows its placeholder bar, a click filling it as a new one would be. The five gray bars (the media blocks' and the formula block's) are one look (`components/placeholder.mjs`): the media gap above and below whatever the block's own gap once filled, the bar 49px high, its icon 25px wide 12px before its words (the formula's TeX mark as wide), one tint under the pointer, the selection 6px past it with corners of 16px. A block goes only when the reader deletes it (Backspace in an empty container, the block menu, Delete on a selection). A formula, link or page block in the text left empty stays too: the "/" menu's 公式, 链接 or 页面 given nothing, or a formula emptied, is `<span class="doc-math-inline">\(\)</span>`, `<a class="doc-link-mention"></a>` or `<a class="doc-link-mention" data-doc-page></a>` (an atom: `source/blocks.mjs` takes the empty link, the kit shows it to no reader and spaces neither), shown on the page as a gray chip holding its icon and name (`data-ge-empty`, `markEmpty`), so that the browser keeps a caret beside it, at a line's end too, and deletes it whole as it does a formula; copied and pasted as one, and a click fills it. All of it shows from the first paint, as once the editor has started: the server serves the page with it (`service.mjs` `preview`: `showEmpty` puts the bars and chips into the page, a formula block left empty is served empty, `EMPTY_STYLE` goes into its head). Their words are the UI's language's: the server, which does not know it (the reader's browser keeps it), serves the bars' and chips' words in Chinese (`inLanguage("zh", …)`, `src/shared/i18n/`), and the page's first script (`emptyWordsScript`, in its head) says them in English as the page has loaded, before the kit shows it, when its root says the UI is English (`data-ge-language`); an empty formula block's words are in `EMPTY_STYLE` in both, the root choosing (scenario `kitWords`), and the kit gives an empty disclosure title, and a callout the file has empty, a line as it does an empty paragraph (scenario `emptyFirstPaint`). A disclosure's content, a highlight block, a callout and a flowchart's node always hold a block on the page (`holdsOne` in their descriptions, `core.mjs` `tidyEmptied`, one of the tidiers every structural step runs, `history.mjs`): a step that leaves one empty (its blocks deleted, cut, dragged out) puts an empty paragraph in it (after a highlight block's or a node's icon) in the same step, and one that leaves a node with another block first (`firstBlock`: its title gone) puts an empty title first, saved as any new empty paragraph; undo takes it out with the rest, and deleting only that empty paragraph does nothing and says nothing (`core.mjs` `keptAnyway`, `selection/delete.mjs`). One the file has empty (an Agent's) shows a placeholder in a disclosure ("空白折叠区块。点击或拖动区块到这里。", `details/page.mjs`), a highlight block of only its icon is a line high (the kit's `highlight.css`), and a click in either makes a paragraph there with the caret (after the icon; `insert/` `clickBlank`) (scenario `disclosureKeepsOne`); copied or cut and pasted, a highlight block or a callout the file has empty comes with an empty paragraph in it (after the icon), as a disclosure made again has one in its content (`own/blocks.mjs` `build`: a box that always holds a block described with none), which the commit writes, where a box of no blocks is written by none (`nodeOf`) and was gone at the next reload, a cut's from the file (scenario `emptyBoxPasted`). What still waits to be written is what cannot stand half done: a detail card without a title and a new row of columns with an empty column (a list item holding blocks with no text of its own is written so, "What changed").
- **Sections.** A section (`section.doc-section`, 分区) is a container of ordinary blocks, and stands in the page or a Sheet panel only. Its title is whatever heading it holds (a plain `h2`; the line under it that says what the section is an ordinary paragraph, there is no wrapper around the two: `.doc-section-header` is gone, `gallery check` says so), and a line above it is an `<hr>` at its top: blocks with handles of their own that change type, move and go like any other. The kit draws nothing for a section itself (it has no stylesheet: one that starts with a divider reads as a chapter set apart by a line), and a section's own `aria-labelledby` keeps no heading in it (`keepReferenced()`; the heading deleted, the label names nothing).
