The block menu of a block standing in the page or a panel, and of a section, has "在下方新建分区" (`sectionAfter()` in `components/section/page.mjs`: a new section right after it holding one empty paragraph, written at once, as `<section class="doc-section">` with its blocks); a section's has "解散分区" (`unwrapSection()`, `unwrapCheck()`: its blocks where it was, set apart as the blocks of the page are, the section gone with its identity; not when something refers to the section itself, `data-ge-keep-own`, or it holds what cannot move, a script say). From the section its first text block takes the caret, undo and redo bringing it back there (no caret stands in a display formula: its first block is selected when no text block is). A block in a section has that row in its own "分区与分栏 ›", as "解散所在分区", in the sections' group, and so have several selected all in one section (`dissolveRow`, `block-menu/`; `dissolvesFrom()`: the innermost section around them that row applies to, so that a section in a section goes with the one around it, none past a detail card): the section's own check, a row greyed with its reason, undo step and words. Where the section's own row puts the caret in its first block, this one leaves the blocks it was asked from selected, on undo and redo too, and the page where it is, for a block may stand far down a long section. What the section holds comes out as it stands: a block in a column keeps its column, the row of columns coming out whole, and a section in a Sheet panel leaves its blocks in the panel, where it stood. A block with no such submenu gets none for it (a list item, a flowchart's node, a quote's later paragraph, a block an item holds), and a section's own menu has no second row. Each is one undo step, and undoing a dissolve makes the section again around its blocks (a new one). Blocks already there become a section with "组成新分区" (its icon: lines between brackets, apart from "在下方新建分区") in the menu of selected blocks or of a single block (`wrapCheck()` and `wrapSection()` in `components/section/page.mjs`): only neighbours in the page or a Sheet panel, none of them a section or Sheet area, with nothing left out between them (a selection with gaps is not quietly completed; the row is disabled with the reason); the new section stands where they stood and holds them in their order, one undo step that selects them again on undo, written as `<section class="doc-section">` around their source. A folded heading that with what it hides is all of a section (but for dividers before it, as an Agent writes a chapter: `<section>`, `<hr>`, its heading) stands for the section: dragged, moved, copied or deleted, the section goes, its divider in it ("Folding a heading's section").
- **Folding a heading's section.** On the page that edits (not in an export, 演示视图 or a copy of the view: what is hidden is marked only while editing, `[data-ge-editing] [data-ge-folded]`, and `data-ge-*` never leaves the page), a heading the outline lists that stands straight in a box and has words (`fold/rule.mjs` `foldable`: not the title, nor one in a list item, a table, a card or a detail card; an empty one has no triangle) folds its section: what follows it in its box up to the first block that ends it, a heading of its level or higher or a block holding one the outline lists (a section, a tabs area, a row of columns, a disclosure, a highlight block), which is the outline's nesting (`outline/tree.mjs` `treeOf`), never past its box; a block's leads go with it. All that shows of the section is hidden, what stands among its blocks without being one too (`rule.mjs` `hiddenAfter`: an `<h4>` the parser closed with an `</h3>`, which the file has no end tag of its own for, a paragraph left open, a `<header>`, an element of the report's own name; only a block ends it). Until 2026-11-17 those stayed in sight, so of an h3's two h4s one could stay under it folded (the user's report). What shows nothing of itself (a script, a style, a detail card's dialog, the editor's own) is neither hidden nor ends it. The ghost of blocks an Agent deleted ("Agent's changes") stands where they were: at a place in a folded section (the block before it hidden by the fold, or the folded heading itself: `folded.mjs` `foldOver`) it goes with the fold, its room too, unless they held a heading of the folded one's level or higher, which would have ended the section (`changes/ghost.mjs` `foldedAway`); until 2026-11-17 the ghost of what was deleted at a folded section's end stayed under it. The user's wish of 2026-11-06 (第 7 条: to move a section, fold it and move its heading); a fold is the reader's view, no undo step, no commit, no notice, never in the file; kept per report in the browser (`gallery-edit:heading-folds`, `{ folds: { <slug>: [key…] } }` as the outline's, 100 reports), a heading keyed `b:<block id>` or with none `t:<tag>:<words>#<n>` (its place among the foldable headings of its tag and words, with an id or not, so one given its id by a save is found again), written at each toggle and at `pagehide`; while the page lives, the folded headings are their identities (`data-ge-id`: turned into another level, or put back by undo, a heading stays folded). What a fold hides is marked `data-ge-folded` (the outermost folded heading's identity; a folded heading a fold around it hides starts no section of its own) and shown as nothing; the page shown again after an Agent's write folds it again in the task editing begins in (`fold/` `start`, from `enter`), what the Agent added in the section hidden with it. The triangle (`.ge-fold`: a 16×16 button, round 4px, the box a press finds, drawn as nothing but `FOLD_ARROW` 12px in `--ge-ico3`; no fill under the pointer, the triangle `--ge-ico2` there as the outline's triangles darken, a short `--ge-press` only while pressed as a code block's arrow; pointing down while open (as drawn) and right while folded (turned -90°), as a code block's and a disclosure's arrows (the user's 2026-11-19: 「小标题的三角标方向反了，应该是展开时朝下、折叠时朝右」; from 2026-11-12, with the badge, it pointed right open, at the badge, and down folded, which was the wrong way round; from their 2026-11-07 第 4 条 it pointed down open and left folded, at the words, and before that it was a 20×20 button filled `--ge-hover` under the pointer, turned -90° to point away from the words), over .2s ease-out, none with reduced motion) stands in the editor's layer 6px right of the end of the heading's last line, its triangle's middle 14px past the words as before, on that line's middle (`foldRectOf`: the last box of the heading's words), so it never touches the words or the file and a heading written to the column's end has it in the margin; it shows while the pointer is at the heading as the handle finds it (`handle.pointed`: of a highlight block, its first block), not while typing, scrolling by the wheel, drawing a box, dragging or dragging a gap, and always while folded, its level badge with it ("A heading's level badge"); its tooltip 折叠 or 展开 with `Ctrl+Enter` (`⌘↩`); with the pointer on it, the 批注 button beside the block steps aside (`aside`). While a press that began off the triangles is held (words being selected, a box drawn, a block dragged), they take no pointer, nor do the level badges (`html[data-ge-fold-held] :is(.ge-fold, .ge-level) { pointer-events: none }`, set by a capturing `pointerdown` on the window before any tool sees the press, gone at `pointerup`, `pointercancel`, `blur` or a move with no button down): those shown stay shown (the handle's block stays while a button is down), none darkens, none folds, and the browser's selection and the page's across blocks (`text-select/` `pointAt`) find the heading's line under them as if they were not there. Under the pointer the triangle was the node the drag hit: outside the heading's editable text, the browser put the selection's moving end at the heading's start, and `pointAt` fell back to the heading's start or end by which half of its box the pointer was in, so pressing right of a heading's words and moving left selected the whole heading while the pointer crossed the triangle, and moving right from its words over the triangle selected back to its start (the user's 2026-11-07 第 6 条; `headingFoldDrag`). A press on it moves no caret and takes no focus; Ctrl/⌘+Enter in a heading's words folds and unfolds it (`components/heading/page.mjs`, the toggle's `commandEnter`). Folding puts a caret, or an end of a text selection, that was in the section at the end of the heading's words, and what was selected of it leaves the selection. A folded heading stands for its section wherever blocks are taken away whole (`fold/` `takenWith`): dragging (into a row of columns too), Ctrl+Shift+arrows, Ctrl+C and Ctrl+X on selected blocks, deleting them (Delete, Backspace, the block menu's 删除), 组成新分区, a row of columns made around it (a block dropped beside it, its block menu's 分两栏, 分三栏, 分四栏, or 分成 n 栏 with blocks next to it: `withHidden`, its column holding what it hides, and neither offered where a column does not take all of that, `columnsCheck`'s `taken`, drag/'s `aside`; it went into the column alone before, its section left under the row and its triangle still turned with nothing under it), and text selected across blocks going on past it ("Selecting text across blocks"); and where it and what it hides are all of a section but for dividers before it, the section itself is taken (an Agent's chapter, `<section>` with its `<hr>` and heading, moves whole). What changes a block itself (turning it into another type, its color, its words, a 批注) changes the heading alone. One rule opens a fold of itself: a block a change of the page puts into a folded section, one that was in sight or new, opens the folds over it, outermost first, unless it came onto the page with its heading in that same change (undo putting a folded section back, a folded section dragged) or stands for a block that was hidden (by its identity: a hidden heading turned into another level by 所有标题增大一级 under a folded 标题 1 stays hidden): Enter at a folded heading's end (the new paragraph is right under it), the handle's "+", 在下方新建, a paste or a drop below it, an h3 turned into an h2 (a longer section) and the heading that ended the section deleted all open it. A folded heading's words deleted open it (it has no section then). Someone else's write taken in where the page stands (the pull, "The pull": `folds.pulled()`, right after it changed the page) opens none: what is folded stays so, and what the write put into a folded section is hidden with it, as on a page shown anew after it (scenario `headingFold`), but the caret or an end of a selection left in what is hidden now opens the folds over it. The caret or an end of a selection put into a hidden block (undo, a program) opens it (`onSelectionChange`); Backspace at a block's start whose line above is hidden, and Delete at a block's end whose line below is, open it before they join (never words joined out of sight); ↑/↓ pass over what is hidden as over anything not shown (`caret.mjs` `places`). Everything that brings a block into sight opens the folds over it first (`page/folded.mjs` `reveal`, then the kit's `DocKit.reveal`): a link to a block, the workbench's `reveal-block`, the outline (a row of a heading a fold hides; a folded heading's own row leaves it folded), a 批注 the workbench's list goes to, a conflict's card, 搜索文档's hit. A 批注 on what a fold hides stands by the folded heading, as one in a closed disclosure by its title (`annotate/` `closedAround`). Enter at a folded heading's end is a heading's Enter, not a heading of its level made after the section: in a flat file that would be a heading the reader never asked for. Scenarios `headingFold`, `headingFoldEdits`, `headingFoldReveal`, `headingFoldDrag`, `headingFoldWhole`; the rule in jsdom in `tests/unit/heading-folds.mjs`.
- **A heading's level badge.** Right of a heading's fold triangle stands its level (the user's 2026-11-12: to see a heading's level as the pointer comes to it), `.ge-level` in `fold/`: `levelIcon(level)` (`components/icons.mjs`), H1–H4, the H and the figure one height, 10px high on a 22×16 drawing, in a 26×22 button (until the next day the type grid's H with its level small beside it, 16px in a 20×20 button, which the user found too small: 「应该就是 H1、H2」), round 4px, 2px into the triangle's button and on its middle (`levelRectOf`), in the triangle's `--ge-ico3`. It shows and goes with the triangle, in the same frames and by the same headings (`foldable`: the heading the pointer is at, and every folded one; none in an export, 演示视图, a detail card or the file: the editor's layer, owned as `heading level`), and the triangle stands left of it, down while the heading is open and right while folded. A flat button, no line: `--ge-hover` fill and `--ge-ico2` under the pointer and while its menu stands, `--ge-press` while pressed; while a press that began elsewhere is held it takes no pointer, as the triangle does. Its tooltip 标题 N, with 调整标题的级别 under it. A press on it moves no caret, takes no focus and changes no selection (blocks.mjs passes presses on the fold tools by); a click opens the editor's menu card below it (`menu.mjs`, the block tools' card, so it and a block menu close each other; never a browser's dropdown), two rows of words, 增大一级 and 减小一级 (that heading alone, by the same rule as every heading; the user's 2026-11-20), a line, then 所有标题增大一级 and 所有标题减小一级, nothing highlighted; a click on the badge again, a press elsewhere, Esc, a scroll, the window resized or left, or a row run closes it, and while it stands its badge stays shown and filled wherever the pointer goes. Its keys go on the window before the editor's, the caret being where it was (in text, maybe): ↑/↓ move in it, Enter runs the row highlighted (with none it closes), Esc closes it, any other key closes it and goes on. The rows (`editor.mjs` `shiftAllHeadings`, `convert.mjs` `headingShifts` and `shiftHeadings`) turn every heading of the document's flows its block menu could turn (`HEADING` outside `LEFT_OUT`, the headings the outline lists: in the page, sections, highlight blocks, columns, disclosures and tab panels, an empty one too; not the report's title, which is no block, a detail card's title, a heading in a list item or a table, nor an author's h5 or h6, which is no type) a level larger (h4 to h3, h2 to h1; 标题 1 stays) or smaller (h1 to h2, h3 to h4; 标题 4 becomes a paragraph whose words are bold throughout: one `<strong>` around them, `formatRange` as Ctrl+B on all of them puts it, none added where they are bold throughout already), the range being the editor's 标题 1–4, as each type's `levels` says ("Component definitions"). Each is turned as its block menu turns it (`convertBlock`: a new element standing for it, `data-ge-convert`, so the commit converts the unit in place, its block id, color, alignment and words with their marks going with it), all of them one `struct` step (`caret: true`: undo puts the caret back where it stood) and one commit; with none to turn (every heading 标题 1, raised) nothing changes and nothing is written, and it is said 没有可以增大一级的标题. A caret or a text selection in a heading turned stays where it was in its words (by offsets, in what stands for the heading now), selected blocks stay selected (a heading turned as what it is now), and it is said 已将 N 个标题增大一级 (减小一级) · Ctrl+Z 撤销. A folded heading turned stays folded (by its identity), and a heading it hides, turned, stays hidden; one made a paragraph folds no more. An Agent changes levels by editing the HTML: no command, the workbench's menus alone being `ui-commands.mjs`'s. Scenario `headingLevels`; the turning, and what the file says after it, in jsdom in `tests/unit/editor.mjs`.
- **Columns.** A row of columns (分栏, `div.doc-columns` holding 2 to 5 `div.doc-column`; `components/columns/`) is a container whose columns are flows: a column takes every block a flow takes but a row of columns (`accepts`: no rows in rows), and a row stands wherever a flow takes a block. Neither the row nor its columns has a handle: their blocks do, empty space in a column finds the block beside it, "选中外层" from a block in a column selects the row (a column is passed over, `outerOf`), and a box selection across columns takes the blocks it touches in each (`pick`). A row is made three ways. A drop beside a block (`drag/drop.mjs` `asideOf`, only for blocks a column takes): in the right third of a block of a flow or right of it (up to 200px out), or left of it, from past all of its handle (52px out: `OUT`, the grip and the "+" column left of it) up to 200px out, the dragged blocks and that block stand side by side in a new row (`rowAround`; a folded heading's column holds what it hides, "Folding a heading's section"), so a drag that goes up or down, from anywhere on the grip and with the drift a hand has, stays between blocks: the band starts 24px left of the grip. A framed Sheet panel's blocks, which its padding sets in, take that start from the page's left edge, 52px out from it, so that the handle of a page block going by there holds off as far as beside the page's own blocks; a detail card's take it from the card's content, left of its padding. In a window with no room for the band, the outline in full taking the column's rooms down to 52px, its start stays in the window, 10px in, 16px clear of the grip kept 26px in there (`FIRST`; the edge room's 64px leave it 12px of its own); the line stands down that side of the block, the block tinted, with no label. In a row, within 16px of the last column's right edge (in its right third) and past it, left of the first column by the same band, or between two columns in a band 32px wide left of the next column's handle (`BETWEEN`, ending 52px out from that column as a block's band starts: the 20px gap, narrower than the handle standing in it, leaves the band only the end of the column before; the line stands in the gap), they go into a new column there (`addColumn`, `ratiosAfterAdd`: it takes 1/n of the row and the others make room in proportion); when they are all the blocks of one column of that row, that column moves instead, keeping its width. Over the handle of a column past the first, all 52px of it, a point counts as in that column (`overHandle`), as one left of the page counts at the page's edge, so that a block of that column dragged up or down, with the drift a hand has, moves among that column's blocks. At most 5 columns (a reading width holds no more). The columns rows of a block's menu, in "分区与分栏 ›" (an operation on the block as a whole; `columnsCheck`, `components/columns/page.mjs`, `columnsIcon`): "分两栏", "分三栏", "分四栏" make a block in a flow the left column of a new row whose other columns hold a new empty paragraph each, the caret in the first of them; in a column, "在右侧新增一栏", a new column right of its own; several blocks next to each other in one container, "分成 n 栏", a column each. Every structural change keeps rows tidy (`tidyRows`, a tidier `editor.mjs` gives `history.mjs`: `struct` records the rows around the containers it touches, their columns and the columns' widths with the step, and runs its `settleRows` inside the step): a column left empty goes (its share to the others in proportion, `ratiosAfterRemove`), and a row left with one column gives its blocks back where it stood, so a drag, a deletion, a cut or a merge never leaves an empty column or a row of one. Widths: each column's share of the row, `--doc-column-ratio` in its style attribute (a size, `SIZES`), on every column of a row or on none (equal); the kit lays them out in proportion. The gaps (`columns/`): with the pointer in the gap between two columns a 2px gray line stands down it (`--ge-guide-hot`, fainter above the row on the way to its + , `--ge-guide`; never a bright blue, the user's of 2026-11-07), with a col-resize cursor; dragging it moves only the two columns beside it (`ratiosAfterResize`: snapping to their equal split and to tenths of the row within 5px, `SNAP`, the line then darkest, `--ge-guide-snap`; 1% of the row until 2026-11-07, which the user found too strong; each at least 1/24), shown as it goes (the columns' `flex-grow`, nothing written) with the row's shares in the bottom notice once the pointer has moved ("栏宽：40% / 60%", no badge on the columns), and letting go writes the shares as one structural undo step (Esc cancels); a double click makes the row's columns equal again. The same tool resizes the nodes of a flowchart's row ("Flowcharts"): each kind of row (`KINDS`) says how its parts are found, measured, shown while dragged and written. A press in a gap is the tool's: no box selection starts there (`selection/marquee.mjs` yields to `data-ge-gap`, its listener coming first), the handle steps aside while a gap is dragged (`handle/`), and a double click is told by two presses on the same gap within 500ms and 4px (the first press's drag holds the pointer on the document element, so the browser counts no double click there). Above each gap, and right of the last column, a gray dot turns on hover into a round + (a white disc with a hairline and a gray +, the look of the editor's small floating buttons): a click adds a column there holding an empty paragraph with the caret. While the pointer is over a row or the caret in it, its columns are tinted (a light grey, 4px past them). A click below a column's last block writes in that column (`endOf`). Committed, a row made on the page is `{ type: "columns", children }` of `{ type: "column", ratio?, children }` (`nodeOf`), written once each of its columns has something to write (a new paragraph its first text; until then, once one of them holds something, the empty ones are a block not finished: kept in the crash buffer, holding off the reload, and said so: the column type's `blankSays`, its row's `blanks`; a unit of the file moved into it stays as the file has it, its conversion and move going with the row once it is written), as `<div class="doc-columns">` with `<div class="doc-column" style="--doc-column-ratio: …">` (`components/columns/file.mjs`); undo putting back a row that went while its blocks went elsewhere makes it again, with its columns (`revive`).
- **Code blocks.** A code block (`div.doc-code`; `components/code/`) is its title above its code. The kit draws the header around the title (`code.js` `decorate`): a fold arrow before it and, after it, a toolbar shown while the pointer is over the block (the language; copy; wrap). Its language is its `data-language`, else the one it looks like (自动识别, `code.js` `autoLanguage`): its title's file extension when the kit knows it, else its code's shape (`guessLanguage()` in `src/shared/code-languages.mjs`, which also lists the languages `code.js` bundles grammars for), so a block written without one is highlighted all the same, and one being typed follows its code (and its title). Long lines wrap; in a reading view the arrow and the wrap button switch the block's classes `doc-code--folded` (only its header shows) and `doc-code--nowrap` (long lines scroll instead); while the page is edited the editor takes those clicks (`headerClick`) and makes each switch one undo step, saved as a class switch (`TOGGLES`; several switches of one block in one commit are written together). Folded, its toolbar is its language and copy alone (no bar), shown as an open block's only while the pointer is over the block or the focus is in it (showing them all the time the user turned down on 2026-11-06), and its language is plain text, not picked then (folding closes its menu). Unfolded, the language label opens a menu below it (the card of `menu.mjs` with its search field, `search`): 自动识别 first, with the language it looks like beside it, then the bundled languages, the current one checked; what is picked is set as `data-language` (自动识别 removes it), one undo step (`look` with `attributes`), saved as an attribute change (`ATTRIBUTES`), and the block is drawn again in it, the open code by the editor, else its lines by the kit (`DocCode.draw`), whenever the attribute changes (undo and redo too). Open code wraps as the kit's does, its line numbers following the rows (`syncGutter()`: a wrapped line's number at its first row, blank ones under it, measured again as the code's width changes); folded, the open code hides too. A line that starts a structure folds (`src/render/kit/code-folds.js`, its scanners: brackets, indented bodies, elements, statements, shell keywords, Markdown sections; the regions shared by the kit and the editor): an arrow left of its number, in a column every code block keeps under its own fold arrow (centered 14px from the block's edge, as the header's; so nothing stands between the numbers and the code) (the numbers left-aligned on the line its title's text starts on, as wide as the last one, `--doc-code-digits`; without numbers, the code there) (the kit's in its lines, `foldLines`; the open code's in its gutter, `ge-code-fold`), rounded and light, shown while the pointer is over the block, a folded one always; folding hides the lines inside the structure and puts "…" after the line (a click on it unfolds it). The lines folded are the block's `data-folded-lines` (their numbers from 1): an arrow or "…" clicked while the page is edited is one undo step (`look` with `attributes`), saved as an attribute change, a press on either opening no code and moving no caret, and the block folds again as it says (`refold`: the kit's lines, or the open code's). Open code is drawn a span a line (`.ge-code-line`, each but the first starting with its line break, so a hidden line takes its break with it), marked foldable, folded or hidden; through typing the folds follow their lines (`carriedFolds`: a folded line's span keeps its place through the browser's edit; a fold whose structure went, or a line made or joined with the caret inside a folded structure, opens it), and the gutter writes the block's `data-folded-lines` from the lines, so undoing text brings its folds back with it. A code block made on the page carries its folded lines into the file with it (`folded` in its node). The title is a text block of its own (`codeTitle`: plain text, may be empty; "请输入代码块名称" while empty under the caret; Enter goes into the code, unfolding the block first, `titleEnter`). Copying a code block takes its title, its picked language and the number its first line shows with its code (`lineStart`: the copy numbers from it too, and the file writes it, `codeHtml`). A code block numbers its lines from its `data-line-start` (an Agent's excerpt numbered as in its file, taken in by naturalize; 1 without one), the kit in its lines and the editor in its gutter (`syncGutter`), which keeps the attribute and sets it only on a pasted copy of one (`data-folded-lines` counts the block's lines from 1 all the same; turned into text, the block leaves it, `codeAttribute`). A `pre` written straight in the text, without its block, is no code block (`gallery check` says how to write one); nor does the block read its own attributes on its `pre` or code (`data-line-start`, `data-start`, `data-line-numbers`, `data-folded-lines`, `data-title`), which `gallery check` says go on the block, nor a `data-line-start` that writes no number, which it says too (`component-checks.mjs` `codeFailures`).
- **Tables.** A table (`div.doc-table-wrap` holding `table.doc-table`, `components/table/`) is one unit whose cells are text blocks (`cell`: inline text and links). Its rows and columns change on the page as one operation of the table (`grid.mjs`): the page keeps each table's grid (its rows and cells by identity, each cell's tag, scope, look and its column's width) as the last commit left it (`captureLayout`), and a commit whose grid changed sends `{ table: { rows } }` (`tableChange`): each row and cell by where it was then (`from`) or new, a cell's text only where it changed (a new cell's always), its look and width only where they changed; the server writes the table's new inside (`source/table.mjs` `rewriteTable`, guarded by the table's hash: someone else's change meanwhile is a conflict), what stayed byte for byte (a row whose cells all stay is copied whole, a cell keeps its start tag and text; one whose look, width, scope or spans changed has those attributes changed where they stand, the rest of its tag and a style's declarations as written: `source/tag.mjs`, which writes a unit's sizes and attributes too), new rows on lines of their own, the colgroup and caption where they were; the page then adopts the cells as text blocks where the file has them. The cells it showed are those blocks still: the layout's grid names them by identity before the page's first marks, and the commits learn each such element by its mark all the same (`learn()`). Where someone else wrote the file meanwhile (仍然调整 over a cell they changed), a cell that stays keeps the text the page had of it as its `before`, not the file's, so that their words stay and the reader's next ones are merged with them. Only a table written as the kit writes one has these tools, and whether it is was judged once, by the server, from the source (`source/table.mjs` `plainTable`: explicit start and end tags on the table, its one thead at most (before the tbody), its one tbody, its rows and cells, at least one row, merged cells tiling a rectangle with the others): `instrument()` marks the unit `data-ge-grid` (the map's unit says `grid`, a commit's answer repeats it and `sync-state.mjs` writes it again), the page only reads the mark (`tableAt`, `gridOf` is the grid the commits compare, built only for a marked unit) and `rewriteTable` turns down an operation on a table it does not judge so, so a table the file would refuse has no tools; a table made on the page has the mark from the start (`newTable`, a copy of a table keeps it) until the file says. Any other keeps its cells' text only. Rows, columns and the selection go by slots (`src/shared/table-slots.mjs`, the HTML table model, one reading for the kit, the editor and the server; the operations' own helpers are `components/table/slots.mjs`: each row has a slot in each column, a merged cell covers a rectangle of them; a cell is where its top left slot is): a row (column) put in across a merged cell makes it a row taller (a column wider), one taken out of it smaller (its top row taken out moves it down to the row after), rows (columns) a merged cell ties together move and are copied together and never into another such group, the header row is never tied to the body; the operation sends a cell's spans where they changed (`rowspan`, `colspan`) and the server writes them, checking that the new rows tile a rectangle (`tileSpans`). The page the editor serves holds the file's row order: its head declares `data-ge-order="file"` on the document (`service.mjs` `preview`), and the kit's tables do not apply the order they open in (a header's `data-sort-default`, the sort a bound key holds) there, so no one puts rows back; sorting by a header still sorts (while the page edits the arrow's press is the editor's, an edit; the arrow hides while the caret is in its header, so as not to be pressed by mistake, and stands 3px off the header's words). The tools (`page/table/`, their rules decided 2026-10-18): one selection of cells (a rectangle), rows or columns (`index.mjs`), drawn as a 2px blue frame with a light blue fill (not around the caret's cell: the caret is the text's); a selection of cells grows until it cuts no merged cell; a grip on a column's top edge and a row's left edge, on the edge's line (an 18×6 pill, a white button with drag dots under the pointer), each shown only with the pointer at its edge (`REACH`: within 12px outside the table's top edge or 8px inside it, over that column; the same at its left edge beside that row; the pointer, or the caret, just in a cell shows neither: the user's, 2026-10-23), a selected row's or column's for as long as it is selected (a press selects the row or column, taking the caret and the focus out of text (a caret left elsewhere would let the selection go), letting go opens its menu, as wide as the block menu and with no search field (its rows are few; ↑/↓, →/← for its submenus, Enter and Esc work in it, ahead of the page's keys): 标题行 / 标题列 on the first, switched in place (both only make the cells th, which stick; 标题行 gives them looks too: a cell that becomes a header row's cell takes bold, `.doc-bold`, and the gray fill unless it has a color of its own, one that stops being one loses them, and a header row's cell made beside another (a row put in it, a column) takes that one's color and bold, `lookLike`; 标题列 gives none and takes none (the user's, 2026-11-06: a header column only makes the first column stay at the left when the frame scrolls sideways), so a cell keeps the looks it has through on and off, a cell made in a header column (a row put in, a column put in first, a merged cell split) is a plain `th scope="row"`, and an older file's header column written with the look keeps it (the reader takes it off with 颜色 › 默认 and 加粗); a cell going between the header row and the header column (标题行 switched while a header column is on, a row moved into or out of the header row) takes or loses the header row's look as the rest of its row, `wearsHeaderLook` in `components/table/page.mjs` saying which cells wear it (a th that is no row's header), a new element then so that undo puts its classes back; the reader may change the looks as any cell's; the cells' bar's 加粗 and Ctrl+B on selected cells set `.doc-bold`, the words typed later bold too, and take it off with their words' bold), 颜色 ›, 对齐 › for columns (左对齐, 居中, 右对齐, then 顶端对齐, 垂直居中, 底端对齐: `doc-valign-*`, the cells' bar's 对齐 list the same), insert either side (the new one selected), 均分列宽 and 自动调整列宽 (一键调整列宽 before 2026-11-07) for columns, 创建副本, 清除内容, 删除 when not the last; dragged past 3px it moves (with the rows or columns a merged cell ties to it): a copy of it goes with the pointer, its cells as they look on the table's background with a shadow, the selection's frame and its grip, blue, on it (the frame and grips no longer drawn where it was), and a blue line where it goes, Esc cancelling; a row or column moved first is the header one); the + points where two columns (rows) meet on the table's edges, the two at the ends of a column (row) with its grip; with the pointer on the table, one round + just past its bottom right corner (no bars along its edges): a click adds a row, a drag adds rows down and columns right, one more per 32px (rows) or 120px (columns), across, down or both at once, back taking the empty last ones off, the frame scrolled to its end where they went in. A table wider or taller than its frame scrolls in it: the tools stand by what the frame shows of it (`geometry`'s `view`), the grips, + points and strips of the rows and columns in sight, the selection's frame cut to it. The pointer going onto a frame of its own document (an embed's, whose moves the page never hears) takes the tools that follow it away, near the table or not, as the block handle counts reaching a frame as the page's last move; a selection stays. Cells (`cells.mjs`): a drag from one cell into another selects the cells between (the cross-block text selection gives the press up: `claims`), Shift+click from the selection's anchor; until a press in a cell is let go (cells or text being selected), the tools standing by the table (the lines' strips, the grips, the + points and the corner) take no pointer (`data-ge-tpress` on the document), so none lights, shows its cursor or draws its line as the pointer goes over it (the user's, 2026-10-26: the line between two columns flickered under a drag selecting cells), and let go they answer the pointer as before; a press on empty space beside a table (`selection/marquee.mjs`, `data-ge-marquee` on the document: a box selection, or a click) is not working the table, so the tools the pointer showed there before the press (a row's grip and + points, the edges' strips, the corner) go at its first move and come back with the next move after it ends, a box selection showing its covered blocks' fills alone (before, they stayed lit for the whole box); the caret in a cell makes it the selection; in a cell Tab and Shift+Tab go to the next and previous cell, Enter to the one below, Shift+Enter breaks the line, Esc selects the cell (again its row, again the table as a block); with cells selected the arrows move a one-cell selection (a row's up and down, a column's left and right), Shift stretches its far edge, Enter edits the focus cell, a typed character goes at the end of the last cell, Delete takes selected rows or columns out or empties the cells with their color, Ctrl+D fills down (copies rows or columns), Ctrl+R right, the format keys format whole cells; copied, cells are the page's own (`application/x-gallery-cells`, { v, cells, report: the document they were copied from, whose files their pictures are, project: its workspace where it is not the first, as blocks name it }: `cellsPayload`; a paste in another workspace's document of the same name copies their pictures from that workspace, `table/pictures.mjs`; and the old `application/x-report-studio-cells` beside it as the blocks' are, `cellsData`, `CELLS_MIME` in `components/table/index.mjs`), an HTML table (its pictures where the server serves them) and tab-separated text as Excel writes it (a cell holding a tab, a line break or a quote in quotes, its quotes doubled; its lines its line breaks and list items, a picture its alt), pasted elsewhere the page's own too (see "Pasting": one cell its text, more a table of them). A paste into a table's cells, with cells selected or with the caret in a cell (the user's, 2026-11-09: two cells copied in one table and pasted with the caret in another's cell went into that one cell, Excel's too), reads the clipboard as cells (`cellsOf` of `clipboard/`, which the table tools are given): the page's own cells; its text copied in a block, a table copied alone as its cells and other blocks as one cell of their words, all with their marks, no list's marker or Markdown (`ownCells`); another program's table, HTML that holds one table and no words outside it (Excel's Office HTML, Google Sheets', Feishu's, Notion's, Yuque's, a web page's: `import/html.mjs` `tableRows`), each cell read as the import reads a table's (its marks, links, colors mapped as the import maps them, line breaks, spans spread into empty cells, pictures), a table of one column too; a Markdown table (its text alone: Yuque's Markdown, a .md file's), read as a paste's Markdown, its cells' pictures kept (`import/inline.mjs` `parseCellMarkdown`); tab-separated lines (`tabbed`: two lines or more of as many cells, two or more; a cell in quotes holding tabs, line breaks and "" a quote, as Excel and Google Sheets write one); else its text, one cell holding its lines. They fill the cells from the selection's top left or the caret's cell, right and down, repeated over a selection a whole number of times larger, rows and columns put in where they overflow (merged cells by their top left slot), the cells pasted then selected, each cell's text its nodes as a paste makes them (`pastedLines`: the format bar's marks, formulas, its lines with line breaks between them, its pictures: "Pictures in a cell"), one undo step of the table's operation (`change`). With the caret in a cell a single value is the editor's paste at the caret (its marks, an address a link: "Pasting"), but one holding a picture, which goes in at the caret (`atCaret`); picture files on selected cells fill the first of them; Excel's picture of its cells, beside its table, is no picture to paste. Over selected cells stands the cells' bar (`cell-bar.mjs`, the format bar's own look: `formatLook`, its swatches): the "A" (a cell's one color, a text color or a fill, as a block's), bold, the alignment, 清除内容 and "更多" (rows and columns put in beside them or theirs taken out). A cell's color and alignment are its classes (`CELL_LOOKS`, the kit's `doc-text-*` / `doc-highlight-*` and `doc-align-*`), changed as one undo step (`look`; a structural step records the classes it gives and takes too: `history.mjs` `struct`). Column widths (`widths.mjs`): the kit's own (`tables/layout.js`) until a strip on a column's right edge is dragged (the line is 3px, the strip that takes a press 8px, 4 each side of the cell's edge, the user's 2026-11-20 after a 3px one was hard to hit: `GRAB`; the bottom edge's strip, which arms the table's height, reaches 5px below the table, `GRAB_BELOW`, it was 8; the last column's strip still reaches 8px past the right edge; 35px at least, shown as it goes, the table's own width off for the while; the last column's strip is the table's right border, and within 6px of the columns together filling the text exactly it snaps there, the last column taking what the others leave, rounded down so that the frame never scrolls by a hair, the bottom notice saying 表格宽度：铺满正文 while it holds and 已调整表格宽度：铺满正文 after, the table full already and moved a few px writing nothing: `snapFull`, `fillOf`, the room the kit's `manualLayout` fills; the widths it starts from are those of the cells, not the distances between their edges (`shown`), as a table scrolled sideways keeps its first column in sight (a header column, sticky) and the column under the stuck cells would read as the part of it in sight, every width summing to the frame's: the first move of the right border of a wide table then squeezed the columns before it and the table seemed full, now the border stays where it is on the screen while the table narrows, the columns before it unchanged and moving right as the frame's scroll follows, it snaps once where the table is exactly as wide as the text and the drag goes on past it: scenario `wideEdgeNoJump`, and a middle column's strip in a scrolled table writes its own width alone; the user's, 2026-11-09: the resize tool's snap went with the right edge when the table's own strip took the plain drag of it on 2026-10-18); then every column's width is on the first row's cell, `--doc-column-width` (the kit lays the columns out at them: `manualLayout`), one undo step; a double click fits the column to its words (the words an Agent took out that the page shows in a cell, "Agent's changes", out of the layout while it is measured, `hidden`); 自动调整列宽 takes them off (the kit's widths from the words; sizing a column fixes them again), 均分列宽 shares selected columns' room (in the block menu, every column's); sizing columns takes the table's own width (`--doc-span`) off, so the table is as wide as its columns. A column's width is held by the topmost cell of that column alone (`widthHolder`: a merged cell holds none; the operations that move or merge such cells write the widths again, `keepWidths`), and its strip runs only along the rows where its edge is a cell's (not across a merged cell). The table's size: the last column's strip covers the table's right edge too, and a strip runs along its bottom edge; a click on either arms it (a thicker line with ↔ or ↕ on it; no resting), and a drag then is the resize tool's drag (`resize/` `startTable`, its snapping and its label): the table's width (原宽度, 铺满正文), written as `--doc-span`, the kit filling the frame with every column in proportion, or its scroll height (原高度, 显示全部行), written as `--doc-table-height`, the rows past it scrolling under the header; dragged, with the pointer off it, on a press elsewhere or Esc it is plain again (a quick press after the arming click let go where pressed is a double click: the last column fitted). The resize tool leaves both edges of a table the tools manage to them (`owns`). Merged cells (合并单元格): with several cells selected the cells' bar has 合并单元格 (`mergeCells`: the top left one spans them, the others' contents after its own, each on a line of its own, the others gone; not across the header row and the body), with one merged cell 拆分单元格 (`splitCell`: its content in its top left slot, empty cells in the others), one undo step each (the step records the cells' spans and widths: `struct`'s `attributes`); Tab goes from cell to cell, the arrows and Enter step past a merged cell, copies and pastes go by its top left slot. Lists in a cell (单元格里的列表, `table/lists.mjs`): a cell stays one text block and a list is part of its text (`<td>…<ul><li>…</li></ul>…</td>`; `serializeInner` writes it, the server's `inlineProblem` takes it in a cell: no attributes, explicit items of inline content, no list in an item); "- " (or "* ") at the start of one of its lines makes that line a bulleted list's item, "1. " a numbered one's (into the list of its kind beside it), Enter in an item the next item, Enter in an empty one an empty line out of the list, Backspace at an item's start a line again, each one undo step of the cell's text. A new table comes from a paste or from 表格 in the "/" menu (`convert.mjs`: three columns, a header row and two rows, the caret in its first cell, two fifths as wide as the text it stands in with its columns equal and fixed: `sharedWidths`; 自动调整列宽 is the reader's to choose, a pasted table has the kit's widths), its cells text blocks at once (`newCell`), written by its type with its header row and column, looks and widths as it has them then (`tableData`, `file.mjs`). A document that had no table has no `tables.js` on its page: the first table made loads it (`newTable`, as a first code block loads `code.js`), so the kit lays it out at once (before, it stayed unlaid until a reload, and dragging its width moved its frame away from it). Its block menu has its own rows (`menuItems`) under the menu's one title (表格), its layout's after them in the same group (the block menu puts a block's own rows and its layout's together): 标题行 and 标题列, the only switches (checked when on); then actions, each applied once and never checked: 一键优化 › (a wand: `optimizeTable`, one undo step: 自动调整列宽, a header row, the first column a header column (which only sticks: no look) when its body cells read as the rows' names (each written, none a number by the kit's reading, `src/shared/number-reading.mjs`, no two alike: `labelColumn`, `rowNames`), 识别数字列, and a frame shown whole (显示全部行) that is taller than the kit's scroll height scrolling again; a table pasted from elsewhere comes in so: `outside` on its paste block, the page's own copy as it was), a split row (`menu.mjs`, "Block colors"; the user's, 2026-11-07): a click on its words runs it, its › opens a submenu of two of its parts to run alone, each one undo step with its notice, 自动调整列宽 and 识别数字列 (the kit reads the table's numeric columns: `data-number-columns="off"` on its frame taken off; a table made from "/" starts with it on its frame, so typing numbers never centers or sorts a column; the Agent's tables have none), which until then stood on the menu's top level; 
then 均分列宽 and 铺满正文宽度 (no check either; a table's menu has no 视图 ›: a view of a table is a block of "/", "Table views"). Its 对齐 › is the block menu's own row above them, the one every block that takes an alignment has: the whole table left, centred or at the right ("Alignment and indent"); the cells' alignments are the column menu's and the cells' bar's. 
A sortable header's arrow (the kit's control after its words: the header's words stay a text block, a press on them sorts nothing) pressed while the page is edited sorts the rows by its column as an edit (the kit goes round no sort, ascending, descending, no sort, render.md "A table's numeric columns", and asks for the rows by its `doc-table-sort-edit` event, which the table tools answer: one undo step, saved as the table's order, the third press putting back the order the rows had before the first; by the kit's reading of the cells, `parsedValue` of `src/shared/number-reading.mjs`, missing values last; a press that moves no row says so), not the kit's view of them. The arrow says the sort only while the rows are in its order, so an undo shows it at no sort.
- **Pictures in a cell.** 单元格里的图片 (the user's, 2026-11-09: no image block, a light picture amid a cell's words, its size adjustable; Markdown tables hold pictures): an `<img>` in a table cell's text, a picture of the document's folder, `<img src="assets/<file>" alt="…" width="N">`, its width in pixels (none: the kit's own size, at most 240px; never past its cell, its height by its ratio, its middle on the line's, so a row grows with it: `styles/table.css`), no other attribute (`source/blocks.mjs` `cellPictureProblem`, read by `inlineProblem` for a cell's text, a picture anywhere in it, a link's, a mark's, a list item's: a commit's text and a table's operation are checked so, `checks.mjs` `validCell`; a cell holding any other `<img>` is read-only, `natural.mjs` saying the form; no other text block takes one). On the page it shows where the server serves the folder (`page/served.mjs`, given the asset route by `editor.mjs`), the file in data-ge-src, as an image block's picture: the cell host's `pictures` (`components/table/index.mjs`: `show` as the block is prepared from the file, `write` as its text is serialized; `core.mjs` reads the field), its attributes written in the order its element has them, one the browser put in from elsewhere, of no file of the folder, no part of the text. Its tools (`page/table/pictures.mjs`; the plainest that works, flat): a press selects it (the selection around it and a 2px blue frame, no caret: Delete or Backspace takes it out, a typed character takes its place, Esc puts the caret after it; the arrows pass it as a character); a gray bar at its right edge (the image block's handle) under the pointer or while it is selected, dragged, sets its width (16px at least, its table's frame at most), one undo step on letting go (`recordStep`), Esc putting it back; the frame and the bar go with the picture at once (its cell watched: Delete, undo, someone else's words). Where pictures come from: a picture file pasted with the caret in a cell (a screenshot, a copied picture; 10 MB at most, sent to the document's folder as an image block's: `fromFiles`), in at the caret, one undo step; the pictures of cells pasted ("Tables": another program's table, a Markdown table, the page's own cells of another document, text copied in a cell) and of a table pasted anywhere, each made a file of the folder before the paste goes in (`resolve`: one of the folder as it is, another document's copied in, a data: one sent, a web one downloaded by the server as an image block's link is), then put in by what `pastedLines` makes of each (`maker`: one that could not be had a link to it for a web one, else its alt words); an Agent's `<img>` in a td or th, which `natural.mjs` leaves as written; a .md file imported with `![alt](path)` in a cell, or an HTML page with an `<img>` in one (`transfer/import.mjs` `tablePictures`: found as an image's file is and kept as one, one not found its alt words, noted at its row's line). The preview serves it, an export embeds it and `gallery check` checks its file as any reference to the folder (`render/asset-references.mjs`); a double click opens it large (`shared/zoom.mjs`). The table's tools take a cell holding one as any other: a copy of its cells carries it, a sort reads the cell's words.
- **Table views.** A view of a table (a task board, a count, a chart card of a custom block's) is an instance of a custom block whose manifest's needs hold `tables`, bound to a table of the document by the key the table's frame carries for a chart (`data-doc-from` on the instance, `data-doc-chart-source` on the frame: `VIEW_FROM`, `CHART_SOURCE` of `src/shared/table-chart.mjs`), never by an id or the blocks' order, so that the reader moves, copies or deletes either block as any, a table has any number of charts and views, and a view whose block is not there (or does not load) leaves the table a table, edited as any (scenario `tableViewMissing`). It reads the table as a chart does, in the file's order (the kit's `context.table()`, [render.md](render.md) "A custom block on a page"), and changes it only by asking: a cancelable `doc-table-intent` on the instance, `{ table, op, row, before, after, cells }` (the row elements, each cell by its column's place in the header), which `page/table/views.mjs` answers at once, synchronously, cancelling it to say it made the change, so that a view has no commit, undo or conflict of its own and nothing of the commit engine is new. `set` is one cell's words: the cell's content replaced by the text (plain words, its formatting gone with the old ones), recorded as a text step of that cell (`recordStep`, as formatting's is), which the commit sends as typing's text is, with its `before`, and merges word by word with someone else's change of the cell (`mergeText`; scenario `tableViewIntents`), undo going three ways as for any typing after a merge. A `set` may name several rows (`rows`: a board's group renamed, a chart's bar, each row of it) and carry a write of the view's own parts (`edit`, what its `context.edit` asks, `{ parts, run }`: the board's group in the order of its settings, renamed with it), checked as that edit is (the `drafted` of the installed page object, the same function its `doc-component-edit` goes through: the change run on drafts, refused where it wrote the part on the page or made what the file cannot hold); then the cells' words and the parts' drafts are one step of `struct` (no parents, the cells and the parts touched, as the format bar's change of several cells is), so that one undo takes all of it back and a redo puts it again, and the commit finds both as it finds any change of them, in one commit where nothing waits: the cells' words as typing's, the parts what the component wrote (`dataChanges`), each going in or held for the reader as its own. So where someone else changed the view's parts meanwhile, the cells go in and the parts wait on a card: 保留 Agent 的 leaves the cells renamed and the settings the other's (a board's group then comes after those its order names, an empty one of the old name in its place: nothing lost, the card seen). Holding the cells back with the parts was tried and taken out (the review of 2026-11-20): it split every rename into two commits, the file showing the one without the other for a moment each time, and held back the reader's own later words in those cells. A struct step holding a part `fold` reaches too (above: 仍然调整's undo, two undos giving the Agent's settings back and then the cells, never the page's old settings over the Agent's; scenarios `shippedKanbanWords`, `shippedViewsWords`, `shippedKanbanRenameConflict`). `move` (a row put before or after another, the cells it names set in the same step: a card dropped in another column, its status and its place), `add` (a row put in after another or at the end, the cells it names written in its new cells) and `remove` (a row taken out, never the last of the body, as the tools' 删除) are the table tools' own operations (`moveRow`, `insertRow`, `removeRows` of `components/table/page.mjs`) in one undo step each (the tools' `change`, which records the rows' child lists and the cells it touches), committed as the table's operation, `{ table: { rows } }`, guarded by the table's hash: an Agent's change of the table meanwhile holds it back as the table's conflict, with its card, 仍然调整 writing it with their words in their cells (scenario `tableViewConflict`). None says anything in the status line: the view shows what changed. Only a table these tools manage (`GRID`, written as the kit writes one) with no merged cell takes an intent, and only in its rows of data, those after the row a view reads its columns' names from (the head's rows, or with no head its body's first row), which no intent moves a row into or takes out; any other answer is false, a view of any other table being read-only, as it is in an export, in 演示视图 and before the page edits. `check` changes nothing and is answered as the others would be (the kit's `table().canEdit()`), so that a view shows a table these tools do not manage (a `<tfoot>`, rows of other lengths) read-only before a gesture finds it out (scenario `shippedKanbanReadOnly`; the kit asks nothing then: [render.md](render.md)). A row a view read before it was taken out answers false; a column the header no longer names throws in the kit before anything is asked (a renamed column: scenario `tableViewRenamed`, where the view says it in its own words). **Made from a menu, and its table.** A view is a row of every menu that makes a block ("/", 在下方新建, the handle's +, 转换成) like any custom block, found by its words and the initials of its pinyin: a table's block menu has no 视图 › (the user, 2026-11-09, who never asked for one: a view is added as any block is, and `variantKeys` of `page-objects.mjs` leaves none out). One made there is bound to the nearest table above it in the document's order (`tableAbove` of `page/table/binding.mjs`: the last table frame before it that does not hold it; with none above it stays unbound and says so in its own words), in the same undo step: the table's key where its frame names it (the first frame carrying it, as the kit's `frameOf` and the checks read a key), or a new one (the first of t1, t2 … the page does not hold) put on its frame (a table made from "/" has none; the second of two an Agent wrote with one key is not the one it names). The variant's `create` (`installed/page.mjs`) binds the instance once it is placed (`bindAbove`), and the three entries that run it (`convertHost`, `insertBelow` and `convertUnit` of `editor.mjs`) record every table frame's key in their undo step (`viewFrames`, `struct`'s `attributes`), as the table it will read is known only as it is made: one undo takes the view and the key away, one redo brings both back (scenarios `tableViewNew`, `shippedKanban`). The instance is written with its key (the installed type's `extras` sends `from`, which `installed/file.mjs` `source` writes on the markup it writes, the template's or what a paste carried, in place of any key it carried; one carried with no key on the page, `from` null, with none). The table a view reads is changed in its 设置 ("What the handle and its menu show"): the first field, 表格, lists the document's tables, each by its place and the words of its first row (`tableChoices`) and （未选择） in the board's own menu card, and a pick is one undo step (`readTable`, a step of look: `data-doc-from` on the view and the key on the table's frame, a new key on a table with none; `history.mjs` `rekeyed` follows a key the commit gives anew in look steps as in struct's), written by the commit as an attribute of the unit (the `installed` kind has `attributes: { data-doc-from }` as a table's frame has `data-doc-chart-source`: the operation `{ id, attribute, value }`); none chosen takes the key off the view (scenario `tableViewSettings`). A table copied with its view is a pair of its own, in its document or another, a view copied alone reads the original's table in its document and none in another (scenario `tableViewPastedElsewhere`), and the commit makes sure of every key against the file, repairing and never refusing ("Copying blocks", `relinkMade`; scenario `tableViewRepaired`): a key it gives the pair anew the page puts on both and in the undo steps that recorded the table's (`history.mjs` `rekeyed`), so that a view made from "/" undone and redone brings back the pair the file has, never the table on the key its view no longer carries (scenario `tableViewKeyUndone`).
- **Display formulas.** A display formula (`div.doc-math-block` holding only `\[…\]`, `components/math/`) is a text block of kind `tex`: its source the formula's TeX between `\[ \]`, shown as the kit drew it in one chip (`prepareTex`, an atom whose source is the block's text, as an inline formula's). A click opens the formula box below it (`components/math/page.mjs`: hover tint, radius 10; empty, a TeX icon and 添加一个 TeX 公式; TeX that does not read, the same bar saying 无效的公式, as it is typed and once the box is left (one look, so that nothing moves as the box closes: KaTeX's reason is the box's); these tints, under the pointer and while the box is open, are inset shadows, never the block's background, so a selected formula is filled as text, 2px, 4px round, whatever the pointer does, and an empty one as its bar: scenario `formulaFill`): what is typed redraws the block as it goes, and however the box is left it is kept (Ctrl+Enter, Esc, a press elsewhere, Enter with all of the field selected, else Enter is a new line; one undo step: its text changed); emptied it stays, an empty block (`\[\]`). Nothing is typed into the block itself: the keys pass over it as over a block with no text, and with the caret on it a key that types or edits selects it, the next being the selected block's ("The order of keys"); a paste goes after it ("Pasting"). TeX that does not read stays in the file as typed (`texText`: a `\]` in it is written `\&#93;`, so that the text is one formula still, `texSource`) and the block is the gray bar of an empty one saying 无效的公式, as the kit shows it to a reader too (render.md, the kit's `math.js`; scenario `formulaInvalid`). The box (`formula/panel.mjs`, shared with inline formulas) has its symbol bar above the field (`formula/symbols.mjs`: six groups by name, see `formula/` below), each opening a card of all its symbols, drawn by KaTeX; the box stands below the formula, or above it where the tallest group's card has no room there, or on the side with more room where it has none on either (`stand`): chosen as it opens and again as the page (or a box it is in) scrolls or the window changes size, so that no group and no symbol clicked moves the field or the formula. The card and whatever else comes and goes around the field (the lines it grows by, KaTeX's reason) go on the far side from the formula; where the window leaves a card too little room the card scrolls inside what is left, never the field, 96px of it staying in the window at the least: the field's height is capped for that and the box stands over the formula rather than leave it no room. Inside the window while the formula shows in it, the box goes with it once it has scrolled out, and with it as what is typed draws it taller; a click inserts one at the caret and moves nothing at all, for the next click to find what it found: with the pointer on the box the field keeps its height, the text going on inside it, its caret's line in view, and KaTeX's reason waits, until a key is pressed or the pointer has left the box (`land`). KaTeX's reason ("无效的公式：", 12px, the danger red) is two lines at the most, its end "…" (the room the window keeps for it); resting on it 600ms (`REVEAL`) shows all of it there, KaTeX's quote of the TeX (fifteen characters each side of the place) given whole (`tex.mjs` renderTex `whole`, only where the place is in what was typed), growing on the box's far side to what the window leaves it there (`place()` keeping a group's card room) and scrolling inside that, with 点击复制 under it; a click (hover tint, pointer cursor) copies "无效的公式：" and the whole reason, 已复制 there and 已复制报错 on the status line, the box open and the keys in the field; the pointer leaving or a key typed shows the two lines again (scenario `formulaInvalid`). A press anywhere on the box leaves the keys in the field, and the gap between the card and the box is the box's. A template's first slot comes selected and Tab (Shift+Tab) goes on to the next (previous) one. 公式块 is in the "/" menu's 高级区块.
- **The page's icon.** `components/icon/` (the kit's `icon` component, a whole block). An icon stands right above the document's title, left-aligned with its text, in the header right before the `h1`. It is one of three kinds (`src/shared/emoji/icon-value.mjs`): an emoji (`<div class="doc-icon">🎯</div>`), an icon of the 图标 tab in a color (`<div class="doc-icon" data-icon="/icons/airplane_blue.svg"></div>`: MingCute's, or `/icons/tabler/<name>_<color>.svg`, Tabler's) or an image of the document's folder (`<div class="doc-icon"><img src="assets/icon-….png" alt=""></div>`).
  - **Adding one:** with the pointer in the header and no icon yet, 添加图标 shows above the title. It is 28px high and fades in. Hidden, it receives neither pointer hits nor focus, and a queued reveal cannot show it after the pointer leaves. A click only works while shown, editing, and still without an icon (checked again after loading emoji data), so a stale click never replaces an existing icon (`pageIcon`). It puts a random emoji there: an activity, an object or a place (`randomEmoji` of `src/shared/emoji/data.mjs`).
  - **The picker:** a click on the icon opens the icon picker right below it, fading in where it stands (`emoji-picker/`; a second click closes it): its three tabs below, its look (`styles.mjs`) the editor's palette (`page/look.mjs`). Text tabs as the formula editor's symbol bar, the one shown the only gray block, 移除 at the bar's right end; buttons 28px without a frame; the field focused a deeper gray line inside it, a cell reached by the keys a deeper gray fill; nothing in blue (保存 a text button, the checkbox gray) but 每次先选颜色's switch on, every switch's calm `--ge-control-blue` (the finish of 2026-11-17: gray until then); 408 × 390, 4px under the icon, a card 14px round ("Look" above), its controls 8px round, its cells too. Its classes are `ge-picker` and, for its parts, `ge-picker-*` (the color its line drawings take `--ge-picker-icon`); it and the media menu open as a `ge-popup` in the popups' layer, `ge-popup-layer` (`support.mjs`). The same picker serves the workbench's sidebar (workbench.md "The sidebar", **Row icons**) from a frame of its own (`icon-frame.mjs`, at `/__gallery/edit/icon-picker`, which writes nothing: it hands each pick to the workbench, a custom emoji first copied into the document's folder); for it `openMediaPicker` takes `onClose` (told as the picker closes, before a pick it hands over: the frame says `closed` after its picks) and `pictures: false` (where an icon may be no picture: no 上传 tab, no 自定义 in 表情符号, none of the custom emojis among the recent). Its tabs:
    - **表情符号:** the emojis offered are exactly those the Twemoji pack draws, 1574 and their skin tones, their groups, names and keywords made from emojibase-data (MIT), whose names and keywords are the Unicode CLDR annotations, by `src/cli/import-emoji-data.mjs` (`src/shared/emoji/data/README.md` says what each file is, the version and how to make them again; `tests/unit/icons.mjs` holds the files to what it makes). It shows 最近, the 推荐 group when a highlight block or a flow step opened it (two rows Gallery chose for what such a block says: a tip, a pin, a warning, done, a question, a note, a time, data, people, a result; `CALLOUT_EMOJIS`), the groups in the Unicode order (笑脸与人物, which holds the Unicode groups Smileys & Emotion and People & Body, 动物与自然, 食物与饮料, 旅行与地点, 活动, 物品, 符号), 自定义, and a bar of them below. No 旗帜: the data and the pack hold no flag (2026-11, the owner's word; a flag a document holds is still a valid icon, in the system's font). The filter (`emoji-search.mjs` `rank`) finds an emoji by its words in Chinese and English (its name, its short codes as Slack and GitHub name them, its CLDR keywords, its emoticons; a custom emoji by its name), best first: the emoji itself typed, a word equal to the filter (its name before a short code, a short code before a keyword; an English name holding the filter as words of it counts as a keyword, so thumbs finds "thumbs up" as it finds the keyword of 👎), a word starting with it (the same order), every word of the filter starting one of its words, a word holding it. Between two equal ones: the one whose one-word name the filter makes up more of (心 finds 红心 before the faces whose keywords say 心); then an everyday emoji before one that is not (sad finds the loudly crying face before "sad but relieved face", green the green heart before "leafy green"); then an English name holding the filter (party finds "party popper" before the faces whose keywords say party), of two such the one it ends, before a "with …" and the like (what the emoji is: "full moon" before "moon cake", "money with wings" before "money-mouth face"), then the one of fewer words; then the one people use more (idea finds 💡 before 💭, 爱 ❤️ before 😍, love ❤️ before 🏩, yes and 好 👍 before 🫡, time ⏰ before ⌛); then the data's order, Unicode's. Which emojis are everyday ones, and in what order, is Gallery's list of four hundred (`src/shared/emoji/everyday.mjs`), in the order a document reaches for them: the marks a document is written with first (done, not done, warning, an idea, a pin, the status dots), then what it is about (notes, dates, charts, files, tools), then the common faces, hands and hearts, weather and plants, food, animals, places; it only decides between emojis ranked alike, so a name equal to the filter still comes first (panda finds 🐼, not an everyday face whose keywords say panda), and the tab without a filter keeps the Unicode order. A small word (a, of, on, with…) or one letter finds no name by it ("face blowing a kiss"). The frequently used go first of all. A filter between colons (`:fire:`) is tried without them too. 笑 finds 😄 first, 猫 🐈 then 🐱, 火 🔥, heart ❤️, cat 🐈 then 🐱, fire 🔥, thumbs 👍, party 🎉, moon 🌔 (its short code) and the moons before 🥮 (`tests/unit/icons.mjs`). Where the data says nothing more, the data's order decides: love finds 💌, 🏩 and 🤟 (names holding it) before the 20-odd emojis whose keywords say love, ❤️ among them; one picked once goes first after. Over 72 results they stay in their groups, each best first; fewer, they are one list. An emoji is found however its variation selectors are written (☺ and ☺️ alike), so a document's emoji always is: a document holds an emoji's character, never a name. A cell's label is the emoji's name in the UI's language. A group's title sticks to the list's top as it scrolls (the next one pushes it away), and a click on a group's button brings its title there (the scroller is positioned, so a group's `offsetTop` counts from it; it once counted from the popup and scrolled about 90px past); a cell the keys reach stays below the stuck title (`grid.mjs`). 最近 lists only emojis the data still knows and custom ones (a flag used before no longer shows; the stored list is left to age out). Every emoji is a Twemoji picture on every system, the skin button's hand too (the system's emoji font only for one the pack lacks, or where the browser cannot unpack it). An emoji with skin tones shows the tones first until one is chosen (one of five picked keeps the tone); once one is, every emoji with skin tones shows in it, two people or two hands both in it (`withSkinTone`: the first five of their 25 are those of one tone).
    - **图标:** two open-source sets of filled icons, MingCute Core Filled (Apache-2.0) and Tabler Filled (MIT), as the 443 things both have one icon for, and Tabler's 40 numbers (0–9 in a circle, a square, a rounded square and a hexagon; MingCute has no series of them) (`src/shared/emoji/glyphs.mjs`, `data/icons.json`, made by `src/cli/import-icons.mjs` from the user's categorized list and subsets), each with a Chinese name and one of 12 categories. As the emoji tab shows its groups, the tab shows 最近 and then the categories, a title each (数字与符号 … 品牌与娱乐, their English through `t()`: `CATEGORY_TITLES` in `icon-tab.mjs`); 数字与符号 ends in the numbers, each shape's 0–9 a row of its own. Filtered by the Chinese name, either set's names (or a word of them) and the icon's search words (the list's English ones, the numbers' 数字/编号…, a name a pair had before), in one flat list best first; in the color chosen once (asked the first time, then kept). MingCute's show unless the reader switched: the button left of 随机 names the set shown, and a click changes every icon in place to its pair's of the other set (where the grid is scrolled to, the filter and the focus stay), for comparing; the choice is kept in this browser (`gallery:glyph-set`, docs/workbench.md "Kept in the browser"). A number has no MingCute twin: it stays as it is through the switch, its tip names Tabler, and picked it is always Tabler's value (`/icons/tabler/circle-number-3_<color>.svg`). Recent icons are kept by pair, so either set shows them. They are drawn as inline SVG: an `<img>` of each is a document of its own, and a grid of hundreds of those held the page up for seconds while a filter was typed.
    - **上传:** a file, a dropped or pasted image, or a link. The server keeps it in the document's folder, `<workspace>/<slug>/assets/`, named by its hash (`editor/media-store.mjs`). It downloads a link, so an export never reaches the web. Images only, 2 MB at most; a raster image larger than 512px is made that size first. With 同时存进表情库 and a name it also goes into the workspace's emoji library, `<workspace>/.gallery/emoji-library/`, which every document's 表情符号 tab lists; the + of 自定义 in that tab (添加自定义表情) adds one there directly. The form says what is wrong in its own words: a file that is no image, a link no image loads from, a name with a character it cannot hold, 50 characters or more, or one an emoji (a custom one, or a standard one's short code or name, compared in lower case as a name is written: ok手势 is OK手势's) has already; a failed save says why on the status line where it is known (an image still over 2 MB once made smaller: 图片太大（最多 2 MB）; the server's refusal in the UI's language; the server not reached), else that it did not save. A custom emoji picked as an icon is copied into the document's folder first. `gallery emoji add <name> <image>` and `gallery emoji list [--json]` (`src/cli/emoji.mjs`) add to and list the same library by the media store's own `addEmoji` (a file is passed as `data`, base64, beside the `url` the picker sends) and `listEmojis`, the name held to the picker's rule (and a standard emoji's name refused as the picker does, `names.json`); `gallery set <document> --icon <name>` (`src/cli/set.mjs`) copies the image into the document's `assets/` through the media store's `upload { library }`, as the picker does, and the copy is the page's icon.
    - 随机 picks one and keeps the picker open, and 移除 takes the icon away.
  - **Undo and saving:** each change is one undo step (`struct`). Changing the icon takes the old one away and puts the new one in.
  - **On the page:** the server serves the page with the icon already a picture (`src/render/emoji.mjs` `embedIcon`), so a refresh never shows the system's emoji first. An emoji is its Twemoji image (a data: URL), a set's icon its SVG inline in the kit's color for it (light and dark), an image the document's file where the server serves it (`/__gallery/edit/asset/<slug>/assets/<file>`; an export of the view embeds it). The value stays the element's own (`data-ge-icon`). One made on the page, or taken in from the file, is drawn the same way (`page.mjs`): the editor draws an emoji as its Twemoji picture on every system too (`src/shared/emoji/data.mjs` reads no user agent, so not even an Apple system's emoji font draws one), so it looks the same before and after a refresh. It has no handle, is never moved or selected, and its inside is no text block (`components`).
  - **In the browser tab:** the tab shows the page's icon. The server, a build and an export put a `<link rel="icon" data-doc-tab-icon>` in the head (`embedIcon`): in the preview the server's image of the value (`/__gallery/edit/icon?value=…&slug=…&tab=1`: a set's icon in its own color, light or dark as the system, an SVG favicon following `prefers-color-scheme`), in a file a data: URL; none without an icon. The editor keeps it as the icon changes (`showTabIcon` in `editor.mjs`, at each change of state: a new icon, one taken away, an undo), and an export of the view embeds it. A page with no icon shows the default document icon in the preview and the workbench (`DEFAULT_TAB_ICON` of `src/shared/emoji/icon-value.mjs`: the tree's page icon, Lucide's plain file outline, its box cropped to `1 1 22 22` so it fills the tab as an emoji does, in the tab glyphs' gray, light or dark as the system, a data: URL), never no image: a tab whose icon link is taken away, or given an image that does not load, keeps the one it showed (Chrome), so a page with no icon opened after one with one showed that one's. The preview's default link is its own (`data-doc-dev`: an export of the view leaves it out, as the file has no icon); the workbench's top bar loads a page's image before it shows it and gives the default for one that does not load (a picture gone). An emoji the pack lacks (newer than it, or a flag) still has an image: the icon route draws its character in an SVG in the system's emoji font (`iconImage`), which the tree, a page block's chip and the tab show.
  - **On the server:** the server writes an `icon` node right before the title whatever place the page gave, and does the same for one brought back by undo (`apply.mjs`). The header (`lines`) takes the icon unit kind, no flow takes it (`fixed`). A commit that makes or takes away the icon returns it, and `service.mjs` (`reicon`) mirrors it in report.json's `icon`.
  - **Workbench and export:** the session's `state` carries the icon to the workbench's tree. An offline export embeds its image (docs/render.md).
- **Lists.** `components/list/`. An item may hold blocks of any kind after its text: its nested lists, and a paragraph, a heading, an image, a code block… (`<li>text<p>…</p><figure>…</figure><ul>…</ul></li>`; the tags are `HELD_TAGS` of the shared description, `src/shared/components/list.mjs`, which the server's `trailingBlocks`, the page's `nestedBlocks` and the kit read; its box, `nest`, takes any block but a section or a row of columns). Its text block is its text alone; the kit puts what it holds under its text at its left edge, the list's gap above each (`flow.css`), headings as in the flow. One with no text of its own over what it holds has its marker on a line of its own above it, the item keeping its title line: the page gives it a line break there (`keepLine()`, see "What changed"), and so does the kit wherever the page is drawn, the preview, an export and print (`runtime/spacing.js`, docs/render.md), by one rule and with one line break (`keepsLine`, `giveLine` and `ITEM_LINE` of `src/shared/components/list.mjs`: an item of a list whose markers the kit draws in a flow, `FLOW_BLOCKS`; never one of a list with a class of its own or of one in a whole block, a card or a cell), the file holding `<li>` and its blocks alone. Chrome edits an item and a paragraph it holds as one editable element (the outermost, which has the focus), so the keys, input and paste go to the block the caret is in (`hostOf`, `innerHost` in `caret.mjs`; the "/" menu's keys too, `key` in `slash-menu/`, so a pick by Enter in a paragraph an item holds turns that paragraph), and the editing frame is that block's. Tab in a paragraph (or with blocks selected) right after a list puts it under that list's last item, at the end of what it holds; Shift+Tab, or Backspace at its start, on the last block an item holds takes it out, right after the item (the item's list split there, the items after it a list of their own from 1); Backspace at the start of its first block joins it to the item's text; Enter at the end of the item's text puts a new item right after it, which takes what the item held (`splitBlock()` `carry`, above) (`canHold`, `holdBlock` in `page.mjs`; scenario `listHeldBlocks`). Enter on an item with no text over what it holds turns it into body text where it stands, what it holds after it, as Backspace at its start does (a nested one goes out a level when it is the last its item holds, else turns into text there: `enterEmpty`, `outdentEmpty`; scenarios `emptyItemWhole`, `nestedEnter`).
