An item holding blocks turned into text sets them after the paragraph; deleted, it goes with them; copied, they are copied with it. The kit draws a list's markers (`flow.css`, for a list without a class of its own in a flow or straight in a flowchart's step, counted from the first depth there): in a blue of their own (`--doc-list-marker`, `light-dark(#2e7cd1, #5492d4)`, a quiet blue: the user's #2e7cd1 of 2026-11-07, a purer blue than the palette's `.doc-text-blue` they had from 2026-11-06 and links now take; an item's text color takes its place), left of the text; numbers 1., a., i. by depth from the `list-item` counter (`start` honoured), right-aligned 6px from the text; bullets a dot, a ring and a square by depth, 16px left of the text, on the middle of the first line; a list giving an item a number of its own (`<li value>`) keeps the browser's numbers in the same blue, since the counter does not count on from a value in Chrome. Numbers count thus: consecutive items are one list from 1, and a block between two lists starts the second at 1 again. So an item turned into text (Backspace at its start, or a conversion) splits its list there, the items after it a list of their own from 1; and lists a structural step sets side by side (the block between them deleted, cut, moved away or merged into the line above; an item turned into a list item between them) become one in the same undo step (`join.mjs`, run after the step by `struct()` in history.mjs through the tidier `tidyLists`, the lists of the flows it changes recorded with it): only pairs the step set side by side (a file's two lists written apart stay so), of one kind, both free (no `data-ge-keep`), the second with no `start` of its own and neither made by the step (an item's lists set out after it as it turns into text stay apart from the items split off after it). A file's list counting on from the numbered list before it (`data-continue`; other blocks between skipped) still shows that list's last number + 1 as its `start`, kept in step whenever lists change (`keepContinuing()`, `numbers.mjs`; written as the page changes, never an undo step of its own); the editor writes `data-continue` no more. While the page is edited a number is the reader's to set: under the pointer it is shaded the neutral grey of every hover (`data-ge-number`, a press shows it darker) and named 设置编号 by the editor's one tooltip (`tooltipAt`, 150ms); its click (a press moves no caret and opens no block) opens, on the card of `menu.mjs`, 开始新列表 (not when it shows 1: the first item's list from 1 again; a middle item's starts a new list there, the list split) and 修改编号值 (a field 新编号为 with 确定: the number typed as its depth writes numbers, 3, c or iii, or in digits, ↑/↓ a step; on the first item the list's start, or, right after a numbered list, that list taken on when it is the number that list counts on to; on a middle item a new list from it at that number; nothing when it already shows it). There is no 继续之前的编号: consecutive items count on by themselves. A list right after another numbered list with a number of its own writes its start, 1 too (`ownStart`), so nothing joins it to that list. Bullets open nothing. Each is one undo step, saved as the list's attributes (`ATTRIBUTES` for the unit kind `list`: `start` from 1, `data-continue`, written bare) or as a new list (its `start`, 1 too, and `continue` in its node, `extras`); a structural step records the attributes of the containers it changes (history.mjs), so a split's are undone with it. "3. " typed at a block's start (any number to 999999999) gives a numbered item showing 3: first in its list, the list starts there; further down a list where it would count otherwise, it begins a list of its own at that number, its start written, and the items after it count on (`numberTyped()`, inside the conversion's undo step); a space the Latin–Chinese spacing put after the marker goes with it. A list item dragged among blocks (any flow) joins a list of its kind right before or after the place, or becomes a list of its own there (`moveTo()` in drag/), an item going anywhere (a flowchart's node the same way: a flowchart of its own, "Flowcharts"; never a list a move leaves empty, which goes and would take them with it); the block handle of an item stands left of its marker (`extentOf()` reads where the kit's marker starts). `markerRect()` of `geometry.mjs` is the one reading of a marker as the browser laid it out (the item's ::before: its width, padding and margin; one drawn inside the item, a flowchart step's number, is none); the handle's reach, a number's tooltip and menu and a to-do's box all ask it (`markerAt()`: the marker's width, 2px more to either side, on the item's first line).
- **Overlays over the window.** A picture viewed (`image/viewer.mjs`) and an embed full screen (`embed/page.mjs`) cover the whole window: in a workbench, its window, sidebar and top bar too, drawn in the workbench's document (the page's iframe is of the same origin: `overlayHost()`, `addStyleTo()`, `mountOver()` in `page/ui.mjs`), their keys heard in either document; a page shown alone covers its own. The embed toolbar's icons are drawn on the grid their paths were made for (the small ones 16 units), so its 查看原始内容 arrow stands in the middle of its box; its block menu takes a 20-unit arrow.
- **Images.** 图片 (`figure.doc-image`, `components/image/`; the kit's `image.css`, `render/images.mjs`) is a picture with a caption. Its tools: a double-click or its toolbar's 展开 views it over the page (`image/viewer.mjs`: fitted to the window, the wheel zooming around the pointer 25%–400%, a zoomed picture dragged about, ‹ › through the page's pictures, 下载, Esc closing). A double-click opens it on a page that can be edited (a click where the page is read), and it opens any picture of the page that opens large for a reader (`src/shared/zoom.mjs`: an image block's, or one in a table's cell, a card, a column, a flowchart's node; not a link's, a control's or an icon, nor one shown under 48px), the viewer's ‹ › going through those (展开 asks for an image block's picture by name: it opens whatever its size, and is gone through with them), 下载 saving the one shown; a single click on a picture stays the editor's (it selects the image: the page object's `click`; the second click of a double-click does nothing more), as the kit's own viewer for readers (`runtime/zoom.js`, [render.md](render.md) "Opening a picture large") opens nothing while `html[data-ge-editing]` and shows no zoom-in cursor; 下载 in its block menu saves the picture by its name in the document's folder; 复制图片 there puts it on the system clipboard as a PNG, with the clipboard's own data ("Copying blocks"); 裁剪图片 (`image/crop.mjs`) marks what is kept with a box (moved, sized by its corners, 自由 or 1:1, 4:3, 3:2, 16:9) and 保存 puts a PNG of it (2000px wide at most) in the document's folder and in the picture's place, its caption, side and width kept, one undo step. Nothing is lost: the picture says the one it was cropped from and what of it is kept (`data-original`, `data-crop="x y width height"` in % of it), so 裁剪图片 again shows that original with the box kept then, what was cut away within reach; the box made the whole picture brings the original back (no new file). A picture pasted or uploaded from this computer shows as large as it was on its screen: its pixels over the device pixel ratio, as a share of the reading width, never past the width it stands in (decided 2026-10-16); one that would stand taller than 80% of the window so (a phone's screenshot) takes no span, the kit showing it at most that tall on every reader's window (`naturalShare`). The "/" menu's 图片 (an empty block only, as a divider: `typedTargets`) puts a placeholder where the block was (`newImage`: 添加图片, gray) and opens the 上传 / 链接 menu below it (`page/media-menu/`, in the icon picker's popup layer; files, bookmarks and embeds share it). A file (an image, 10 MB at most) goes to `POST /__gallery/edit/upload` with kind `image` (`media-store.mjs`: `assets/image-<hash>.<ext>`, the request up to 28 MB: the biggest upload as base64); a link's picture is downloaded there by the server, so an export never reaches the web. The picture in is one undo step; the image is written with it as type `image` `{ src, caption, classes?, span? }` (`image/file.mjs`), and one left empty as an empty figure (see "Empty blocks"). The preview serves its picture from the document's folder and says the file (`data-ge-src`, `embedImages`); the editor keeps working with the file. Its caption is a text block of its own kind (`mediaCaption`: may be empty, never removed), always written, shown while editing once it has text, has the caret or 标题 asks for it (with a 写一个标题… placeholder; one the reader empties goes out of sight only when the caret leaves it, the file keeping an empty one); Enter there makes a paragraph after the image. It is plain text while edited, no fill under the pointer or the caret (`--ge-text-tint: none`; the user, 2026-11-06). Backspace or Delete in an empty one (no words and no chip: a `<br>` or a zero-width space the browser leaves is nothing, a space is something) closes it: out of sight as when the caret leaves it, the file unchanged and no undo step, and the image selected, as Esc there does (Backspace again deletes it, Ctrl+Z bringing it back); a key held down stops at the emptied caption (`repeat`), so that holding Backspace to clear its words never takes the image with them. 标题 (the toolbar's or the block menu's) lets go of the selected blocks before it puts the caret in the caption, where the block menu's left the image's selection fill over it. The pointer on a picture shows nothing; while the image is the one block selected (a click on its picture, its handle, the keys, a picture just put in; none a component keeps), a short gray L stands at each of the picture's four corners and the toolbar at its top right (and no pale-blue selection fill over the picture while it is the one block selected: the image unit's unfilled, registry UNFILLED_ALONE; a placeholder with no picture, or the image among other blocks, is filled as any block) (对齐 with its popover of three sides, 标题, 展开, 更多: the block menu); the corners are the user's of 2026-11-09, taking the place of a 15px bar at each side. They are a part of the block tools (`blockTools`: `follow(picked)` places them in the block tools' frame, so they follow scrolling and reflow as the fills do; `holds` keeps the selection on a press on them or in the 对齐 popover), drawn in the overlay layer over the block's fill (`IN_LAYER.button`). An L is a 3px gray line running 12px along the picture's two edges from its corner, centered a little outside them, with no edge line round it (the user's of 2026-11-09: the edge of the page's own color, which made it read on a black picture in light mode and a white one in dark mode, was no longer wanted, "adding a line looks worse"): one neutral gray (#767676, 4.5 to 1 on white and on black alike, so it reads on the page and on any picture) with a soft shadow, darker (lighter, in dark) under the pointer, where the old bars, a translucent gray of the page's ink, vanished. Its box (24px, the corner 8px in from its outer corner) is what the pointer takes, with the diagonal resize cursor. The toolbar stands as far from the picture's top as from its right edge (`INSET`, 16px each way, which is just clear of the top right grip's box; the user's of 2026-11-09: "at least as far from the top as from the right", where it stood 6px below the top and 18px from the right); on a picture too narrow to hold it with that room each side, above the picture with its right edge on the picture's. Dragging a corner resizes the picture live, its height following (its shape kept): by how far the pointer moves across (a left corner the other way), as a share of the reading width, at most the width it stands in (`--doc-span`, a picture's share of the reading width wherever it stands, so a move into a column keeps its size; twice the move while centered, as it grows on both sides; snapping to the whole width it stands in, a share of 1), one look step on letting go, the image staying selected; Esc (which goes no further: the image stays selected), the window losing the focus or the page hidden cancels it (`cancelOnEscape`, `endsGesture`). A double-click shows the picture alone (a click or Esc closes it). The toolbar's icons are Gallery's own (`media/icons.mjs`, the user's of 2026-11-09: the bars against a guide line drawn before them were too busy), plain lines of a few strokes: 对齐's sides the editor's own alignment lines, those of a text block's and a table's 对齐 › (`table/icons.mjs`), 标题 a picture (its frame, one hill in it) over a line of words, 展开 one line corner to corner with a head at each end; the three dots of 更多 kept. The bar is placed every frame while the image is selected, and a button puts a new icon in only when it changed (`media/toolbar.mjs` `draw()`): one put in anew each frame took the svg a press landed on out of the page before the button was let go, and the browser fired no click, so no button worked by hand (a test's click, down and up at once, let no frame pass between). The block menu has what the toolbar has not (`menuItems`; the toolbar shows whenever the menu of the one image is open): 同步宽度到全局 first, where the page has another picture (every other image of the page with its picture, not one a component keeps, given the width this one shows at as a share of the reading width, 1 within the snap of the whole of it: one look step of them all, written as one change, undo selecting this one again; this one is left as it is; nothing to change says so), 替换图片 (a new image in its place, with its caption, side and width), 恢复原始大小, 裁剪图片 and 下载. An image file pasted with no text (a screenshot) makes an image right after the block, where a block pasted there goes (in a list's item or a quote's paragraph the list or quote splits after it: "Pasting") (`fromFiles`); one dropped in from outside, or a picture dragged from another page, an image where the line shows, and on an empty image its picture ("Dropping in from outside"). Its handle stands at its top (a block's handle stands at its first line: its first words, or the first picture standing as a block, as an image's before its caption). A picture with no side set is centered whatever it stands in (the page, a column, a list item, a panel), and its caption under it centered (as the user chose: centering only the page's own blocks and putting the others at the left would need a class that says "centered", a new `doc-image--center`, to write where the reader meant it). A file's, a bookmark's and an embed's captions are at the left (`media.css`, one rule for the four).
- **Files.** 文件 (`figure.doc-file`, `components/file/`; the kit's `file.css`, `render/files.mjs`) is a file kept with the document. The "/" menu's 文件 puts a placeholder (上传文件) and opens the 300px 上传 / 链接 menu (选择文件, the blue button; a link's file downloaded by the server). A file (20 MB at most, any type) goes to `POST /__gallery/edit/upload` with kind `file` (`media-store.mjs`: `assets/file-<hash>.<extension>`, answering its name and size; the request up to 28 MB); the block shows its row (a file icon, its name, its size as `12.1 KiB`, a download mark), written as type `file` `{ href, name, size?, caption }` (`file/file.mjs`, the name escaped). The preview hands the file out from `/__gallery/edit/file/<slug>/assets/<file>` for saving only (`attachment`, `nosniff`: an HTML or SVG file never runs in the editor's origin), the link keeping the file in `data-ge-href`. Its caption is a media block's (`mediaCaption`, as an image's). A file pasted that is no image makes a file block right after the block, where a block pasted there goes (in a list's item or a quote's paragraph the list or quote splits after it: "Pasting") (`fromFiles`); one dropped in from outside, a video among them (Gallery has no block of its own for one), a file block where the line shows, and on an empty file block its file ("Dropping in from outside"). Choosing a file (or an image's picture) puts a new block in the placeholder's place, never the same element, so undo brings the placeholder back unwritten and the file loses the block. The block menu adds 替换文件, 下载 and 标题.
- **Bookmarks.** 网页书签 (`figure.doc-bookmark`, `components/bookmark/`; the kit's `bookmark.css`) is a card of a web page. What the server fetches from the web (a page's title, description, icon and cover, whether a page lets frames show it, a picture given by its link) goes through this computer's proxy as its browser does (`editor/net-fetch.mjs`: HTTPS_PROXY and its kin, else Windows' Internet Options or macOS' scutil, read every 30s; NO_PROXY, this computer and the local network direct; a proxy not reached passed over for a direct try), so a site that blocks some regions still gives its card. The "/" menu's 网页书签 puts a placeholder (添加 Web 书签) and opens its link menu (300px, no tabs, the field focused); given a link it says 获取预览中… while the server reads the page (`POST /__gallery/edit/unfurl`, `editor/unfurl.mjs`: og:/twitter:/`<title>` title and description, the page's icon (or /favicon.ico) and og:image cover), keeping the icon and cover in the document's folder (`bookmark-icon-…`, `bookmark-cover-…`; .ico taken too). The card is written as type `bookmark` `{ href, title, description, icon, cover, caption }` (`bookmark/file.mjs`: text escaped, a page with no title showing its host); a page that cannot be read still makes a card. Its icon and cover are served and embedded as an image's picture (`render/images.mjs`); its caption is a media block's. A click on the card opens the page in a new tab. The block menu adds 标题, 拷贝原始链接 and 刷新预览 (the page read again, a new card in its place).
- **Embeds.** 嵌入 (`figure.doc-embed`, `components/embed/`; the kit's `embed.css`, `render/embeds.mjs`, `runtime/embeds.js`) shows a web page or a file in a frame. The "/" menu's 嵌入 puts a placeholder and opens its 300px menu: 链接, an https page (a YouTube video shown as its player, a Google document as its preview: `frameAddress`), which the server asks first (`POST /__gallery/edit/frameable`, `unfurl.mjs frameable`: X-Frame-Options, frame-ancestors); one that refuses frames becomes a bookmark in its place (`bookmarks.fromLink`). 上传, an HTML or PDF file (kind `embed`, 20 MB, a PDF's bytes checked), kept as `embed-<hash>.<extension>`. Written as type `embed` `{ src, height?, caption }` (`embed/file.mjs`): a web page's frame sandboxed, an HTML file's with no `allow-same-origin`, a PDF's with no sandbox (`doc-embed--file` for a file). The preview shows a file from `/__gallery/edit/embed/<slug>/assets/<file>`, an HTML file under a CSP sandbox (an opaque origin: the editor cannot reach it, nor it the editor), a PDF as is; the frame keeps the file in `data-ge-src`. An export embeds a file as a data: URL; the runtime shows a PDF's through a blob: address (`runtime/embeds.js`, written back by an export of the view), and the export's policy lets frames show https pages, data: and blob: addresses. With the pointer on the frame (its `mouseover`: the moves inside are the frame's own document's; the block handle takes it too), a toolbar (全屏, 查看原始内容, 更多) and a handle at each bottom corner (a gray bar bent round it, no border): dragging one sets the frame's width (`--doc-span`, a share of the width it stands in, the frame centered so both sides move) and `--doc-embed-height` (96–2000px) together, one look step, Esc cancelling; while a block or a handle is dragged the frames let the pointer through. 全屏 shows the page or file over the page in a frame of its own, sandboxed as its frame is (Esc, 关闭 or a click beside it closes it). An embed asks for no caption (one the file has still shows). The block menu adds 替换, 全屏, 查看原始内容, 复制链接 (where it shows from) and 恢复默认大小.
- **What the four media blocks share.** `components/media/`: `parts.mjs` (the figure of a new block with its placeholder and marks, the caption node and its words, `showFile`/`fileOf`: the pair of a shown file and what the file says, `MEDIA_HOSTS`), `write.mjs` (the empty figure the writers use; their text is made safe by `shared/escape.mjs`), `icons.mjs` (the toolbar and menu icons), `toolbar.mjs` (the bar and its buttons, an image's and an embed's), `size.mjs` (the width a column offers, the snap) and `page.mjs`, made once, whose `create(ctx)` takes what the editor gives every block (undo steps, the status line, `active()`, selecting, the block menu, new paragraphs, `upload(kind, body)`). It owns the one click on a placeholder (opening its block's menu), the caption's behaviour (shown while it has the caret, an empty one going out of sight when the caret leaves it, Enter making a paragraph after the block, no fill while edited (`--ge-text-tint: none`), Backspace or Delete in an empty one closing it and selecting its block, a key held down stopping there; its Enter, Backspace and Delete heard before `onKeydown`, document listeners of `create`), and, per block, `block(id)`: `attempt` (the placeholder's words say what is under way and go back if it fails, the reason said), `pick` (the menu), `uploadFile`/`uploadUrl`, `place` (the new element in the placeholder's place: one undo step, never the same element, so undo brings the unwritten placeholder back), `caption` (no block left selected), `paste` (a pasted file's new block, put where the paste's `put` says, clipboard/, then filled), `forFile` (a dropped file's new block, not on the page yet, and its fill once the drop put it there: drop-in/), `empty` (whether a block still shows its placeholder, which `takesDrop` asks). Each block's `page.mjs` keeps what is its own (its picture, row, card or frame, its menu's words, the tools and handles of an image and an embed), its `index.mjs` what it is, the parts `place` puts in among it (`pictureParts`, `fileParts`, `frameParts`, `cardParts`: pure DOM, so that a block the clipboard makes again of its data, which has no page, is built of the same; `captionHolding` and `shownFile` in `parts.mjs`), and what its copy carries ("Copying blocks"); `captionWords` writes a caption by `serializeInner` where the writer has it (a formula or a link chip pasted in a caption as the file holds it). The engines ask each block as any component (`menuItems`, `acceptsFile`, `fromFiles`, `fromLink`); `media` finds a block's page object in `ctx.pages` by its id for what is its own (`pick`). A new media block is a folder with those three files and a line in `components/pages.mjs`. What is chosen goes in through `place()`, which gives the placeholder that goes its own words back (`idle`: not "正在上传…" or "获取预览中…", which `busy` put there), so undo brings it back as it was (scenarios `fileBlock`, `bookmarkBlock`).
- **Tabs.** A new Sheet area (选项卡: in the "/" menu's 高级区块 and 在下方新建, for an empty block or below a block in a box that takes chapters, with its own tabs icon) is three tabs, 选项卡 1–3, their panels empty, under a name of its own (`tabs-xxxx`), no caret (`newSheets` in `components/sheets/page.mjs`); the kit's `sheets.js` is put on the page when it has none (`ensureSheetsKit`: a page with no area is served without it), and the commit's file gets its tag too (the service's `finish` hook, `withNeededKitTags` in render/assets.mjs, the version the page takes being the file's as written). It is written as the kit writes one (`components/sheets/file.mjs`: the row of tab buttons, then one panel per tab with its blocks). A tab's emoji (`data-tab-icon`) is drawn as its Twemoji picture, never the system's font: `embedIcon` (render/emoji.mjs) gives each such tab `--doc-tab-icon` when the page is served (the server's image), built or exported (a data: URL), the kit draws it before the name (sheets.css), `pills.mjs` sets it again as the emoji changes, and a view copy embeds it; the file keeps the emoji alone. A Sheet area (选项卡区块; `components/sheets/pills.mjs`): its tabs are pills with no handles of their own, and the area's handle stands on its tab row (the pointer there). A click on a tab not shown switches to it (`sheets.js`, as when reading); a click on the tab shown opens its menu below it, 180px wide (重命名, 删除: the block menu's tab branch), a second click closes it; the right button opens any tab's, switching first. Pressing a tab and moving it more than 4px drags it along its row (`drag/`; no handle shows meanwhile). With the pointer on a tab row, + (28px, round) stands after the last tab: it makes 选项卡 N at the end (`page.mjs newTab`: a tab made on the page under a key the area has not used, and its empty panel, a box made on the page), shows it and renames it at once; one undo step. The commit writes it as type `tab` (`{ title, sheet, children }`, `source/apply.mjs insertTab`: the tab after the one it follows, its panel after that one's panel, set apart as the panels are) and names the area too, so the answer gives the new panel's box (`commit.mjs adopt`, the box keeping its key); the tab's name is its own text, in its span. An empty panel is a box (`source/units.mjs`): blocks go into it, and while edited it shows a placeholder (空白选项卡。点击或拖动区块到这里。), where a click makes a paragraph (`insert/ clickBlank`); the placeholder takes that paragraph's room (a line of body text, no margin: a panel's last block takes none below it, `styles/flow.css`, and a paragraph's top margin falls into the tabs' own), its fill under the pointer a 3px shadow that takes no room, so the area's bottom does not move when the paragraph comes (`components/sheets/pills.mjs`). Deleting a tab takes its panel, and the one right of it shows (left of it, when it was the last); the source a deletion gives back is the tab and its panel (`text.mjs tabSource`), so undo puts both back in the file, and what the panel holds goes and comes back with them ("Commits"). Undo or redo landing in a panel out of sight shows it (`showLanded` in `editor.mjs`). Whenever a row's tabs change (+, deleting, moving, undo, redo) the area starts again (`DocKit.destroy` and `mount`), so `sheets.js` knows them; a tab shown after an undo is clicked once the area has started again. The frame (`doc-sheet-shell--plain` off) is the block menu's 显示边框 (`menuItems` of the Sheet area's page module, `components/sheets/page.mjs`). A tab's menu has 编辑图标 too (the whole menu is the Sheet page object's `menu`, which the block menu asks first, as it asks each component for its `menuItems`): the emoji picker below the tab (表情符号 only: `openMediaPicker` `only`), the emoji kept as the tab button's `data-tab-icon` (a unit attribute, `ATTRIBUTES.tab`), one undo step; the kit shows it before the name (`sheets.css`). 显示边框 is a switch (a `checked` menu row, clicked again to turn it off).
- **Highlight blocks.** 高亮块 (`.doc-panel`, the opening summary `.doc-summary` being one; the kit's `highlight.css`) is a light gray box, 10px round, its blocks 12px in. An icon may come first in it: the icon component's (`components/icon/`, the page's icon's: an emoji, an icon, an image), standing left of its blocks, 24px, on their first line. A click on it opens the icon picker (移除 takes it away); the block menu has 颜色 › (its color classes: a background, or its text colored with a thin border) and 添加图标 / 更换图标 (`rowBeside`). The server writes an icon first in its highlight block whatever place the page gave (`apply.mjs`), and only the header's icon is report.json's. A block turned into a highlight block makes a new one with 💡 (`iconElement`), never joining one next to it; one dissolved into its blocks drops its icon. Its first block (after the icon) has no handle of its own: the highlight block's stands on that line; so a quote's first paragraph, the quote's (`firstOfPanel`: a box's `firstLineActs`, declared in its description). A block dropped before its icon goes right after it; a copied highlight block carries its icon.
- **Callouts.** 提示块 (`div.doc-callout`, `components/callout/`; the kit's `callout.css`) is a container of blocks as a highlight block is (the user's, 2026-11-07, before which it held inline text alone, one text block that did not split): one rule for both, its own look apart. Its description says the highlight block's box fields (`boxes.callout`: `role: "flow"`, `leaveByEnter`, `endIsPadding`, `firstLineActs`, `groups`, `holdsOne`, `selectAll`) and type fields (`container`, `takesAsIs`, a `holder`, `nests: false`, `joins: false`), its unit `removedEmpty`, and its clipboard the highlight block's hooks (`components/set-apart.mjs`: its blocks, words straight in it a paragraph of them; `kind: "aside"`); none of a lead (no icon comes with a new one, `leadIcon`), and it takes a background alone, blue with none (`colorDefaults`). So Enter at the end of its words makes a paragraph in it and Enter on its empty last paragraph moves that paragraph out after it (in a highlight block or a node, into that), Backspace in its one empty block deletes it, "/" and a drop put any block a flow takes in it (a list, a code block, a table…; no callout in a callout from the menus, a file or a drop may still nest one), its first block shows no handle of its own (the callout's stands on that line, as a highlight block's), Ctrl+A takes its blocks a step, a click under its last block is its padding, a block turned into a callout goes in as it is (an item a list in it; from "/" an empty block stands for it, an empty paragraph in it), and it turns into a disclosure, a quote or a highlight block and back with what it holds (the highlight block's flowchart conversions are the highlight block's alone: they carry its icon and color to a node). The kit lays its blocks out as a highlight block's (flow.css's flows, `highlight.css`'s first and last margins: `.doc-callout` beside `.doc-panel` in both). One written the old way, its words straight in it (every callout of a document before, and what an Agent may still write), holds them as one paragraph: natural HTML makes the paragraph ("Natural HTML": a box that always holds a block), in memory while the page is only read, in the file the first time the page writes it, its lines kept; one the file has empty is a box holding none, a line high, and a click in it makes a paragraph there; copied or cut, it is pasted with an empty paragraph in it, which the commit writes ("Empty blocks": a box that always holds a block). Copied, it carries its blocks and its color and is pasted a callout of them; for other programs and in a page export it is the highlight block's `<aside>` (the `aside` writer, "Copying blocks"), its HTML a `<div>` of its blocks. Its fill is its color taken 30% toward the page's background (`color-mix(in srgb, <fill> 70%, var(--doc-bg))`: a little lighter than the color's own, the user's of the same day), its left line that color's deep shade as before (scenarios `calloutBlocks`, `calloutHandle`, `calloutEnter`, `calloutParagraph`, `calloutLook`, `calloutOnTheClipboard`).
- **To-do lists.** 待办事项 is a list of the kit's class, `ul.doc-todo`, a third kind of list beside bullets and numbers (`components/list/`: the `todo` type, `listType()`; its look the kit's `todo.css`). Each item has a box where a bullet would be, 16px with 5px corners and a 1.25px gray line (`--doc-check-line`), its middle on the first line's; a checked item (`li[data-checked]`) has it filled with the calm control blue (`--doc-control-blue`, a switch's when on, `#4a81d4`, `#4f7fcb` dark) with a white check and its own text struck through and faded, the lists it holds as they are (the product's one checkbox of 2026-11-17). A click on the box checks an item or takes its check away, the selected to-do items with it when it is one of them (all to the same state), one undo step (`lookStep` with the item's attribute: ATTRIBUTES); Ctrl+Enter does it for the item with the caret. The box under the pointer has its gray line darker at once (`--doc-check-line-hover`), pressed a faint fill under it too (6% of the field gray: `data-ge-todo-box` on the item, the page's only, since a pseudo-element has no `:hover` of its own), and a check's blue comes in over .2s; a disclosure's arrow is a code block's on the page that edits (no fill under the pointer, the user's 2026-11-19; a 20px square of `--doc-code-pressed` only while pressed, its summary's `::after`, `details/page.mjs`; until then a 24px square filled `--doc-hover` under the pointer and `--doc-press` pressed). `[] `, `[ ] ` and `【】` at a block's start make it a to-do, `[x] ` a checked one (markdown/). Everything else is any list's: Enter goes on with an unchecked item, Enter in an empty one turns it into text, Tab nests a to-do list under the item before, items drag and copy as to-do items (checked ones as checked). A bulleted list and a to-do list turn into each other by the list's class switch (`toggles` of the list kind, sent with the conversion: `convertedUnits`); the server takes a list carrying only its kind's classes as a box of items (`boxKind` in `source/units.mjs`). An item turned into another type leaves its check behind, on the page (`standIn`) and in the file (carried attributes leave out what the old kind sets). Items made or split off on the page carry their check (`attributes` on a new node or part, checked against ATTRIBUTES: `validAttributes`).
- **Flowcharts.** 步骤流程图 (the kit's 流程图) is an ordered list of the kit's class, `ol.doc-flow` (for the kit a kind of list: its description is the list's, `src/shared/components/list.mjs`; its look the kit's `flowchart.css`, its layout the kit's runtime: docs/render.md "Design defaults"), and for the editor a block of its own (`components/flowchart/`: unit kind `flowchart`, a box of kind `flowchart` that takes steps alone; the `flowchart` type, the list kinds' last in the "/" menu and the type grid, right after 待办事项, made by no Markdown; `listType()`, `LIST_TYPES`). Its steps are no list items: each `li` of it is a unit of kind `step` (a match rule by its parent, `registry.mjs` `parent`) and a node, a container as a highlight block is (the user's, 2026-10-23 and 2026-10-25), a box of kind `node` (a flow led by its icon, its first line showing the node's handle, an empty paragraph put back once it is emptied, and `firstBlock: "paragraph"`): its first block (after its icon) always a paragraph, its title, bold as the file writes it (`<p><strong>…</strong></p>`: real markup, the reader's to take off), the blocks after it its content, any blocks a flow takes (lists of any kind, pictures, code, a flowchart: cards in a card), drawn as ordinary blocks in its card (a list there with the kit's markers from the first depth again, `flow.css`). `core.mjs` `parentItem`, `holderItem`, `previousLine` and `nextLine` stop at a node, so the list machinery never reaches it: the keys in a node are the highlight block's (the mock's keys table, `components/flowchart/page.mjs`): Enter splits the block the caret is in, inside the node (at the end of the title a plain paragraph below it, in its middle the rest a plain paragraph, its bold taken off when it covered the whole title: `splitBlock` of a first line, never a block before it, never a new node or a list; at the very start of a title holding words a new node before this one, its title empty, the words, their bold and the caret staying where they are, one undo step, as a list makes an empty item before an item: nothing goes before a title in its node, so the empty line is a node of its own, `enterAtStart`); Enter on the empty last block of a node (not its title) makes it the first line of a new node after it, and Enter on the empty only line of the flowchart's last node takes the node out of the flowchart, a body paragraph after it (`typing/leave.mjs` `nodes`); Backspace at the start of a block of a node joins it to the block above inside the node (an empty one deleted, the caret at the end of the block above), an item of a list in it a paragraph of the node first (nested items out a level within the list), never out of the node; at the start of a node's title Backspace deletes the node when nothing is written in it (the step is `removedEmpty`: `typing/` `emptyContainer`, one undo step, its icon with it, the flowchart with its last node), and otherwise changes nothing, the caret going to the end of the node before where ← takes it (`caret.mjs` `arrow`: the end of its last block, a code block there opened at the end of its code, a closed disclosure's title; the status line saying so in the first node); Delete at the end of a node's last block takes nothing in (neither the next node nor the block after the flowchart), and joins the block after it anywhere else in the node; Backspace at the start of the block after a flowchart selects the flowchart, as after a highlight block (its line above is the flowchart: nothing joins a node from outside it); Tab and Shift+Tab indent only within a node's lists, never a node into another (elsewhere in a node a hint). The title is turned into nothing (`convert.mjs` `convertible`: no 转换为 in the format bar, no Markdown shortcut there), what the "/" menu makes from it goes below it (`slash-menu/`), nothing dropped or pasted lands before it (`drag/drop.mjs` `headOf`), and a structural step that leaves a node with another block first (its title deleted or dragged away) puts an empty title back first (`core.mjs` `tidyEmptied`); an empty title says 节点标题 and has bold armed (`format-bar/`: the caret-kept bold Ctrl+B with nothing selected keeps, armed in a new node, an emptied one and one a conversion made, let go by Ctrl+B there until something is typed; what is pasted there is bold too, as typed: "Pasting"). A node is added after one by the round "+" centred on its right border at its vertical middle while the pointer is on it (`components/flowchart/add.mjs`: 22px, the card's surface lifted by a soft shadow and no line round it, a thin plus that goes darker under the pointer, the surface gray only while pressed, drawn in the layer over the page, over the handle and a section's bar there: `IN_LAYER` `button`, so the half of a row's last node's button over its section's bar is the button's) and by 在后面添加节点 in its block menu (which makes no block below a node), one undo step each, the caret in its empty title. Its icon is a highlight block's (`components/icon/page.mjs`): first in it, drawn in its number's place, picked from the picker a click on the icon or on the number opens (the pointer a hand over either; or 添加图标 / 更换图标 in its block menu), 移除 bringing the number back; an icon of Tabler's numbers shows a fixed number. Its colors (颜色: a background fills the card, a text color its words, number and border), its own `data-direction` and its weight in its row (`--doc-flow-ratio`, a share) are the node's; the flowchart's direction and its first number (`start`) are the flowchart's. 方向 (`menuItems()` in `components/flowchart/page.mjs`; the mock's interpretation A, the user's of 2026-10-25; its row's icon a node and its two ways out, its choices' 20-unit line icons a node and a spark for 跟随整体 and 自动换行, two nodes side by side and the arrow between them for 接在右边 and 排成一行, one below the other for 接在下边 and 排成一列, each choice its words alone, no note under it: the user's of 2026-10-25): on a node, under 这个节点之后, the joint after it, its `data-direction` (跟随整体 none, the flowchart's; 接在右边 `row`, the next node beside it, past the kit's count while the cards keep their least width, in a 排成一列 flowchart too; 接在下边 `column`, the row ends after it and the arrow turns from this row's end to the next row's start), the node's choice named beside the row; below them, under 整个流程图, the flowchart's own, the list's `data-direction` (自动换行 none, the kit filling rows as wide as read well and wrapping the rest: the user's decision of 2026-10-25; 排成一行 `row`, one row, wrapping only where a card would be too narrow; 排成一列 `column`, one node a row), since the handles a reader finds on a flowchart are its nodes' (as a list's are its items'); the flowchart's own block menu holds its three alone, under 流程图的方向. A choice is one undo step (a look step) that selects the block whose menu it was again. Widths: the gap between two nodes of a row, as the kit laid them out, is the gap tool of 分栏 (`page/columns/`, "Columns"): with the pointer in it a grey line down its middle along the cards, starting 6px under the arrow across the gap so the arrow stays in sight, a col-resize cursor and that arrow dimmed; dragging it (the line a darker gray, darkest where it snaps) moves the two nodes beside it, snapping to their equal split and to tenths of the row within 5px, neither narrower than the kit lets a card be beside others (116px and its longest word with what stands beside it, measured where it is drawn: `leastWidth()`, a pixel to spare), so the row never wraps under the pointer, the row's shares in the bottom notice ("节点宽度：30% / 20% / 25% / 25%"; no badge on the cards since 2026-11-07); the weights are written on the row's nodes as it goes, so the kit lays the row out as it will be (its widths, the turning arrow from its last node), commits waiting meanwhile (`dragging`, `editor.mjs` `blocked`), and letting go puts them back and writes every node of that row its weight (`--doc-flow-ratio`, three places) as one look step (Esc leaves them as they were); a double click on the gap takes the row's weights off, equal widths again, one undo step. Each row's weights are its own (`rowsOfFlow()`, `rowShares()`: the shared `flowShares()` the kit lays a row out by), and in 自动 they change no row: the kit counts a row at equal widths and the weights only where a card would be under its least (docs/render.md). Turning a list into 步骤流程图 is a structural conversion (`flowchartFromList()`): each item a node, its words its title (made bold), what it holds its content as it is; back into a list (`listFromFlowchart()`) each node an item, its title's words its own (the bold taken off when it covered them all), its content held, its icon left behind (the items and nodes made anew: their ids stay behind; what they hold keeps its own); a highlight block turns into a flowchart of one node holding its blocks (`flowchartFromPanel()`), and a node from its own menu into another kind of list (an item of its own between the flowchart's two parts, joining a list of that kind beside it) or a highlight block (`stepTargets()`, `convertStep()`), its color with it in the file too ("Block colors"); a block turned into 步骤流程图 is a node of the flowchart beside it or of a new one, its words its title (`convertBlock`). A flowchart split in two keeps its direction on both parts (`newTail()` in `core.mjs`), and two flowcharts a change sets side by side join only when they are laid out alike (`joins()`); a numbered list next to a flowchart is no list it counts on from or joins (`previousList()`, `ownStart`), and a node's number opens no number menu. While edited a node keeps its card's corners and the blocks in it a text block's (`--ge-text-radius`), and a selected node's fill and the frame of the one edited follow them (the kit's `--doc-box-radius` on a step, read by `boxRadius()`); a whole flowchart selected is filled with its cards' corners (scenario `groupFillCorners`). What the kit set on a flowchart (`data-doc-flow-key`, the key of the stylesheet that lays it out, and the name 流程图) is not carried to the list it becomes. Its number in a circle is drawn inside the card, which `markerRect()` takes for no marker, so a node's extent, handle and frame are its card's; its arrows are `::after`s. A node beside another in its row has its grip in the middle of the 28px gap between them (left of the node it would cover the card before it and the arrow), the arrow under it dimmed while it shows (the mock's grip; `handle/`, `nodeBefore()`), its bar and "+" over the corner of the card before; a row's first node has its grip at its left as any block. Copied, a flowchart carries its direction and first number and each node its blocks (its icon among them), color, direction and weight (`clipboard/own/`: a `step` block); a node copied on its own pasted where no flowchart takes it is a flowchart of its own, and by a block of a node goes after that node (nodes never nest); text selected across a flowchart copies it as a flowchart ("Copying text"), and one copied from a reader's page pastes as one ("Pasting"). A node dragged by its handle out of its flowchart goes among the blocks of any flow outside a flowchart, a flowchart of its own there, or of the one it lands right beside (as a list item makes a list: drag/ `dropAt`, `moveTo`); never into another node or under a list item. Natural HTML: `<ol data-doc-chart="flow">` and `<ol data-flow>` are made `ol.doc-flow`, and a step written the short way (its own words first) or empty (`<li></li>`, its icon alone) a node ("Natural HTML"), which is what the page always sees; the server reads a node as a box of kind `node` (`source/units.mjs` `flowNode`), its blocks units of it, its title among them, and no text block of the step's own (`source/blocks.mjs`), and writes a node made on the page as `<li>` holding its blocks, with its color, direction and weight (`components/flowchart/file.mjs`); a unit put back by undo is of the kind it is in the box it goes into (`kindIn`: an `li` a step in a flowchart). An icon given to a node goes first in it, as a highlight block's (`source/apply.mjs`: any box an icon leads), and one taken out of a node is never the page's (report.json's stays). The file's Markdown export writes it as an ordered list, each node's emoji and title (bold as written) on its line and its content under it, 4 spaces in (an icon of the 图标 tab or a picture gives no emoji; a flowchart in a node is its item's nested list: `src/transfer/markdown.mjs`); it prints, and the workbench's PDF shows it, as a screen as wide as the paper shows it, the same rows, widths, directions and arrows, 自动 measured at the printed page's width, a browser's own print (Ctrl+P) too (the kit lays it out again as the print media comes); one that fits on a page is kept on one, one taller breaks between its rows where it stands, the arrows a page's break parts from their rows left out in a browser's print (no layout rule for print: docs/render.md "Design defaults", docs/workbench.md "PDF"). Scenarios `flowBlock`, `flowNodes`, `flowNodeKeys`, `flowAddNode`, `flowEmptyNodeBackspace`, `flowNodeIcon`, `flowListRoundTrip`, `flowRows`, `flowWidths`, `flowNodeDirection`, `flowPrint`, `flowPrintPages`, `flowCopyAcross`, `flowNodeColorKept`, `flowNodeDragOut`, `flowTitlePaste`, `flowNodeHandles`.
- **Detail cards.** 详情卡片 (`dialog.doc-dialog`, `components/dialog/`; the kit's `dialogs.js` and `styles/dialog.css`) opens as for a reader, by a click on its entry (links/), and closes as for a reader, every time (it has no close button): a press outside it, on its backdrop (dialogs.js, on the press, a listener of the document's, so a card the page made or put back closes the same way, and a press inside it that ends outside, selecting its text, keeps it open, as does the second press of a double click on its entry, which is no press on the card either (`ui.mjs` `swallowNext`); before, a click closed it, and a click whose press began inside ended on the backdrop closed it too); Esc, after what the page's Esc takes first: the format bar, a selection across its blocks (which Esc makes those blocks selected), selected blocks (which it lets go), so a caret in its text closes it at once. However it closes, nothing stands where it was (`dialog/page.mjs`, on the kit's `close`): what opens over the page goes, blocks selected in it are let go, and a caret left in it comes out at its entry, outside it (in the card on top when that holds the entry, else that card's first text). In a workbench the card stands in the middle of the workbench's window, as the workbench's own dialogs (组件列表, 写作偏好) do, not of the document's frame right of the sidebar and under the top bar: the page's session says where that middle falls in the page from the frame's place (`session.mjs` `centreCards`; the kit's `dialog.css` moves the card there, as far as the frame lets it stay 24px clear of its sides: render.md, "Detail cards"). Its backdrop covers only the document's frame, so the workbench covers its own bars (`features/report/cards.ts`, from the session's `card`): dimmed alike, a press there closes the card on top (`close-card`) and does nothing else, and Esc with the focus in the workbench closes it too (docs/workbench.md, "Session protocol"); the cover goes however the card closes, and with the page (reloaded, swapped after an Agent's write, another document shown: the session's `card` is the new page's). It is an ordinary editing area and nothing else: no title and no bar, its content a box as a disclosure's. A new one (更多 › 详情卡片) holds a heading 3 of the selected words, copied once and never tied to its entry, and an empty paragraph with the caret in it (`dialog/page.mjs`). That heading has no privilege: it splits, moves, merges, converts and goes as any block, and no id, `aria-labelledby` or name binds it; a card is named where a name is needed, by its first heading, else by the words that open it (`dialogHeading`, `entryOf`: the kit as it opens, the entry's notice, `gallery qa`, Markdown). Emptied of all its blocks it keeps an empty paragraph with the caret in it (`holdsOne`, the disclosure's rule). An undo or redo that lands in a card closed meanwhile opens it, and a new card redone comes back as the modal it was (`modalOps`'s `show`, `history.mjs` `restore`); so does a word 搜索文档 found in one (`find/`). A page shown again (reloadView, the workbench's swap) has the cards it had, the same one on top, each scrolled where it was, the caret where it was (`modalOps`'s `kept` and `reopen`, `keepWith`); opened with no key or press between them, they would be one to the browser's Esc, so with two or more open the page's Esc closes the one on top itself (`closeTop`, which the workbench's `close-card` runs too). As a disclosure, it takes no sections or tabs, and 批注 is not offered in it. No block of the page's flow, a card is in no selection, so no copy holds one; a page's Markdown (导出, 复制页面内容) writes every card something opens after the page's blocks, in file order, each a disclosure of its first heading, else of the words that open it, a heading so used not written again in its body (`clipboard.apart` on its unit, `read` giving `without`, read by `src/transfer/markdown.mjs`: the element is left out, the content never cloned), a chart in it pictured with the card open and as tall as what it holds, the window grown by the chart's height (`chartPictures`; the card's own fixed height would cut a tall chart off); a card nothing opens shows nowhere and is left out (`reached`). Its entry goes whole, words and icon, by Backspace at the stop right of it or Delete at the stop left of it, one undo step, as a chip of the text goes (`typing/code-edges.mjs` `takeEntry`, on the key's keydown and on its `beforeinput` where an input method's keydown says 229, which onKeydown passes by: the browser alone took the pad and put the caret in its words, left of its icon, the user's report of 2026-11-06); inside it, at the end of its words, Backspace takes a character, its words edited from inside it as a link's. The caret comes to the stop right of it however it comes there: a card closed by a press on its backdrop (the card closes on the press, its caret put there before the release, and the release of a press that came down on no text settles the caret where it is, `settleCodeEdge`, picking no side by the press's x, `codeClick`), or words typed after it and deleted back (`deletedBack`). Its entry taken away, a new card is not saved and one of the file stays in it, as nothing on the page opens it (the notice says so). Its size is the kit's, the same while edited and for a reader, a little smaller than the workbench's settings dialogs (写作偏好): 760 by 80% of the window, at most 760, centred over the window whatever it holds and wherever the outline is (in a workbench the workbench's window, `centreCards` in session.mjs), its text the reading column in the middle of it (render.md, "Detail cards"), with no rule of the editor's. Its blocks' handle stands inside the card: where the card is wide enough for its text to stand 84px in (880, a page's style making it so) the handle is the page's; on the kit's 760 card and narrower ones (48px in) the grip stands 12px clear of its edge, the square of the bar and the "+" left of it half its width there and the "+" 16px, 10.5px across, its lines as heavy (`data-tight`, handle/: what is left of the grip is cut off, `shape.clip`), so that it never stands over the backdrop, where a press closes the card. In its content blocks are selected as on the page: a box drawn from its empty space (`marquee.mjs` `topOf`: the content of the card the press is in, its own top as for Ctrl+A, never the page behind it; the rectangle drawn in the card, its start kept on the content as the content scrolls, by the wheel or near its edges: `edge-scroll.mjs`, the content of that card, never one under it), and a click on its empty space is a click there (`insert/` `clickBlank` with the card's content as the page: below its last block a paragraph or the caret into the empty one, beside a line the caret on it); text across its blocks is `text-select/`'s, Ctrl+A `select-all/`'s.
- **Merging and conflicts.** `commitEdits()` finds each block the page names in the file as it is now (see "Commits"), and `applyEdits()` writes into that file: its edits are text blocks by their index in `findBlocks()`, its operations units by their index in `findUnits()`, and nothing in it looks for a block again, so every byte outside the ranges changed stays. A text both sides changed is merged by `commitEdits()` with the file's version word by word (`mergeText()`, a three-way merge over what the page last committed, the reader's and the file's inline HTML): tokens are tags, entities, words, runs of spaces and single other characters (each Chinese character on its own), compared by meaning (entities decoded, attribute quotes and spacing ignored), so the editor's rewrite of text it did not change matches the source; changes with at least one unchanged token other than a space between them both go in, and the result is validated like any edit. Both sides changing a block to the same text is no conflict. Changes to the same or neighbouring places (or an invalid result) stay a conflict of that block alone, answered with the file's version (`theirs`). Invalid content, overlapping ranges or a block something refers to reject an edit: `applyEdits()` fails the whole call, or, with `partial` (what commits use), applies the rest and lists what it left out. Every piece of a write is of the edit or operation it came from (`of`): one that cannot go in (overlapping another, a block put inside itself) is a conflict of that edit or operation, never left out while the answer says it was written; the items split off a list item go with it when it moves (once they were written at its old place, which the move then cut away). A list whose items' texts are all taken out is removed with them, unless the write deletes it already.
- **Commits.** The autosave's way of writing (`page/commit.mjs`, `commitEdits()` in `source/commit.mjs`, `POST /__gallery/edit/commit`). A commit is what changed since the last one, found by `collectEdits()` against the layout and baselines that commit left, and names nothing by where it was in some version: a unit by its `data-block-id`, or, untouched since the page opened and without one yet, by its number in the version the page names its units by (`opening`: the one it opened on, or the one it last took in where it stands, "The pull"; each unit's number there kept by unit, `openUnits`) and its source hash (its block ids left out: "Block ids"; the one unit of the file with that hash, none the page knows by an id; else where the opening version had it, the endpoint finding that version by its hash among the snapshots; identical units by their order among their twins when the file has as many as the opening version; the only one with that hash is taken for it); a text block by its unit, as the unit's own text (`self`) or a field of it by its structural path (`[root, [tag, index, count]…]`, from the unit down, with the sibling counts), the document's title or, outside any unit, by its text; a box by the unit it is in and its kind (and key), or, in no unit, by its kind and key alone (the page, the document's header, a detail card's content); and every text carries the source it had at the last commit (`before`; after 保留我的 the file's words the card showed, with the source the reader's were written from as `from`, which the file's commit leaves alone and the review of the Agent's changes replays from: "Conflicts", "Agent's changes"). No cell gets an id for this: when the structure along a field's path changed, only its text can say which one it is, once in its unit, or it is a `structure` conflict, never a guess. Where the file still has `before`, the text goes in; where it changed, both changes merge word by word (`mergeText()`) or that block alone is a conflict. A deletion or conversion carries the hash the page saw (the file changed the unit: a conflict, which gives the hash the file has; told to do it anyway, the operation carries that one: "Conflicts"); moves, sizes and switched classes do not depend on its content, and deleting a unit the file no longer has is done already. A Sheet tab's hash covers its panel (`unitHash()`), so a panel someone else changed makes deleting the tab a `changed` conflict, and the page names the tab in `touch` whenever a commit changes anything in its panel (a text, a block made, moved or taken out: `boxOwner()`, the `owner` of the panel's box in its description), so that its hash is the file's after the commit and deleting it later is no false conflict. A list or quote the page deletes names the units it held (`items`): where the file has it changed (someone added an item), the deletion goes item by item, what was added stays, and an item they changed is a conflict of the deletion (the list went as "applied" while the reader's item stayed in the file); an item the file refuses to take out once the rest goes (something it holds refers to it: `referenced`) makes the deletion a conflict that names the items that went all the same (`went`, by the page's keys). A unit without an id found only as the one most like it (where it stood: `counterpart()`, the file changed since) counts only while it still says much the same (`resembles()`: the pairs of characters of their words, Dice's coefficient at least one half); surroundings alone also fit a unit deleted with what was around it where something like it stands elsewhere (the last paragraph of another section), and what the page put next to it, into it or did to it would land there. Every unit a commit names is one unit of the file, and no two are the same one: a unit found only as the one most like it that another of the commit's names is found as too is gone instead, and one of the page's units acts on a unit of the file, never two (`ambiguous`); two acting on one would write it twice (a block deleted by the Agent, found as another the same commit moved, came out three times). A container deleted that the file has otherwise goes all the same when the commit leaves nothing in it (`hollow`: all it holds leaves, or is what the page says goes as the page saw it): the change left with its blocks (a section dissolved over the Agent's change to one), and it would stand empty and unseen in the file. A unit deleted while blocks in it, or in what it takes along, go elsewhere (a section dissolved) is deleted only when every one of them does: when one has no place (its neighbour gone), the deletion is refused too (`position`), or the block would go with it; the page then tries the moves at the end of their box and the deletion again, once, before either is the reader's to decide. Blocks inside a deleted unit that the page shows elsewhere but the commit places nowhere (they stand in a container not written yet: a new row of columns whose other column is still empty) go from the file with it; the page makes them blocks of its own from then on (`leftBehind` in `commit.mjs`), written anew once their container is (fuzz seed 3042 with block operations: an item typed into one of them later was refused, its list gone from the file). A list (or quote) whose every unit a commit deletes or takes elsewhere, with nothing put into it, goes too (the page may still show it around a new item not written yet, empty), unless a block is placed next to it: then it stays, empty, as the page shows it (going, it took that block along: a section moved after a list whose only item was deleted went from the file, model seed 367); a unit whose deletion or move the file refused, or whose new container it refused, stays, and so does the list around it (going, the list once took along an item whose move was refused); a list with no items left still takes them, as the kit's containers do. The answer names the units the file no longer has after a commit (`gone`): one the page did not delete but still shows is taken as deleted (no source to put back: a container is made anew around its blocks, a text block is a new block). Every unit whose text the commit changes, the units around it, those inside a unit it moves (they go to a new indentation with it) and the units right beside a block it makes (the nearest on each side in its box, `touchBeside`; not one undo puts back) are named in `touch`, so that each has an id afterwards: the revert check finds where a block the reader made stood by those ids, and tells a write from before the commit that made it, which has them without ("Someone else's writes"). The receipt gives the ids and hashes of what the page named or made, in tables of their own (`ids` and `hashes` for the units the page named, by the key it named each by, `made` for the blocks it made or put back, by the key it made each under, and `hashes` for those too), and what the file now says of them (`file`, `fileFacts()` in `source/commit.mjs`: by id, each one's kind, hash and what refers to it, the unit around it, its boxes with the units in each, its text blocks' texts, and those of every unit inside a block the commit made or put back). The text blocks outside any unit, the document's title and one written beside a detail card's content, are said apart in `outside` (`outsideFacts()`: their texts by key, `outsideKey()` of `shared/ids.mjs`: `title` for the document's, `id:<id>` for the others, by the element's own id, so that an element whose id is `title` is never the document's title), which is what each starts from (no unit says it for them). A detail card is no unit (the file knows it by its id and it carries no block id), so the receipt says apart what the file holds of the keyed blocks the commit made, written now or there already (`keyed`, `keyedFacts()`: the units of its content), and the page takes each in as a block of the file (`adoptKeyed()` in `commit.mjs`): its content a box of the map, the units the commit wrote in it taken in as above. Nothing of it is made again (before, the page sent the card with every commit, the file already had it, the blocks typed into it were never written and the commits went on without end). Its making undone after it was saved, the entry goes from the file and the card stays there with nothing opening it, as one whose entry the reader took out (it is no unit to delete): nothing in it is sent while it is off the page (`keyedAway`), and redone, the page shows the file's again. The page takes the receipt in without reloading and knows of the file only what the file says, never what it sent and expects the file to hold (`adopt()` in `commit.mjs`; working that out case by case is where the page and the file used to drift apart): the units the commit named or made are found on the page by their ids, whether or not they are still on it (a block deleted while the commit writing it was on its way is in the file all the same; known, it goes with the next commit), and join the map with their boxes (every item the file has is the box for lists under it) and their text blocks (each found by its path in its unit, `blockElement`, from the unit's text blocks indexed by path once for all of them, `pathIndex`, again only after `asLoaded` made one anew: looked for anew for each, a commit in a table of 500 rows held the page still for a minute, every cell walked past its row's 500 siblings for each of the thousand the answer named), each text's `before` being the file's own text (whitespace a structural change left in it too); a text block that joins the map so, not sent by the commit, starts from what the page shows when that says what the file's text says (`matchesSource()`: a copy shows its text as it was pasted), and otherwise from the file's text as the page shows it (`shownAs()`, with the formulas `fileFacts()` places in it), so that words the page shows and the file does not have go with the next commit (typed while a commit was on its way into a block the file kept though the page took it for gone, they were once never sent). When nobody else wrote the file since the page showed it, the page takes in everything the file says of them, the order of each of their boxes included; otherwise only what its own commit wrote (the blocks it made, where they stand and their boxes, the texts it sent: a block it made is in the unit the file has it in, and where the page shows it in another, the next commit moves it there; the hash of a unit the file holds as the commit wrote it, put back from its source, converted whole (`replace`) or made, `hashes` giving the hash of each block it made, by its key, but of one made around a unit someone else changed, or copied from one, which holds their change: that block has no hash the page saw (`null`, which the server takes for changed); of a unit retagged, or whose table's rows and cells changed (the rest of it as the file had it), and of the others, the hash `hashes` gives, which says nothing of a unit the file had otherwise than the page saw it, so that it keeps the one it saw and deleting, converting or rewriting it asks first, words the page wrote into it meanwhile or not; the hashes of the parts a component writes likewise, but of those the commit wrote, which are the file's now), since the rest holds someone else's changes the page does not show yet, which its next commit must not undo (move a block back, write over their words); the page shows those once nothing is left to commit. A merge is shown when the block is still as sent and holds no formula (otherwise it merges again next time, from what the page sent, which stays the block's `before`: the file's text taken for it would have the next commit write their words out), and the next layout is the snapshot taken when the commit was built (`snapshotLayout()`, `captureLayout(root, map, snapshot)`) with the file's orders taken in, so typing and moves made while it was on its way go with the next commit; a block made on the page meanwhile has no id in the file and stays new. A block the file dropped because it was empty (a text emptied in place, whatever split off it went in; a block converted while empty, as "- " in an empty paragraph makes a list with an empty item) is, wherever the page shows it (the block, or what it became meanwhile: an empty last item turned into a paragraph after its list), a block made on the page from then on (its identity `dropped`, marked by `asMade()` in `sync-state.mjs`, its box for lists going with it): not written while empty, written as a new block where it stands once it has text, never named as the one the file no longer has. It goes with the commit that empties it, and that commit names nothing else of it: no move of it, no block placed next to it, no block made on the page taking it along (`collectEdits()` places it nowhere, as a block converted and then emptied); asked to anyway (an older page's buffer), the file moves it nowhere (it goes) and refuses a place next to it or a container taking it along (`position`, `invalid`). An item emptied and lifted out of its list (Enter in an empty nested item) once went with its move, which the file made with the item's old text, the emptying left out (fuzz seed 5139). An item the file has holding lists that the page turned into another type (an indent undone while it was on its way, its list then turned into paragraphs) is converted, and moved, by the commit after the one that takes its lists out of it: the file converts no item holding lists. The file is written so that it says what its blocks say: the last lists of an item going take the line break ahead of them along, its end tag coming back to its text (`cutLines()`), as an item without lists is written. A kit section with no blocks left still takes them (`findUnits()`), as the kit's other containers do. A conflict is held back, that block or operation alone, until the reader decides. Undo reaches across commits by making its inverse a change like any other: a unit deleted whole comes back from the source the receipt gave for it (`removed`: dedented, sealed with a keyed hash of the endpoint's token and the slug, so only markup the server took out of that document is written back; its id and its text blocks come back with it); a container deleted while its blocks went elsewhere (a dissolved section, a list turned into paragraphs) has its source given too, marked `around`, and becomes a container made on the page, so undo makes it again around them: one whose blocks stand straight in it (`holdsStraight`: a section, a list, a quote, a highlight block, a row of columns, a flowchart's node; not a disclosure, with a title and a content region of its own) goes as `{ restore, type, children }`, and the file writes the comments above it, its start tag, the comments after its last block and its end tag as the source has them around what it holds now (each block on a line of its own, blank lines between them not kept), so that it comes back with its block id, its `id` and all it carried, the unit of the page it was (`adopt()`; once the file got a plain new section each time, the page still showing what the file had lost); what the file dropped and the page writes anew proposes the id it had, as a row's columns do when the row comes back; what a deletion took with it (`deletedWith`) is what stood in it or in a box it takes along (a tab's panel: `takesBox`), and leaves the commits with it, its text blocks too (a tab's panel was none of it: a second commit took its blocks out one by one, and undo wrote them again in the panel the tab brought back); a unit deleted by itself is none of it, by an earlier commit or by this one, and comes back by its own undo from its own source, its id, its comment and what stood against its box's rule with it (a section's dissolve undone before the deletion in it); only in a unit deleted whole is what the same commit deleted part of what that unit took (`registerDeletions`: a selection across blocks takes a list's items out one by one), for the source holds it: it has no source of its own, which would be left over once the list came back and write the item a second time, and it comes back as the unit it was, its text blocks with it; a container an undo put back while the commit deleting it was on its way is taken the same way, the next commit writing it back from its source or making it again around its blocks (`registerRemoved()`; it was once taken for a new paragraph, and what was written in it then refused); `accepts()` says what the reader may put in a box, and a file may hold more (a section in a section, a Sheet area in a section, a row of columns in a column), so what the page opened on standing against it is noted (`stood`: unit -> the kind of that box), and every move of it, block put back or unit taken along says so, the file taking it back into a box of that kind: a dissolve, a deletion or a drag out undone otherwise was refused, and saving stopped at held; the element a conversion replaced is the same unit (one identity), so putting it back converts back, also when an undo put it back while the commit converting it was on its way (its type is not the one the unit shows as in the file: the next commit converts it; its text goes with the conversion); a unit an undo put back while the commit deleting it was on its way is written back from its source by the next commit, of the type it had (a card is never made into a paragraph), unless the page shows it as another type by then (an item put back, then its list turned into paragraphs): its source cannot stand there, so it is a block made on the page, as is what went with it; what changed meanwhile in a unit being put back (converted, resized, moved within it, a column taken out of a table) waits for the commit after it, once the file has it back, against what the layout had of it as it went (its look and its table's grid, kept with it: `looks`), since what the file put back is its source, a conversion with its text alone (the old text block is not also sent emptied: `covered()` in `core.mjs` takes a unit an element stands for as converted, model seed 5087); units that went with a unit deleted whole and stand on the page again without it (undo put it back, then the reader took them out of it: a section dissolved; or undo put back a block made on the page holding them) bring it back first, from its source, holding them, where the first of them stands, and the commit after takes them out of it and it away (a unit is in the one box the file has it in, matched by hash where it has no id, wherever it stands on the page); a code block converts back into the text block it was (the server takes a code block as a conversion writes it into text only, its language and height staying with the code, `codeAttribute()`). `tests/unit/commit.mjs` runs the page's side in jsdom against the server's. While the Agent's changes are under review, each commit that wrote is applied again to their baseline with the same engine, in the review's own queue, once it is answered, what the file took of it alone ("Agent's changes"), so that the reader's own edits never count as the Agent's.
- **Natural HTML.** `source/natural.mjs` `naturalize()`. First the format's old names (`report-*`, `data-report-*`, `--report-*`, `Report*`) are made the new ones, silently (`newNames()`: docs/render.md, "Names"), and so is a detail card of the kit's older form, a header of its title and a close button in its panel: in place, so that no line moves, the content region's start tag takes the header's place, the title becomes a plain `<h3>` first in it (it keeps an id only some other element or script names), and the card's `aria-labelledby` and the rest of the header go (`headedCard`). A card with anything else in its header stays as written, and `gallery check` fails it (`GONE`). What an Agent writes as any web page would, where the kit has markup of its own, is rewritten in place in `report.html`, so the preview edits it as the block it is and the kit shows it as one: `\(…\)` in text in the kit's `span.doc-math-inline`; a paragraph, or a `<div>` of no class, of one display formula (`\[…\]` or `$$…$$`, its TeX alone as the formula editor takes it: `texSource`) a `div.doc-math-block` (its attributes kept); a paragraph or a heading (`h1`–`h4`) whose style says `text-align: center` or `right` (the last such declaration wins, `!important` and case read) the class `doc-align-center` or `doc-align-right` and that declaration off its style (the attribute gone with none left, the others as written; `left`, `justify`, an `h5` and a cell stay as written; rule `alignment`); a `<details>` without the kit's parts `details.doc-details` (its summary's words in `.doc-details-summary-label`, the rest in `.doc-details-content`, its loose words paragraphs, blocks or not); a `<dialog id="x">` of no other attribute with a plain `<h2>` or `<h3>` first in it (x an id a card may take, no other element's) a detail card as `dialog/file.mjs` writes one (its panel, and in `.doc-dialog-content` the heading, an `<h3>`, and the rest, its loose words paragraphs), a link to a detail card, `<a href="#x">` of no other attribute, its trigger (`a.doc-dialog-trigger`), and a detail card outside `main` (an Agent's `</main><dialog …>`) at `main`'s end, where a page's blocks are looked for; a Sheet area written the short way, `<section data-doc-sheets="name">` (the name may be left out) holding `<section>`s alone (blanks and comments between them), each of no attribute and closed, starting with its heading (`h2`–`h4`, with words; nothing before it) its tab's name (`shortSheets` in `src/shared/components/sheets.mjs`), the kit's area as `sheets/file.mjs` writes one: the shell's start tag with `doc-sheet-shell` first, its name (one made when it has none, `tabs-<n>`, no other area's) and its `aria-label` in the file's language when it has none, and right after it, on its line, its row of tabs, a `button.doc-sheet-tab` a section, its name the heading's words on one line (its inline tags and attributes left out, noted), its key ASCII made of them (else `tab-<n>`, its place; no other tab's of the area); each section a panel, `div[data-doc-sheet]` of its key, of the rest (its loose words paragraphs), the heading's lines left blank, so the file keeps its lines; an area of the kit's (a row of tabs or a panel in it) is never touched, and one in neither form (words or another element beside its sections, a section of an attribute, of no heading or of a heading of no words) the checks fail in one line saying why and what to write (`componentFailures`); a paragraph in a flow holding only `<a class="doc-bookmark" href="…">` (its class alone, a `title`, a `target`, a `rel` at most) of words alone a bookmark as `bookmark/file.mjs` writes one: the paragraph a `figure.doc-bookmark` (its attributes kept), its words the title (the page's host when it has none), the `title` the description, the address the URL, the card's own `target` and `rel` (another noted), an empty caption, nothing fetched; a link that is no web address fails the checks as the kit's card does (`render/images.mjs`), and an `a.doc-bookmark` left (a paragraph holding more, a link of another attribute or holding an element) fails them saying what to write; a fill (`doc-highlight-<color>`, a block color) written on a `<details>`, an `<aside>` or a `<pre>` (or its code), the only class on the first two, goes on the kit's block it becomes; a `<pre>` outside a code block, a class of its own or not, or a `<figure>` of no class holding a pre and at most one `<figcaption>` (before or after it) of words alone (text and inline tags, no attribute, no comment), a `div.doc-code` (its language from a `language-x` or `lang-x` class, `data-language` or `data-lang`, its `id`, the figure's, the pre's or else its code's, an anchor's target, its block id, and its own attributes, each the first of the figure's, the pre's and the code's that says one: the number its first line shows, the first `data-line-start` or Prism's `data-start` writing a whole number from 1 (`038` too), as `data-line-start`, `data-line-numbers` (`true` or `false`) and `data-folded-lines`, on the block; its long lines kept each on one line, scrolled (a directory tree's, a log's), by `data-wrap="false"` beside them, or by the kit's `doc-code--nowrap` written on the pre or its code, where it does nothing: the block's `doc-code--nowrap` (`data-wrap="true"` wraps them, as the block does anyway;
