one that says neither left out, noted); its title the caption's words on one line, the inline tags in them left out, noted (not a `code`, `kbd`, `samp`, `tt` or `var` of no attribute, which a title is anyway), a line break a space, its blanks one space and the lines they took left blank after the title (a caption of no words no title), else the first `data-title`, else empty; every other one of those, one that says none, one another said before it, a `data-title` beside a caption, left out and noted, so none stands where the kit does not read it, nor twice on the block; a caption after the pre moved before it, with its line when it had one of its own, the pre then taking a line of its own after the lines it took, so the file keeps its lines; what else the pre and its code carry, a style, a `tabindex`, a highlighter's class, kept on them as written, and what else the figure carries on the block, noted; a pre in a figure of no class that is no code figure but holds a caption says why the caption stays beside the block: a comment or words in the figure, more than the pre and one caption, a caption with an attribute, not closed, or holding a comment, a picture or a block); a `<table>` outside a wrapper (one of no class, or the kit's with a class of the document's own beside it) `div.doc-table-wrap › table.doc-table` (its block id on the wrapper, the block, and so is a chart's key written on the table, `data-doc-chart-source`, which the kit reads on the frame: a card declared elsewhere, `data-doc-chart-data`, draws a table the Agent folded in a disclosure under it), and only then, when the kit takes it in (a table wrapped already is never touched again, so a look the reader took off stays off): each th of it but a row's header (`scope="row"`: the header column's, which only sticks), not a nested table's, the header's look as the editor's 标题行 and a paste's header row give it (`HEADER_LOOK` in `components/table/index.mjs`: `doc-bold doc-highlight-gray`, bold alone on a th with a color of its own; an alignment is no color), and when its rows are one group (a tbody, written or the parser's) a first row all th (none spanning rows: a `rowspan` of 1, `"01"` too, as `table.mjs` reads it) with rows after it a `<thead>`, as a paste takes a header row (an explicit `<tbody>` gives way to it and opens again after it, its attributes kept; a table of a header row alone keeps it in its tbody, as a table without a body row has no table tools); neither the look nor the thead in a document whose own `<style>` styles `th` or `thead` as a type (the kit's fill of a header row would cover the document's; the table's change says so in a note); the rows written straight in the table, after a written `<thead>` too, a `<tbody>` (the browser makes the same), so the table is one the page's table tools are for (`plainTable`); a last row that is a total (合计, Total) left in the body (a table with a `<tfoot>` is not one the page's table tools are for, `plainTable`, nor does the kit's table script read its rows for a column's numbers; a chart drawn from the table reads a total row left in the body as a row like any other; only `<tfoot>` rows are left out, `tableRows` in `src/shared/table-chart.mjs`); rows not closed in the file stay as written; and its `<caption>` of words alone (text and inline elements, no attribute of its own, no control, no block inside an inline element, not one display formula alone, which as a paragraph would be a formula block next time) a `p.doc-note` right above the wrapper (its words where they were, the tags around them changed, the blanks moved after it: the file keeps its lines; not in a document whose own `<style>` styles `caption`; any other caption with something in it stays and is named read-only); a `<table data-doc-chart="slope">` (a type the document's script defines) is such a table and the data of a chart too: its wrapper carries `data-doc-chart-source="t1"` (the key written on the table, else one no table or chart of the document has, the first of `t1`, `t2` …), the chart's attribute off the table and its others kept, and right after the wrapper stands a chart card, `figure.doc-figure` (the table's caption of words its `h3`; the line of small text above the table stays with its table and never titles the card) holding `<div data-doc-chart="slope" data-doc-chart-data="t1"></div>`, no JSON: its data are the table's rows (inside a disclosure's content that ends there, after the content's end, as an element closing there would be); a table of the kit's written with `data-doc-chart` (an Agent charting a table while revising) gets the same card after its wrapper, its caption the card's title too, its wrapper taking the key (or keeping its own). The caption's words move into the card's `h3` (`chartCard`: a splice whose text is made of the source's text it takes, with the splices inside it, so its inline tags and formulas are the kit's as a paragraph's are) and the caption goes from the table, with the line it stood on when it had one of its own, the card then taking a line of its own, so the file keeps its lines; the chart's change is at the card's line. The card is a card (moved and resized whole, its title text) and the table an ordinary table: a number typed into a cell draws the chart again at once with the table's rows (one that reads as nothing for a moment, merged cells split again, leaves the chart standing), and deleting the table leaves the card saying so, the preview's note counting it among its failures (docs/render.md, "Charts from a table"). A table written with `data-doc-chart` where a card stands in no flow (in a `<figure>`, a cell) is left as written, and the checks say how to write it; nothing reads `data-doc-chart-options`, which the checks fail as an attribute the kit does not know, wherever it is left; a figure of one picture, a picture standing in a flow or a paragraph's only picture `figure.doc-image` with its caption, the picture's `width` (`"N"` or `"Npx"`, pixels, or `"N%"`) the figure's `--doc-span`, a share of the reading width wherever the picture stands (`styles/sizes.css`, at most the width it stands in; N / 712, the reading width `READING_WIDTH` in `shared/media.mjs`; a percent of the width it stands in, as HTML means it: in a column of the kit's, N / 100 of the column's share of the reading width, its `--doc-column-ratio` of the row less the gaps, `COLUMN_GAP`, else N / 100; within 0.1 to 1, three places, as the image's resize writes it), the attribute taken off the picture and the change noted (a figure's own style kept before the span; a width of another unit, or a figure with a span of its own, leaves the picture as written), since the kit's image sizes a picture by its span alone; any other `<figure>` (no picture's, no media card, no code figure), bare or of classes of its own, standing where a block does (its parent a flow, `FLOW_PARENTS`, and no figure around it: one in a bare card's drawing or in a cell is part of it, left as written, its inside too), a card (卡片块, decided 2026-10-27): `doc-figure` added after its own classes, a bare one's inside taken in as anywhere (its tables, its code blocks, its formulas), one of classes of its own (the document's drawing) left as written, as a whole block's inside is (a figure is never a whole block, and the whole-block note says a figure goes in `figure.doc-figure`); an `<aside>` of no class `aside.doc-panel`, a highlight panel (its tag and attributes kept; not when the document's own `<style>` styles `aside` as a type, a selector or an at-rule's condition naming it, a class or id of that name aside), its words paragraphs whether it holds blocks or not; the editor takes it for the highlight block a `div.doc-panel` is (its menu's types too: the type's container is `.doc-panel`, whatever its tag); a GFM task list (markdown-it's, GitHub's, marked's: a `<ul>` of no class but `contains-task-list` / `task-list-item`, every item, none with a `data-checked` of its own, starting, after blanks, with its box, an `<input type="checkbox">` of a `type`, `checked`, `disabled` and the class `task-list-item-checkbox` at most) the kit's to-do list, `ul.doc-todo` (its other attributes kept), a checked item `li[data-checked]`, each box and the one space after it gone, GFM's classes gone, nested ones too, and items written so in one of the kit's to-do lists (an Agent adding to it as Markdown writes) taken in the same way; not in a document whose own `<style>` styles GFM's classes or whose own script (one written in the page) reads checkboxes or those classes, as the boxes and classes are its own then (a list where an item has none, a box with an attribute of its own, a numbered list or a list left so stays and is named read-only once, with what to write); `<ol data-doc-chart="flow">`, or `<ol data-flow>` on a list no chart is asked of, of no class of the document's own, `ol.doc-flow`, a flowchart (its tag as written but the mark; one holding anything besides its steps named by `writingNotes`); a flowchart's step written the short way, its own words first (after its icon, when one comes first: `<li>收集数据<p>…</p></li>`), a node: its words its first block, a paragraph, its title, bold (`<p><strong>收集数据</strong></p>`; a `<p>` alone around words all in `<strong>` or `<b>` already), its inline tags then the kit's as in any paragraph, words after one of its blocks a paragraph of its content (loose words), a run of one display formula a formula block (rule `step title`, the node's form noted once a flowchart, at its first step rewritten; an empty step, or one of its icon alone, an empty title after its icon, `<li><p></p></li>`, the same rule; a step whose first block, after its icon, is no paragraph named by `writingNotes`; its `data-direction`, its `--doc-flow-ratio` and an icon first in it are a node's as written); loose words in a container of no class of its own (`main`, a section, a div, an article, an aside, a quote, a disclosure's content, the kit's flows: `FLOWS`) that holds blocks too, or in a box of the kit's that always holds a block (`holdsOne`: a highlight block, a callout, a disclosure's content) whether it holds blocks or not (a callout written the old way, its words straight in it, the way every callout was written before 2026-11-07, holds them as its paragraph then: "Callouts"): each run of words, inline elements, controls and comments between its blocks (a block, or what is no text, a script or a style, ends one; comments and blanks at its ends stay outside; a comment or a control amid the words stays in their paragraph, as the page shows them on one line, the paragraph read-only then and named; controls alone are no paragraph, nor are zero-width characters; a run with an element not closed in the file, or with an inline element holding a block, a link around a card, a span around a paragraph, which the browser would cut the paragraph at, stays as written, named read-only) a `<p>`, a run of one display formula a formula block, its inline tags then the kit's as in any paragraph and a picture amid them kept in it (named read-only; one standing between blocks is a figure), so the container's blocks are blocks of their own (words straight in a section made it one block the preview could not reach: units.mjs takes a container with words in it as one); `mark` the yellow highlight, `del`/`strike` `s`, `ins` `u`, `kbd`/`samp`/`tt`/`var` `code`, `small` gray text, `q` quotes, a span with only a style the kit's colors and formats (or its words) when every declaration of the style is one of them (a color or background the nearest of the nine, `colors.mjs`, the paste's color rule; bold, italic, underline, line-through), a bare span or `<font>` its words, and `abbr`, `cite`, `dfn`, `time`, `bdi` or `data` with no attribute but `title`, `datetime` or `value` their words. Only those tags change (and a formula's span is added); the rest of the file stays byte for byte, and the result has nothing natural left (once is enough). Nothing goes silently: what a rule would lose stays as written, its text read-only in the preview and named with what it carries (a span whose style says something the kit has no format for, `display:none`, `white-space`, a font size…, or a text color that hides the words or puts back the browser's own, `transparent`, an alpha of 0, `initial`, `revert`; the style's declarations split as CSS splits them); a text color that is the block's (`inherit`, `unset`, `currentcolor`) and a background that is none go without a word. What a rule drops, writes near or keeps where the kit's markup has no place of its own for it is a `note`, one a thing (a color written as the kit's nearest, `color #3a7bd5 written as the kit's blue`, or left out as the text's own; the `title`, `datetime`, `value` or `cite` of a tag taken off, `title "…" dropped: write the full name in the text`, its value on one line in JSON's quotes; what a code block's pre or code keeps, `style, tabindex kept on its <pre>`, though not a style holding only the frame's `--doc-code-height`, which is where the kit reads it). Each change is `{ line, rule, note? }`, its line the file's, a tag with two notes two changes; `changeLines` says them for every writer (`gallery export` and `add-block-ids.mjs` once they have written, the dev server's log when a reader's first edit wrote them, `gallery check` as not written yet): `natural <workspace>/<slug>/doc.html: formula (lines 12, 15), code (line 30) rewritten in this file as the kit's markup (nothing to fix)` (the file's absolute path, as the system writes it), and a line for each note. No line asks the Agent to read the file again (`re-read the file before editing it` until 2026-11-13): the lines say what was written, and what to read again is the Agent's to judge (the user's word). Left alone: what is the kit's already, code (`pre`, `code`, `kbd`…), scripts, styles and drawings, a chart's host and the kit's cards, controls, code blocks and title, and everything inside an element with a class of the document's own that is no text block, no part of a table and none of the kit's elements (a whole block: its tags are its layout and its styles; `p.lead` is a paragraph still, a row `tr.highlight` the table's row, and one of the kit's elements with a class of the document's own beside the kit's, `section.doc-section.appendix`, `div.doc-panel.note`, `figure.doc-figure.wide`, stays the kit's element, its inside walked as the kit's, as the editor's scanner finds it by its class: units.mjs `boxKind`, `unitKind`, `isComponent`; `tests/unit/natural.mjs` holds the two together for each kit container and card; a list's `doc-todo` is a switch of the list, as a color is, so a list with a class of its own is a whole block on both sides), and anything outside `main`. **When the file is written.** Only at moments the Agent knows of: the reader's first edit in the preview, and the Agent's export (the hand-in); also `add-block-ids.mjs` (maintenance) and 导入 for a Gallery document it brings in. Reading never writes: the preview and `gallery check` naturalize in memory (`shownSource()`: naturalize's text with the kit tags its blocks then need, `finish`, or the file itself when nothing in it is natural), so opening a page leaves the file byte for byte, as the Agent wrote it. The edit service's `read(slug)` is the one place that says what a document is to its pages: the text served and instrumented, the version the page opens on (the hash of that text, its snapshot the merge base) and every version the server tells a page (the live reload's notice, a commit's `foreign` test and receipt, the revert check), so a natural file is never someone else's write to a page that shows it (the page would otherwise reload, again and again). A commit reads the file, applies itself to its text as pages see it and writes the result (with `expect` the file as read, retried as any commit when it changed): the first commit that changes something writes the kit's markup, its typing in it; one that changes nothing (nothing sent, all of it there already) writes nothing, so a natural file the reader only looks at stays as written; a change the file refused still gives the units around it their ids (the page knows them by those from then on), and so writes the kit's markup. The dev server's log says what that commit wrote as the kit's markup (the edit service's `log`; so does a hand-in the reader asked for, the workbench's 导出 or the export dialog, which the Agent did not ask for either: `handIn(slug, { reader: true })`), and the edit service keeps it for the Agent (`rewrites.mjs`: when, and each rule at its line of the file as the Agent had left it, a chart with its key; in the state directory under `rewrites/<directory of reports>/<slug>/`, the last 20 of a document): `gallery check` and `gallery export` say it first, once (what is said is taken away), so the Agent learns why its file changed: `preview <workspace>/<slug>/doc.html: at 14:32, when the reader edited the page, the preview wrote your natural HTML as the kit's markup: line 120 details, line 140 chart t1, line 140 table`, a line for each note, then `  those lines are the file's as you had left it` (the line numbers are those of the file before the preview wrote it; a time of another day with its date). What the reader types as text that is natural HTML itself (`\(…\)` amid words, a paragraph of `$$…$$`, which the kit draws as a formula too) is written as typed; its page sees a formula there (`read()`), so it reloads once to show one, and the next commit writes the kit's span. The export writes the kit's markup too, with every block's id (`handedInSource()`, "Block ids"), and the document's name and icon in step with its page (`inStep()`, the hand-in's, as `retitle` and `reicon` keep them on the reader's edits: `<title>` takes the h1's words when it does not hold them, report.json's old title replaced where `<title>` holds it once, words of the old title that hold the new one's not counting as holding them, "销售报告（草稿）" for a new "销售报告"; `report.json`'s title becomes the h1's and its icon the header's own, the icon unit the editor finds in the header; a header with no icon leaves `report.json`'s, and the export says so: an icon left out is not one taken away; docs/render.md): through the edit service's queue while a preview server runs (`POST /__gallery/edit/hand-in`, `handIn` for the workbench's 导出 and the workspace's export), else by the command itself with the same check (docs/render.md). What stays read-only after it (a picture, a comment or a control amid words, a display formula amid words, a table's caption it did not take, a list of checkboxes that is no task list, once for the list, words straight in a container it could not make paragraphs) `gallery check` names (`readOnlyTexts`), and what the preview takes otherwise than as blocks (each whole block once, its outermost, not one with no words and no picture, a host the document's own script draws in or a drawing; a table's column widths, which the kit sets itself: `writingNotes`), and a formula between single dollars (`$x^2$`) fails the check, as the kit draws none (see docs/render.md). (`tests/unit/natural.mjs`.)
- **Block ids.** `data-block-id` (`IDENTITY` in `components/registry.mjs`, shared by the page and the server) is a block's identity: an opaque id with no meaning, eight letters and digits. Only units carry one (paragraphs, headings, lists and their items, quotes, code, formulas, callouts, cards, rows of columns and their columns, tables, disclosures, sections, Sheet tabs, document widgets); a card's text, cells and inline elements never do. It binds nothing: a block carrying only it still splits, merges and converts (on the page too: `convertible()` in `convert.mjs` ignores it), and it does not count as a script hook. `commitEdits()` looks a unit up by its id, and only an id exactly one unit carries counts (a repeated one is `ambiguous`; one the file dropped falls back to the unit as it was when the page opened, `open` and `openHash`, and a component's write (`source`) of one whose id the file dropped to the one instance carrying no id the page knows whose parts are all as the write says the file holds them, which gets the id again); a text block is looked up through the id of its unit (its own, or the card or table it is in, by its structural path). A unit found by its id whose source the file changed is `changed` (text merges word by word as above; a deletion is a conflict). A unit with no id yet (as an Agent writes new blocks) is found by what it was when the page opened, and what stays unclear is a conflict, never a guess: ids make the surer way for blocks moved and changed, identical paragraphs and reformatted files. **A unit's hash leaves its ids out** (`unitHash()` in `source/units.mjs`): the `data-block-id` attributes of the unit's start tag and of every start tag inside it, however the value is quoted, with the one whitespace character before each (`blockIdSpans()`: an id is given as ` data-block-id="…"` right before the tag's `>` or `/>`, after whatever whitespace the tag ends in, `<hr />` or a `>` on a line of its own, so taking out exactly that reads the tag as it was), never text that merely spells one (in code, a comment, a script); and finding a unit or text block of the opening version in the file as it is now by its text (`locate()`, `counterpart()` in `source/locate.mjs`) reads both versions without their ids too. An id is who a block is, not what it says, so giving ids changes no hash, and a page open on a version without them (typing not yet saved, a commit on its way) still finds every block it names by the hash it saw, exactly where it would have: the edit lands in the same block, a deletion or conversion finds the unit as the page saw it, twins are told apart by their order or what comes before them, and the unit found keeps the id the file has now (the receipt gives it to the page). As the hash no longer tells a block with an id from one without, a unit the page knows by an id is never one it names by what it said when it opened: every commit and revert check carries the page's ids (`pageIds`: its units' and those it proposed for the blocks it made), and `commitEdits()` leaves the units carrying one of them out of those it finds that way (an empty paragraph the page made right before an empty one of the file says the same, and comes first after what came before it). So ids may be given at any moment. Opening a page never writes one; a commit (`ids` in `applyEdits()`, on in the commit endpoint) gives one to each unit it edits (for text, the unit the text is in), moves, resizes, converts or switches a class of for the first time, and to every block made on the page; a converted block keeps its id, and a unit keeps the one it has. **The export gives every block one**: `gallery export` (and the workbench's 导出) hands the document in, its file written as pages see it with an id for every unit that has none (`handedInSource()`: `addBlockIds()` after `shownSource()`), so a document is fully covered from its first export and later additions get theirs lazily; a second export writes nothing. A document where an id repeats is not written, and its export fails as its check says it (`repeatedIdFailures()`, `data-block-id repeats: …`), at the file's lines (which one is the copy is the author's to say; none is renamed). `node src/cli/add-block-ids.mjs <slug>…` (`--all`, `--dry-run`) stays as a maintenance tool doing the same with a backup (its usage says the export does it now): it gives every unit without an id one, its natural HTML made the kit's first, changing nothing else (a document where an id repeats it leaves as it is, listing each repeated id with the lines of its elements; `addBlockIds()` itself, which the browser tests' fixtures go through, keeps an id on its first unit and gives the others new ones, and writes an empty one, `data-block-id=""`, over with a new one, as a browser reads the first of two); the document as it was and the changed lines go to `backups/<slug>/` (not packaged), and the file is written with the same check as the preview's own writes. A page open on the document meanwhile goes on saving. After that, a commit with nothing in it still leaves the file byte for byte. `gallery check` refuses a repeated id (a copied block must not keep it; `SKILL.md` tells Agents to keep ids as they are and give copies none). The Component Catalog has none until it is edited or exported, as any document written by hand. An id is also what a link to a block names (`#<block id>`, `../<slug>/doc.html#<block id>`, [workbench.md](workbench.md) "A document's addresses"). 复制块链接, one block's row in the block menu (none for several blocks, none on a tab's menu), copies `http://…/#<link>/<block id>`, the link the address bar shows for the page, by the title the page shows, saved or not (the list held ready: [workbench.md](workbench.md) "A document's addresses"): the row asks `commits.idFor` only as it is clicked, so that opening the menu writes nothing, and a block with no id is named by a commit that goes at once. The clipboard is written in the click itself (`ui.mjs copyAddress`, the link card's and the embed's too). `revealBlock` (`editor.mjs`) goes to a block: what hides it opened (the kit's `DocKit.reveal`), in the middle of the window, selected. A link to a block of the page goes there by `jumpTo`, a step of the history (decided 2026-11-06): in a workbench the workbench's (`jump`), on a page alone in a tab its own, its address as it is (`docPlace` in the entry left, `docBlock` in the one made); the way back is `showPlace`, where the reader was ([workbench.md](workbench.md) `show-place`); a later step started past the waits of an earlier one (a quick Back after a link, Back and Forth held) leaves the earlier one there (`stepping`). The outline, the 批注 list and 搜索文档 go to blocks with no step.
- **Autosave.** `autosave.mjs`. There is no edit mode and no save button: the page commits what changed by itself, after 1.6s without typing, at most 8s after a change while the reader keeps typing, at once for a discrete operation (a block deleted, moved, split, converted or resized, a formatting step, undo and redo); the phrase an input method puts in is typed text, committed after the pause as keys are (never during a composition; a key, an input, a press or a paste that is no part of a composition ends it too, as Chrome sends no `compositionend` when a paste replaces the text being composed, which once left the page waiting for good with no format bar; each phrase was once a commit of its own, a file written per phrase); Ctrl+S commits whatever waits. One commit is on its way at a time, and every write of the page goes through one queue (commits, and the reader's decision on a text someone else put back); what changes meanwhile goes with the next. Each commit carries an id of its own (`request`, 16 letters and digits, made by the page): the server keeps its answer under it (`receipts/<slug>/<id>.json` in the state directory, the last 200 of each document, written once the file is), and a commit sent again gets the answer it had (`again`), whatever the file says now, so the page takes in exactly what that commit did (its ids, its deleted units' sources) and does not take its own write for someone else's; with other contents under the same id it is refused (`request`). A commit that does not get through (the server unreachable, `busy`, a server error) is tried again after 1, 2, 5 and 10 seconds, then every 30 (at once when the browser is online again), and always as it went: its answer may be all that was lost (the file took it), and a commit built anew from the page would name what it wrote as still to be written (the server finds the block it made already there and takes it as done, so what the reader wrote in it since was never written; or the reader's later words in a paragraph conflict with their own). Nothing newer is built until it is answered; what changes meanwhile goes with the next. One the server refuses whatever the page does stops autosave and says why (a token the server no longer knows: the page reloads, and the crash buffer carries the edits over, that commit first). The workbench's top bar says where saving stands (已保存, 待保存, 正在保存…, offline, conflicts); the page says what the reader needs to know when it happens (commits stop getting through, get through again, a merge, a conflict, a block that cannot be saved where it is, a new title still empty, the crash buffer that cannot be written), and `data-ge-save` on the root says where saving stands in one word (`saved`, `waiting`, `syncing`, `offline`, `held`, `stopped`, `reloading`; `held` is for conflicts: a block no commit can carry yet is flagged on the page instead, and Ctrl+S says it is not saved). Undo and redo work across commits (see "Commits"). Once a commit showed someone else's words merged into a block (the caret staying among the reader's words: counted from the block's end where the words after it are as they were, theirs having gone in before it, from its start where the words before it are, theirs having gone in after it, else right after as much of the reader's words before it as the merged text holds: `caretKept` of the commits), the typing step in it ends there, and a step recorded before undoes (and redoes) the reader's change only: three ways (`mergeText()`, from `page/merge.mjs`, the server's too) against what the block holds now, so the merged words stay; when the two touch the same place it is not undone, and the page says so. Leaving is never a question: `beforeunload` warns only while something waits, and that is in the crash buffer anyway. The page's requests to the preview server are one `postJson` (`post.mjs`: the JSON body and the token header); each caller keeps its own reading of a failure. The ids the page proposes for blocks it makes and sends with commits are made, and their formats checked by the server, in one module (`src/shared/ids.mjs`).
- **Sync state.** What of the file each element the commits know is (a unit, a text block, a box) lives in a table in `commit.mjs` under the element's identity, `data-ge-id` (`core.mjs identityOf()`), which an element gets once and never changes: a block turned into another type keeps the identity of the one it stands for (`standsFor()`, `retagList()`), a copy has none of its own (copies drop every mark), undo brings an element back with its own. Elements the commits have not seen yet get theirs when the page is next looked at (`learn()`), taking the block their marks name when they have marks (the page as served; a code block opened for editing). The marks the rest of the editor reads (`data-ge-unit`, `data-ge-block`, `data-ge-box`, `data-ge-new`, `data-ge-convert`, `data-block-id`, `data-ge-keep`, `data-ge-grid`) are written from the table by `sync-state.mjs project()` before every look at the page (`commits.collect()`), after every answer and after every structural change (undo among them), and nowhere else once the page is edited: so an element undo puts back is marked from what the file has now. A unit is converted when its element's own type (`convert.mjs ownTypeOf()`: a quote's paragraph taken out of its quote is the same paragraph) differs from the type it showed as when last committed (the layout's `types`); its text goes with the conversion. Ids a block made on the page proposes are kept for its identity. The layout a commit leaves keeps only units the file has (a unit deleted and not put back is none). No two elements on the page carry one identity (`commits.doubled()`; the model test checks it after every step, and that no element's identity changes). The save engines name no component: what they need of one is in its description (`components/<name>/index.mjs`, read through `registry.mjs`): a type's `textHost` (where its text is: a code block's code), `extras` (what goes with it: a code block's language, folds, height and title, a column's share), `bare` (no text: a divider), `content` and `title` (a disclosure's content region and title, a detail card's content region), `keyed` (known to the file by its id, not as a unit: a detail card), `written` (when it is written at all: a row once each column has something, a detail card while a link opens it), `blanks` and `blankSays` (what the reader must fill before it is written, and what the page says of it: a row's empty columns) and `part` (an element a new block's text blocks are part of); a box's `find` (where a unit's element holds it: a list item's lists, a disclosure's content, a Sheet area's tab row and panels); `blockTypeOf()`, `boxIn()`, `PART_SELECTOR`. A new component, or a new way of editing, needs nothing in the save engines.
- **Crash buffer.** What the file may not have is kept in `localStorage` under `gallery-edit:<slug>:pending:<tab>` (`{ v: 2, sent, request, keep }`): the commit on its way or whose answer never came (`sent`), the commit that would carry everything else (`request`: held conflicts and blocks the file refused too), and the texts no commit carries (`keep`: blocks no commit can carry yet, what someone else's write put back: texts, and what a component wrote with its block's id and the parts as the page holds them, listed as that block to copy). It is stored with the first change and then at most once a second, when a commit goes and when it is answered, and when the page is hidden or closed (then, when no commit is on its way, the very commit the buffer holds is tried with `keepalive`, its answer never read: a try, not a guarantee, and the next page sending it again gets its answer), and removed only once none of that is left; with no commit on its way, a store finding nothing changed since the buffer was written (the page and what the commits know of it, `commits.current()`, and what someone else put back) leaves it as it is, and a flush finding nothing waiting as last counted and nothing changed since builds nothing, so a page that is left (the workbench's `prepare-close`, then `pagehide` and `visibilitychange` as its frame goes) looks at its blocks once, not twelve to fifteen times, and the next document is asked for about 40 ms after the click instead of 0.4-1 s (more under load); whatever changed since, counted or not, is stored as it goes; a write that fails is said (the page says the changes are only on the page). Each tab has its own id (kept in `sessionStorage` over reloads), so tabs never overwrite each other's; a duplicated tab, answered by the original on a `BroadcastChannel`, takes a new one. A page opening on the document first sends what its own tab left (it reloaded, crashed or lost the server), then, once the open tabs have answered (150ms), what tabs that are gone left (`recover()`), and reloads to show it. Commits name everything by identity and the page proposes the ids of the blocks it makes, so sending one again writes it once. With a commit whose answer is not known, the buffer also keeps the texts of the page's blocks (`page`): once a page has sent it and shows the file, those it does not show are listed too (`checkLeft()`), a safety net for whatever rebuilding what followed misses. And while that commit's answer is not known and it waits to be sent again, and whenever the page goes away (from `pagehide` on, the flush of the `visibilitychange` that follows it sending that commit again included), what the commits know and the page itself are kept under `gallery-edit:<slug>:state:<tab>` (`commits.keep()`: `save()` as JSON with the page's body and the trees of the elements it names that are off the page, and the sources of the chips there, an inline formula's TeX among them, which only the page that made them knows, `sync-state.mjs pack()`), for that very commit (`sent`, by its id). Of the version it names its units by, `save()` keeps each unit's number and hash there and each outside block's number (`openUnits`, `openHashes`, `openBlocks`: a pull changes that version, "The pull"); what a page of an earlier Gallery kept, the count and hashes of the units and blocks it opened on (`openingUnits`, `openingBlocks`, `openingHashes`), is read as those numbers. Of a buffer, `sent` goes first, as it was (its answer, kept by its id, comes again if it went in before); then, with what the page knew, the page takes it up in a document of its own (`rehydrate()`: `unpack()`, which makes those chips known to it, `knowAtoms()`, `createCommits({ restore })`), takes in that answer as it would have, and builds everything not in the file as it would have (held conflicts too): exact, whatever the page did after that commit (blocks added into one it made, blocks put in another order); that commit is kept in the buffer before it goes, so a page that cannot send it sends that very one. Without it (a crash before the page kept it), `request` goes on from what `sent` wrote (`rebase()`): a text both carry starts from what the file took of it; a block `sent` made (a split-off part, a block made on the page, the blocks in it) is not made again, and what the page did to it since goes as done to that block by the id the answer gives (its text an edit of what `sent` wrote, a move a move); the units both name carry the ids and hashes the answer gives them, as a page takes in an answer. Blocks added into or taken out of a block `sent` made, or put in another order there, have no start the file knows here: what it holds is listed instead. So is order changed inside a unit `sent` put back, or a split-off part moved: words come back, not always the order of blocks in or next to what `sent` did (kept exactly once the page keeps its sync state apart from its elements). A buffer whose commits are not answered (the server unreachable, busy or failing) stays for the next page. What the file does not take (a text changed there meanwhile or gone, a block whose place is gone), and the texts no commit carried, are kept under `gallery-edit:<slug>:lost` before the buffer goes, and listed with the reader's words to copy at every opening until the reader says they are dealt with (都处理好了，不再保留; closing the list only hides it). What a component wrote is no words: where its instance stands on the page it is listed as that block holding what the reader wrote (`withData` of the page object, from the parts the commit carried and the instance's id, `unit` and `parts` of the item), copied by 复制这一块 as the block menu copies one, so that it pastes back as an instance of its custom block. A buffer of a format this page does not know (damaged, or from before `v: 2`) is not sent: its text is listed with the rest.
- **To-dos checked from another page.** A block's `context.workspace.setTodo` (我的待办 on the home page) checks or clears an item of another document's to-do list: the page holding the token (a custom page's board, a document's editor) takes the kit's `doc-todo-set` (`page/todo-writes.mjs`), each document's checks in a line of their own (those asked while one write of it is out go together in the next, at most 50), `POST /__gallery/workspace/todo`; the edit service's `setTodos` finds each in the file as its pages see it (`src/editor/todos.mjs` `locateTodos`, the to-dos read as the workspace's facts read them, `src/shared/todos.mjs`): by its block id; without one, while the file is still the version the ref was read from (its `version`), by its place among the items of its words under its headings (`nth`); in a file changed since, as the one such item, several refused (`ambiguous`); its words not the ref's, or none, refused (`changed`: the conflict, which the block reads again after); an item that is there as read but that no editor block holds (a list in a table cell, in a block of the document's own, where the document's own page cannot check it either) refused `locked`, not `changed`, which the block would read again after for good. What changes the file goes as one commit through `handleCommit` (exported on the service for a caller in the queue), in the queue once the file is not held, an attribute op (`data-checked`) each, the last asked of an item winning, the item without an id named by what it said in that text (its opening version kept as a page's is) and given one as every touched unit is: the reader's edit (`writerOf` "reader", the record of edits, the review's baseline, kept among the recent versions), which an open page of the document takes as any write it did not make. On a custom page each check that changed its document is one step of the page's own undo (`{ kind: "checked", slug, ref, done }`, `board/history.mjs`), its undo the check the other way by the id the write gave the item, never the document's undo; a document's editor keeps none. The step is pushed as the write is answered, and the checks are no tasks of the board's client, so the board's undo and redo tasks wait for the checks asked before them (`todos.idle()`): a Ctrl+Z pressed before a check is answered takes that check back, not the step before it (whose redo the late push would clear).
- **Someone else's writes.** A write that changed nothing but the words of text blocks the page has is taken in where the page stands, at once ("The pull", below); any other, and every one after it until then, the page shows by loading itself anew, as here: once nothing is left to commit and the reader pauses (1.5s, `QUIET`). A write the page shows anew heard within 15s of the last one before it that the page showed anew too (with the pull off, every write; kept for the tab, `heardKey`, so that the page shown anew goes on with it) is one of a burst (an Agent writing), and the page then waits until the file has been still for one and a half of that gap, 4 to 12s, every write heard meanwhile one of it, so that it is shown again once at the burst's end, live all along; a burst going on is shown every 20s to a reader who has pressed no key, pressed nothing and turned no wheel for 3s (`autosave.mjs` `waitLeft`; the user's 2026-11-12: each write had the workbench load the page anew, out of reach meanwhile, and the next write started that again, so the page froze while an Agent wrote). A write the pull takes in is no part of a burst (it costs the reader nothing), and one heard while the page waits only for the reader's pause to show a lone one anew is shown by that load and makes no burst of it (`showAnew`, `hearWrite`): when every write heard counted, an Agent's script added a moment after paragraphs the pull had taken in waited for the Agent to stop writing, 4 to 12s after its last write, the page showing none of it meanwhile while the bar counted the changes (the user's 2026-11-14; scenario `agentPullScriptAfterPull`). A page asked to show the file by the reader (`reloadSoon`) waits for no burst. The page shown again is put back by the element at the window's top (`viewNow`'s `top`, `shared/reading-place.mjs`), so what the Agent added above it moves nothing the reader sees. The server also records such a write in the review of the Agent's changes ("Agent's changes"), which the page shown again marks; a revert of them and its undo are writes the reader asked for, and the page that asked is shown again at once with no revert check (`showOwn`). While the editor runs it sets `window.__galleryEditBusy`, so the live-reload script dispatches `doc-source-changed` with the new version instead of reloading; a version the page's own commits wrote is its own. A version the page's own commits wrote (or the one it opened on; a commit that changed nothing, all of it there already or refused, wrote none: `wrote: false` in its answer) is its own for a few seconds (the live-reload channel announces it a little later), and only in the order the page wrote them, which is the order the channel announces writes in: one announced after a later one of the page's was heard, or after those seconds, is someone else writing the file back to it (an Agent writing what it read when the page opened; a version is a text, not a write). What the channel announces while a commit is on its way or waits to be sent again (its answer lost) is judged once that commit is answered, as of when it was announced: it may be that commit's own write, or a late word of the one before. When someone else wrote the file (an Agent, another tab; a commit's answer says so too, `foreign`), commits merge with it on the server, and once everything the page shows is in the file (nothing waits, nothing is on its way or waits to be sent again, nothing is held for the reader, no block waits for the reader to finish it, such as a new disclosure whose title is still empty) and the reader has changed nothing for a moment (1.5s) and is not in the middle of a press or drag, the page shows it again where the reader was: the element at the top of the window there again, as far down (by its block, so what was written above it moves nothing), the detail cards open and the caret in its block (`reloadView()`; a merge just made is said again after it), until the reader moves the page themselves ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "Where the reader was"). Every page also leaves that view as it goes (`pagehide`), for the workbench loading anew the page it showed, and says it to the workbench as the reader goes to another page (`window.__galleryViewNow`: the view it was given while it is not back there yet, else `viewNow()`), which hands it back on the frame when the reader comes back (`data-view`, taken by `takeView` through `kept-view.mjs` `handedView`; [workbench.md](workbench.md) "Back where the reader left"). A page given a view scrolls there first by the element at the top of the window (`goBackTo`), the scroll position only where it gave none, so a page the workbench already put there as it swapped it in moves nothing; the caret comes back with the focus, and where the workbench gave the frame itself the focus before the page put it back (`data-no-focus` taken off then, the frame the workbench's active element) it is put back at once. On its own the page reloads; in a workbench it stops (`autosave.close()`: nothing more committed or stored) and asks the workbench (`reload`, session.mjs), which loads the document out of sight and swaps it in once drawn, so no reload is seen (docs/workbench.md). The page tells a workbench it is ready (`ready`) only once it is back where the reader was. It asks which texts it committed that write put back as they were before one of its commits (`POST /__gallery/edit/reverted`, `revertedTexts()`: an Agent writing the whole file from what it read before; as the first replaced it, or as a later one left it, the newest 20 of those, `earlier` of `committedText`; a text committed back as it was first is asked of what it was between, an undo being a commit like any other), of those committed in this load (each named as the page knows it now: a later commit may have given its unit an id) and, for an hour, in earlier pages of the document (the journal, below: each under a key of its own, an earlier load's keys naming its own blocks, and one text of each block, as it was before the first load that committed it and what each later commit replaced; two the server finds naming one block are asked of one by one), as soon as it hears of someone else's write (the live-reload channel, or a commit's answer saying someone wrote first), whatever else waits, and once more right before it shows the write; those become conflicts instead (恢复我的 commits the reader's text again, 接受 Agent 的 keeps the write), and the page reloads once they are decided. It asks the same of the blocks it made and moved in this load, a block taken along by what undo put back standing where the file has it again and asked of no more (`commits.placedUnits()`: each by the file's id, with the id of the unit the commit left it in, for one it moved, of the one it was in before, and the units before and after it in its box as the page's layout has them, `between`, on each side the nearest first, three at most (`BESIDE`), each by its id and the hash the page knows of it, then null where the box's start or end comes, and those the page shows inside it, `holds`, by id, or by hash where the file gives one none; the journal keeps both with the blocks an earlier load made; `revertedUnits()`): one the write took out (gone from the file) or put back in the unit it was in before is a conflict too, but one the write took out and put blocks in the place of is the writer's replacement of it, shown as any change of theirs (the pull, the review of the Agent's changes): blocks standing between the nearest unit on each side the file still has, each found in it once (by id; by the hash the page knows of one it knows no id of, so one the write changed is not found), in one box of the unit it stood in and in that order, or between the box's start or end and the one beside it where it stood first or last: one beside it the file has by neither its id nor its words the write took out with it (a picture in place of the reader's paragraph and the one after it) and is stepped over, a run of the page's blocks the write took out together is one place (`replacedHere`), but one the file has without the id the page knows it by, its words the same, says the write is from before the commit that named it (a block made names those beside it: "Commits"), and nothing tells the place; each new to the page: none of the units it shows (`pageIds`), took out this load (`taken`, `commits.takenOut()`: a paragraph the reader deleted and wrote anew, put back where the new one stood) or showed inside that block (a container the reader made around blocks, a section an undo put back, is put back by a write that leaves its blocks standing where it stood: scenario `dissolveUndoAgent`), as a block the page shows elsewhere moved there cannot be told from what a write from an earlier read leaves; what stood inside one replaced went with it; unless the same write put back anything else of the page's (a text, a block moved back, a part a component wrote: `revertCheck`, which answers the three together), which says it was written from an earlier read. The user's 2026-11-17: an Agent asked to put a picture in place of a paragraph the reader had written raised this card, with nothing unsaved; the page held behind it, the picture not shown, and 恢复我的, the card's first button, undid what was asked. A block taken out with nothing in its place, or one alone in its box, stays a card, the page unable to tell that from a write from an earlier read. A write from an earlier read that put blocks of its own exactly where the reader's new block stood, and nothing else of theirs back, is told by the units the commit that made the block named, beside it and around it: where they had no ids before, the write has them without, and it is a card (scenario `agentReplacesAround`, which also has the replacement with the paragraph after the reader's reworded by the same write, and with the block after it replaced too); where they all had ids before the Agent read the file, nothing in the file tells such a write from a replacement, and it is taken for one, as a write from an earlier read that changed the very text the reader changed is the writer's change (the review of the Agent's changes marks the block deleted, 撤回 brings it back, 历史版本 keeps it). The review of 2026-11-17 found that case (the Agent's paragraph appended where the reader had appended theirs, in a document with ids), and it stands so on purpose: a notice for it would come with every replacement asked for, the noise the feedback was about. Its card (`reverted-unit`): 恢复我的 makes the block again as one made on the page, around its blocks from its source when undo put it back so, or moves it again where the page has it; 接受 Agent 的 keeps the write. A card for a block that the file, asked of again for a later write, no longer puts back (that write put the block back, or blocks of its own where it stood: an Agent taking the reader's paragraph out, then putting the picture it was asked for there, two writes apart) goes undecided, the file taken as 接受 Agent 的 takes it (`lapsed`; before, a card held was never judged again, the page stayed behind it). One taken out inside another taken out (a block in a disclosure or a slot the page made) has no card of its own: it went with that one, whose card decides it too (`heldWithin`; the card's `within`, which `putBack` makes blocks made on the page again with it, and only those: one inside it the file still has stays the file's, taken along by it). It asks the same of what its components wrote that went in (`commits.written()`, kept with the committed texts, in this load and, for an hour, in earlier ones: each instance by the id it has now, each part it wrote by every hash the part had in the file as the page wrote it (before each of its writes, and as each left it) but the one it has now, and that one, `noteWrite`, a commit that leaves the part as the file held it, written over theirs as the reader said to, settling it there too; an undo is a write like any other, as it is of a text; `revertedParts`, which finds an instance whose id the write dropped, as a file read before the page gave it one does, by its parts: the one instance carrying no id the page knows that holds a part as one of those hashes): a part the file holds as before one of them, and not as the last left it, is a conflict too (`reverted-source`: its card, as a held write of a component's, prints no version, the reader's being the block as it stands on the page, flagged, and 复制这一块 copies that block; 恢复我的 has the next commit write the part as the page holds it over the hash the file holds of it, `rewrite`, which asks again, as a change of theirs, should they have written it since; 接受 Agent 的 keeps the write and asks of it no more, and so does 保留 Agent 的 on a held write of the component's, of the parts it held), unless the page holds it as the file does, however the file spells it (`heldOtherwise`: the instance as the file holds it, which the answer gives, read as its component reads one, `dataIn`). A part the file holds otherwise (theirs, or theirs merged with the reader's) is the writer's choice, as a text is; an instance the write took out is a block put back where the page made or moved it, and otherwise the writer's deletion, which asks nothing; one deleted, held for the reader or no longer on the page is not asked of while it is. Whatever happened while that question was on its way (a change, a commit and its conflicts), the page asks all of this again before it reloads. That last question is answered about the file as it is then, and the page shown again is served the file as it is a moment later: an Agent's write of the whole file landing between the two (from what it read before the reader's edits) is the file the page shown again opens on, which it never hears of as someone else's write (the live-reload channel tells it the version it was served), and the reader's words once went without a trace, the file, the page and the list all without them (scenario `agentRevertAtReload`; fuzz seed 4102's timing, on a busy machine). The same goes for every write no page of the document is there to hear: the reader reloads the page (F5) as the Agent writes, opens another document in the tab and comes back, or opens the document again in a new tab (`agentRevertAfterReload`, `agentRevertReopened`, `agentRevertNewTab`). So every page, as it goes (to show the file again, on pagehide, after a write the reader asked for), keeps what it committed, made and wrote in the document's journal (`keepJournal`: `gallery-edit:<slug>:journal` in `localStorage`, one departure a tab, `{ known, time, entries }`), with the version of the file it last knew (`known`: the one it opened on, then each one its own commit wrote over it with no one else's write between, each one a revert check was answered about, the one a write the reader asked for left, which the changes route answers with, `showOwn`; none while a question about what it took from earlier pages is not answered yet, nor after a write the reader asked for whose version it was not told). The texts of commits still on their way or in the crash buffer go in as committed (`folded`), so that the reader's own words sent as the page goes, an undo back to what an earlier commit replaced, are not taken for someone else's write. A page takes its own tab's departure as it is made, and those of tabs no longer showing the document once it knows which do (`autosave.opened(open)`, after the tabs answer on the `BroadcastChannel`, as for the crash buffers of tabs that are gone), each taken out of the journal; a page done with before then (a workbench showing it again at once) or going to be shown again takes none, the next page does (one whose write the reader asked for failed takes them then). Of one block, the texts of several pages go in the order they were last committed (`at`, which `committedText` records), not the order the page took them or the pages went: two tabs' pages editing one text in turn, the closed one taken last made the reader's latest words read as put back. where it opened on another version than one of them last knew, it asks all it took at once: a text put back is a card as when it hears of such a write, and a block an earlier page made that the file no longer has, on no page to hold a card, is listed with its words (`lostKey`, why `reverted`, 被 Agent 的写入改回去的修改), the list shown at once. On the very version it asks nothing, and where nothing of the reader's was put back nothing shows. What it took is its own from then on: the texts asked of for the hour whenever it hears of a write, the blocks made as long as the page shows them (`commits.unitOf`: one the file was found with and the page no longer shows, the reader deleted, is asked of no more), all of it kept again as it goes, with what it did itself. A commit of a page's that deletes blocks takes them, and the blocks made inside them, out of the departures the journal still holds too (`forgetMade`): a block one tab's page made and the reader deleted in another tab, open before the first went and so not holding its departure, is the reader's own deletion, never listed. (While both tabs are open, the page that made it asks of it on hearing the other's write, as of any block it made this load.) The journal keeps an entry for an hour and, of one document, the newest 100,000 characters of entries (one longer left out; a write that fails leaves the crash buffer the room); a page that opens takes away the journals of documents whose every entry is older than an hour. A Gallery before 0.2.0 kept it in `sessionStorage` for its tab alone (`{ asked, entries }`, or the entries): a page reads that once as its tab's departure, asking at once where it does not know the version that page last knew. A commit sent again after a lost answer gets the answer its first send had (by its id), so it is not taken for someone else's write; only when the server lost that answer too (it stopped between writing the file and keeping the answer) is it, which only makes the page reload once nothing waits. A unit named by an id such a write dropped is found by what it was when the page opened, exactly or not at all, and an instance a component's write names, or the revert check asks of, also by its parts, as above.
- **The pull.** `page/pull/` (the user's 2026-11-12, "用户写用户的，agent 更新，基本无感"). Someone else's write is taken in where the page stands, no reload and no swap, the undo steps, the selection, an input method and whatever is open all kept, when it changed the words of text blocks the page has, or put blocks in, took them out or moved them (paragraphs, headings, items of a list, sections and the like), or changed what no words of a block show (a formula, a link shown with its icon, a code block, a display formula, a table, a picture or a card, an instance of a package with its slots, a detail block, a Sheet, a callout recoloured, a picture's source): a unit the page cannot show the file's of in place is replaced, whole, the page's taken out and the file's put in as a load makes it. Only the head, the page's styles and scripts, the kit's tags and the components' catalog (the document's `frame`, and `doc-components-changed`), a block the page found a script had changed as it loaded, ids the file has twice, and what the plan cannot place, show the page anew. The frame is looked at first, as soon as the page is read and whatever version it read, and so are the kit's files the page is served with (`pull/apply.mjs` `kitMissing`, `kit`): the server puts on a page the tags of the kit's files its blocks need (`render/assets.mjs` `usedAssets`), which no file writes and no frame tells, so a write putting in the first block of a kind (a Sheet area, a chart, a formula, a code block) has the page served with a file of the kit it never loaded, and what the pull would put in nothing draws (no script runs for what it puts in, and the kit's runtime mounts only what a component it loaded knows: a Sheet area so put in showed every panel at once and no tab chosen until the page was loaded anew, the user's 2026-11-14, scenario `agentPullSheetsArrive`); one the editor loaded since by its address (`ui.mjs` `loadScript`: a code block or a table made on the page; `ensureSheetsKit`) is the page's. Either is shown anew, which asks the revert check itself right before it goes (`maybeReload`), so an Agent writing on, a write landing between each question and the read, never keeps it from going (asked again and again as `stale`, below). The autosave tries it (`tryPull`) as soon as it hears the write and again after each commit's answer, whenever no commit is on its way or waits to be sent again (commits wait while the page reads itself and takes it in, `pulling`: a read given up after 10 s, `unread`, is tried again 2 s later), nothing is held for the reader (a conflict, a text put back: each decision asks again) and the reader is in the middle of nothing it would break (a press, an input method composing, the "/" or "@" menu, a formula's box, a 批注 being written: asked again 300ms later); words the reader typed and has not saved are no reason to wait, and neither is a burst. A page asked to show the file by the reader (`reloadSoon`) is loaded anew as before. First the revert check is asked about the file as it is, once for each write heard, commit answered and decision sent (`generation`, `checkedAbout`); then, with no other revert check on its way (one a page opening asked names what it holds by the version it knew then; while one runs, the page asks again 300ms later), the page reads itself as it is served now (its own address, `cache: "no-store"`, parsed with `DOMParser`: no script runs, nothing is laid out; serving it keeps that version as a merge base, as a load does) and takes in only the version the check answered about, or, where the check had nothing to ask of, whatever it reads (another version read: the check asked again, three times at once, then a second later), so that a write putting the reader's saved words back that lands between the two is a card, never a text shown as the file has it (scenario `agentRevertAtPull`). The plan (`pull/plan.mjs` `planPull`, a pure function, `tests/unit/pull.mjs`) puts what the page knows the file has (`commits.pullView()`: the version it names its units by, the document's frame, the file's units it shows with their kinds, ids, hashes and shells, the boxes it shows and the units in each as it shows them, each text block with the source it knows) beside the file's map (`#doc-edit-map` of the page read, with the order of the units in each of its boxes, `ordersIn`): the units of the two are paired by their block ids first (one the page knows by an id is the file's of that id wherever the file has it now; an id two units have on one side, `id`; of another kind, `kind`); then box by box from the top (the boxes outside any unit by kind and key, each paired unit's boxes by kind and key: one a paired unit has on one side alone, `boxes`), those of one hash and kind in the order both have them (`common`, the longest such run: units nobody changed, which may have moved; what both boxes have alike at their start and their end paired as it stands, the table filled only between, and past 400,000 cells no plan, `large`: a page loaded anew costs less than planning a box of thousands of units with one moved from its top to its middle); between the units so paired, one the page has and one the file has at the same place in a gap holding as many on each side, when of one kind (words changed in place; an id the file gives one the page knew none for is the page's from then on: an export's), unless the file's holds what another unit of that box held before (`shift`: units moved around it, never paired so, which would land the caret in other words); a unit holding boxes and no words of its own (a section, a list; not an item) left alone of its kind in the box on each side, the same one moved around others (the reader's paragraph put before a section, the file having it after, the section's words changed). These two pair by where a unit stands alone (`guessed`), and never one the page knows by an id the file no longer has (its id lost by a file written from an earlier read, or the unit gone and another put where it stood: a review of 2026-11-13 found the reader's unsaved words in a section the Agent replaced with an unrelated one merged silently into the Agent's paragraph, and two steps written back swapped without their ids each taking the other's words): it is taken out and the file's put in, as a page shown anew shows them, its unsaved words committed first and the file's card for them (`missing`); a step of words in a block whose words the pull shows of a unit paired so is refused from then on (`unsure`, below); across boxes, a hash one unit alone has on each side (a unit moved elsewhere unchanged), after which every box is gone through again (the gap such a unit leaves may pair one to one: a section moved out of a Sheet's panel, the Sheet's own units then paired). A unit of the page nothing pairs is taken out (`removed`), one of the file's nothing pairs put in (`inserted`), a paired one standing in another box than the paired one of where it stood or out of the order the units around it keep moved (`moved`): planned so, by place where nothing else tells (a paragraph taken out at the top and one added at the end, an item taken out and another added, are that, never words changed in place). With `replace` (the page's always), a paired unit whose shell the file has otherwise, a text block of which it cannot show the words of in place (a code block's, a display formula's, words in an instance), whose text blocks or boxes are otherwise, whose hash nothing explains, or whose id the file gives a unit of another kind (a paragraph made a heading), is not paired after all (`replaced`): taken out, and the file's put in, the plan made again without that pair, what is in it paired by itself and moved into the one put in (a callout recoloured keeps its paragraphs, the reader's words and caret with them); the caller leaves no unit to be replaced that holds a block the page found a script had changed as it loaded (locked `script`: what a load would make of it again no pull can tell), nor one a script of the document's draws into or wires (the map's keep `script`, the page's `data-ge-keep`), or holding one, and puts in no such unit (`insert`): no script runs for what a pull puts in, a load runs them all. Every pair a pass of the plan cannot keep goes at once (its blocks, then the shells, then the hashes nothing explains: a unit in a container replaced explains the container's hash, which is replaced only where nothing explains it then, so a callout recoloured in a section replaces the callout, and a class on an item of a sub-list that item, never the section or the lists around it), as many passes as there are units replaced inside others, not one a unit (600 paragraphs given a class each took 0.8 s so). It plans nothing (`structure`: shown anew as before, and so is every write after it until then, `swapping`) unless the document is the same besides its units and its title's words (its `frame`, `source/units.mjs` `frameShell`: its head, scripts, header and every element no unit holds, `<title>`'s words left out); each unit whose hash changed is the same besides its words (its `shell`, `unitShell`: its source and its companion's (a Sheet tab's panel) with its own text blocks' insides, the units in it, what its companion holds (the units and words of a tab's panel, the Sheet's) and its block ids taken out and its whitespace folded, but in a `<pre>` or `<textarea>` no text block is and in a quoted attribute's value, where it is content, so that units added, taken out or moved in it and its indentation change no shell, a class, an attribute or a picture do; the page's map carries both, each commit's `file` facts and `shells` (by the keys of its `hashes`) a unit's, its `frame` the document's, made after the server's finishing adds the kit's tags, taken as hashes are: the frame only when the page was alone); each unit whose hash changed has something that explains it: words of its own or of a unit in it changed, units put in, taken out of or moved in it or in a unit in it or in the box that goes with it elsewhere (a tab's panel: the map's `companion`, the box whose changes change the tab's hash), or it moved, and so what is in it (`unseen` otherwise: whitespace the shell does not tell, in a script, nothing on the page to show for it); each text block is where it was and of its kind; and every block whose words changed can be shown anew in place (`patchable`: a host kind no component opens or draws, a Sheet tab's name in its span and words the reader gave up for the file's included, read-only for no reason, in no instance; a block that is not is its unit's to replace), and no block the file locks now (an element elsewhere refers to it, a script reads it) is one the page leaves the reader to write in (`lock`). Then, in one task: no change of a block's the reader gave up for the file's (a conversion, a deletion, a move: 保留 Agent 的) may stand, nor a conversion's stand-in on the page (`given-up`: shown anew, as that choice asks; fuzz `--instances` seed 2 found a pull replacing the stand-in); the commits must hold no change but texts (`pendingOf`: none of a block made, moved or converted, a look, a block holding structure; else `busy`, committed first, its answer asking again), and no text the reader changed may stand in a unit with no id yet (`idless`: committed first, its commit giving it one, so that no unit is named by its place in two versions); for a plan that puts units in, takes them out or moves them (`structural`), what changes must be the page's to change as a load would have made it (`arrangeable`): no block made on the page and not committed yet (`busy` `draft`: committed first); none put in between the ends of a selection that runs across blocks, where it would join the selection and the reader's next Delete take it out unseen (`held` `selection`, as a move through one); each unit taken out or moved an element of the page's, in no instance and with no lead of its own beside it (`remove`, `move`), none taken out holding words the reader has not saved (`busy`: committed first, and the file, having the block no more, says so: a card with the reader's words, nothing lost; scenario `agentPullDeleteTyping`) or the caret or a selection's end (`held` `caret`), none moved that a selection across blocks runs through (`held` `selection`: read again a moment after the selection changes, and meanwhile 1.5 s later, twice as long each time up to 15 s, the page behind until then; once the reader and the file have been still as long as a burst of writes waits for, `waitLeft`, the page is shown anew as before, `swapping`, so that no write is held back for as long as the caret stays); each unit put in of a kind the page knows (`insert` otherwise), made as `enter()` makes a load's (any kind, a table with its scope line, a code block, a picture, a card, an instance and its slots, a detail block, a Sheet; each unit taken out with its leads, each placed with them); what of a unit taken out is its own (not a unit in it the plan keeps, which moves into the one put in) holding words the reader has not saved, the caret or a selection's end, the same as above, and a unit replaced that a selection runs across held; the crash buffer is written; the units are arranged as the file has them (`commits.pulled` `arrangeAs`): box after box from the top, each box of the file holding in its order the element of the page's unit paired with each of its units, moved there where it stands elsewhere (the same element, read before anything moves, as one in a unit taken out goes off the page with it until it is put where the file has it; the reader's words and the caret in it, which taken off the page and put back is put back among its words) or made of the file's (its element taken from the page as it is served now, with its boxes emptied of units, which go in them as in any box; the unit, its boxes and its text blocks the map's as the file has them; its formulas drawn as the kit draws a page's as it loads (`drawFormulas`, the made element alone marked as a place formulas render in for the moment, so that no `\(` the reader typed around it is drawn), its leads (a table's scope line) taken from the page served with it; once everything stands where the file has it, `editableAhead` and `prepareBlock` making each of its blocks editable as a load does (a display formula's by its component's `prepare`, a Sheet tab's name in its span, the file's atoms with each), a block a component keeps itself (a code block's, `closed`) a block of the map that component `prepare`s once the marks name the unit by the page's number, after the component's `arrived` hook has done what the kit does of a unit of its kind as a page loads (a code block decorated and its lines drawn: the kit draws none put in later), the layout read again once the marks say what each unit is (a table's grid names its rows by identity then), and the kit's kernel mounting what it mounts of an element put in (charts, tables, sheets, flowcharts, a package's instance, which `installed/` also hears arrive) as of any the editor puts in, a list item with the list nested in it read-only in it and its line kept, and once the commits' marks say what is a box and a text block, the frames of what was put in marked, `markFrames`: a review of 2026-11-13 found every element put in framed, a section's heading with a component's frame around it, a nested list editable in its item; `tests/unit/pull.mjs` holds each kind it puts in against what a load makes of the same file); a list item of the page's that the write gives sub-items, or takes its last one from, left as a load leaves it (its nested list read-only, `data-ge-nested`, its line kept; or none of that); a unit placed with the elements that lead it (a table's scope line) and before those of the one it goes before (a review found a paragraph put at a box's top landing between a scope line and its table), nothing that is where it should be moved; a unit of the page that no box of the file holds is taken out (`drop`); the layout is read again from the page as it is now (`captureLayout`: nothing else waits to be committed); the undo steps recorded before are rebased (`history.pulled`: each step's lists of what stood where lose the elements taken out and take those put in or moved (within the container too) after the one before them, but one the step itself made or took out only on the side that held it (the reader's paragraph split off, which the Agent moved within its section, still goes with the split's undo), so that undoing the reader's own split or deletion moves none of the Agent's blocks back or out; refused from then on, `stale`, "Agent 拿走或改动了这一步改过的块：没有撤销，可以直接改。", a step whose parent went with the write, one whose own block (the paragraph Enter split off) the write took out or moved into another container (the split's undo would join its words into the first and leave it where the Agent put it: twice, as a review found), one whose words it sets in a block the write took out, a step of words, a look or a source in a block the write took out (once spent on a block off the page), and a step of words in a block whose words the write shows of a unit paired by where it stands alone, `unsure`), the folds kept (`folds.pulled()`: what is folded stays so, what the write put under a folded heading hidden with it), blocks selected that the write took out no longer selected, and the page is told its layout changed (`doc-layout-change`: the outline, 批注 and the Agent's changes follow); each block shows the file's words as a load shows them (`prepareBlock`), the selection in it put back among its words (`caretKept`), its undo steps undoing three ways from then on (`history.merged`), but a block the reader changed and has not saved, one a selection runs across into another block, and one holding the block the caret is in (a list item's paragraph), which keep the page's words (`held`: such a block's commit merges with the file's, as any); the element at the window's top stays where it stood (`reading-place.mjs` `elementOf`; Chrome's scroll anchoring does most of it). 用文件里的 on a card about words (`decide` "theirs" of a text, `autosave.giveUp`) gives up what the page holds of the block now (`commits.textNow`: its words and those of the blocks split off it), which the reader may have written on after the version the card shows was sent (in it, or in a block split off it: Enter, the commit on its way): what the card did not show is listed (`lostKey`, why `changed`, or `refused` of a card for another reason, 复制文字), as what a page could not put back is, pull on as off, never dropped without a word (fuzz `--words` 9304 found it dropped, a review a product bug, not the fuzz's criterion). Then it asks for such a pull (`showFile`) instead of a page shown anew: a block whose words the reader gave up (`discard`) is no change of theirs to the pull (not dirty, never held, its own `given-up` lock no bar) and shows the file's words whether or not they changed, unlocked, the reader's to write in again; one the pull could not show leaves the page shown anew as before (`reloadSoon`), and so does one with a block split off it, given up with its words (the page shown anew at once; the pull itself refuses one, `given-up`): showing the file's words in place left that block on the page, live and uncounted, written later with the next change (its words twice). A pull's last check (nothing to commit but what was) excuses a block given up only where the write took it off the page; the autosave counts what waits anew after every pull it takes in (`took`), a commit armed for it. 撤回 of the Agent's changes and its 撤销 (`changes/`, `showOwn`) take the write they asked for in the same way, as a write of the page's own (no revert check: the reader asked for it), and offer 撤销 where the page stands (`"pulled"`); where the pull cannot (another write landed, a unit held), the page is shown anew as before. What the reader typed while that write was on its way (changed() counts it then, arming nothing: `reloading`) is committed once the write is taken in or failed (`resume`), not only at their next change. A page opened on its own (not in the workbench) pulls as the workbench's does, its fallback the browser's reload (`setReloader`).
