What the page committed in a block the write takes out (or turns into another kind: a paragraph made a heading is one taken out and one put in) goes with the version as a page shown anew takes it (`keepJournal`): asked of for the hour (`journal`, carried), so that a later write putting it back as it was before the reader's edit is a card (a review found it dropped with the block's source, no card). The page names its units by that version from then on (`commits.pulled()`: each unit's number and hash there, `openUnits` and `openHashes`, and each text block's outside any unit, `openBlocks`, in place of the count and hashes of what it opened on; `map.version`, which the next commit's `opening` and the revert check's carry), each unit takes the file's hash and shell but those holding a held block (the page did not see all the file has of them: deleting or converting one asks first, as of any someone else changed), and one put in in place of the page's (replaced) holding a held block has the hash of no version (`null`: the same; it took the file's, and the Agent's words in that block, never shown, went with its deletion without a card), every unit the plan paired its keep, grid and parts and every block its capabilities as the file has them (a block an element elsewhere refers to now keeps itself), and the texts it had committed in blocks that show the file's words now are asked of for the hour, as an earlier page's are (`carried`). The autosave knows the file as that version (`known`, `seen`; it is its own in `own` while it is the version the page knows, `strict`, so that its announcement, coming later, is no write of anyone else's, and so, with the pull on, is the one it opened on: that version written back once the page knows another, an Agent undoing its last edit or writing back what it read before the reader's commit, is a write of theirs at once, its revert check asked, not the page's own for 5s; a failure of what follows a pull, `after`, takes nothing of this back) and shows the file (`foreign` false: the next commit takes in the file's answer as its own, `alone`) once nothing was held and no other write was heard meanwhile; else it pulls again (each commit's answer asks; while the same blocks of the same version stay held, 3s, then twice as long each time, 15s at most). The review of the Agent's changes is read again (`changes/` `pulled`), the outline, long runs and 批注 following the text by their observers. A merge a commit's answer said a moment ago (`notice`) is not said over by Ctrl+S's 已保存 or by 已重新连上 (`mergeTold`): a page shown anew said it again, and one that takes writes in where it stands has no load to say it. What the pull leaves the page must hold nothing to commit: should the commits find a change they did not hold before it (`pendingOf` after), or anything throw while the page changes, the page is shown anew from the crash buffer written right before (`bail`: closed, nothing stored over it, the journal as it would have been kept right before the pull, `stash`, not of the page half changed, said on the console, which the scenarios and the fuzz take for a page error). Off (`localStorage` `gallery-edit:pull` set to `off`; the scenarios' and the fuzz's `GALLERY_TEST_PULL=off`), every write is shown by loading the page anew. The page counts its pulls on its root (`data-ge-pulls`, which `t.reloaded()` of the scenarios waits on as on a reload) and says when it does not show all the file has yet (`data-ge-behind`). An undo or redo that takes off the page a block someone else's words were merged into since it was recorded (a paragraph split off, the Agent's words pulled into it, the split undone; a deletion redone; a conversion undone) goes on, and the page claims to have seen none of the file's of the units that went (`history.mjs` `mergedOff`, `commits.unseen`: their hash null), so that the file refuses their deletion or conversion for someone else's change and the reader is asked (仍然删除, 保留 Agent 的), as on a page that never showed those words (with the pull, each unit takes the file's hash, which the server would have let the deletion go by: the Agent's words went with the reader's block, no card); a text step there undoes three ways, or is refused, as after any merge. Scenarios `agentPullInPlace`, `agentPullUndo`, `agentPullUndoSplit`, `agentPullIme`, `agentPullStructure`, `agentPullAppend`, `agentPullInsertAbove`, `agentPullDelete`, `agentPullMoveSection`, `agentPullDeleteTyping`, `agentPullReplacedSection`, `agentPullUndoSplitMoved`, `agentPullImeStructure`, `agentPullBurst`, `agentPullFormula`, `agentPullInstance`, `agentPullTableAbove`, `agentPullTheirs`, `agentPullStandalone`, `agentRevertAtPull`, `workbenchSwapTab`, `agentPullScriptAfterPull`, `agentPullSheetsArrive`; `workbenchSwap` and `agentBurstStaysLive` write the page's head too, for the swap (a frame no pull takes in).
- **A file of the folder written again.** `page/assets-live.mjs` (the feedback of 2026-11-17: an Agent copied a new picture over `assets/shot.png` and the open page went on showing the old one, written alone, with a write of the document beside it or of the picture's own block, until it was loaded anew; the Agent renamed the file to get round it). Chrome never fetches an address a page has used again while that page stays open, whatever the answer's `no-cache` said, and a write of the document is taken in by the pull, which loads nothing (before the pull, such a write showed the page anew, which fetched the picture; a picture written alone never was). So the page names each file of its folder by its stamp, `?v=<stamp>` (the server's render, [render.md](render.md) "The files of the document's folder"), and the server tells it the stamps a file of its folder has now (`asset new <file>=<stamp>` once the file is whole and quiet, `asset now …` as the page connects, for every file told so while the server ran: [render.md](render.md) "Live preview"); its live script keeps them in `window.__galleryAssets` and says the files whose stamp moved (`doc-files-written`), and `followFiles` swaps every address of the page that names such a file by another stamp, or by none, for the one heard last, one pass at a time, each by the stamps heard last as it starts (a file told again while a pass still loads its picture is swapped by the next pass; two passes from the same old value once left the page at the first's stamp): an element's `src`, `srcset`, `href`, `xlink:href`, `poster`, `data`, `background` and `style`, and a `<style>`'s text, wherever it stands (an image block, a cell's picture, the page icon, a bookmark's cover, a file's link, an embed's frame, the picture open large), under another workspace's base or an origin too, a fragment kept; an address with a query of its own, another file's or another document's left. A picture's new address is loaded and decoded out of sight first and swapped in only then (one load for every place of it), so the picture never shows blank or broken while it changes, and the one shown is kept when the new bytes do not load; a frame, a link, a media element's source take it at once. An address that already names the stamp is left, so a picture that did not change is neither fetched nor drawn again. The swap changes what the page shows, never what it writes: the editor writes what `data-ge-<attribute>` says (`core.mjs`), so no swap is a change to commit, and an element whose address changed meanwhile (the editor showing it anew) is left to what changed it. The editor's own addresses of the folder's files (`editor.mjs` `assetUrl`, `fileUrl`, `embedUrl`, and a cell's picture through `served.mjs`) name the stamp the page heard last (`stampedUrl`), so a picture it shows anew after a write is the new one; What the page puts back of what it held before a write it heard of names the file by its old stamp: an undo or redo restoring a block's or a cell's HTML (a letter typed in a cell holding the picture, then Ctrl+Z), a block deleted while the file was written and brought back by undo, a pull's unit served a moment before, a block pasted from the page's own copy (the review of 2026-11-17 found the first two showing the old picture until a reload). So once the page has heard of a file written again, a `MutationObserver` looks at every address that comes into the page, or that an attribute takes, and one naming such a file by another stamp is swapped as the news would swap it; a file the page heard nothing of is left as it comes. The two places that read a served address back take it with its stamp: a cell's picture pasted from another program's copy of the table tools' (`table/pictures.mjs` `SERVED`: the folder's own file, never a copy downloaded) and a link chip's icon pasted (`core.mjs` `mentionOf`). In 演示视图 too (`present-links.mjs`). How many addresses were swapped is on the page's root, `data-ge-files-swapped` (the scenarios wait on it). A file of a custom page's `assets/` written again shows the page again ([render.md](render.md) "Its live channel"), which asks for its pictures anew. A picture page icon shown by the icon route (the workbench's sidebar and tab bar, a page chip, the home page's cards) hears no news: written again under its name, it shows the new picture when what shows it is loaded anew, its unchanged pictures answered 304 ([render.md](render.md) "The files of the document's folder"); the picker names an icon's picture by its content, so only a copy over one makes it. Scenarios `agentReplacesPicture`, `agentPictureComesBack`, `pagePictureWrittenAgain`; `tests/unit/written-files.mjs`.
- **Conflicts.** `conflicts.mjs`. What the file refused is held back alone: the block is flagged while any card is about it, and a card for each conflict stands in a panel at the bottom left, fading in and out as the bar beside it does (`ui.mjs` `fadeIn`, `fadeOut`: 120ms and 100ms, 收起 too; the room it took given back once it has gone; `role="alert"` on its first show), the card looked at grayed only where there are several (a click on the flagged block shows its card; its 定位 scrolls to the block, what hides it opened first: `DocKit.reveal`, a tab not shown, a closed disclosure). What the file cannot hold whatever stands in it now (`unfit`: the server's reasons `invalid`, `fixed`, `empty`, `source/checks.mjs`) is held the same way but is no conflict with a newer version: the notice, its card, the panel's head and Ctrl+S say the document cannot store it, and the card takes the block away or goes back to the file's text, with no place nearby to try. The reader's version of a text is everything its commit carried, the blocks split off it too (written with it); copying takes what the block and the blocks split off it hold now. Words changed on both sides in the same place: 保留我的 (the reader's words go over the file's: the file's, the card's `theirs`, made their `before`, what they were written from sent along as `from`, by which the review of the Agent's changes takes them as the reader's, "Agent's changes"), 用文件里的 (the change is given up, and the page shows the file's; what the reader wrote in the block, or in one split off it, after the version the card shows was sent is listed as what a page could not put back: `autosave.giveUp`, "The pull"). The card prints the two versions, 你的版本 and 文件里的（Agent 的）, and where they differ each marks its own words in place (`markedVersions`, the word diff of `merge.mjs` `wordDiff`; since 2026-11-17): the reader's on a neutral gray (`--ge-diff-mine`, 14% of the text's ink, 22% white on a dark card, where a fainter gray did not read), never red, which on the page is what the Agent took out; the file's as the Agent's added words look on the page (`--ge-diff-add` under a 2px `--ge-diff-add-line`), the words in both of the text's color. Where more than half of either is marked (a rewrite, not an edit) or the diff does not give either back whole (it reads 1500 words of each), both are printed plain; every card that prints both versions marks them so (`reverted`, a unit's change), and none has a toggle (查看局部差异, the one merged sentence with chips 你的 and Agent 的, is gone). 用文件里的 was 使用最新版本 until 2026-11-17, a name the owner could not read: it says whose version it takes, as the version's label does, and is true on every card that offers it, whoever wrote the file (an Agent, another tab of the reader's, a hand edit) or where nobody did (what the document cannot hold, `unfit`: the file as it was). A block the reader edited that the Agent deleted: 恢复我的 (a plain paragraph or heading is made again as a new block), 接受删除, or copying the text; a field whose component the Agent restructured: 用文件里的 or copying; a part of an instance its custom block no longer edits as text (`package`: the instance as the page has it, and no such text block), the custom block gone or changed since the page opened: the same, in words of its own. What a component wrote by a manifest that changed since (`definition`) has no card: the page is shown again by the new one ("A component writes back"). What a component wrote to a part someone else changed meanwhile (`changed`), or to an instance they deleted (`missing`), is held as a unit's operation is; a part they left alone goes in beside whatever else of the instance they changed (`parts`), and an instance they moved takes the write where it stands now. The parts one edit of the page wrote together go or wait together, any other part by itself (`together`, from `together` of the page object, which keeps them on the instance, `data-ge-together`, so that a page taking up what one that is gone left sends them so too): what goes is written, and the rest waits on the card (the answer's `wrote` names what went in); what one commit carries of components' writes takes or leaves such parts whole (`within`); a write of another part made while one waits goes the same way (`heldParts`), and refused it joins that card, one of the instance. An instance found only by what stands around it and what it says (one with no id yet, most like the one the page named) takes no write by its parts: it may be another block standing where that one stood. Its card prints no version: the reader's is the block as it stands on the page, flagged, never a table's words run together, and 复制这一块 copies that block as Ctrl+C copies it selected (`copyBlocks` of `clipboard/`, by the browser's copy command), which pastes as an instance of the same custom block holding what the page held, so that the reader's side can be had whatever is chosen. 仍然调整 writes what the page holds over each part as the card showed it (the refusal's `parts`, their hashes, which the page takes as what it saw: written again by someone else since, it asks again, the card showing what the file holds then), and makes what the file held (`theirs`, each part's inside) a step of its own before it (`overwrite` of the page object), so that one undo gives the file's back and writes it again, the component drawing it, and a redo the reader's. Once that write went in, every part the card held (`forced` of `apply`: a move of the instance carries none of it, and a write cut off at what one commit carries waits with the word), no undo goes below it (`fold` of the history: the reader's earlier steps on those parts hold them no more, a `source` step's and a structural step's that recorded them with a table's cells alike (a view's group renamed: "Table views"), the cells staying in it, or a second undo would write what the page had before over the file's; below it are the steps made before it, by the order steps are made in, wherever it stands: undone, or on neither stack, a newer change made since), and the page is not shown again for someone else's write before the reader's next change, that undo among them, or 30 seconds (`keepsOverwrite`): shown again, the page has no undo steps, so the undo lasts until then. A showing the page asks for itself (保留 Agent 的 on another card, a custom block changed: `reloadSoon`) is not held so (`holding(asked)` of the autosave), and ends the undo. Refused again, the step stands for nothing and goes. 保留 Agent 的 gives that write up and that alone, and shows the file's once nothing else waits; 接受删除 takes the block off the page. Words typed in its text part meanwhile are a text block's, merged with someone else's as any, and go while its write waits on a card, or once that write is given up: what a component writes holds none of them; a write after them still meets a change of its part as a conflict, the page keeping the hash it saw of the instance. A block the reader deleted or converted that the Agent changed: 仍然删除 / 仍然转换类型 (`force`) or 保留 Agent 的, and a conversion's text can be copied (it carries the block's text); the card shows the block's words as the file holds them, those of the blocks in it too (`theirs`: a section deleted whole that the Agent wrote in), beside the reader's, what 仍然… writes over and, of a deletion, which block it is. The deletion or the conversion undone (or the block converted deleted since), the card goes: the page makes no change of that kind any more (`undoneRefusal`), and what the reader does to the block then goes, or asks, as any change does (a deletion then has a card of its own). The reader's word (as 仍然调整's, `forced` of the commits) is for a change of the kind the card was about, over the unit as the card showed it: the refusal gives the unit's hash in the file (`hash`), as the file holds it once that commit is written (which may move the unit or what holds it, and so indent it otherwise; so are a component's parts), which the forced operation sends back as its own, the server finding by it a unit the page's naming finds no more (one with no id, what stood around it changed) where no other block the page opened on had that hash (that one may stand there still, the card's own gone: it asks, `ambiguous`), one the page named by an id the file has no more being gone, and written again since, it is refused again, the card then saying the Agent changed the block again after the reader saw it (`again`), so that the next press is seen to be a new decision. It lasts until that change goes in; a change of another kind is held to what the page saw, as any is (the block converted anyway deleted while the card stood: the deletion's card, the Agent's words staying), and so is whatever the reader does to it after. A block the file has twice now (the Agent wrote the same block again): 用文件里的 or copying; a unit the Agent deleted that the reader moved or resized: 接受删除. A block made on the page whose place is gone first goes to the end of its box, once; then it is held like the others (by the id it proposes, `i<id>` in `commits.issues`, with what it holds: its card shows it, copying takes what the block holds now): 放到附近 moves it (one undo step) right after the block it stands in, in the page's own flow, or to the end of that flow when it stands there already, and the next commit writes it there (where the file has none of that block, at the end of the page), and 撤销这一块 takes it off the page. A conflict's flag follows its block: after every structural change (an undo, a conversion) it is on the element that block is now (`reflag()`). Texts put back by someone else's write: see above. A held unit keeps in the page's layout all it had (its element, type, looks, table's grid and place), so its change still shows until decided and 仍然调整 sends it, and so does whatever else the reader did to it meanwhile, which no commit sends while it is held (moved while the card of its column stands, a table goes with its operation); what of it went in with the commit that held it is the file's, taken from what that commit left (`went`, by what each change is about: `layoutOf`), not sent again (a table's grid kept for a move refused for its place would send its operation twice, against a table it no longer names; a conversion that went in wrote the block's looks with its element, which would otherwise go again over a color someone gave it since); what its component writes is what the commits say the file holds (`data`), which holds what went in and not what waits. Its place is its own only, among the others as the commit left them (taking a whole box back undid their moves too, and sent them again without end): where the page shows it in its place among the units around it (no move of it, `moves` of the commit), the file has it there too, whatever the commit moved around it; moved on the page and not sent, or off the page, it goes back after the unit that stood before it that the commit left where it was (one the commit moved stands elsewhere in the file). What is placed after a unit goes after those the layout has right after it that wait off the page where the file has them there still (a deletion held or given up, of a unit the file has one of, not `missing` or `ambiguous`: `waitingAfter`, `pastWaiting`; a section dissolved after a paragraph whose deletion waits keeps its blocks after that paragraph), and where the unit leaves a container the commit deletes, they go with it, their place alone (`along`: a section dissolved around a paragraph whose deletion waits keeps it among its blocks, its card standing, and leaves no section around it). The texts in a held unit wait with it only where its change carries them (a conversion, a table's operation, a deletion the blocks it took off the page, given up with it) or the file has no one unit for them (`missing`, `ambiguous`): in one whose move or look waits they go as words do. 保留 Agent 的 and 用文件里的 give up the change the card was about, as it was, and that alone (`discarded`, by key: the change, its kind, its reason, whether its words go with it; of what a component wrote, each part as it was: `givenParts`): it is never sent again, and whatever else the reader does to the block goes as any change does, another of that kind too (moved while the card stood, it goes where the page has it; moved elsewhere after (where the page has it: a block moved whose place went goes to the end of its box once before its card, never after it), converted to another type, its table's rows changed, another part of an instance written or that part written anew, it goes, or asks where the file has the block otherwise); of a unit the file has no one of, all of it. A unit the file deleted since (the block deleted, 仍然删除 over someone else's change of it) waits for nothing, whatever was held or given up of it: none of it stays in the layout, which would send its deletion again with every commit, and what was given up of it stays given up should undo put it back. What no commit will send any more, the words of a block given up so (and of a text given up), is read-only until the page shows the file's (`data-ge-lock="given-up"`, written with the marks; a hover says why), whatever element stands for it: typed there they were lost with it. Conflicts do not keep the reader in: they are in the crash buffer, and come back as texts to copy; but the page never reloads by itself over one.
- **Recent versions** (历史版本: `src/editor/history.mjs`; the workbench's 历史版本, [workbench.md](workbench.md) "History"; `gallery history`). The service keeps a document's file as it was before and after each write of its own (a commit of any page, a revert and its undo, a restore and its undo, a hand-in, a renaming) and as each write of anyone else's left it, taken as the review of the Agent's changes takes one (`keepTaken`: a page served, a write announced, a commit's or a request's read of the file, the look as the server starts, each when the text is not the service's own last write: an Agent's `Write` or `Edit`, a hand edit, a `git checkout`, a write made while no server ran), in the workspace's `.gallery/state/history/<slug>/<time>-<version>.html` (its file is `doc.html`, or `report.html` in a document made before Gallery 0.1: the service reads and writes each under its own name, `src/shared/workspace.mjs` `documentFiles`), the time to the millisecond and always later than the last one kept (two in one millisecond, a clock set back), the version the text's hash; beside each, `<time>-<version>.json` says who made it, `{ by, kind, from? }`: `by` is `"reader"` (a commit of the reader's page, 撤回 and 撤销 on the page, the workbench's restore and its undo, the sidebar's renaming), `"agent"` (a write outside the service after the first version kept, a commit of a page of the Agent's own, `?agent`, `gallery revert`, `gallery history --restore`, the Agent's hand-in) or `null` (not known: the first version kept of a document, and each one kept before 2026-11-11, which has no `.json`; a version named to the second); `kind` is `commit`, `write`, `revert`, `undo`, `restore` (`from`: the version restored), `hand-in`, `rename` or `page-icon` (the sidebar's row icon of a document not open: the workspace's `POST icon`, `reiconReport`). None twice in a row: the same text again is the one kept, and a write of the service's own says over a take who made it (after a restart the reader's last commit, served, is taken as anyone's). Kept: the last 30, and of those before them the last of each hour, 50 more at most (`thinned`: yesterday's version is still there after a morning of edits); the rest deleted with their `.json`. A folder the workspace makes, copies to or deletes for good (`forgetChanges`: the workspace's create, duplicate, copy and destroy; `gallery new`'s `noteNew` too) has the versions kept under its name forgotten with its review, so a document made under the name of one deleted before takes over none of them (历史版本 would list them as its own, and a restore of the first of them bring back the old document's text: a review of 2026-11-11). So an Agent writing over the reader's committed work right away loses nothing for good, and the reader or the Agent can go back to any of them. `POST /__gallery/edit/history` (`historyAction`; `gallery history` and `gallery revert` in their own process when no server runs, on the same state, `src/cli/edit-ask.mjs`, never while one does: an older server's answer that is none is said, nothing written): `versions` (outside the queue, the file taken first) answers the versions newest first, each with `current` (the newest, when the file holds it), what it changed of the one before it, block by block (`source/changes.mjs` `changedBlocks`: `diffRows`' own, each block `{ change, kind, name (what the editor calls its kind), tag, id (its data-block-id, null for a deleted one), text (its first 40 characters), title? }`; `counts`, 8 blocks at most, `more`; none for the first; worked out once for each pair of versions and kept beside them, `history.mjs` `summaries.json`, `{ v, pairs: { "<version before> <version>": … } }`, `readSummariesIn`, the pairs of versions no longer kept let go as it is written again; the newest first, for 250 ms of one answer at most, `LIST_BUDGET`, `createEditService`'s `listBudget`, one at least each answer, the rest with no `counts` and `later` saying how many, which the list asked again works out next: the workbench asks until none is left, `gallery history` asks with `all` for every one at once; so a document of 80 versions, or a long one, lists at once, where one pair of the Component Catalog takes about 70 ms and every version's text was read on every opening until 2026-11-19), and `view`, the address of its page (`viewAddress`: `<base>/generated/<slug>.html?present&version=<id>&key=<key>`, the page the workbench's 历史版本 shows, [render.md](render.md) "Live preview"), whose key is an HMAC of the token's over the document and the version (`viewKey`): a frame's request carries no token, so the list, asked with the token, the Host and the Origin, is the one way to an address, which opens that version alone (`versionShown(slug, id, key)`: the version's text as its pages see it, `shownSource`'s, the text as kept, and `marked`, that text with what the version changed of the one kept before it marked in it for its page to draw, null for the first or when no block changed: `source/changes.mjs` `markedVersion`, `diffRows`' own blocks, aligned by their ids and kinds as the review's, each marked in its start tag, `data-ge-hist="add"` on a block added, the outermost, and on one changed with no words to tell, `"words"` on one whose words changed with `data-ge-hist-was`, its words in the version before, on each of its text blocks that changed, and `data-ge-hist-before`, `-after` or, with no block left, `-into` on the main where blocks were deleted, their groups in a `script[data-ge-hist-ghosts]` before `</body>`; never a file's text; or null for another key, a name that is no version's or none kept; it reads nothing but that version's file and the one before it, `history.mjs` `readVersionIn`, whose id is held to `VERSION_NAME`, no path; the page drawn from the marked text loads `editor/page/changes/version.mjs` under the editor's stamp, which draws the marks with the review's own modules: workbench.md "History"); `show` one version's words, a row each block with words of its own (`textRows`: the ghost's rows; what the workbench showed of a version before it showed its page, 2026-11-12); `changes` (outside the queue, the file taken first) what the version named changed of the one kept before it, every block with its words, as 历史版本 marks it on the version's page (`source/changes.mjs` `blockChanges`: `diffRows`' own in the file's order, the deleted ones where they stood, each `{ change, kind, tag, id, text, title?, texts?: [{ before, now }] of one changed, whole? of one changed with no words to tell }`; `previous`, the id of the one before it, null and no `counts` for the first: `gallery history --changes`, the Agent's way to what the reader sees there); `restore` (in the commits' queue, never of a file half written: the file kept among the versions first, `expect`ed) writes the version named (its id, its version, or the start of either, six characters at least: `versionNamed`; the latest of those of one text) as the file, `doc.json`'s title and icon following the page's (`inStep`, as a revert's), and answers the id of an undo kept 10 minutes (`undo`, a revert's as well: `undoWrite`, while the file is still what it wrote, the review put back as it was), the version that holds what it wrote over (`kept`) and the one it made (`made`); the file saying it already, `same`, nothing written. The reader's restore (the workbench's) ends the review of the Agent's changes, the reader having said what the document is; the Agent's (`agent: true`: `gallery history --restore`) is a write of the Agent's, which the review takes in as one (started or grown, as any), and answers the blocks it took back of the file too (40 at most). Each tells the document's pages, the write being the service's own (shown at once), and the workbench has the open document's own page ask for its restore (session commands `restore-version`, `undo-version`), so that it shows it as a write of its own (`autosave.mjs` `showOwn`: nothing asked of what it put back). The token and the last 50 versions pages were opened on (the ones used last; one is used only if it still hashes to its version) are kept there too (`snapshots/`), so a page opened before the preview server restarted keeps committing, and names what has no id yet by its opening version; `createEditService()` without a `stateDirectory` keeps those in memory only, and no recent versions. New blocks reuse the original line break (CRLF is kept) and indentation; a removed block that sat on its own line takes the line with it; when every item of a list (or every paragraph of a quote) is removed and the file has nothing else in it, the list goes too. A revert of the Agent's changes ("Agent's changes") keeps the file it writes over among them first.
- **Agent's changes.** What an Agent wrote to a document since the reader last kept or reverted it is marked on its page (the user's request), and a bar at the window's bottom offers to keep or revert it. An Agent's write is any write of `doc.html` the edit service did not make (`settle.wrote` knows its own: a commit of any tab, a hand-in, a renaming, a revert and its undo), an Agent's `Write`/`Edit` and a hand edit in another editor or a `git checkout` alike. The server keeps the review in `.gallery/state/changes/<slug>/` (`editor/changes.mjs`; in memory without a state directory): `seen.html`, what it last took of the file; `base.html`, while something is under review, the baseline: the document as its pages saw it before the first write under review (`shownSource`'s text), with the reader's own commits since applied to it too; `state.json`, `{ v, born, seen, base, since, at, writes }` (the folder's birth time, the two texts' hashes, the first and last write's time, how many; the `draft` an older Gallery may have left there, a first writing's, below, is read as none, `readState`, and goes with the next save). The disk is what counts: each look reads `state.json`, so the folder deleted is the review forgotten (a test's reset), and a folder born again under the same name (`born`), or one the workspace makes, copies to or deletes for good (`forgetChanges`), takes over nothing. Every take of the file is looked at (`look`, in a queue of the review's own, apart from the commits'): the announcement of a write (`tell`), each read of a commit's attempt (so that a write no announcement told yet, still in its quiet time, is recorded as the Agent's before the commit's own replaces `seen`), a hand-in's read, a page served, and as the server starts each document of the workspace once (`takeAll`, from `src/server/routes.mjs` beside the record of edits' catch-up; one held half written left to its announcement), so that a write made while no server ran starts its review as the server starts, against what the last server took (`seen`, on disk), with no page opened and no command of the Agent's: the review's baseline is the server's own take of the file before the write, never a step of the Agent's (since 2026-11-11; `gallery open` writes the qa baseline and asks nothing of the review). Decided for the user: the last known version is `seen` alone, never a recent version or a snapshot, which a test's reset of the review or a folder made again under the same name would make the baseline of what is no longer the document; a document no server ever took is taken as it is at its first take, nothing marked. Each take that finds a write of anyone else's keeps it among the recent versions too ("Recent versions": `keepTaken`). The same text as `seen`, nothing; a change of ids, white space or natural HTML's writing alone moves `seen` only; else, with nothing under review, a review starts with `seen` as its baseline (never the 写作偏好 page's), unless `seen` is a new document's (below), with one under review it grows (`writes`), and it ends by itself when the file says what the baseline says again. A new document's starter filled is no change to review, so that a document an Agent writes from nothing is not all green under 「Agent 已修改」: an Agent's write taken while `seen` is a new document's (its title, its header and the starter's `data-doc-placeholder` section and nothing else with words or media, `isNewDocument`), `gallery new`'s or one the workbench's 新建 made, an Agent filling it alike, moves `seen` alone: nothing is marked, no bar, nothing told the pages. Nothing of it lasts: the Agent's next write, as any write, starts a review against the document as that first write left it, with no step between, so a new document written in several writes is marked from its second write on (the user accepts it, 2026-11-13), which `gallery new`'s `下一步：` line says. `gallery new` has the review take its starter on disk at once (`src/cli/hand-in.mjs` `noteNew`), so that a server whose first look at the document is the Agent's first write (started after it, or no page served before) takes that write against the starter, as one that served the page does. Decided by the user on 2026-11-13, in place of a document's first writing (2026-11-07 to 11-13, the user's word of 2026-11-07 that a new document written in several writes had each after the first marked: `draft` in `state.json`, the time of the Agent's last write, while which each write of the Agent's moved `seen` alone; it ended with the Agent's next `gallery open <slug>`, told the server by `POST /__gallery/edit/round`, `service.mjs` `beginRound`, or written on disk by `src/cli/hand-in.mjs` `roundBegins`, with the reader's first commit, not one of a page of the Agent's own, or with 30 minutes and no write of the Agent's): it needed a step of the Agent's to end, and once SKILL.md no longer had the Agent run `gallery open` before a revision (the server keeps the version before an outside change by itself), it did not end in real use, a revision within half an hour of the last write was taken in as the first writing's, and no revision was marked. All of it is gone, the route, `beginRound`, `roundBegins`, `DRAFT_QUIET` and `createEditService`'s `reviewing` (its clock, for tests) with it; a `state.json` an older Gallery left with `draft` is read without it, its next write reviewed, and `gallery revert` with nothing under review says so. A page of the Agent's own (`?agent`, on the Agent 自用 line of `gallery new`, `gallery import` and `gallery open`, and `gallery block try`'s page) still sends `agent: true` with its commits (`autosave.mjs`), which makes its writes and versions the Agent's in the recent versions and the record of edits; to the review it is a commit as any, applied to the baseline. A document not new when an Agent first wrote it (one `gallery import` made, one the reader typed in first) is reviewed from that write on; one whose `seen` is a new document's text again with nothing under review (all of it deleted and kept) takes the Agent's next write as its starter filled. The reader's edits never count: each commit that wrote is applied again to the baseline once it is answered (`rebaseOnto`: the same `commitEdits` with the same `opening`, texts, units, `touch`, `pageIds`, seal, and the ids it gave, in order), only what went into the file (the commit's answer, by key: a text or an operation the file refused, a conflict held on a card, stays out of the baseline too, and of what a component wrote that partly went in, only the parts that did, `wrote`, of a list deleted item by item, only the items that did, `went`), so that what the reader gives up on a card (用文件里的, 保留 Agent 的) is never the baseline's; until 2026-11-17 the replay took everything the commit carried, and in a commit that wrote something else too (another block, or only the ids it gave) the words held on the card were the baseline's, marked once given up as words the Agent took out and written by 撤回, though the file never held them (so were the reader's words in a block the Agent deleted, its ghost holding them), and a deletion the file refused took the block out of the baseline, the Agent's change of it counted as added and 撤回 deleting it. What the reader then says to do anyway (`force`: 仍然删除, 仍然转换类型, 仍然调整) goes over the unit as the card showed it in the file, the hash the refusal gave (and a component's parts as it gave them), which the baseline never held: on the baseline it goes over the unit as the baseline holds it, the one of that id (its hash, and the hashes of the parts written), so that the reader's word is the reader's there too (replayed as it went to the file it was refused, the item deleted anyway counted as deleted by the Agent and 撤回 putting it back, a part written anyway marked and written back as it was); a unit the baseline has no one of by that id is replayed from the hash the page saw, as any change. Each text goes in from the words the reader's were written from: its `before`, or, for words 保留我的 wrote over the file's (their `before` then the card's `theirs`), its `from` (the page's `keptOver`: the block's `before` when the reader kept them, the same when kept again after the words kept were refused, sent while `before` is still that `theirs` and until its text goes in), but from `before` where the baseline holds those very words (tried alone first, giving no ids: the review kept, 确认, while the card stood and the Agent writing again, the new baseline holds the Agent's words the card showed, which from `from` the reader's met as the Agent's change, marked so and 撤回 writing the Agent's over them). Who a change belongs to is so one rule, the review's: what the file holds and the baseline does not is the Agent's, and the baseline takes every edit of the reader's it can as the reader made it. A block the Agent did not touch takes it whole; one the Agent changed merges it word by word (`mergeText`): the reader's words apart from the Agent's (an unchanged word between) go in, the Agent's alone stay marked; the words 保留我的 kept are the reader's, the Agent's change of that block given up and nothing of it marked, the review ending when nothing else of the Agent's is left, or counting the other blocks of the same write alone (until 2026-11-14 the replay went from the card's `theirs`, which the baseline did not hold, and the words kept showed as the Agent's change, 「Agent 已修改 +1 −1」); 用文件里的 sends nothing, the Agent's words staying the Agent's. What it cannot take stays out: what the reader typed in a block the Agent added, or over or right beside words it changed, the review cannot tell from the Agent's: it counts as the Agent's and goes with a revert. The sidebar's renaming is applied to the baseline as `renameReport` applies it (`retitledBase`); a hand-in changes nothing a review counts. What changed is counted by blocks (`source/changes.mjs` `diffRows`, of the baseline and the file as pages see it): each unit a row, its key its kind and its own content (its source and a Sheet tab's panel, the units in it taken out, its ids too, white space as one space), the title a row of its own; the rows aligned on their keys by `commonRun` (past 2000 by 2000, the keys each side holds once first, the longest run of them in order, then each gap), and in each gap the rows of one block id, then of one kind that say much the same (`resembles`), paired in order as a block changed, the rest added or deleted (a block moved is both). `+N` is the blocks added and changed, `−M` those deleted and changed, as a diff counts a changed line twice: a container that only holds blocks, with no words of its own (a list, a quote, columns and a column, a flowchart, a Sheet area, a section, a plain `div`; read from what it holds, never from its kind's name), counts as the blocks it brought or took, each one, or as one when it brought or took none (put around blocks that stay, or taken from around them); resting on the numbers says how many were added, changed and deleted. `POST /__gallery/edit/changes` (`service.mjs` `changesRequest`): `read` (outside the commits' queue, of the file as the preview takes it) answers `{ pending: false, version }` or `{ pending: true, version, stamp, since, writes, counts, added: [{ ref }] (the outermost), changed: [{ ref, texts: [{ nth, base }] | whole: true }], title?: { base }, removed: [{ before | after: ref | into: "main", count, rows: [{ kind, tag, depth, text, marker?, number? }], more? }] }`, a block named as the page finds it in the file now (`{ id }` where the file has that id once, else `{ hash, nth }`, its `unitHash` and which of the units of that hash it is in the file's order, or `{ title: true }`), a text block of a unit by its order among the unit's own (`nth`, a list item holding blocks its own words alone), the last four read kept; in the commits' queue, never of a file half written, `accept` with the stamp read (`<since>|<writes>`: a commit of the reader's changes nothing of it) ends the review, the baseline gone and `seen` kept, 409 `changed` when a write grew it since (the page reads it again); `revert` writes the baseline as the file (the file as it was kept among the recent versions first, `expect`ing the file it read), report.json's title and icon following the page's (`inStep`, as a hand-in), and answers the id of an undo kept 10 minutes; with `agent: true` (`gallery revert`, `src/cli/revert.mjs`: `read`, then `revert` with the stamp read, read again once when a write grew the review meanwhile) the version it leaves is the Agent's, and it answers the blocks it took back (`changedBlocks` of the baseline and the file, 40 at most) and the version that holds what it wrote over (`kept`, which `gallery history --restore` brings back: the page's 撤销 is the tab's that reverted on the page); with nothing under review, a new document's starter filled among it, `gallery revert` writes nothing and says so (exit 1; until 2026-11-13 it restored a first writing's newest version no write of the Agent's made); `undo` writes back what the revert wrote over while the file is still the revert's (409 `moved-on` otherwise, 404 `gone` once used or old), and the review with it. Each tells the document's pages, as starting, growing or ending one does: the event stream's `changes <stamp>`. On the page (`page/changes/`, read as the editor starts and never holding it back, drawn once it is ready, so a page the workbench swaps in has its marks from the start), everything shows at once (the user's request of 2026-11-07: no card to rest the pointer on) and nothing of it reaches the file, a commit, an undo step, a copy, the search, the outline or the word count: a block added is filled green (`fills.mjs`, `.ge-diff-halo`, the outermost only, so a container the Agent put around blocks that were there is filled whole and they are not counted), as is a block changed with no words to tell (an attribute, a picture's address, a chart's or a component's data) or whose words cannot be compared (another count of text blocks, past 1500 words); in a block changed, its text read as the server reads it (`annotate/anchors.mjs` `readText`, a list item's own words alone) is compared word by word with the baseline's (`merge.mjs` `wordDiff`, `changes/words.mjs`): the words added get the 批注's look in green (a CSS highlight, `ge-diff-add`, under the 批注's own, its ranges live as the reader types), the words taken out stand where they were, in red struck through on the conflict card's red (`--ge-diff-del`, `--ge-diff-del-text`): before the character that follows them, or after the last one before them at a line's end or the block's (`changes/gone-words.mjs` `placeOf`), beside a formula or a chip and never inside one; the arrow keys pass one in a step, as a chip. Each is an empty inline span of the editor's own in the text block (`span.ge-diff-del`, its words in `data-ge-gone-words` drawn by its `::before`, a space at either end of them drawn apart, neither struck nor filled, where the text beside has none: `data-ge-gone-lead`, `-tail`, `contenteditable="false"`, `aria-hidden`, `data-doc-dev`, no pointer, no selection), put in from the block's last place to its first (splitting the text node it stands in: the selection and the green ranges follow) and owned as an overlay of `changes`, so a copy of one anywhere is a stray the audit takes off; a copy a change makes (whatever clones a block's content: a cell turned into a header cell or back, cells merged or filled, a disclosure's title made a paragraph, a block duplicated) is taken out at once as the change settles (`onSettle`, before the audit would say it is a stray), and the review read again after its commit draws the words taken out where they are then. It holds no text node, so whatever reads the block's words reads none of it (`textContent`, a selection's text, `readText`, the search, the outline, `matchesSource`, the server's word count from the file); what reads its markup leaves it out: `serializeInner` (`core.mjs` `serializeNodes`: any `data-doc-dev` element in a text block is no content, and the text on either side of one is written as one text, so a space split from the non-breaking one after it is still the one space it was), `ownHTML` (`GONE_WORDS`: what undo and redo record, so no step ever puts one back; a block deleted or turned into another has them taken out as it goes, `onSettle`, as undo puts that very element back), a copy (`clipboard/own/` drops `data-doc-dev`, a copy of the view too). The reader editing a block holding them takes them out first, before the edit touches it (listeners on the window, capturing, ahead of the editor's own): a key that may edit in it (any but the arrows, Home, End, Page Up and Down, Escape, the modifiers and F-keys, and Ctrl/⌘ with C, A, F, S or P: `editsBy`; an input method's Process key edits), `beforeinput`, `compositionstart`, `paste`, `cut`, `drop` or `dragstart` whose target is in it or whose selection reaches it (the browser's, or the page's across blocks, `text-select/`), and a press on the format bar over it; each goes with the text either side of it one text node again (`takeMark`, the caret kept where it was, and a selection across blocks on the same words: its ends are points of its own that a text node joined would leave off the page, so its pieces' live ranges are handed to `takeMark` and it is drawn again from them, `redraw`; so too while the review is read again and drawn anew with it held), the block's words added marked anew (a green range that ended in the half that went would cover other words), and the block is `edited`: drawn without them while the selection is in it, the review read again 150ms after the caret leaves it (once nothing waits to be saved) and drawn with them again. A block's words are compared only while the page holds them as the file does: a text block holding words of the reader's the file does not have (a change of it waiting, on its way, held back by a conflict card, or given up for the file's words the page does not show yet: what the commits' `collect()` finds of it) has none of its words marked, added or taken out, since the baseline compared with them would make the reader's words the Agent's (until 2026-11-17 the block of a conflict card about words both changed showed the reader's words green and those they replaced red struck through, the other way round from the card, and the Agent's words were nowhere on the page; scenario `agentChangesConflictMarks`); the card says what the Agent wrote there, the bar still counts the block (the Agent's change of it stands in the file), and its words are marked once they are saved (the review read again after the commit), shown as the file's (用文件里的, the pull) or, read with the caret in the block, as the caret leaves it (words undone back to the file's are saved by no commit). Beside every block marked green (filled, or with words of its own marked) stands a bar in the left margin, so that the eye runs down one margin and finds every place the Agent touched, a one-character edit too (the owner's pick of 2026-11-17: the bar and the fill both): 2px wide, round 1px, its right edge 8px left of the block's own edge whatever its fill reaches past it (10 to 8px left of words and of a card's frame alike, so that the bars of a run of blocks stand on one line: a card's, 4px left of its own fill, stood 4px further out than its neighbours', 2026-11-17), clear of the grip's box (10px left of the block) and of every fill (6px from a text block's, 2px from a card's), as tall as that fill, on a scaled screen its edges on device pixels and its width whole ones (`overlay-layer.mjs` `onDevice`, `deviceSize`: 2.4px at 125%, three crisp columns, where 2px from a whole CSS pixel was three soft ones; the ghost's red one the same, the ghost drawn on device pixels and its `::before` at `--ge-bar-at`, `--ge-bar-w`), so two neighbours' bars keep the hairline apart their fills keep (`fills.mjs` `createBars`, `.ge-diff-bar`, `--ge-diff-add-line`: a node of its own in the block's layer, cut above and below as the block is by a box that scrolls and never at its sides, where a fill's `::before` would be cut away by the fill's own clip-path). It stands beside the unit the review names (a list item, a table whole, a card, the title), the block whose fill it would be; a block whose words wait unsaved has none of its words marked and so no bar. A ghost has the same bar in red (its `::before`, `--ge-diff-del-line`). The +N switch takes the green bars with what they stand beside, −N the red ones with the ghosts. A write arriving while the page is open lights what it brought, once, and settles: what is under review now and was not as the page last drew it (`known`: a unit by the ref it was first seen by, kept by element, so that a ref by its hash that becomes one by its id once the reader edits the block is the same; a ghost by the words deleted) starts at `--ge-diff-add-arrive` / `--ge-diff-del-arrive` (about three times the resting tints on a light page, less on a dark one: 16% and 17%, where a proposed 26% green was too loud), holds a seventh of the time and eases to its resting tint over 1100ms (`cubic-bezier(.2, .8, .2, 1)`, `data-ge-arriving`, taken off once over so that a fill taken off the page and put back, in a Sheet not shown, plays nothing again); a unit whose words alone changed gets a fill of its own for that time (`.ge-diff-glow`, from the arrival's green to none: a CSS highlight cannot be animated), its words' marks as they are. Nothing replays: what was known stays known whatever is drawn again (the reread after a commit, a switch of the bar, the caret leaving a block, 显示变更 switched on, a 确认 refused); a redraw within the 1100ms goes on with the light where it was (a negative `animation-delay`); the page's first draw lights nothing (a document opened with changes under review), unless it is a page shown anew for someone else's write (the tab heard it less than 15s before, `shared/session.mjs` `heardKey`: what the page before it drew, kept in the tab's `sessionStorage` at each draw, is known); a reader who prefers reduced motion has the light too, as it is colour only, over 400ms with no hold (`ge-diff-*-still`); out of sight (another tab, the window minimized) what came is held and lit as the page shows again. The editor's frame loop does not count these animations as moving anything (`frames.mjs`: an animation in a `.ge-layer` moves no block), and `__galleryOverlays().changes` says how many bars there are and how many marks are arriving. On 确认 what is drawn fades out over 160ms ease-out before it is taken down (the fills, bars, glows, ghosts and words taken out: every node the module owns; the words' green goes at once, a CSS highlight cannot fade; at once for reduced motion, as the editor's fades are; the server is asked meanwhile); 撤回 takes it down at once. Where blocks were deleted their ghost stands where they were (`changes/ghost.mjs`): a block's words a line each in red struck through on a fainter red (`--ge-diff-del-fill`), a heading larger and bold, an item with its marker (its list's bullet, or its number) and a nested one further in, a table a line each row its cells apart by " · ", a block with no words its kind's name, past 50 blocks "…" (the server's `rows`, `source/changes.mjs` `ghostRow`). It is drawn in the overlay layer of the block after the place (`before`; at the document's end the last block, below it, `after`; with no block left the body's top, `into`), pointer-less, never a selection, a block, a drop target or part of a copy, in room made for it: a margin of that block (`margin-top`, `margin-bottom`, the body's `padding-top`) in a stylesheet of the editor's own (`style[data-ge-changes-room]`, for the screen alone, a rule keyed by the block's `data-ge-id`, `!important` to add to whatever the kit's more specific rules give it; never a style attribute, which the file would get), as tall as the ghost measured at the block's width with the gap the page had there (6–20px) above and below it (a margin that collapsed with the one above it made good by a second measure; that shift is measured in the page's own coordinates, the block's place less what the page and the boxes around it are scrolled: `ghost.mjs` `pageTop`, because with the reader scrolled past the place the browser keeps what is on the screen where it is by scrolling (scroll anchoring) as the margin is written, and a block pushed down measured in the window has not moved, the room taken for made short and made again, twice as tall, with 78px above the ghost and 12 below it). A block that stands beside others (in a row of a flex box, a grid of more than one column, a line: a Sheet's tab in its bar, a flowchart's node in its row, a column of 分栏) has the room made before the row it stands in instead, the ghost as wide as the row, since a margin of its own would push it out of line; and what stands at one place is one ghost in one room, the later group's lines after the earlier one's: two groups before one row (two tabs of a bar deleted), or one before a block and one before the first of its blocks that stays, standing level with it (the paragraph before a list and the list's first item: the inner margin would fold into the outer one's, the two ghosts drawn over each other). It is placed again with the overlays (the frames' `FOLLOW`), and the room made again when its height changes (the window narrowed); a block hidden (a closed disclosure, a folded heading's section, another tab) has neither. A block the page cannot find (a change of structure the reader has not saved yet) is counted and not drawn; one hidden (a closed disclosure, a folded heading's section, another tab) neither. Hidden is one rule for every fill and ghost: `extentOf` (`geometry.mjs`) finds no place for a block the page does not show, whether the browser gives it no rectangle (`display: none`: a folded heading's section, a Sheet tab not shown) or lays it out and gives it one it draws nothing in (a closed disclosure's content, which Chrome places under the title and over the blocks after the disclosure: `undrawn`, `checkVisibility()`, which the ghosts ask too), so a table the Agent added inside a closed disclosure is filled when the disclosure is opened and not before, and not over the section below it, while the disclosure's own title (its marks) and a disclosure the Agent added (filled whole, its table none of its own) show as ever; the frames follow the page, so opening or closing one moves its fills and ghosts at once (the 批注's and the selection's fills, the flags and the dashed frame read the same extent and follow the same rule). The bar (`changes/bar.mjs`, a third bottom panel, `panel.mjs`, in the middle of the page, 16px up like the others; a notice stands above it) stays on that bottom line beside the other panels (the user's 2026-11-14, over the picture of two writing at once: the conflict card at the left lifted it to stand over the card, up the page): where a conflict card at the left or the lost texts at the right would come within 8px of it across, it moves sideways, no further than it must (`barPlace`: the nearest place to the middle that clears each panel by 8px and the page's sides by 16px), so that it rests against the card, 8px from it, as the window narrows, follows the middle again with no jump once the middle clears the card, goes to the middle at once as the card is hidden (收起) and beside it again as it is shown, and stands between the two panels when both are open and there is room; only where no such place is wide enough for it (a phone, both panels in a window under about 1130px) does it stay in the middle and stand 8px above the panels under it. It is placed again as a panel comes, goes or changes size, as the window is resized and as a detail card holds the page still (a class on the root, `--doc-page-bar`: the page's scrollbar room narrows the page by that much on the right, the panels' and the bar's places with it), and tells the notices when it went up or down (`panelMoved`, since its top is part of the room a notice stands above); it says 「Agent 已修改 +N −M」, the green number and the red number each a switch (the user's request of 2026-11-11), with 撤回 and 确认 (primary), buttons of the editor's one button look (the Look item above: no line, no fill, `--ge-hover` under the pointer, `--ge-press` pressed, 8px corners), 确认 the pale blue primary (the workbench dialogs'). Its label, what the bar is, is in the text's ink, a 1px `--ge-line` hairline 18px high stands 10px from the switches and from 撤回; the bar is 44px high, its corners 14px (a card's, never a pill) and its shadow a bar's lighter one (`--ge-shadow-bar`), 撤回, 确认 and 撤销 32px high and the switches 26 (at least 38 wide), all 8px round (the finish of 2026-11-17; 40px high, 12px round, buttons 28 and switches 24 and 6px round until then), and its numbers take an ink deeper than the marks' on their fills (`--ge-diff-add-chip`, `--ge-diff-del-chip`, 4.9:1 and 4.7:1; the same on and off); what each switch, 撤回 and 确认 does is said by the editor's tooltip, not the browser's title, and to a screen reader by their names and descriptions; once placed it glides sideways (180ms) and fades in and out (a design review, 2026-11-17). 确认 ends the review, nothing said. 撤回 saves what waits first and does nothing but say so while something still waits, is offline or waits for the reader (a conflict); then the page is shown again as after a write of its own (`autosave.mjs` `showOwn`: no revert check, the reader asked for it; what the live-reload channel says meanwhile is its own write), and the bar there says 「已撤回 Agent 的修改」 with 撤销 for 6 seconds (the undo's id kept in `sessionStorage` over the page shown again, in the workbench too). A commit of the page's own that wrote, while the bar shows, reads the review again 1.5s after (later while the reader types on), and so does the event. The two numbers are switches (`bar.mjs`: buttons of the one button look with `aria-pressed`; on, a pale green and a pale red fill with the numbers in the green and red of the marks, `--ge-diff-add-btn*` and `--ge-diff-del-btn*` in `look.mjs`; off, no fill, the numbers still green and red (gray until 2026-11-12, when the user found them black), `--ge-hover` under the pointer; a number shows only while it counts something, so a review of deletions alone has the red one alone), both on until switched off and kept in the browser as the reader's preferences (`shared/preferences.mjs` `showChangesAdded` and `showChangesRemoved` in the one `gallery:preferences` key, as 显示变更 is, so no key of its own in `renamed-storage.mjs`; every page of the origin follows a press by the storage event, the marks drawn anew from the review the page holds with no new reading). Decided for the user: the green one is what the Agent added or changed, off, the fills and the words added are not marked (the content stays on the page, as the file has it); the red one is what it took out, off, the words struck through and the ghosts of the blocks deleted, with their rooms, are not shown; both off, the page shows the document alone, both on, everything. The counts, 撤回 and 确认 do not change with the switches, and a review stays under review whatever is shown (显示变更 off is what keeps it); a press touches neither the file, the review nor the undo steps (`changes/index.mjs` `draw`). 设置 › 偏好 › 显示变更 (显示修改差异 until 2026-11-07, renamed at the user's word; on by default, the first switch to be) off, nothing is drawn and a review the page reads is kept at once, quietly, so that switched on again it shows only what comes after; switched off while the bar shows, it is kept then, and with it the bar and its two switches go. Nothing of it on a page on its own in a frame (`?solo`), printed or presented. 历史版本's view of a recent version marks what it changed of the one before it in the same look, drawn by the same modules (`changes/version.mjs`: the fills and bars of `fills.mjs`, the words of `words.mjs`, whose `WORDS_STYLE` both use, and `gone-words.mjs`, the ghosts of `ghost.mjs`), with no bar, no arrival and none of the editor: "Recent versions", [workbench.md](workbench.md) "History".
- **Title.** The document's title may be empty: an **untitled** document, as the workbench's 新建 makes one (the user's of 2026-11-09), the caret in it as the page shows (`edit-title`). Empty, it says 未命名文档 in gray (`HOSTS.title` `untitled`, its `placeholder`: `typing/` `untitled()`, asked on every move of the caret, every input and every change, so an undo, a deletion across blocks or the workbench's renaming are followed), the caret in it or not, as an empty heading says 标题 1…4 and a disclosure's title 折叠列表标题 (a type's or a host's `untitled`; only under the caret until 2026-11-17: a blank bar at rest), while a paragraph, and the text in a callout or a highlight, say theirs only under the caret, as a heading's placeholder looks (the muted gray at 35%); never as text of the file: it is saved empty (`<h1 class="doc-title"></h1>`, `<title></title>`, `doc.json`'s `"title": ""`; the server writes the first title of an empty `<title>` whole, `source/text.mjs` `retitle`), no blank to hold back (it once said 文档主标题还空着). What names the document says 未命名文档 for it (workbench.md "The title is content"); the page reports its emptied title as `""` (`title: null` when it has none at all); a build and an export put 未命名文档 (Untitled for `lang="en"`) in an empty `<title>` (render's `untitledTitle`); `gallery check` takes an empty `<title>` when the `h1.doc-title` is empty too, and the pipeline reads a `doc.json` whose title is empty. **Enter** in the title goes to the body (`opensBody`: `typing/` `enterBody`): a selection in it goes first, what follows the caret leaves the title for a new paragraph right below it (before the header's first line when it has one, else first after the header), the caret at that paragraph's start; at the title's end the paragraph is empty, an untitled document's too; never a line break in the title. One undo step, which puts the caret back where it was (`struct`'s `caret`). The Enter that commits an input method's letters is not one (the keys wait while it composes). A selection across blocks may begin or end in the title ("Selecting text across blocks"). Committing a changed `h1` also replaces the old title text in `<title>` when it occurs there exactly once, and updates `report.json`'s `title` when it equals the old title (a targeted replacement that keeps the file's formatting). The answer's `notes` say it in the reader's words, never the files' (`service.mjs` `retitle`): 标题已更新, then what did not follow the title, named where the reader sees it (`<title>`: the page's name when it is opened on its own or exported; `report.json`: the name search and the sidebar show while the page is not open; in the workbench the tab and the sidebar show the open page's own title), when either already differed from the old title, and the page shows them as one sentence in the UI's language. `report.html` is written first; if `report.json` then cannot be written, the commit still succeeds, the note says the name in search and the sidebar did not follow, and the dev server's log says why (the title there has to be fixed by hand). A changed page icon is carried into `report.json` the same way (`reicon`; a failure: 页面图标已更换，但搜索和侧栏里（没打开这份文档时）的图标没能跟着换). An Agent's `gallery set` changes a title or an icon in the header and `doc.json` alike (`headerChanged()` in `source/natural.mjs`, render.md "gallery set"), not through the edit service: to a page it is a write of the Agent's like any, shown and merged as one, and the review of the Agent's changes counts it (the editor's own renaming is the reader's, `retitledBase`).
- **Writing.** The new text goes to a sibling temp file that is renamed over the document, retried briefly on `EPERM`/`EBUSY`/`EACCES` (Windows locks), and removed on failure. Agents keep writing `report.html` directly, so right before each rename attempt `writeAtomic(file, text, { expect })` reads the file again, and if it no longer holds the text read for the commit (the commit is applied to that text as pages see it: "Natural HTML"), it writes nothing; the commit is then applied again to the file as it is now, up to three times (`busy` after that, nothing written, and the page tries again later). This keeps direct writes and shrinks the window in which one of them could be overwritten to the moment between that read and the rename. An export's write (the hand-in, "Natural HTML") goes through the same queue and check while the preview server runs, so it never races a commit. It is not a lock: a writer that does not go through the service can still land in that moment, and the service gives no strict guarantee against it. **When the page takes the file.** An Agent changes a structure in several edits (an edit replaces one stretch of the file), and between two of them the file is half written: a disclosure's `<details>` written before its `</details>`, a section's end cut before the section is put back, a Sheet area's tabs before their panels. The preview takes no such file (`settle.mjs`). It is half written when `halfWritten()` (`source/whole.mjs`) finds a sign of it: no `<main>` in the file (a write caught between emptying it and filling it, or before the head is done, which would otherwise be taken as a page of nothing, every 批注 deleted with its words gone), a block element (`main`, `section`, `article`, `aside`, `nav`, `header`, `footer`, `div`, `details`, `dialog`, `figure`, `blockquote`, `table`, `ul`, `ol`, `dl`) whose end tag is not in the file, an end tag of one that closes nothing (read on parse5's tree with source locations: an element its own end tag closed has it, and every other end tag of those names in the markup is one the parser dropped), or a component whose parts that go together do not pair one to one (its description's `pairs`: each Sheet tab its panel, as `sheets.js` asks); never an element whose end tag may be left out (`p`, `li`, `td`…: a browser closes it as its author meant), and nothing in a comment, an attribute's value, a script, a style, code (`pre`, `code`, `kbd`, `samp`, `var`), a textarea, a template or a drawing. While it is held, its pages are not told of it (they keep what they show); a page's load waits for it (`whenTaken`: 10s at most) and is then served what the preview last took of it, which the document's other pages show (`readTaken`; a server started since, which has taken none of it, serves the file as it is); 复制页面内容 reads that at once; a commit and the revert check wait for it outside the queue (what else runs in it goes on), look again once in it, and after 10s in all are answered `busy` with nothing written (the page tries again later, as after any busy answer), while a commit sent again whose answer is kept gets that at once; and the reader's hand-in (the workbench's 导出, the export dialog: the dialog's waits for it outside the queue first) is answered `busy` with nothing written. So no page shows a half-written file but after a restart, no commit or hand-in of the reader is applied to one, and no snapshot or recent version of one is kept. It is taken once it is whole, or once it has not been written for 30s (an Agent's next edit of a change comes sooner, however long the change takes as a whole: four edits over 29s were seen), or has been held for 120s from the write that made it so (a file that stays so is its author's, odd as it is, and the checks speak of it); the signs it was taken with then hold that document no more, while a new one still does (a sign is told from another of its kind by its tag as written: a `<div class="wrap">` opened where a taken `<div class="own">` was closed holds it), and a file the server first sees half written long after it was written is taken at once. Its pages are told of a write once the file is taken and has not changed for 1.5s (an Agent's next edit usually follows within it: a section moved in two edits leaves a whole file between them), and at once when the edit service wrote it (a commit, the hand-in, a rename: the service's own writes are whole, and nothing follows them); `src/server/announce.mjs` looks at a held file again every second at least (a change no watcher event said is found then), and a change that comes while it looks at an earlier one is looked at in its own time, never put off by the earlier look's hold. (`tests/unit/settle.mjs`, the browser scenario `agentStepwise`.)
- **Never exported.** The CLI export never runs the preview plugin. The browser "导出当前视图副本" control (view copy) removes `[data-doc-dev]` nodes and, on elements carrying `data-ge-*` markers (or `data-rs-*`, their name before 2026-10-28, which a file may keep), those markers and `contenteditable`; a document's own `contenteditable` is kept (and never becomes an editor block).
- **Where the reader is** (`page/focus.mjs`, for `gallery status`; docs/workbench.md "Status"). `watchFocus` reads the caret's block (`unitElement` of the editable text the selection is in: its `data-block-id`, null while the file has none, and its words by `readText`, the annotations' reading) or what is selected (the blocks selected whole; text across blocks; words in one block, by `textSelection`, the annotations' own, else the browser's words in the block they start in), 250ms after the last `selectionchange`, press, key or focus event, and tells the workbench (`focus`) only when it differs from the last it told. Words are cut to 200 characters there, before they leave the page; nothing is read in a page of its own in a frame (`?solo`).
- **Workbench.** In a same-origin iframe the page speaks the session protocol of [`workbench.md`](workbench.md) (`session.mjs`): it sends `ready`, `state` (its title; editing, something waiting, a commit on its way, offline, conflicts, changed outside) and `notice` (what it tells the reader, shown as the workbench's toast, above the page's bottom panels: `noticeRoom` tells the room they take as it changes, `panelRoom()` of `page/panel.mjs`, a `ResizeObserver` on each panel; on a page alone its own toast follows the bar of the Agent's changes instead (`notices.mjs` `place`, `panel.mjs` `barPlace`, since 2026-11-17: the owner's rule for the bar of 2026-11-14): on the bottom line, moved sideways no further than it must to clear a card by 8px, 8px above the bar where the two would meet, over the cards only where no place beside them is wide enough (a narrow window); the workbench's toast, told only the room, still stands above them (its half waits for the workbench to be told the panels' places); while panels stand, the page's `scroll-padding-bottom` is the room they take and 8px (a sheet of the editor's own), so the caret followed after a key, and `reveal()`, keep the line typed on above them; both toasts are as wide as their words, at most 640px and the window less 32px, in the middle of the window, the words wrapping past that; with 设置 › 减少通知 on, `page/quiet.mjs` leaves out the notices of everyday operations, as docs/workbench.md "Preferences" lists, and `notices.mjs` a `hint`, what a click on text that cannot be edited says and its hover hint says too) and answers `save` (as Ctrl+S), `prepare-close` (it commits what waits, for a second at most, and always lets the page go: the rest is in the crash buffer), `export` (once what waits is saved), `edit-title`, `set-title` (the title renamed from the workbench's sidebar, a step of its own) (`COMMANDS` in `editor.mjs`). It never sends content. A page on its own in a frame (`?solo`: the 写作偏好 dialog's) speaks no session and shows its own notices; the frame's owner saves it through `window.__galleryEditFlush()` (whatever waits commits now, quietly; it resolves with Ctrl+S's outcome, `saved` or `nothing` when all of it is in the file). `/__gallery/workspace/*` (`src/workbench/service.mjs`) handles whole reports: its writes use the edit service's checks and queue, it keeps nothing of its own but the outline (`.gallery/outline.json`), and the trash is `.gallery/trash/` (listing skips dot entries). `tests/unit/workspace.mjs` covers the commands; `workbenchSession`, `workbenchLeave` and `workbenchGuard` in the browser suite cover the session.
- **Trust.** The preview page runs the document's own scripts next to the editor, on the same origin as the commit endpoint; such a script could read the token (it is in the served page) and commit. The Host/Origin checks and the token keep other sites and pages out, not the document's own code: the live preview assumes a trusted local project, as the documents an Agent writes for its user are. A deleted unit comes back only as the server sealed it ("Commits"), so undo cannot be used to write markup the server did not take out.
- **Icons.** The editor's icons are its own plain line drawings: 20 units, round caps, a line 1.25 wide, built by `svg()` in `components/icons.mjs` (the toolbar's are `format-bar/bar.mjs` ICONS; the table tools' `table/icons.mjs`; the picker's `emoji-picker/icons.mjs`; the bookmark, file, embed and media toolbar icons are each component's `icons.mjs`; 分栏 is `columnsIcon(2)`). One icon is one command: 替换 (an image's, a file's, an embed's) is `ICONS.swap`, two arrows one each way, and 恢复默认大小 `ICONS.reset`, an arrow turning back (`menu.mjs`; one arrow stood for both in an embed's menu until 2026-11-17). A drawing carries no size, line or color of its own: whoever shows it sets them (a menu row, the toolbar's 24px button, the handle, a gray bar, the picker: `--ge-picker-icon`), so the placeholder bars need no `!important` and a bar's class comes from `barClass`. The kit draws its own too (code.js copy, check and wrap; the file row's two masks in `styles/file.css`, the same drawings as the editor's `FILE_ICONS.upload` and `download`; the to-do check). `tests/unit/own-icons.mjs` holds every path under `src/` against fingerprints of drawings none may match (`tests/unit/fixtures/icons/`). The 图标 tab's icons are two open-source sets, MingCute (Apache-2.0) and Tabler (MIT), their licenses beside them (`src/shared/emoji/data/`). A heading's fold triangle is `FOLD_ARROW` of `src/shared/code-folds.mjs`, the shape of a code line's fold and a disclosure's arrow. One drawing is not the editor's own: a page with no icon (`PAGE_ICON`: the "/" menu's 页面, an empty page block's chip, a page in a search's rows) is Lucide's plain file outline redrawn in 20 units, as the workbench (`components/icons.ts` `page`), the kit's page block (`mention.css`) and the browser tab draw it (2026-11-06); Lucide's ISC license is beside the icon sets' (`src/shared/emoji/data/LICENSE-lucide.txt`) and in THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. The icons' darker gray (`--ge-ico`) is the workbench's `--icoPri`, the warm dark gray the user chose for the workbench's icons on 2026-11-06 (`#5c5a55`).
- **Known limits.** Supported: desktop Chromium browsers (Chrome, Edge). Chinese IME input has been checked by hand on Windows; Safari/Firefox, macOS input methods and touch devices have not. Charts, dialog triggers, script-filled text and code drawn by a script are not editable. Blocks can be deleted, moved anywhere their kind may go, resized (cards and tables), copied and pasted, and made or turned into the types above (tables, formulas, code, images, files, bookmarks, embeds and Sheet areas are made from the "/" menu or a paste; a chart card is not made on the page); a code block keeps its type and its language is picked in its header's menu or told from its title and its code, and a block the document's script refers to keeps its type. Units a document's script refers to (by id, say: a card whose chart it asks for with `DocCharts.get("#id")`) can move but cannot be deleted on the page; markup refers to nothing (every chart is declared, linkage goes by bound keys), so in a document whose script only defines chart types every block can go.
