Editing a card title does not change the chart's own accessible `title`, and a table's sort arrows stay while it is edited (the kit reads its numeric columns and keeps the arrows in line with its headers as its cells change; an arrow pressed sorts the rows as an edit). The Agent's changes, in their first phase: a deleted block keeps no room where it was (a red line and its card only); a block moved is shown deleted and added; what changed in a hidden tab, a closed disclosure or a folded heading's section is counted, not drawn, and nothing leads to it; a change of `<head>` or of styles is not shown; a hand edit in another editor counts as the Agent's; what the reader typed in a block the Agent added, or over words it changed, counts as the Agent's and goes with a revert; a baseline kept while Gallery's rules of natural HTML change may show a few more changes than the Agent made.

## Annotations

批注 are the reader's suggestions for a local Agent, kept in `<workspace>/<slug>/annotations.json` by the edit service (`editor/annotations.mjs`, never `doc.html`); what the page and the server share is `src/shared/annotations.mjs`, held by `tests/unit/annotations.mjs`. The workbench's button lists those there are (docs/workbench.md "Annotations").

- **Always there.** `page/annotate/` runs from the editor's start on every page of a session (not a page on its own in a frame, `?solo`: nothing would show them). What is there is read from the server right after the page says `ready` (the page's start never waits for it, and what waits in the browser goes then), its cards or markers and the marks on its words are drawn from then on (those it starts with just stand; one that comes later fades in), and the page follows the words through the reader's edits and tells the server before commits. A page with none has no layer, no observer and no pointer listener, only the listener for the server's word that the file changed; the observer comes with the first annotation, the layer and what follows the page (scroll, resize, the pointer) stand while something is drawn. The editor's own keys, presses and clipboard leave the cards and markers alone (`holds`), and the card's keys are a field's (Ctrl+Z its own and, past what the field can undo, nothing: Chrome has one undo stack for a page, and `onBeforeInput` keeps it off the page's words; Ctrl+S saves the annotation and the document). While a card is being written (it has the focus, or what was typed waits to be saved) the page is not shown again for someone else's write (autosave's `holding`: an Agent writing the document meanwhile shows once the card closes), so no key typed in it is lost.
- **Cards in the margin.** With room at the column's right (`margin.mjs`, "The room of 批注" below: `margin().cards`, a card's width) every annotation the page shows stands as a card there, between the column and the outline, and its words are edited right in it: a field and the delete button are all a card is, no author, no time, no replies, no resolving; the delete button shows only where it can be used (under the pointer, the keys in the card, the card in front; always where nothing hovers: a design review, 2026-11-17). The layout is `cardTops` (pure, `shared/annotations.mjs`; `CARD` its measures): by place on the page (those level with each other, the one made first first), each 18 above its anchor (its first line's top, `lineOf`; one nearer the page's top stays there), pushed down by the one above with 10 between them; the one in front, pushed down, pulled back beside its anchor, those touching before it going up with it and all those after it following. `top` moves over 350ms from the second layout on, never on the first paint (`data-moves` comes a frame after the first layout); under the last are 50px to scroll it up to, so cards past the page's end lengthen the page. A card's left is 10px right of where the column is going (`MARGIN.lane`, where a marker stands: the user's 2026-11-12, who found 60 and then 32 too far), not where a slide has it now; its width 308, or what the room leaves, down to 180 (a card as narrow as the page leaves it rather than a marker, the user's); its field grows with its text up to 220px and scrolls past that. The looks: at rest a 1px `--ge-line` border, radius 14 (`--ge-r-card`, a card's since the finish of 2026-11-17), the page's own background (`--doc-bg`); under the pointer a shade (`--ge-note-shade`); in front no line, `--ge-shadow-card` and `--ge-bg`. None goes left of its place (a card moved 5px left under the pointer and 20px in front, from 10px out, would reach the text). A card stands **in front** while its field has the focus, or from a press on its marked words (the caret staying in them, the words being for editing; a press that selects words brings nothing) or the workbench's list (`goTo`), until a press elsewhere (`pressed`, `clicked`), in the page or in the workbench (the frame's blur; after the list, whose row leaves the focus in the workbench, its word `away`); a press on what stands over the words is not on them (`markedBy`). Its words light up, its blocks fill. A card under the pointer lights its words and fills its blocks too. A press begun out of the margin leaves the cards out of the pointer's way until it moves with no button down (`data-pressed`), so a selection dragged over one shows no hover and lights nothing. A press in a card places the caret where pressed, or beside the field at the end of its text, and brings what it is about into the window where that is out of it (64px in, smoothly). **The button beside a block** stands where a marker would (`markerSpot`: 10px right of the column, 28 to 52 past a section's strip), in the cards' lane: it shows only where no card stands on its place (`markerOn` counts the cards in the margin as the markers; such a block keeps 批注 in its menu and Ctrl+Alt+M), and none while a card stands in front, the handle taking the block's whole width. **A new one** opens in the margin beside its anchor, 14 further down, in front and focused, where with no room it opens at the format bar's place or below its blocks. Its keys are a card's (below), the caret going back to its words. **A card opened for writing** (a new one, or Ctrl+Alt+M on marked words) whose place is out of the window, as with a caret at the end of a long block, blocks selected across screens or a long selection, stands 64px into the window instead from its first frame (`standWithin`): the page moves for neither it nor its first key, and once closed it goes beside its anchor. A block anchor keeps its bar. In a closed disclosure a card stands by its title; in a tab not shown there is none until it shows (it still counts and keeps the room); in a detail card's dialog, where the margin is not the page's, there is none. None of it is printed, or in 演示视图 or an export. **The form follows each width at once**, as the window's edge or the sidebar's drag goes, with no fade and no move: the cards go into markers and back, a card being written stays the same node, so its field, caret, undo and an input method's letters stay, by its words with no room; a card only in front goes. **A page with many stays quick**: nothing is measured or written that a key did not change. Words typed in a block measure only the annotations found again in it (`stale`); the page's size or structure changing (`resizer`, `observed`) measures all of them, and so does the size of a block in what takes its height from the tallest beside it (a unit's `sharesHeight`: a column of 分栏, a flowchart's step), which keeps that from the page's height; a key in a card writes its own field's height and lays out the others only when that height changed; a card's style is written only when it differs, and the marks are drawn again only when something of them changed, a `Highlight` made anew repainting them all. With a hundred, keys in a card or in the page take 1 to 2ms and no frame reaches 50ms. On what a folded heading hides (`fold/`), the card stands by the folded heading's line, as by a closed disclosure's title.
- **Starting one.** Nothing is offered by a selection alone; one is always started on purpose. **The format bar's 批注**: the last row of its "更多" list, after 详情卡片, its shortcut beside it; the row shows only where the selection can be annotated (`canSelection()`; elsewhere the list has no such row). It works for a selection across blocks (text-select/ lets its strips go once the card has it), where the list's 链接 and 详情卡片 are off and it is not, and a press keeps the selection until the card has taken it. A code block has no format bar: words in it are annotated by Ctrl+Alt+M, the block by the button beside it. **The button beside a block**: with the pointer right of the middle line of the block hovered (left of it the handle shows as ever: the line at 50%, what stands there staying until the pointer is 3px past the line, so that one resting on it leaves it; up to the 150px the hover reaches, `geometry.mjs hoverAt`), neither grip nor bar shows and a 24×24 button does (`handle/index.mjs`: radius 6, our bubble with a plus at a marker's 18px in the grip's quiet gray, the grip's fill under the pointer, tooltip 批注). It and a marker are one thing in two states: it stands exactly where a marker of that block alone stands (`markerSpot`: `markerAcross` from the column and `markerTop` on the block's first line, the clamps a marker has, shared with `draw`), so a press and a sentence leave the marker on the button's pixel. For what is in a section, a marker on its words too and one of blocks that run into a section from before it, that place is right of the section's strip, as far out as the pointer finds the strip (`handle-sizes.mjs` `BAR.reach`, 28px out where the column's 14px would stand on it; where a Sheet panel's padding ends the strip short of the 14px, the 14px), kept in the column's right room (`margin.mjs` `rightRoom`: beside the outline in full at its tightest, 24px out, still clear of the strip). The strip moves for nothing, and takes the pointer at every height, the button's too. The way from the block's right edge to the button keeps the block at the heights where it is that block's, across the strip (`onWay`, as `inGutter` at the left), so it stands still for a pointer going to it. Typing and the wheel hide it as they hide the handle; a box selection shows neither; a block dragged keeps the handle it holds, with no button; with a menu open the button does not show. A block that cannot take an annotation, and one standing beside another, keeps the handle across its whole width and has no button (`registry.mjs standsBeside`, asked of the block and what holds it: a unit's `beside`, a row of columns, or its `inRow(element)`, a flowchart's node when a node shares its line). **A block that has an annotation** (`annotated`: any whose blocks include it, of its words or of it whole; a container's first line counts its first block's) is one that cannot take one, one rule: no button, the caret in it no target of the shortcut; its marker is how to reach what is there, and words in it can still be annotated from the format bar. **A block whose button's place a marker stands on** (`markerSpot` says no: a marker stacked down from the block above, a closed disclosure's on its title for a block in it, a section's on its heading) has no button either, the pointer there being that marker's, and keeps the handle across its whole width; the shortcut still annotates it. **Several blocks**: with blocks selected the pointer right of the middle line of any of them that has no annotation shows the button beside it, and a press (the selection kept until the card has taken it) makes one annotation of all of them, those already annotated too (`canBlocks`: blocks that can be, one at least with none yet; not the whole page). A button beside a block outside the selection annotates that block alone. The block menu has no 批注: the button and the key are how blocks are annotated. **Ctrl+Alt+M** (⌘⌥M): the words selected, else the blocks selected, else the caret's block, else, a block with an annotation taking none, the card of the marked words the caret is in; with nothing to annotate the page leaves the key to the workbench, where it opens or closes the list. Not on a caret in the title, whitespace alone, a tab's name, a field of the editor's own, anything in a detail card's dialog (v1), or the whole page (select-all/'s top step: every block of it). Words of an annotation already there show its card rather than a second one (`sameAs`). With no room for cards in the margin the card opens with the caret in its field: words have it where the format bar stood (`placeBox` as the bar's: its left 4px right of their last character, 8px below their last line, above the words where the window has no room below, 12px inside the window), blocks at the last one's bottom right (8px below it, its right edge on the block's), at the window's bottom where that bottom is below the window (the visible part of a long block; 70px up, above the notices there: the workbench's toast 24px up and about 37px tall), above the blocks where even their top is too low for it; always inside the column (280px wide, or the column's width), never in the gutter. One card shows at a time: a card being written closes before another starts. Text and blocks selected give way to the annotation's own mark (their selection let go, its line in the status gone).
- **Writing.** While it is written the words keep a warm mark (`--ge-note-hot`), blocks the fill selected blocks have in a warm colour, and a card by its words keeps the side it opened on, below or above its words or blocks (below blocks counting the window's bottom it stood at by a long one): growing as it is written, it grows from where it stands and never jumps over them, and it is held in the window (scrolled away, or growing at the window's bottom, it stays inside it with the caret, so typing never scrolls the page). An annotation has words: one with none is none (the user's word of 2026-11-06, a card opened by a slip having been kept as a mark; this takes back what a change of 2026-11-02 allowed, a plain mark of a place). A new one is no annotation until something is typed in it (white space is nothing): no marker, no count, nothing in the file, and it goes with its words or blocks should they go first; left with nothing typed (a press elsewhere, Enter, Esc, Tab, Ctrl+Alt+M, the caret going, the page closing) it leaves nothing, and Ctrl+S saves nothing of it (`drafted`: made on the page, its text white space at most). A field holds what a note holds, 20000 characters (`TEXT_MOST`). Behind a modal dialog nothing can take the focus, so nothing is begun. Typing saves 500ms after the last key, and at once when it closes or the page goes (`prepare-close`, `pagehide` with `keepalive`) (never a text with no words); the letters of an input method's composition are not saved, the phrase it puts in is. What could not be saved (offline) waits in the browser with the time it was typed and goes the next time a page reads them; a later save or delete of it that reaches the server takes it out, and the server never puts an older text over a newer one (`restore` compares `updated`). An error is said once (批注没能保存). Enter (not Shift+Enter, never while an input method composes), Esc, Tab and Ctrl+Alt+M (the keys that opened it close it) close it with the caret back at the end of its words, collapsed, or at the end of its last block where the keys opened it; a card of blocks the pointer opened (the button, a marker) had no caret to give back, so it places none and the page stays where it is; a press elsewhere closes it too. A saved one emptied in its card is deleted as the card closes (by any of those), as by its delete button: `annotations-removed` with it as the file last held it (`filed`: its last words), the workbench's toast offering it back; while the card is open a text with no words is never saved (the file keeps its last words, so retyping changes nothing there), and the page going with it emptied deletes nothing (no 撤销 could be offered). A card has no send, so an emptied card closed says no words. Besides, the **delete button** at its right deletes it, or 全部删除, or its words or blocks going. The button stands on the field's first line (24×24, radius 6, the menus' trash in quiet gray, their fill under the pointer, tooltip 删除批注), on every card, one with nothing written too: the field stops short of it, so text never runs under it. A press on it moves nothing, the card as it was and its field keeping the focus; a click deletes the annotation at once, the card and the marker going and a caret that was in the card going back to its words, and what was typed and not saved yet is not saved. The page says `annotations-removed` with the item, and the workbench shows 全部删除's toast, whose 撤销 puts it back; one that was never saved has nothing to bring back and says nothing. Closed, with room it goes back among the cards; with none it is a **marker** (a grey speech bubble, round with its lower left a corner, filled, two lines cut out of it, 24px) in the column's right gutter, at its first line, or at the middle of its blocks when they are several. Resting 150ms on a marker (only a pointer that moved onto it: one drawn under a still pointer opens nothing) shows its card **where it was written**: by its words, where the format bar would stand, or at its blocks' bottom right, the words lit with the warm mark (blocks, their fill) and the marker with them, so it is always the same card in the same place. It stays while the pointer is over the card or its marker, and while the pointer is on its way from the marker to the card (nearer to the card than when last looked, every 300ms); otherwise it goes 300ms after the pointer has left both. Resting on another marker while a card shows by hover switches at once. A click on a marker edits its annotation (the caret at the end of its text), the card staying until a press elsewhere. A card being written is one whose field has the focus (`shown.focus` follows the field's focus, also when another marker is clicked while one is written and the field keeps it): no hover takes it over or hides it, and every key goes to the annotation it shows. The pointer over marked words makes them and their marker hot. The mark is a faint fill under a 2px underline (`--ge-note`, `--ge-note-line`), so it is never taken for the document's own yellow background.
- **The markers.** With no room for cards they stand in the column's own right gutter (the 64px the handle's room keeps at both sides while editing), 14px right of the column, 5px right of a block anchor's bar (right of a section's strip, 6 to 22px out, for what is in that section, as the button beside a block stands; a table's corner +, 2 to 20px out, stands under the table, clear of the markers on its lines, a marker stacked onto it leaving it the 12px left of the marker; at the column's edge, inside it, on a page whose gutter is narrower), each on its first line (a picture standing as a block has it at its top, as the handle, not by its caption) or, for an annotation of several blocks, at their vertical middle, from the first's top to the last's bottom, following them as they change height, and stack without overlapping (`stackItems`: the first at its line, the next pushed down, 4px apart, nothing above the page's top room and nothing past the page's end, 16px short of it), in a layer of their own at the page's origin (z level `note`: over the blocks, the fills and the handle, under the format bar, menus and cards; the card is drawn in it too, over the text; none of it, nor the marks on the words, is printed). Annotations whose lines are within 4px of each other in different columns of 分栏 stand the column further right first (its marker beside its own line, the left column's after it). With no room for their cards the page's right side (`page/margin.mjs`) stands as it would with none: one coming, or a page opened or swapped in with them, moves neither the column nor the outline (目录), in full or as its strip. A block anchor has a 2px bar 7px right of its blocks (left of a section bar's line), and its blocks are filled while its card shows, by the painter that fills selected blocks (`page/fills.mjs`): one fill per block, with the reach, the corners and the joins between neighbours selecting those blocks gives, a list's markers inside it, only the colour its own (`--ge-note`). In a closed disclosure (its content laid out though hidden) the marker, bar and card stand at its title's line and the fill is the disclosure's, as selected; in a tab not shown, or a detail card's content, nothing is drawn (it still counts). In a folded heading's section the marker, bar and card stand at the heading's line and the fill is the heading's, as for a closed disclosure.
- **Anchors.** `{ kind: "text", blocks, quote, prefix, suffix }` or `{ kind: "blocks", blocks, quote }`: the `data-block-id`s of the blocks it is in (one block, or the blocks right inside the one holding both ends of a selection across blocks; a cell's words its table; the header's lines under the title too, never the title or the page's icon), its words (2000 characters at most; a block anchor's text cut at 200, for the copy) and up to 32 characters before and after them. A block's text is read alike on both sides (`treeText` from the parsed file, `anchors.mjs readText` from the page with where each character is): its words, a formula as the file writes it (its TeX in `\(…\)`, or `\[…\]` for one shown apart, `$…$` read so too), so a quote can be found in the file, the blocks in it a line each, white space as one; what the kit or the editor adds is no part of it. Texts are compared with their white space out.
- **Finding them again** (`findQuote`, `locateAnchor`): the quote in its blocks' text as it is (of several, the one whose prefix and suffix agree most). Else, edited in part: the shortest stretch keeping the most of its characters in their order, kept while that is at least half of them, the stretch no longer than one and a half times the quote (a character typed between costs a tenth of one kept), **and standing where the quote stood**: the last 3 characters of its prefix still right before it and the first 3 of its suffix right after it (a few characters between allowed, up to half the quote's length, for words that replaced its first or last ones), or the text's edge where it had nothing before or after it. So a quote deleted never moves onto other words sharing its characters (a parallel sentence beside it: Chinese text shares many), and one edited where what is around it was rewritten too is taken as gone. A quote under 10 characters must be found as it is. Split by Enter in its middle: its words as they are across its blocks and the one right after them, begun in its own (not the same words further on in a container after it); its blocks are then both. None of its blocks left, its words found exactly once in the document, in the innermost block holding them. Found otherwise than it was, the anchor is rewritten to what the file holds now (so the copy quotes the file). This strict rule is the server's, for writes the page did not make (an Agent's, another tab's).
- **Done ones** (`done: true`, set by `gallery notes done` through the annotations route; `markItems`, `withoutItems`, `unknownIds` in `shared/annotations.mjs`, `createAnnotations`' `done` / `undone` / `removeIds` / `removeDone`). An Agent says it has acted on one; the reader sees it quiet and deletes it or all of them (docs/workbench.md "Annotations"). On the page the card of a done annotation is drawn at 55% (`data-done` on the card, set by `cardOf`; in front, while the reader reads or edits it, whole), its marker at 60% and its bar at 40%, and its words are left out of the `ge-note` highlight unless it is the one in front (`paintMarks`). A done mark is the Agent's word about words the reader wrote: the reader rewriting the suggestion makes a new one, so a `put` keeps `done` only while its text is the same (the page saving unchanged words after a card was opened keeps it); `restore` brings it back with the item. 复制批注 leaves done ones out. An id the request names that no annotation has refuses the whole request (404 `ids`). `by: "command"` on a removal (`gallery notes remove`) keeps what it took for two minutes (`commandRemoved` in every answer until a `put` or a `restore`): a page whose `take` meets one it has not told (by its `at`) says `removed(items)` as for a card's delete button, so the workbench offers 撤销 (docs/workbench.md "Annotations"). `createAnnotations({ verify: false })` is the store `gallery notes` uses with no server running: it reads and writes the file as it is and never checks an annotation against the document (nothing deleted, rewritten or reordered by looking), reading the document only to say where each one is.
- **Following the reader's edits** (`page/annotate/`). On the page each annotation's words are a live `Range`: it grows with what is typed into them and shrinks with what is deleted of them. An annotation is found, in this order: its words as they are, as the page last found them, as the file says, or as they were earlier in this visit (an undo puts them back so: up to 12 anchors kept); else where the live range took them (`tighten`: from its first character of text to its last), so words typed into it or deleted from it (a short quote's too) keep it; else by the server's rule. It is gone on the page only when nothing of its words is left (the range empty: they were deleted, or replaced by typing over them) or its blocks were removed. What the page found is told to the server **right before the commit that writes the edit** (`POST { slug, anchors: { id: anchor } }`, autosave's `before`, waited for 1.5s at most; only when its blocks, its quote or the ends of its context the check reads changed: `sameWords`), so the check after that commit finds the words as they are and keeps the annotation; a page going away tells it with `keepalive`.
- **Gone with its words.** The check (`checkAnnotations`) runs on the server after the document's file is written by anyone (the preview's announcement: a commit, an Agent, another tab), 300ms after the last write of a burst, in the annotations' own queue (`notes` in `service.mjs`: never ahead of a commit; their reads and writes go through it too), and before every answer; at once (`settleNotes()`, the one waiting taken instead) before the server answers an export's hand-in and when `gallery check` or `gallery qa` asks (`POST /__gallery/edit/settle`), so a command run right after the Agent's write says what it deleted (docs/render.md "Build pipeline"); it deletes what is no longer found, rewrites what moved, keeps page order, and writes the file only when that changed something (none left: no file). A document's parse is kept while its text stays the same. On the page an anchor is found again after a change of the page touching its blocks or its words (a frame later; a block's id given or a block moved: all of them): one no longer found loses its mark and marker at once. One the check deleted whose words the page had lost and brings back during the same visit (an undo) is saved again as it was; after a reload it is gone for good. What the check deleted is said, never only gone (an Agent that rewrote the words saw it vanish and said it had left it be): `createAnnotations({ dropped })` tells the edit service, which says it in the dev server's log and keeps it (`dropped-annotations.mjs`) for the Agent's next `gallery check` or `gallery export` to say once, each annotation's id, text and quote (docs/workbench.md "Annotations").
- **Ids.** An annotation's first save names its blocks: `commits.idFor` gives the id a block has in the file, or the one the page proposes for it, which the next commit (alone when nothing else waits: `touch`) writes into its start tag and nothing else. A commit can be slower than the reader, and a new annotation can also be about words typed a moment ago: until the file holds what it is about, its words and all its blocks, the annotation is pending, not gone. The server keeps it as it was sent, whatever is saved or written meanwhile, its blocks all there for the page and for 复制批注 (`waiting` in `editor/annotations.mjs`, for what a page saves new and what waited in the browser, in its memory, 10 minutes at most: a commit that never comes leaves an annotation of nothing, which goes as any other); from the check that finds it whole it is judged as any other. One the file has already is kept for the save alone.

## Components

Each kit component the editor knows has a folder in `src/editor/components/`: `paragraph` (文本, 小字), `heading`, `list`, `quote`, `callout`, `highlight`, `details`, `code`, `divider`, `section`, `sheets`, `table`, `figure`, `columns`, `math`, `dialog`, `icon` (the page's icon, and a highlight block's), the media blocks `image`, `file`, `bookmark` and `embed` (what they share is `media/`: see "What the four media blocks share") `widget` (a document's own block) and `installed` (an instance of a custom block: "Instances of custom blocks" below). A folder holds up to three modules (and what only it uses: a table's grid and slots, a list's numbers and joining, an image's crop and viewer, the Sheet area's pills):

- **`index.mjs`: what it is.** Its unit kinds (the rules an element is recognized by, ranked, first match winning; whether it is a chapter, a card, never inside a flow; its sizes and class switches), its box kinds and their roles, its component classes (one block, nothing in or out), its kinds of text block and what each can do (the server's capabilities), and the block types it offers (tag and class, what a block turned into it becomes, its menu row: name, icon, Markdown hint, search words; a placeholder), and what an empty part of its markup says under the caret (a disclosure's title, a code block's: `placeholders`, by the part its shared description names, whatever the block's source). Besides, the helpers about its markup the engines need (a disclosure's title and content, a detail card's, a Sheet tab's panel, a code block's text model). It imports no engine, so the page and the server both load it. Its header says where the engines still handle it.
- **`page.mjs`: what it does on the page** beyond what every block does: code blocks are opened, typed in and highlighted; list items indent and outdent, and have their own Enter and Backspace; disclosures are made and take the caret from their titles into their content, detail cards are made; sections are made, made from blocks and dissolved; Sheet tabs are renamed. Its `create(ctx)` takes what it works with from the one `ctx` of `editor.mjs` (`struct`, `say`, `recordText` and so on: "Page modules" below) and returns its page object, whose hooks the engines read ("Component definitions" below). A key of its own is in its `keys`, the order they are asked in being the engine's ("The order of keys"). A press of its own is its `press(event)`, asked at the top of `onPointerDown` of the component of what it came down on, before closing what opens over the page. What such a press closes first itself is `closeTools()`: the format bar, the "/" and "@" menus, the block menu, the link card, the formula box and an editor open over the page (`panel`). The builders of new blocks (`newCode()`, `newDetails()`, `newDivider()`, `newDialog()`) are here too; a conversion and `clipboard/own/` come to them through `create` and `build`.
- **`file.mjs`: how the server writes it** into the document's file where it does so its own way (a code block, a disclosure, a detail card, a list item holding lists) and checks what the page sends for it; `components/files.mjs` gathers them for `source/apply.mjs`. Any other type is written in the kit's plain markup from its description.

What a kit component is in markup is its description's (`src/shared/components/`, docs/internals.md "Components"): `components/kit.mjs` gives the editor the facts it finds blocks by (a Sheet area's tab row, tabs and panels and the attributes that pair them, a detail card's and a disclosure's title and content, a table's frame and scope line, the page's header, the kit's section), for the page (`matches`, `querySelector`) and the server's scanner (`source/units.mjs`, with the descriptions' `matchesNode` and `partOf`) alike; `tests/unit/components.mjs` fails a `doc-*` or `data-doc-*` name the editor spells that no description has (the scanner spells none of the classes a description names). The text blocks that are parts of a component are made by the scanner from the description: a host kind names the parts it is (`HOSTS` `parts`, [component, part] of a part edited as text: a code block's title, a disclosure's title, a Sheet's tabs, a media block's caption), so a component with a text field is a part in its description and a line of its host; the document's title (the page's `title`) and small text's end part (the paragraph's `tail`) are the descriptions' too, and a host that must keep its element's behaviour (`wrapped`: a Sheet tab's button) gets its text in a span the page makes inside it. What an engine does by kind is declared on the editor component's description too, never by a kind name in the engine (`registry.mjs` reads it): a unit kind's `handle` (`handleOf`: "line", the default, at its first line; "bar", a section's; "none", the pointer finds none at it; "outer", a tab's, its Sheet area's), `block: false` (`isBlock`: a column is no block of its own for a box selection or 选中外层) and `below: false` (`takesBelow`); a box's `lead` (`leadOf`: a highlight block's icon, no block to pick, move among or fall between), `firstLineActs` (a highlight block's and a quote's first block shows the container's handle), `outer: false` (`hasOuter`: a detail card's blocks have no block around them for 选中外层) and `groups` (`GROUPING`: a plain `<div>` groups blocks in it, the same boxes as the shared descriptions' `GROUPING_FLOWS`, which `tests/unit/components.mjs` holds it to). What marks a block is one definition (`UNIT`, `unitsOf` in `core.mjs`) and which elements are a highlight block one (`HIGHLIGHT` in `kit.mjs`). Each editor component is the editor's side of the description of its name, and each of its unit kinds is of that description's kind: a basic block, a container, a card or a whole block (`UNIT_CATEGORY`, `CATEGORY_NAMES`; docs/internals.md "Four kinds of block"); a card, and a kind marked `copy: "itself"` (a table, a row of columns), is copied as itself (`COPIED_AS_ITSELF`, which clipboard/own/ and the server's commit share).  `components/registry.mjs` gathers the descriptions into the tables the page and the server use: `UNIT_CATEGORY`, `COPIED_AS_ITSELF`, `UNIT_KINDS`, `BOX_CLASSES`, `COMPONENT_CLASSES`, `KIND_NAMES`, `BOX_NAMES`, `HOSTS` (`HOST_NAMES`), `BLOCK_TYPES`, `TURN_INTO` (the menus' order), `TYPE_INFO`, `TEXT_TYPES`, `HEADING_TYPES`, `boxRole()` and `accepts()`, `TOGGLES`, `SIZES` and `sizeValue()`, and `IDENTITY`. What every block shares stays in the engines and the editing tools (see "Where the editing tools live"): the text model and the save (`core.mjs`, `editor.mjs`), undo (`history.mjs`), conversions (`convert.mjs`), handles, selection and moving (`blocks.mjs` with its tools, `geometry.mjs`), resizing (`resize/`), copying (`clipboard/`), commits (`commit.mjs`), and reading, patching and merging the file (`source.mjs`, a module per task in `source/`). A component's page module may use the engines; an engine reaches a component only through its `index.mjs` or the page module the editor hands it, so imports never go round in a circle. `page/made.mjs` collects what a structural change made, for conversions and the components' changes alike. The preview serves `src/editor/components/` as it serves `page/`, never a `file.mjs` or `files.mjs` (the server's), and `tests/unit/boundaries.mjs` keeps the browser modules free of Node, of the server's modules and of bare imports.

A new component: a folder with its `index.mjs` and a line in `COMPONENTS` (`registry.mjs`); its shared description (`src/shared/components/<id>.mjs`, with a line in that folder's `index.mjs`), its look in the kit (`src/render/kit/styles/`, with a line in `index.css`), and the English of its words in `en.mjs`; a `page.mjs` with a line in `components/pages.mjs` when it does something of its own on the page, a line in `builders.mjs` when a `build` of it needs the page, and a `file.mjs` (with a line in `files.mjs`) when the server writes it its own way. What an Agent writes for it is its `glance`, and `node src/cli/glance-table.mjs` writes that into `skills/gallery/references/writing-html.md`.

A block that comes out of the tree comes out the same way: its folder and those lines, the English no string uses any more, then the table and the numbers of `engine-names` written again. What documents hold of it stays as they hold it. With its shared description and its look still there, the page cannot edit it but moves and deletes it whole, as a card when it is a figure and as a whole block otherwise, and it exports as ever, its caption too; with those gone as well, `gallery check` and `gallery export` name its classes as none of the kit's and the document stays as it was. A kind of text block no component defines any more is no text block (`scan` in `source/blocks.mjs`; with `widget` gone, whose `text` keeps a paragraph in a whole block to its words, that paragraph stays the text block it is), a paste or an import of what the block was takes what it holds as blocks of their own (`lowered` in `clipboard/import/to-blocks.mjs`), a page object no component made is simply not there (`pages.bookmark`, asked by an embed; `modalOps`, `sectionOps` and `columnOps`, which fall back to nothing), and only the media blocks that have a type have a placeholder. `tests/unit/boundaries.mjs` lists the blocks that come out so: nothing but the manifests imports their folders. Others have an engine that imports them: a list, a table, a figure, a Sheet area, a detail card, a flowchart, columns, the icon and small text's end part, and a formula, whose `texSource` the server reads.

## Instances of custom blocks

A custom block (自定义块, what the code calls a package: render.md "The catalog of custom blocks") is an external component of the workspace's: `<workspace>/components/<scope>/<name>/`, its files keeping the word component. Its instance is an element the file marks, `data-doc-component="<scope>/<name>"` on a block-level `div` or `section`, and that selector (`INSTANCE`, `src/shared/components/installed.mjs`; `isInstance` of a parsed element) depends on no catalog, so the boundary holds with no custom block installed. To the engines every custom block is one component, `installed`, which uses only what a definition offers any block: `units.installed` ranked ahead of every other rule of `UNIT_KINDS` (`rank: 0`), `attributes` so that `isComponent` holds and no box is ever made of the instance itself, `copy: "itself"`, and one box kind, `slot`: an instance is a unit and no box, but its slots are boxes (**Slots.** below).

- **The file.** The scan stops at an instance, and the walks past a flow (a card's parts, a chart's host, a disclosure) stop there too. Every walk of `natural.mjs` that writes or says something skips one whole (`isInstance`: the rewriting walk, a stray card's, a card link's, an old card's, what `readOnlyTexts` and `writingNotes` read and a separator's), and so does `newNames`: what an instance holds stays byte for byte. So it does as a commit moves it, or what holds it, to a place of another depth: the lines of a moved unit take the indentation of their new place but those an instance holds, as those of a script (`reindent`, `text.mjs`), so that a part reads in the file as its page and its component last had it: an instance moved to another depth has its start tag at the new indentation and every line after it, its end tag's too, at the old one. What only reads the whole file for what it holds (the ids, the chart keys, the names a script or a style uses) goes on reading it: an id there is still taken. A slot is the exception to all of it: the scan goes on in an instance's slots (`boxes` of `installed/file.mjs`, read by `heldBoxesOf` of `units.mjs` and the text-block scan of `blocks.mjs`), and every walk of `natural.mjs` and `newNames` passes the instance's own region by and goes on in its slots (`instanceSlots`, `src/shared/components/index.mjs`), where what it holds is natural HTML made the kit's as in any flow (a slot is a loose holder, `looseHolder`, whatever class its component gives it). `reindent` is as it was: an instance moved keeps its lines, its slots' among them.
- **Events by region, one rule.** What comes down in an instance's own region (`componentAt`, `shared/components/installed.mjs`: not in one of its slots, not on the instance itself, whose frame is Gallery's) and in no text block of it belongs to the component, and the editor lets go: `keepsKeys` of the page object, one guard at the head of the block keys and the clipboard for all that stands in an instance, so that its fields, buttons and what it draws are alike. The document-level entries read it through `keptByComponent()` (`page-objects.mjs`): the head of `onKeydown`, ahead of `annotate`'s key, as Ctrl+A, Enter and Ctrl+S there are the component's, and Ctrl+Z in a field of it (`FIELDS` of `shared/components/installed.mjs`: on a button or anything else it draws, Ctrl+Z is the page's undo, `undoHere`); `paste`, `copy` and `cut`; and the press of `text-select/`, which would otherwise take the block whole and draw a selection across blocks from it. `readOnlyReason` is the definition's `readOnly`, a rule ranked ahead of the page's that says `null`, so that a button there raises no word that controls cannot be changed; a press there still closes what stands open and clears selected blocks, as a press anywhere, and the format bar goes with it, coming back over no words selected before it once it is released (`pressed` of `format-bar/`, told so), as the editor answers nothing of that press. The other entries need nothing, as `editable()`, where they start from, gives no text block for an element of an instance: `sealed`, its component's field, says no text block around it reaches in, a list item holding one being the host `closest` would find, whatever `contenteditable="false"` says between them. `onBeforeInput`, `onInput`, `onSelectionChange`, a press beside a line and a drop act on that; and `blankTarget` takes no element in one, none being a box, so a box selection never starts there. One thing comes back from the browser all the same: Chrome has one undo stack for a page, so past what a field of a component can undo itself its Ctrl+Z lands on the last text block typed in, as a `historyUndo` there, and `onBeforeInput` answers that with nothing while the focus is on what a component keeps. What goes by a kit's class on the page and is no document-level entry asks `keptByComponent()` too (a to-do's box, an icon and a node's number, a media block's bar, a tab, the tools over a picture or a frame), so a class in an instance reaches the component alone. A link there opens as any link outside text does (`followOutside`) unless the component prevented it. Its text parts are the editor's: the keys, undo, Ctrl+A and a selection go as in any text block, a selection dragged out of one taking the block whole; Enter makes a paragraph after the block, the caret in it (`keys.enter`). Its outer frame and its handle are Gallery's: `target === instance` is no part of the rule. Ctrl+S in a field of a component is the browser's, as in the field of a code block's language menu.
- **Moved, copied, deleted whole.** Moved and deleted it goes whole, what its slots hold with it, and undo puts it back editable. One with a slot is copied by what it carries alone, in its document too: a copy of its unit's source would hold blocks the server writes whole, with no ids an answer gives the page (`restoredText` names the block alone), so its page object says that no such copy stands for it (`copiesAsItself` false, asked by `describe` and `copied` of `clipboard/own/describe.mjs`), and `data` carries its slots' blocks apart, `flows` (by the part's name, each block described as any, `holding`), its markup's slots empty, so that a paste makes them blocks of the page's as a disclosure's (`build` of `installed/page.mjs`: each slot a box made on the page, filled by `inner`, an empty paragraph where it holds none), which the commit writes apart and the answer names ("Made from its manifest"). One whose slot is no box (words straight in it) or holds a block the page cannot make carries none and is no copy at all, its copy refused, the word naming that block (`unmade`); a cut is refused where a copy in a slot carries nothing either (`unmadeCopy` goes into what a copy's data holds, `holds`). `check` holds its markup's slots empty and each named once in `flows`, whose blocks a slot takes (`valid(inner, "slot")`), and the server refuses a carried `html` whose slot holds anything (`invalid`). Any other is `copy: "itself"`: within a document a copy is a copy of its source, ids taken out but those it names itself, a label's field among them (`copiedSource`, "Copying blocks"), while the instance on the page holds what was copied (`copied` compares its markup: a write of its component may change nothing of its words, a row marked done, and a copy of the file's source would hold the file's). What it carries besides (`clipboard.data`) makes it anew where its unit is not, or holds something else now, after a cut and in another document: `{ type: "installed", package, html, blocks }`, `html` its markup as the kit would start it (`DocKit.dehydrate`, the marks off, the ids it names itself kept), `blocks` what it says as `markdown` says, read as Markdown by other programs and as words by a field of plain text (`joinedHtml`), pictures left out; in the clipboard's HTML a text part that is a heading is that heading (`as`), in Markdown a paragraph as any. Any page can write a clipboard, so that markup is the one instance no file vouches for: what would run (`refusedInCopy`, `src/shared/components/installed.mjs`: a script but one of data, a handler, an address of code, one an animation writes, a frame, a plugin, `<base>`, `<meta>`, `<link>`), says it is the editor's (`data-ge-*`) or leaves its block for the rest of the page (a `<style>`, a `style` that positions it `fixed`, `absolute` or `sticky`, or that hides what it says in an escape or a comment) keeps it from being made, on the page before anything is on it (`clipboard.check`, as for any block of the clipboard's) and on the server before it is written (`source`). So does markup that is not whole: what the page's check takes is what reads back as itself, as an element's `outerHTML` does, and the server takes one element closed by its own end tag and writes it anew from what it read, reading that again for what it checks (`instanceMarkup`, `src/shared/component-catalog.mjs`), so that no end tag left out, comment left open or end tag of nothing here takes in or closes what stands around the block in the file. A block holding such markup itself carries no `data`: it stays a copy within its document, and the cut says why, as for a card. On the page the copy is made as a new instance (`data-ge-copy` holds the markup the commit sends), the ids it names given a prefix (`prefixCopies`); in the file it gets a new id and a prefix of the file's own, text parts and data as they came. A pasted instance holds no text block until the commit names them, as a card's copy does.
- **In the menus.** "/" has a third group, 自定义块 (组件 before custom blocks were called so; Custom blocks in English, the instance's kind 自定义块 too), after 高级区块, and 在下方新建 and the handle's "+" the same rows: `menuVariants` on the host, each custom block with a template a key `installed:<id>` (`menuEntry`, `menuRow` and `variantKeys` in `page-objects.mjs`), named by the reader's language and found by either name, its id and its `words`, and by the initials of its name and of the words that read as pinyin (`searchWords` of `src/shared/pinyin.mjs`: 倒计时 by djs as 公式 is by gs; the name's initials from the platform's pinyin collation where it has one, so that a name its words do not spell is found by them), every custom block with the one mark `BLOCK_ICON` in these rows, whatever picture it has (the picture is shown beside its row, below). `menuVariants` reads a custom block's `state` for its rows, each there only for `normal` (`inDocumentMenus` of its `places` and gallery.json's lists: a custom page's block alone is `hidden` until the reader switches it on, `shown`; `followSettings` makes each entry's state again from the `settings` line's lists (hidden, disabled, shown and unlisted: the master switch of 设置 › 组件列表 off takes a block out of every menu), the server's rule run on the page), and `entryOf` reads it for all else: one hidden stays, whose instances run, copy and go by its name, and one turned off is none from the moment the page hears, with no list come. `offered.mjs` knows block types alone. They are on the page object, not in `index.mjs` as a type's own rows are, as the catalog reaches the page alone. A template no new block can be is none, `template` being null in the map (`templateProblem`, render.md "The catalog of custom blocks"). Chosen, in an empty block the new one takes its place (`put`, no conversion of the unit: the empty paragraph goes and an instance is inserted), in one with text it goes below, the caret in its first text part, its words selected (`sample`: a template's words are there to be typed over, with no format bar over them while the file has not named the part), or with none in an empty paragraph after it, as for a divider. `convert.mjs` `variantsIn` offers one where a table goes (`takesType`): below a list item's own line or a quote's it stands where the list or the quote is, taking it apart there (`convertBlock`), an empty item's line offering neither; not where `accepts` refuses (the header, a tab row); a table's cell, a text part, a title and a caption make no block of "/" at all.
- **Made from its manifest.** The page makes the element from the map's `template`, its text parts text blocks at once (`data-ge-part`, holding what each says, until the answer names them, `DocKit.description` saying which they are) and its id proposed as it comes on the page (`commit.mjs marks()`, for what is `sealed`), as the kit keeps a component's state under that id. A commit sends `{ type: "installed", package }` and the server writes what the manifest says, not what the page drew (`FILES.installed.source`, the template's one element with ids as a copy's, written as a unit put back is), and refuses a template that is not there as `invalid`, and one whose custom block is gone as `no-package`, whose card says so. The page adopts what the file says (`adopt`): a part as it was made shows the file's words, a list the page holds being old, and holds what a load holds (`prepareBlock`) where the file names atoms in it, a word of a template the editor has no format for staying as written; words typed before the commit went are the next commit's, and listed for the reader where the page goes before any commit could carry them (`buffer`'s `typed`); a part is found by its path, or among the instance's own by its place in the file where what a component drew beside it moved that path; and one the file takes for no text block, more than words in it, is read-only from then on (`notText`), as a load shows it, holding what the file does, the caret on in the next. One undo step takes it away; undo and redo put the same node back, and the kit mounts it again. Its slots are boxes made on the page at once (`slotsMade`), each holding what the template has there made blocks of the page's, read as HTML pasted from elsewhere (`clipboard/import/`, `toBlocks`), or an empty paragraph where it has none, so that the reader writes in one before anything is saved and Enter in a text part goes into it; the commit sends their blocks apart (`extras`: `children`, each in the slot `boxKeys` names, nodeOf's `nodes`), the server writes the template with its slots emptied and then those blocks in them a step in from the slot's line (`renderNode`, the instance's own lines standing as its markup has them), each block with the id the answer gives its key, and the page adopts them as a column's made on the page (`boxIn`, the slot by its name). A template whose slot holds blocks with nothing carried apart (a page from before) is `invalid`.
- **A first instance.** An instance of a custom block the page was served without brings it, however it came: "/", a paste, undo, redo (an Agent's write is a page shown again, with its tags). `bring` puts in the map's tags once, the kit's files it asks for that the page has not (`kit`, `assetTags`), then `packageTags`, styles then scripts, where the server would have put them, a script made anew to run. Its description goes in at once, so that `DocKit.component` and the first text part find it; `ARRIVING` on its script keeps the kit from calling an instance added meanwhile one whose script started nothing, and once it has loaded or failed `DocKit.mount` asks again of each instance. A struct step records no tag in the body that loads the page's files (`history.mjs`), the kit's, the vendors' and a custom block's alike, or undo would take them out while the page goes on running them: they stay for the next instance, and a view copy holds them. What arrives is seen by one observer of the page's added nodes (`install`), and one decision is made of it (`arrive`): the page keeps which custom blocks it was told changed (`followCatalog`, `installed/served.mjs`, `isBehind`), and of one of those an instance that cannot come as it is, its list having no such custom block, not having come, or saying another version than the page runs, asks for the page to be shown again (`doc-components-changed`), as for a page holding one when it changed. Reads of the list may cross: the last one begun is what counts (`readList`), and until it has answered nothing comes as it is. One that came with no text block, of a custom block that came after it, gets them.
- **What the handle and its menu show.** The block menu has no grid of types, and at its head the custom block's name and the one mark of a custom block (`nameOf`, `iconOf`: `BLOCK_ICON`, never the block's picture, which is shown beside its row in "/"), then its own rows (`menuItems`: 设置…, where it has a form, below), then what a whole block has: 在下方新建, 复制块链接 and 删除.
- **Its 设置.** `menuItems` of the page object (`installed/page.mjs`, `formOf`) gives the row 设置… in the handle's menu of an instance that runs (not in its fallback, not past its manifest's `schema`) where its manifest lists `settings` (the map's `settings`, `menuOf`) and the instance holds the part `settings` (a script of JSON edited as data), or where its needs hold `tables`. It opens the form of a custom page's card (`page/board/settings.mjs` `openSettings`: a popup card, the manifest's fields in order, a choice from a list the editor's own menu card and never a native `<select>`, 保存 or Enter writing what changed, 取消, Escape or a press outside nothing), under the block's top, with a field of the page's own first for a view, 表格 (type `choice`, see "Table views"). The values of the manifest's fields go into the part as `context.editJSON` writes them: `write` of the page object runs the change on a draft of the part, the values merged over what it holds and laid out as it was (`jsonIn`), one `source` step of undo, committed, merged and held back as a conflict as any data part is; what the block's own component writes (kanban's ⚙) is the same part, so a change of either shows in the other. A save that changes both the table and the fields is two steps. Scenarios `customBlockSettings`, `tableViewSettings`.
- **Its picture in "/" and in 添加自定义块** (the owner, 2026-11-17: the list stays rows, and a row shows the block's real, whole picture, not a drawing of it). A row of a block with its two pictures (the catalog's `picture`, `{ light, dark }`: `preview-light.webp` and `preview-dark.webp` of its folder, `component-catalog.mjs` `PICTURES`, which `gallery block check` makes: [render.md](render.md) "Making one"; with `en: { light, dark }` where the block has an English pair too, as Gallery's own have) shows, while the pointer rests on it (150 ms, then at once along the rows while one shows or just went, 300 ms) or the keys move the highlight onto it, a card beside the menu (`block-preview.mjs`, one per page, `menu.mjs` handing it the rows whose item has `preview.picture` and the keys' moves, `moved`): the picture of the page's theme now (`data-doc-effective-theme`) and of the UI's language where the block has a pair in it, else its own (`pictureAddress`), at its own size (its pixels over 2, at most `WIDEST`, 480 CSS px, and 480 high, smaller by one scale where the room is less), its description under it, wrapped at the picture's width; the floating cards' look, by their tokens (`look.mjs`: `--ge-r-card`, `--ge-card-pad`, `--ge-shadow-card`; the picture's corners `--ge-r-row`, with a hairline drawn over its edge by an inset shadow). Its edges stand on whole device pixels as the layout keeps lengths (in 64ths of a CSS pixel): its place, its padding, the picture's size and its height are each on `pixelStep` (1 at 100%, 4 at 125%, the least length that is a whole number of device pixels there, 2 at 150%, a half at 200%), so its padding is 4 at 125% (5, 6.25 device pixels, smeared the picture's hairline over two), and the hairline is a whole number of device pixels wide, one at 125% (an outline is drawn 1.25 of them there). It stands right of the menu, its top by the row's, else left of it, else (a window too narrow beside the menu) over the menu under the row, else above it, and nowhere over the row: none where nothing leaves the row clear (`previewPlace`). The picture is fetched as the rows are built and the card placed only once it is decoded, at its size, before it is seen, then faded in (120 ms, opacity alone; none with reduced motion; none from one row to the next); it follows its row as the list scrolls, and goes with the menu, with new rows (a search) and with a press, faded out as the menus are (`ui.mjs` `fadeAway`, 90 ms). A row of a block without both pictures keeps a row `tooltip` of its description at the row's right (`tooltip.mjs`, `beside`). Scenarios `blockPictureCard` (添加自定义块), `packageBlockTools` and `customBlockMenus` ("/").
- **Taken out by an Agent's write.** 恢复我的 (`putBack`) writes what the reader has: the page object's `renew`, reached from `asMade` ahead of the engine's rules, which go by tag and class and know no instance, marks it as carrying its markup (`data-ge-copy`, `copyOf`, its slots that are boxes emptied), so that the commit goes as a clipboard's does and not from the template, what its slots hold made on the page again and carried apart. The blocks of its slots, made this load too, went with it: the page's revert check gives one card, the instance's (a unit made this load inside another such that the write took out goes with it: `heldWithin`, `autosave.mjs`), and 恢复我的 or 接受 Agent 的 decides them with it (the card's `within`, those of them inside it, which `putBack` makes blocks made on the page again). What stands inside it that the file still has (a block of the file dragged into its slot that the write put back where it stood, one kept where the Agent took the instance off around it) is none of those: it stays the file's unit, which the instance made again takes along into its slot, decided by its own card if the write moved it, so that its words are never in the file twice. One holding what no clipboard may bring stays on the page, its card saying the file cannot hold it.
- **With no custom block, or one that does not load.** Its slots are the document's all the same, boxes whose blocks are edited as anywhere, none of the kit's fallback look or inert reaching in (render.md "Runtime"). The scan finds no text part, so the rest of its inside is read-only, and its fields and buttons are inert by the kit; `readOnly` says why at a press on it (`[data-doc-fallback]`, the kit's mark, render.md "Runtime"). So it is of an instance whose component threw or whose script started nothing (render.md "A custom block on a page"): the file has text parts there, and the page shuts them for as long as the kit shows it so (`followFallback`, on that mark, and `entered` for one the kit marked first), a later mount opening them. It moves, copies and deletes whole like any other, its note being its top line to the handle (a `[data-doc-dev]` node right in a block is that block's; any other is no part of the page), and the kit's marks and note, data of the page, reach no file. Nor does what the kit draws of a script of data there (`.doc-component-data`, render.md "Runtime"), read-only as the rest: the server never sees it and the page finds no part of an instance in it (`elementsOf`), so that none is marked there whenever an instance's text parts are, as it is made or comes back onto the page by a move or an undo, its root is no element a text part can be, a copy takes it off with the kit's marks (`DocKit.dehydrate`), and what a commit compares a data part with is read after that.
- **What a page holds of the catalog.** The page's map carries `components` (`menuOf`, `src/shared/component-catalog.mjs`), one entry for each custom block that loads, sorted by id: `{ id, version, state, name: { zh, en }, description: { zh, en }, words, definition, stamp, template, preview, markdown, tags, kit }`, `template` being the markup "/" puts in, or null, `preview` the address of its picture under the custom block's stamp, or null, `definition` the manifest's stamp, what a commit will be held to, `tags` what `packageTags` writes and `kit` the tags of the kit's files it asks for. A custom block's parts reach a page by `DocKit.describe` alone, every custom block being told in every preview and one that came after by its tags, the line that says them, so that an instance a paste or the menu brings has its description as one written in the file does; `DocKit.description(id)` reads it back. A page holding no instance of a custom block that changes stays as it is (render.md, "The catalog of custom blocks"), and the list it was served would go old: told `doc-catalog-changed`, the page reads `components` from its own address, what a load would have read, nothing of it changing: no swap, no new route, and the page kept by the catalog's stamp is the next load's. A row chosen in the moment before that is written as the manifest says now, or refused where the custom block is gone.
- **Text parts.** `readCatalog()` (`src/shared/component-catalog.mjs`, [render.md](render.md) "The catalog of custom blocks") reads `<workspace>/components/*/*/component.json`, each custom block a description as a kit component's is, with the grammar of `parts` of `src/shared/components/` (`parsePart`: what `partOf` reads in the file and a browser's selectors read on the page alike), `edit` being `text`, `data`, `flow` (a slot, **Slots.** below) or `none`; a custom block with a problem does not load, says why and counts as no custom block. The scan asks for it at the first instance of a page and takes the parts the instance's custom block edits as text, of its own region and no other element in it (none in a slot, no slot itself, none in an instance in it), as text blocks of kind `installed` (`textParts` of its `file.mjs`), which belong to the instance as a card's title does to the card: words, emptied but never split, joined or taken out on their own. One that leaves the page while its instance stays, a component's drawing taking it out, stays in the file, there being no way to take it out, and nothing waits on it (`skipText`, `commit.mjs`). They hold no inline formula (`formula: false`): the kit draws none in an instance's own region, so the "/" menu, the format bar and Ctrl+Shift+E offer none and one pasted stays its words. It reads the catalog in use (`currentCatalog`), which the server takes anew when a custom block changes, an open page holding an instance of that custom block being told to load again. A word typed across that moment, which comes after the catalog is taken as a commit waits for a pause, finds no such text block in an instance as the page has it: it is held as a conflict of its own (`package`, below), nothing written, and the page keeps it to copy.
- **Slots.** A part a manifest edits as `flow` (`"body": { "edit": "flow" }`, render.md "The catalog of custom blocks") is a slot: the element of the instance's own markup marked `data-doc-slot="body"`, a block-level `div` or `section`, whose inside is the document's blocks, by the mark and no catalog, so that a slot is the reader's with no custom block there too. Whose an element is goes by one rule, folded from the outermost region in (`heldBy`, `shared/components/installed.mjs`; `heldByNode` of `components/index.mjs` on the server's tree, and the kit's runtime the same): an instance in the document's region opens its component's, its own slot opens the document's again, and an instance or a slot in a component's region stays that component's. A slot itself is its instance's (its component places it), and so are its tag and attributes; `ownerOf` (`registry.mjs`) finds what is one block inside the slot around an element (`slotAround`), so that a block in a slot is its own component's. To the engines a slot is a box of kind `slot` (`boxes` of `installed/index.mjs`), the instance its unit and the part's name its key (`find`), of role `flow` as a column's: `groups` (a plain `div` in it a group), `holdsOne` (emptied, an empty paragraph; one the file has empty says 空白内容区。点击或拖动区块到这里。 while edited, a click making a paragraph, as a disclosure's content), `selectAll` 内容区里的全部内容; no `leaveByEnter`, `lead` or `firstLineActs`. Its blocks are typed in, split, turned, moved by their handles and keys, dragged in and out, undone and saved as anywhere; Tab, Shift+Tab, Backspace and the arrows go by the rules of any flow (Shift+Tab takes no block out, Backspace at its first block's start joins nothing into a text part, which takes no `merge`). Enter in a text part goes into the first slot after it, at its first block's start or in a paragraph made first in it (one structural step), as from a disclosure's title (`keys.enter` of `installed/page.mjs`); with none after it, `captionEnter`. The component reads a slot (`context.part` gives the element) and hears what changes in it (`source`), and writes nothing there: a slot of the page's taken off an instance that stays (a component redrawing its markup) is put back where it stood at once, what it holds with it, and the console says so (`keepSlots`, `installed/page.mjs`), so that no commit takes the reader's blocks out. What a component writes in a slot as the kit runs it (its mount, an update, a frame, a timer of `context`'s) the kit itself puts back in that same call and fails the instance (render.md, "A custom block on a page": **A slot is the document's.**), so that the page hears a change undone in the task it was made in, and commits nothing of it. An instance's hash covers what its slots hold, a change there touching it as any unit around a block. Markdown and the clipboard's words write a slot as its blocks (`instanceParts`: `how.lines`, unindented, as a column's). `tests/browser/scenarios.mjs` `instanceSlotBlocks`, `instanceSlotMoves`, `instanceSlotEvents`, `instanceSlotKit`, `instanceSlotFallback`, `packageSlotExport`, `packageSlotMarkdown`, `packageSlotConflict`, `packageSlotInsert`, `packageSlotCarried`, `packageSlotRestored` and `packageSlotRestoredMoved` hold it, on the fixtures' `local/brief` (要点卡).
- **A component writes back.** A part a manifest edits as `data` is the component's, as all that is no text block in an instance is, and what writes it is a table's way of writing, with no save, undo or conflict of its own. The operation is `{ source, hash, parts, definition }` of the unit: `source` the inside of each part that changed, by the part's name, of each part the first element `at` finds, as `context.part` does (`dataParts`, `installed/file.mjs`, one part in another being written with that one); `hash` the unit's, as for a deletion or a conversion, and `parts` the hash of what each part it writes held as the page last had it from the file (the map's unit and the answer's facts say them, as the scan gives them: `dataOf` of `installed/file.mjs`, each part's inside as the file holds it), a mismatch of the unit's being the conflict `changed` only where a part it writes changed too: someone else's words in a text part, a part it does not write or what stands beside its parts go in beside the write, as words in two cells of a table do, the instance found by its id or, with none yet, by what it said as the page opened; `definition` the stamp of the manifest the page runs the component by, and another than the custom block's now is refused before all else, `force` or not (`definition`, a reason of the commit's answer like the others). `FILES.installed.rewrite` then writes each part's inside alone, in the file's line endings, by `rewriteUnit` in `apply.mjs`, the table's way: every byte around it stays, and the unit gets an id as any unit written does. What it refuses it refuses whoever sent it (`invalid`): more than one commit writes (`DATA_MOST`), a `table` operation, which goes round `definition`, what does not read as that inside and no more (`fits`: the part's own tags around it read as the one element closed by its own end tag, every byte of it some element's in order, each closed by its end tag, so that an end tag of nothing, a comment left open, a row with no tbody or what a table sends out of itself goes no further), what `refusedInData` says (`src/shared/components/installed.mjs`: what no copy may hold, any script, a block's identity), an element a page holds as words (`textarea`, `title`, `template`), a script of code, and in a script of data anything but JSON that keeps it closed (`fitsDataScript`). An instance whose `data-doc-schema` is past its manifest's `schema`, or that says none that is a number, is refused too (`schemaAhead`), and is read-only whole: none of its parts is a text block either, on the page or on the server. What stands in a data part, or holds one, is no text block, whatever else a manifest says (`textParts`, on the page and on the server): the region rule, so that a commit's two ways of writing never meet.

  On the page it goes as a table's goes, at the same places. The baseline is `data` of the layout (`captureLayout`, beside `tables`), by unit and part: as the page opens, what `dataOf` of the page object says, each part's inside on a copy of the instance `DocKit.dehydrate` has been over, so that what a component leaves on a part by its manifest's `runtime`, and the kit's marks of one shown as it stands, are no change. A part differing from it is noticed by `dataChanges` (`core.mjs`, as `tableChanges`), and only that: the baseline is read from the page and so spells the part as the page does, and however otherwise the file spells it nothing is sent of a part left alone, which is what keeps a component that cannot read old content from writing it. The operation goes in `build` (`commit.mjs`), `definition` being the stamp the component that wrote it ran by (`definitionOf`: the instance's `data-ge-definition`, which `write` sets, else what the page was served with or brought, however its list has moved since), so that a page taking up what one that is gone left sends it by that page's manifest. What one commit carries of components' parts is `dataMost` of the page object (`DATA_MOST`) in all, the first part whatever it is: the rest goes with the next commit, as it still differs, and a commit stays well inside what the endpoint reads (`maxBody`, `service.mjs`, which reads a body past it to its end and answers 413, so that the page says what happened rather than taking the server for gone). The crash buffer keeps all of it (`whole`). A commit refused keeps the baseline it had, as a held unit keeps all it had (`changed`: 仍然调整 writes what the page holds), and the reader's version is the part's inside. `definition` is for no reader to decide: `apply` gives the operation up, and that alone, what else of the unit waits going its way (`outdated`), and says so, and `committed` asks for the page to be shown again (`reloadSoon`, no visible reload in a workbench, "Shown again without a reload", [workbench.md](workbench.md)), which says why as it comes back. A page that takes up what one that is gone left (`recover`, `autosave.mjs`) lists nothing of such a write among what it could not put back, and says the same (`outdated`). After a commit the baseline is what the file says, never what the page shows: of a part the commit wrote, what it sent; of a unit it wrote whole, made from a template, a copy or what a clipboard carried, or put back by undo from the source it went with, what the answer's facts give of its markup (`markup`, `fileFacts`, for a unit whose operation is held to a `definition`), read as the page reads one on it (`dataIn` of the page object, in `adopt`); of anything else what the last commit left. A write the commit did not carry (one of a unit put back, of a block not finished, or past what it carries) still differs from that, and goes with the next, so that nothing stands saved that the file does not hold. `tests/unit/write-back.mjs` holds each way.

  A block's `context.workspace.open` (the kit's cancelable `doc-open-request` on the window, render.md "A custom block on a page") is answered by the editor as a link to that place is followed (`goTo`: in a workbench its `open`, `#@<slug>` for a custom page; on its own, the page left once what waits is saved), so that a block reading the workspace in a document opens what it lists as a link would.

  What writes a part is `context.edit`: the kit asks (`doc-component-edit`, render.md "A custom block on a page"), and the page object answers at once (`write`). It takes it where a block tool may act (`active`), makes a draft of each part the edit names by `cloneNode`, off the page, and hands them to the change. A throw, a promise, a change made to a part on the page rather than to its draft (set back as it was, as its step would undo the change it left out), or a draft the file could not hold (`draftProblem`, the same rules as the server's, and the part's own tag touched, a draft reading back as anything else, more than `DATA_MOST`) means nothing happened: the parts are as they were, there is no step, the console says why and the answer is false. A change of nothing is taken, with no step and no commit. Otherwise copies of what each draft holds go into its part (`replaceChildren`), so that a draft the component keeps reaches nothing on the page, which the kit's observer sees as any change of it and tells the instance (`source`); the instance is marked with the stamp its component ran by (`data-ge-definition`), and `sourceStep` records one undo step of its own kind, each part's inside before and after, and marks the page changed, the autosave following as for any discrete operation. Each write is a step, every part it names in it, none joined: `recordText` joins typing within a second in a block because a word is no gesture, but a component's write is, as each of a table's operations is, and one that writes in a burst is as many steps as it made. It asks a gesture to write once, naming all the parts it writes. Undo and redo set the part's inside, the kit says `source` and the component redraws; no block is focused, so a caret in a text part stays where it stands, around the step and around its undo. A step undone from what a component keeps: a press on a button of an instance leaves the focus on it, and a button, or anything a component draws, has no undo of its own, so the page takes Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Y there, in no field of it (`input`, `textarea`, `select`, `[contenteditable]`), and goes back through every step from it, its own and any other, undo going from anywhere (`pageUndo`, `keepsKeys`, `undoHere`); a field keeps its own. A component that reads `canEdit()` learns of the page as it comes to edit by `doc-editable`, which the page object sends (`entered`) once the baselines are there.

  A part is written whole: what changed lands inside it, and the file's other bytes stay, as `tests/unit/write-back.mjs` holds, but within a part an entity or an odd space comes back as a browser writes it. A script of data is a part like any other, its text what the component reads and writes.

## Component definitions

A component's definition is what its `index.mjs` exports, gathered by `registry.mjs`: the fields an engine reads about it. An engine reads the field and does not ask which component it has. `tests/unit/engine-names.mjs` counts the places where an engine still names a component, file by file: every string that is a component's name, tables keyed by names, a component's `isX` predicates, `doc-*` selectors and comparisons with a component's tag (docs/testing.md). Those numbers only go down, and what a new block needs an engine to know is a field here.

What each field holds, down to its smallest key, is written once, in the header of `registry.mjs`:

| Field | What it means | Example |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `name` | the component, the same as its shared description's | `"math"` |
| `description` | the shared description it edits, when that has another name | a flowchart's: `"list"` |
| `listed`, `hideable` | `listed`, what 设置 › 组件列表 calls it (a function, the server saying it in both languages), on every component with a type in the "/" menu and on what else the reader meets as a block of its own; its line there shows the icon of its first type in the "/" menu, or its first unit's `icon` where it has none (分区), and the registry throws for a listed component with neither; `hideable: false`, it can never be hidden, its switch there off and locked ("Hiding a block", below) | a list's: `listed: () => t("列表"), hideable: false` |
| `units` | the blocks it defines, by kind: how an element is recognized, what the reader calls it, where it stands | `rule: { name: t("分割线"), match: [{ rank: 230, tag: "hr" }] }` |
| `units[kind].read` | how `gallery read` writes a block of the kind ([internals.md](internals.md), "Reading a document"): its name, its rows of the default view (`read-rows.mjs`), its words, its JSON's data, what the export's Markdown leaves out of it; every kind has one (`tests/unit/read.mjs`), its words the command's, in Chinese, no `t()` | `rule`'s: `{ name: "分割线", rows: (node, { tools }) => [rowOf(node, "---")] }` |
| `boxes` | the containers it defines, by kind: the role that says what each takes, how its blocks behave | a highlight block's: `highlight: { role: "flow", also: ["icon"], leaveByEnter: true, … }` |
| `components`, `attributes` | the classes and the attributes of elements that are one block, nothing going in or out | `components: ["doc-math-block"]` |
| `hosts` | the kinds of text block it defines and what each can do | `tex: { name: t("公式"), caps: () => ["plain"], tex: true }` |
| `placeholders` | what an empty part of its markup says under the caret; on a host, `placeholder`, what any text block of the kind says | `placeholders: { label: t("折叠列表标题") }`; an instance's text part |
