| `types` | the blocks the reader can make or turn a block into: the markup the server writes, the place in the menus, the menu row | `divider: { menu: ["basic", 15], fresh: 1, tag: "hr", kind: "rule", bare: true, … }` |
| `glance` | on a type, and on a component (one or a list) for what no type stands for: its part of a row of "Blocks at a glance" in `skills/gallery/references/writing-html.md`, the table every Agent reads, which `node src/cli/glance-table.mjs` writes between its two marks and `kit:check` holds to these: the row's place, the block's name (a type's is its menu label), what an Agent writes, what the file holds after an export, the section of its rule. Parts of one place are one row, in the order of the definitions. Words for an Agent, so no `t()`. A type that says none has no row | `note: { …, glance: { row: 11, name: "小字", write: "…", rule: "Kit blocks" } }` |

Hooks are the fields that say what a component does: a function, or a value an engine acts on, where a component differs from the rest. A hook with nothing beside its name here is one no engine reads yet.

| Hook | What it means | Example |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `boxed` | on a unit: a block with a frame of its own; a double click between two makes a paragraph, a click to its right past the text area it stands in selects it | `rule: { …, boxed: true }` |
| `beside`, `entersAfter` | on a unit: `beside`, a click to its right past the text area it stands in selects it and Enter on it selected puts a paragraph after it, though it is not boxed; `entersAfter`, that Enter alone | a row of columns' `beside: true`, a Sheet area's `entersAfter: true` |
| `inRow` | on a unit: `inRow(element)`, whether this one stands beside another now; with `beside`, what `standsBeside` reads, so that it and what it holds keep the handle across their width, with no 批注 button | a flowchart's node, when a node shares its line |
| `sharesHeight` | on a unit: its height is the tallest's beside it, so a block growing in it may change no height outside it and 批注's cards follow such a block's size (`SHARES_HEIGHT`) | a column of 分栏, a flowchart's node |
| `resizable` | on a unit: the elements of it whose edges resize, as a selector; `resize/` keeps how each kind is dragged, and the size among its `sizes` that says `height` is what the bottom edge sets | `figure: { …, resizable: "figure[data-ge-unit]" }` |
| `selectAll` | on a box: the steps of Ctrl+A it is, its blocks (`content`), the block it belongs to (`whole`), and whether the steps end there (`top`) | `panel: { …, selectAll: { content: t("这个选项卡里的全部内容"), whole: true } }` |
| `outer` | on a box, `false`: a block straight in it has no block around it for the reader, whatever stands around its element, so no 选中外层 (`hasOuter`, `block-info.mjs` `outerOf`) | a detail card's content |
| `preload` | on a type, `true`: it needs something on the page as soon as a type grid offers it and before a block with text is turned into it; on its page object, `preload(type)` brings that, a promise while it comes, `null` once it is there | `math: { …, preload: true }`; `pages.math.preload` brings KaTeX |
| `corner` | on a type: the type in whose cell of a type grid it sits, as a gray corner | `note: { …, corner: "paragraph" }` |
| `onSplit` | on a type: what the part split off a container of the type keeps of the whole | a flowchart's direction |
| `readOnly` | on a component: why an element of it stays read-only (a type's `hint` is its Markdown shortcut in a menu row), ranked rules tried with the page's own, a reason of `null` for one with a tool of its own; `at` a selector found by `closest`, or a function of the element (an instance's own region, `componentAt`, which no selector tells from its slots) | `readOnly: [{ rank: 100, at: ".doc-icon", says: null }]` |
| `create` | on a type, or on its component's page object where it needs the page: how a type that is no text block is made. It takes `{ type, doc, text, put, stand, paragraph }`, makes the node and places it, and returns what the caret or the choice goes to: there is no block model here, so it places the node itself, and it also runs when a block with text is turned into the type. A type without one is a text block | `image: { …, create: ({ doc, put }) => put(newImage(doc)) }`; `pages.math.create` |
| `menuVariants` | on a page object: `()`, other entries of its type in the menus, by name, each `{ label, icon, words, preview?, sample?, create(what) }`, its key `type:name` (on the page object where they come from what the page holds), `sample` where the words a new one holds are selected, to be typed over | an instance's, one for each custom block with a template |
| `onCreated` | on a page object: what follows a new block of its type, `(block, type)`: made by "/", Markdown or 在下方新建, or turned from a block menu with no text to carry, never a block with text or a group; true when what comes next is its own, so that no caret goes in it and no block is selected | `pages.math.onCreated` opens the formula editor; `pages.image.onCreated` the picker |
| `activate` | on a page object: what Enter does on a selected block, `(unit)`. Every page object is asked and each tests the element itself, as a block's kind and what it is may differ (a formula with a class ranked before math's); true when it was its own, else the caret goes to the end of the block's first text | `pages.math.activate` opens the formula editor |
| `redraw` | on a page object: `(units)`, the blocks of a group turned into its type while what `preload` brings was still coming, drawn again once it is there | `pages.math.redraw` |
| `turnsEmpty` | on a unit: it holds nothing to carry, so what it is turned into is an empty block in its place, the block's own type read from its kind | `rule: { …, turnsEmpty: true }` |
| `leadsChapter` | on a unit: it may stand first in a chapter as the line above its heading (an Agent's `<section><hr><h2>`), so a folded heading with only such blocks before it in its chapter stands for the chapter (`fold/rule.mjs` `takenWith`, registry `LEADS_CHAPTER`) | `rule: { …, leadsChapter: true }` |
| `takesAsIs`, `holder`, `nests`, `joins`, `leadIcon`, `title`, `content` | on a type: what `convert.mjs` asks of a container. `takesAsIs`, a block turned into it goes in as it is; `holder`, its place among the containers that turn into one another; `nests: false`, none inside another of its kind; `joins: false`, a block turned into it never joins the one beside it; `leadIcon`, the icon a new one opens with; `title(element)` and `content(element)`, of one with a text block for a title and a content region that holds its blocks, a new one being what the `build` of its page.mjs makes of nothing | a highlight block's `leadIcon: "💡"` |
| `clipboard` | on a type, and on a unit where its kind needs its own: how a block is copied and written out, in one field; see "The clipboard field" below | `clipboard: { kind: "heading", level: 2 }` |
| `markdown` | on a type: the shortcuts at a block's start, `[{ match, type?, props?(match), alone? }]`, tried in one loop in the order of the definitions. `match` is what the text before the caret must be whole; `type` is the type made, by default the one it stands on. `props` says what the shortcut sets on the block, `null` for not this time, and the page object's `setProps(block, props)` sets it in the same undo step, there being no model to set a field on. `alone`: such a rule holds only in a block holding nothing else. No rule carries the text after the caret into a block below | `todo: { …, markdown: [{ match: /^\[[xX]\] $/, props: () => ({ checked: true }) }, …] }` |
| `keys` | on a page object: the keys of a text block of the component (`ownerOf`: the component of the block the caret is in), each given one `ctx` and true when the key was its, else the engine's own rule follows: `enter`, `shiftEnter`, `commandEnter`, `tab(ctx, back)`, `escape`, `backspace`, `delete`. The rules of every block stay in `typing/` and read fields (`resettable` among them): what is asked when is in "The order of keys" below. `keys.around` holds the same keys for a block of another component that stands in it, or right after it where it says `canHaveChildren`, since no block here says of its own which block it stands in | `pages.code.keys.tab` types two spaces; `pages.list.keys.around.tab` puts a paragraph under the list before it |
| `canHaveChildren` | on a unit: a block right after it goes under it by Tab, so its `keys.around` are asked of that block too, Tab reading the field of the block before | `list: { …, canHaveChildren: true }` |
| `emptyIsNothing` | on a unit, or a box with `classes`: a block of it left empty counts as nothing written, in a container that says `removedEmpty` | a paragraph, a list, a disclosure's content |
| `fromWords` | on a unit: a block of it can be made again from its words alone, when the file lost it (`PLAIN_TEXT`) | a paragraph, a heading |
| `levels` | on a type: what a block of it becomes a level larger (`up`) or smaller (`down`), and `boldDown`, its words bold throughout when it goes down (into a type that is no heading), read by `convert.mjs` `headingShifts` for a heading's level badge ("A heading's level badge") | 标题 2's `levels: { up: "heading1", down: "heading3" }`, 标题 4's `{ up: "heading3", down: "paragraph", boldDown: true }` |
| `emptyToText` | on a type: Backspace in an empty block of it turns it into an empty paragraph where it stands, the caret in it, one undo step (`leave.emptied` in `typing/leave.mjs`; the user's of 2026-11-09), as an emptied item or quote paragraph does through `resettable` | a heading, small text |
| `replacedByFile` | on a unit: a file pasted at the caret in an empty block of it takes the block's place (`replacedByFile(kind)` in `registry.mjs`, read by `clipboard/` `fromFiles`), else the block goes after it | a paragraph |
| `mergeRefused` | on a page object: `(block)`, what it says of a block of it holding blocks that Backspace could not join to the line above | a list item's, which says what the item holds: its items (子项) where it holds lists alone, its content (内容) where it holds any other block |
| `press` | on a page object: `(event)`, what a press does at the top of the editor's own: the component of what it came down on is asked (`pageAt`, for the nearest element around it that `ownerOf` in `registry.mjs` says is of one: all that stands in an element that is one block (`components`, `attributes`) is its, then an element goes by its kind of text block, the text part it is, its unit and last its type), true when it was its own | `pages.code.press` opens the code pressed on; `pages.math.press` the formula editor |
| `menuItems` | on a page object: `(target)`, the rows of the block menu of a block of the component, by the kind of unit it is, each row doing its `run`; what several kinds share is a helper they call (`components/rows.mjs` `lookRow`) | a code block's 显示全部代码 |
| `nameOf`, `iconOf` | on a page object: `(target)`, what the reader calls this block where that is no kind's name, and the mark at the head of its menu | an instance's, its custom block's name and picture |
| `renew` | on a page object: `(unit)`, a unit the file dropped that the page still shows, made ready to be written from what it holds; true when it did, the engine's rules by tag and class passed by (`asMade`) | an instance's, as what a clipboard carried |
| `notText` | on a page object: `(host)`, a text block the page made in a block it made and the file takes for none | an instance's part, read-only as a load shows it |
| `menu` | on a page object: `(target, { rect, reason, remove })`, a menu in place of the block menu, `{ items, label, width }` or null | a tab's 重命名, 编辑图标, 删除 |
| `rowBeside` | on a page object: `(target)`, a row for a block of another component, beside its color: every page object is asked, each testing the block itself | `pages.icon.rowBeside`: a highlight block's and a node's 添加图标 |
| `type` | on a page object: `(host, text)`, text put in a text block of it that takes it as it is (`textFilters`): pasted, or a line break that came with no key (`typedAsIs` in `page-objects.mjs`), and whatever its own `keys` type | `pages.code.type` |
| `opensBefore` | on a host: Enter at its very start, with words in it, puts an empty paragraph before its block where it does not split | `code: { …, opensBefore: false }` keeps Enter inside the code, including at its start |
| `textFilters` | on a host, `false`: what is typed in it is taken as it is, no inline code and no space set by itself | `code: { …, textFilters: false }` |
| `firstSays` | on a box that says `firstBlock`: what that first line says empty | a node's `firstSays: t("节点标题")` |
| `acceptsFile`, `fromFiles` | on a page object: `acceptsFile(file)`, whether a pasted file makes a block of it; `fromFiles(put, files)`, that block made and handed to `put(block)`, the paste's, which puts it where a block pasted at the caret goes, one undo step, and says whether it could; true when it took them. A paste goes to the first page object, in their order, that takes one of its files, with the first of them it takes (`clipboard/`); a file dropped on the page goes to the first that `acceptsFile` too, which makes its block by `forFile` ("Dropping in from outside") | `pages.image`: pictures, `pages.file`: any other file |
| `fromLink` | on a page object: `(figure, url, options)`, a block of the component filled from an address: the figure is on the page first | an image or an embed pasted with its address (`{ pasted: true }`: one undo step with the paste); `pages.bookmark.fromLink` in the place of an embed |
| `closed` | on a host: the page holds no text block for it until the reader opens it; its page object's `prepare(block)` readies it | `code: { …, closed: true }` |
| `inner` | on a host: `(element)`, the text a save takes of a text block of it, as markup, where that is not what its nodes say (`serializeInner` in `core.mjs`) | a code block's code, its last line break not being the code's |
| `click` | on a page object: `(event)`, as `press`, for a click, true when it was its own | `pages.code.click`: its fold arrow, wrap button, language; `pages.details.click` keeps a click in its title from toggling it |
| `keepsKeys` | on a page object: `(node, event)`, whether what happens on a node is its own, the editor letting go of its keys, the clipboard there and a drag from it (`keptByComponent`), `event` the key where one is asked about | the field of a code block's language menu; all of an instance of a custom block but its text |
| `dataOf`, `dataIn`, `dataMost`, `definitionOf` | on a page object: `(unit)`, what of a block its component writes, by part, as the file is to hold it, or nothing where it writes nothing, which `dataChanges` compares; `(html)`, the same of the file's markup of one, a block a commit wrote whole; what one commit carries of it, in bytes; and the stamp of what it was written by, which the commit carries | `installed`, a data part of a custom block |
| `rewrite`, `definition` | on a `file.mjs` entry: `(source, node, op, { validCell, packages })`, a unit's inside written anew, the splice or splices; `(node, packages)`, the stamp an operation on such a unit is held to | a table's rows; an instance's data parts |
| `sealed` | on a component: a selector, what no text block around it reaches into: `editable()` gives a node in one no host but a text block of its own, whatever the browser edits around it | `installed` |
| `formula` | on a host: `false`, no inline formula is offered in it or pasted as one (`takesFormula`), where the kit draws none | `installed`, a custom block's text parts |
| `textParts` | on a `file.mjs` entry: `(node, catalog)`, the elements of a unit that are text blocks by what its custom block says, `{ kind, part }` each, the scan going no further in | `installed` |
| `source`, `unwritten` | on a `file.mjs` entry: `source(node, { packages, written })`, the markup of a block made on the page that the file writes whole from what it holds, or from what this checked: `written(text)` gives it ids as a copy's; null refuses it, for the reason `unwritten(node, { packages })` says | an instance's, from its manifest's template or a copy's markup; `invalid`, or `no-package` for a menu's block whose custom block is gone |
| `key` | on a page object: `(event)`, a key on what is its own, asked of the component of what it came down on before any text block is looked for, true when it was its own | a tab's F2, Enter and Esc in a tab being renamed |
| `undoHere` | on a page object: `(node)`, whether Ctrl+Z with the focus on a node of it is the page's | a tab |
| `field` | on a page object: a text field of it that is no text block of the page's, being edited: the block tools stand back (`active`) and its keys are its own | a tab's name, being renamed |
| `shortcut` | on a page object: `(host, data)`, what `data` just typed in a text block of it makes before any type's `markdown` is read, true when it made something | `- ` at a line's start in a cell |
| `fromSelection` | on a type, `{ label, icon }`: its row of the format bar's 更多; on its page object, `()`, a block of it made from the selected text | `dialog: { …, fromSelection: { label: t("详情卡片"), icon: DIALOG_ICON } }` |
| `fromPage` | on a page object: `(page)`, a block of it made from what a web page says of itself, the link views' | a link turned into a bookmark |
| `entry` | on a component: `{ at, removed }`, a chip in the text that opens something of it, edged as inline code is (`typing/code-edges.mjs`); `removed`, what the page says when Backspace or Delete beside it took it away whole (the keydown's, and its beforeinput's: `takeEntry`) | a link to a detail card |
| `blankSays` | on a host or a type: what the status line says of a text block of the kind or a block of the type left blank where it must hold words (`blankSaysOf`) | a row of columns with an empty column (`columns`); the document's title had one until it could be empty |
| `columnOps`, `sectionOps`, `rowOps` | on a page object: what the block tools do with rows of columns and with sections, and what the gaps in a row resize, read of whichever page object says them (`page-objects.mjs`): the tools hold no page object by name, and without them they offer nothing. `sectionOps` also says where a block stands: `sectionOf(unit)`, by which a 批注's marker stands clear of a section's strip, and `dissolvesFrom(unit)`, by which a block has 解散所在分区 | `columnOps: { split, columnsCheck, … }` |
| `modalOps` | on a page object: what stands open over the page as a modal, read of whichever page object says it (`page-objects.mjs`), and nothing without one: `open()`, those open, the one on top last; `listen(listener)`, told when that may have changed; `closeTop()`, the one on top closed, for the workbench's bars; `show(node)`, what holds `node` shown, for what lands in it, an undo step or a word found, returning where it is; `kept()` and `reopen(kept)`, for a page shown again | `pages.dialog.modalOps` |
| `pageIcon` | on a page object: `()`, the page's own icon as it shows, its value | `pages.icon.pageIcon` |
| `setPageIcon` | on a page object: `(value)`, the page's own icon set from outside the page (the session's `set-icon`: the workbench's sidebar) as a pick of the header's picker, one undo step; resolves to whether the page has a title to stand before | `pages.icon.setPageIcon` |
| `typed`, `afterText` | on a page object: `(host)`, what follows text typed in a text block of it, and a text step undone or redone | `pages.code.typed` highlights it |
| `prepare` | on a page object: `(block, node)`, a text block of a kind that says `closed` or `tex` as editing starts, returning its baseline where it has one | `pages.math.prepare` |
| `panel`, `dismiss`, `holds` | on a page object: an editor of it open over the page, which saving waits for and which ends, what is in it kept, by a press elsewhere, in the workbench around the page too, and by the page saved or left; `holds(node)`, what is no press elsewhere | `pages.math.panel`, and the formula being edited is `holds` |
| `nodes` | on a page object: the elements of it to keep in the top layer when a modal dialog opens | a code block's language menu |
| `install`, `entered`, `blockTools` | on a page object: `install()`, called once the block tools are there; `entered()`, once the page's text blocks are made; `blockTools(tools)`, a part of it made with them, `{ holds(node), hide(), nodes }` | `pages.sheetPills.blockTools`; `entered` an instance's |
| `tidy` | on a page object: `(parents)`, what a structural step may leave untidy around what it changes (`history.mjs`), each asked in turn | `pages.columns.tidy`, `pages.list.tidy` |
| `nestBlocks` | on a page object: `(units, delta)`, selected blocks indented or outdented, true when they were its own | `pages.list.nestBlocks` |
| `addNear` | on a page object: `(unit, before)`, the handle's "+" on a block of it, what the "/" menu opens in, undefined to leave it to the page | a node's next node |
| `openAt` | on a page object: `(pre, point)`, code closed opened from the keys (`caret.mjs`) | `pages.code.openAt` |

### Hiding a block

设置 › 组件列表 ([workbench.md](workbench.md), "Components") hides a built-in component by its name, in gallery.json. It goes from three menus and from nothing else: "/" and 在下方新建, whose rows are one function (`typeItems`, `slash-menu/`), and the handle's grid (`block-menu/`) read `page/offered.mjs`, the one filter, by `TYPE_COMPONENT`. The blocks of a hidden component work as any other: its Markdown shortcut, a paste, the format bar's 转换为, 组成分区 and a conversion reach them, a block of it keeps its type marked in its own grid (`offered(types, own)`), and a list the filter would leave with nothing keeps what it had, that being all the place takes. 文本, 标题, 列表 and 分区 say `hideable: false` in their definitions, so that a name a hand wrote into the file does nothing. The page starts from `hidden` in its map, the built-in names alone (`hiddenBlocks`: a custom block says its own `state`), and follows `doc-settings-changed` from then on, which is told on its event stream and shows no page again ([render.md](render.md), "Settings"). `gallery block list` names the same blocks for an Agent (`blocksHidden`, `src/server/component-settings.mjs`, which reads the definitions: a name a hand wrote that hides nothing is left out).

### The order of keys

Every key goes the same way, through one function (`asked` in `typing/`): first the component of the block (`keys`), then `keys.around` of what stands around it, nearest first (`pagesAround`): the block before it where that says `canHaveChildren`, then what it stands in from where it stands out, by `ownerOf`, each once and its own component none of them. So a paragraph right after a list in a node asks the list and then the node, whatever the order the page objects are made in. A key nobody takes goes on to the rules of every block, which read fields:

- Enter (`typing/ split`, also from `insertParagraph`): before any of this, an empty paragraph before a block that says `opensBefore`, the caret at its very start. After the keys `leave.enter`, the rule of an empty block (a box's `leaveByEnter`, a type's `resettable`), and last the split itself, which `can(host, "split")` may refuse. Where a block does not split, Enter at the very end of one that says `opensAfter` puts a paragraph after it.
- Backspace at a block's start: first, a line above that a folded heading hides opens (`fold/`, so the merge never takes in words out of sight); after the keys an empty container turning into an empty paragraph where it stands, the caret in it (`removedEmpty`, `emptyContainer` in `core.mjs`, which a node's Backspace asks so as to leave such a one alone; `removeContainer`), `leave.backspace`, an empty heading or small text turning into an empty paragraph (`leave.emptied`), an empty block going, the merge, `mergeRefused`, the block above selected. A block going (an empty paragraph, or a container where its box takes no paragraph) puts the caret at the end of the text block before it, or at the start of the one after it when there is none.
- Delete at a block's end: first, a line below that a folded heading hides opens; after the keys, an empty block going, the caret at the start of the text block after it (or the end of the one before it), and the merge.
- Tab, Shift+Enter, Ctrl+Enter and Esc have no rule of their own but Esc's (a heading's Ctrl+Enter is its own `keys.commandEnter`: its section folded or unfolded, `components/heading/page.mjs`), which selects the block around the text; a key nobody takes is the browser's. Esc in what stands in an open detail card is the card's (`keys.around.escape`): nothing is selected, and the browser's Esc closes it, or the page's where two or more are open (`closeTop`).

A formula block asks nobody: a text block whose kind says `tex` (`texOnly` in `registry.mjs`) has no text the keys go into, its TeX changing in its editor alone, and no key leaves the caret on one: the text block a deleted block's caret goes to (`textNear` in `caret.mjs`, which the arrows' `places()` reads too) is never one (Backspace in an empty paragraph right after one puts the caret after it, as after any block of no text: "The caret after a block"), and the browser's Tab or Shift+Tab that puts the focus on one has it go on to the text block beyond it that way, the caret at its start as the browser's Tab puts it, or, with none there, back where it was (`passTab` in `editor.mjs`). With the caret on one all the same (a right click puts it there), Enter opens its editor (`activate`, as on it selected), and any other key that types or edits (a character or an input method's, Backspace, Delete, Tab; no Ctrl, Alt or ⌘: `editsText`) selects it, saying so, and does nothing more, the focus staying on the page: what it types goes nowhere, and the next key is the selected block's (`blocks.mjs`), so that Backspace and Delete delete only a formula the reader has seen selected, as Backspace below a block of no text stops at the caret after it before it deletes it. On an empty one a composition begun with no key before it selects the block too (`compositionstart`); on one drawn, its chip all it holds, the browser begins none, and what an input method puts in is refused as anything typed there is (`beforeinput`, `components/math/page.mjs`). Scenarios `keysOnFormulaBlock`, `passOverFormulaBlock`.

Before any block is looked for, `onKeydown` hears in this order: a 批注's card, which keeps its keys but Ctrl+S; what keeps its own keys (`keepsKeys`); Ctrl+Alt+M; a field of a menu and the icon picker, which keep theirs; the "/" and "@" menus standing on what is typed; a component's `key` on what it came down on. The "@" menu's Enter or Tab with nothing to take leaves a plain "@" and goes on as itself. A caption's Enter and a table cell's keys are listeners of the document's own capture phase, added before `onKeydown`, so they hear a key before it does and ask `typedKey` first: a menu standing on their text comes before them there too.

`ctx` holds what a key reads, the block being an element; a key that needs more adds it then:

| Member | What it is |
| --- | --- |
| `host` | the text block the key is in (the element itself) |
| `enter()` | Enter, the whole of it (`typing/ split`): a code block's Shift+Enter is its Enter. |

### The clipboard field

`clipboard.kind` says how a block is written out: the writer of that name in the one table of writers, `clipboard/own/writers.mjs`, which the copy (`export.mjs`, from the clipboard's description) and the page export (`src/transfer/markdown.mjs`, from the element) both use. A block that says no kind is a paragraph. A type says it for its description; for an element a unit says it itself where its elements are not all alike (`of(element)` gives the clipboard of that one: a list's kinds, a heading's level, a diff in a whole block) or where no type stands for it (a card, a whole block), and what it does not say the first type of its kind does; an element of no kind the editor knows goes as a whole block (`unitClipboard` in `registry.mjs`).

| `clipboard.kind` | Written as | Blocks |
| --- | --- | --- |
| (none) | a paragraph | 文本, 小字, and a list item and a node inside what lists them |
| `heading` (with `level`) | `#` … `####` | 标题 1–4 (`h5` and `h6` as 4) |
| `bullet`, `number`, `todo` | `- `, `1. `, `- [ ] `; what an item holds 4 spaces in. In a page export from the list's first number, and `- [x]  ` for a checked to-do; an item of no words over what it holds writes `&nbsp;` on its line, so that a viewer gives it a line of its own above them as the page does (with nothing there its first block would stand on its line, a list item beginning with at most one empty line), which the import reads as no words. `clipboard.read` gives each entry's line and what it holds, and an entry that says `headed` has its first text block for a line: a flowchart is a `number` whose nodes write so | 符号列表, 编号列表, 待办事项, 步骤流程图 |
| `toggle` | `- ` and its title, its blocks 4 spaces in (`&nbsp;` for a title of no words over them, as an item's), but no list: an empty line stands by a list beside it. It is written as a `bullet` item would be but stands alone, and that empty line is the difference | 折叠列表; 详情卡片, in a page export alone, after the page's blocks (`clipboard.apart`) |
| `quote` | each line `> `, its paragraphs apart | 引用块 |
| `aside` | `<aside>`, its emoji (a highlight block's) and first line, an empty line, its other blocks, `</aside>`; its `text/html` a `<div>` of its blocks. Read back with no data of the page's own, a callout's Markdown is a highlight block (the import's `<aside>`) | 高亮块, 提示块 |
| `code` | a fence with its language, longer than any at the start of its lines; in a page export its title in bold before it, and a diff's lines | 代码块, a declared diff |
| `equation`, `hr` | `$$` on lines of their own (an empty one nothing), `---` | 公式块, 分割线 |
| `table` | `\| a \| b \|`, the `\| --- \|` line after the first row, a merged cell's other slots empty (`clipboard.grid`); the HTML keeps its spans | 表格 |
| `flatten` | only the blocks it holds (`clipboard.blocks`, where those are not the units it holds) | 分区, 分栏, 栏 |
| `tabs` | each tab's name, then its panel's blocks | 选项卡 |
| `image`, `media`, `bookmark` | `![label](file)`, `[label](file)`, `[title](address)`, a caption below as a paragraph where the block has one to write (an embed's is its label). Written by a page export alone: a card is copied as itself | 图片; 文件 and 嵌入; 网页书签 |
| `parts` | the parts `clipboard.read` gives, an empty line between them: a card's title, the picture of its chart, its tables, its caption; of a description, its blocks in turn | 卡片 (only Gallery has it) |
| `words` | an element's blocks of text on one line, then its pictures | 整体块, and an item or a node a file left outside its list |
| `icon` | nothing: it goes with its block's first line (only Gallery has it as a block) | a highlight block's or a node's icon |

`clipboard.html` names the writer of the block's `text/html` where that is not its kind's: `item` and `node` (an `<li>`), `section` (a `<section>`). The two Markdowns are two sides of each writer, `copied(block, how, clip)` and `exported(unit, how, clip)`, with what they share in helpers: their bytes differ (a page export drops empty paragraphs, numbers a list from its start, keeps a code block's title, writes pictures and files of the document's folder through `media` and charts through `chart`, and an item's line breaks are indented), and a difference between the two is a writer's own. A writer reads no element's markup: what it needs of one it asks of `clipboard.read(element, how)`. The names of the table are a vocabulary of its own.

Gallery has no block model: a block is an element, so what is carried for one and how it is made again are its component's. On a type, in `index.mjs`, each given what the engine has for it:

| Hook | What it means | Example |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `describe(node, what)` | the clipboard's JSON for an element: undefined when the element is not its own, null when it is and cannot be copied. An element goes by the unit it is (`of` for one that differs); what a hook leaves undefined goes to the kind its tag gives it, then to the types whose mark it carries (`claims`: `askClipboard` in `registry.mjs`), as an element may have another component's class or attribute besides its own, and a tag whose block holds nothing comes before the unit (an `hr` is a divider whatever its class). A text block needs no hook, nor a list or a quote: they are the engine's own, of their type and of the blocks they hold | a divider's `{ type: "divider" }` |
| `data(node, what)`, `keeps` | for a block copied as itself (`COPIED_AS_ITSELF`: a card, and a unit with `copy: "itself"`): what the copy carries to be made anew where its unit is not, and the editor's marks a pasted copy keeps | a table's `tableData` as far as a description can carry it, `GRID` |
| `check(block, what)` | whether the clipboard's JSON, which is not trusted, is such a block; a text type needs none, any other without it is never pasted. `what.container(block)` checks the blocks of a container as its box takes them | a formula's TeX |
| `pastedIn` | a box that takes it from the clipboard though `accepts()` does not | an item in a flowchart: each is made a node |
| `build(what)` | the block made again; a text type needs none, and a container with no hook is filled with its blocks. On the type where it is DOM alone; where it needs the page's modules it is the `build` of `page.mjs`, listed in `components/builders.mjs`: a description is also built with no editor (the import's worker, the unit tests), where there are no page objects and `pages.mjs` cannot load, as some page modules want a browser as they load | a divider's `newDivider`; `build` in `code/page.mjs` |
| `text(block, lines)` | its lines of text for a place that cannot split (a cell, a title) | a code block's lines |
| `unsaid` | on a unit copied as itself, a selector: what in its markup is none of its words where it has no `data` to read them from (`UNSAID` in `registry.mjs`, with what only a browser reads) | a chart's data |
| `holds(block)` | the blocks a description holds where those are no `blocks` | an item's `lists` |
| `lead`, `run` | `lead`: it opens what it stands with and is no block of its text; `run`: the type a run of them copied on their own makes | an icon; an item's `bullets` |
| `piece(host, html, what)`, `across(unit, what)`, `endsIn(unit, what)` | what a selection across blocks takes: of a text block in part, of a block it goes through, of a block it ends in, each undefined when the element is not its own and asked as `describe` is | a quote its paragraphs in a quote |

`tests/unit/clipboard-kinds.mjs` holds every block type and unit kind to a kind or, on purpose, to the paragraph, and fails a kind with no writer.

### Page modules

`components/pages.mjs` is the page's list of the components' page modules, as `registry.mjs` is of their definitions. Each exports `create(ctx)`, which returns the component's page object; `createPages(ctx)` makes them all, once, in the listed order (each one's styles and listeners go in as it is made, so the order counts), and returns them by component. The flowchart's round "+" (`flowchart/add.mjs`) and a Sheet's tabs as pills (`sheets/pills.mjs`) are page objects of their own, `nodeAdder` and `sheetPills`, as their modules need a page to load in, which `page.mjs` does not; the block tools call the pills' `blockTools` with what they hold (the drag, the menu). An image's page object gives its toolbar and corner grips as such a part too, with a `follow(picked)` the block tools call in each of their frames with the blocks selected (none while blocks are dragged). A page object may have an `install()` that `boot()` calls where the block tools are there; the Sheet tabs' and the "+" have. Where every page object is asked they are asked in this order too, so it is why the columns come first (their `tidy` before the lists').

`editor.mjs` builds one `ctx` and gives it to them all, each `create` taking what it uses of it:

| Member | What it is |
| --- | --- |
| `pages` | the page objects, by component, as they are made: what one component needs of another it reads here when it uses it, never by an import (a flowchart's `pages.code.openAt`, an embed's `pages.bookmark.fromLink`, `pages.media.block(id)` for the four media blocks, made after it) |
| `map`, `baselines`, `lastKnown` | the edit map, each block's baseline, each block's last recorded content |
| `struct`, `look`, `sourceStep`, `recordText`, `closeTyping` | the undo steps (`history.mjs`) |
| `say`, `isEditing()`, `isComposing()`, `active()` | the status line; whether the page edits, an input method composes, a block tool may act |
| `closeTools()`, `hideFormatBar()` | what a press closes that opens over the page |
| `limitText`, `caretAtOffset`, `selectionContext`, `typeOf`, `targetsOf`, `convertHost`, `newParagraph` | text blocks and their types |
| `blocks()`, `tables()` | the block tools and a table's tools (`table/`), asked for when used: `boot()` makes them after the components, as it does the format bar and the "/" and "@" menus |
| `typedKey(event)` | a key that is the "/" or "@" menu's, for a key of a component's that stands on text and comes before the order of keys (a caption's Enter takes the menu's row first) |
| `assetUrl`, `fileUrl`, `embedUrl`, `upload`, `importEmoji` | the report's folder, as the server serves and takes files |
| `unfurl`, `frameable` | a web page as the server reads it |
| `openMediaPicker`, `showAs`, `loadKatex` | the page's tools a component opens: the icon picker, a link's 显示为 rows, KaTeX |

An engine imports what a component is from its `index.mjs` and reaches what it does through `page-objects.mjs` (`connectPages` hands the page objects over): the component of a type (`pageOfType`), of a block by its unit (`pageOfUnit`), of a text block (`pageOf`), of what a press or click came down on (`pageAt`), what stands around a text block (`pagesAround`: `keys.around`), every page object in turn (`pageObjects`: `activate`, `acceptsFile`), or whichever says a thing there is one of (`panelOpen`, `fieldOpen`, `columnOps`, `sectionOps`, `rowOps`, `pageIcon`). `editor.mjs` holds no page object by name. What it does name is a tool of the page's, as it does `resize/` and `columns/`: `table/`, a table's rows and columns, which is in `src/editor/page/` and not in the table's folder, so that the block tools, the resize and Ctrl+A take its `claims`, `holds`, `owns` and selected cells from it, the table's page object reaching it by `tables()`. A hook for each would have one reader and one implementer; moving the folder is the step that ends the name. `tests/unit/boundaries.mjs` holds every engine to that and lists the imports of any other module of a component's folder that remain, each with why: `convert.mjs` for the structural conversions (a block taken into a disclosure, a list and a flowchart made of each other, lists joined), `core.mjs` for a table's baseline, `table/`, the table's own editing, and `source/` for the file's tabs. A block made again from the clipboard's description is the one thing a page module does with no page object (`build`, "The clipboard field"), and `clipboard/own/blocks.mjs` reaches it through `components/builders.mjs`, whose list the same test holds to the page modules. It also fails an import of a module that does not exist.

## Where the editing tools live

`page/editor.mjs` starts the editor and routes the page's events (keys, input, composition, presses, the clipboard, selection changes) to the editing tools. Each tool is a folder of `src/editor/page/` whose `index.mjs` header says what it does and what it is given, so one can be reworked or replaced without reading the others:

| Folder | What it does |
|---|---|
| `typing/` | Enter, Backspace and Delete at a block's edges (a component's own `keys` as "The order of keys" says; Backspace in a code block, highlight block, callout, quote or disclosure with nothing written in it turns it into an empty paragraph in its place, the caret in it, one undo step (the user's of 2026-11-09: it was deleted whole, the caret at the end of the block before; where its box takes no paragraph it still is: `emptyContainer()`, `removeContainer`), and Backspace in an empty heading or small text does the same (`leave.emptied`); Backspace at the start of a text block right after a block of no text that takes a caret after it (a picture, a table, a divider, a code block, a highlight block or callout, a card, a formula block, a custom block's instance, a row of columns…; a section's own divider above its title) puts the caret after that block (`afterAbove`: `after/` `back()`, "The caret after a block"), an empty text block going, and Backspace again deletes it as that caret does; one above that holds no text and takes no such caret (a flowchart) is selected, Backspace again deleting it as a selected block (`selectBlock`; scenarios `caretAfterBackspace`, `backspaceAboveNoteTab`); Tab in 小字 (`isNote()`, in a header too) puts what follows the caret at the line's right end (`span.doc-tab`, the kit's float, `components/paragraph/page.mjs`), as a table's scope and unit, Shift+Tab (or Tab in that end part) back; at the start of a quote's first paragraph with text, that paragraph leaves the quote as body text), the space between Chinese and Latin text and between inline code or a formula and the words typed beside it (an ordinary space, none by punctuation or where one is), and taken out where an edit strands it (`spaces.mjs`, "Browser side"), an empty block's placeholder; inline code's edges (`code-edges.mjs`: a caret stop outside and one inside each edge; ←/→ step through them, also across a space between two pieces; a press lands on its side at once, `codePress`, a drag from it selecting (`followPress` in `caret.mjs`), and at its release `codeClick` picks the side by its x only when the press came down on that text, one that came down elsewhere (the page's empty space, a card's backdrop) settling the caret at the stop it put it at (`settleCodeEdge`); a Backspace that takes the last character between it and the caret after it leaves the caret outside, where the browser would put it in at its end, `deletingBack` and `deletedBack` on the deletion's beforeinput and input; the stops the browser would not keep held by pads, which go with an entry taken whole, so no zero-width space stays in the text) |
| `select-all/` | Ctrl+A widening step by step: the block's text, then everything in its container block as selected blocks, one level a press (a row of columns, a Sheet area, a table whole), then the page |
| `text-select/` | selecting text across blocks: drawn a line high as the browser draws a selection (strips in a page layer; its ranges a CSS highlight), copied, deleted with the blocks between joined |
| `annotate/` | 批注 ("Annotations" below), on every page of a session: started from the format bar, the button beside a block (`handle/`) or Ctrl+Alt+M, the cards in the margin, or with no room the markers in the column's right gutter and the card by the words or blocks (`card.mjs`), the marks on the words (CSS highlights), the fill of its blocks (`page/fills.mjs`), anchors read and found on the page (`anchors.mjs`), saved through the edit service; the room is `page/margin.mjs`'s, which they tell whether there are any |
| `find/` | the workbench's `reveal` (搜索文档 opened at a hit): the words found again in the page's text read as the server reads the file (`shared/search.mjs`), scrolled into view and marked by the highlight `ge-found` until a press or a key; in a detail card, which opens, in its content |
| `emoji-picker/` | the icon picker, its look the editor's palette (`styles.mjs`; `support.mjs` gives it this editor's popups, tooltips and status line, and the server's storage, and keeps the older `--c-*` palette for the media menu and the image's bar): 表情符号 (filter, ranked by `emoji-search.mjs`; groups with no 旗帜, skin tones, the frequently used, custom emojis), 图标 (MingCute or Tabler icons in a color), 上传 (a file or a link, as an icon or a custom emoji), 随机, 移除; the page's icon opens it (`components/icon/`) |
| `insert/` | new blocks: below a block (its menu's "在下方新建一块"), a click below the last block of a box (not a highlight block's or a disclosure's: `endIsPadding`), a paragraph before a block (Enter at its start) or after it |
| `after/` | the caret after a block of no text ("The caret after a block"): a click right of one puts it at its bottom right (`insert/` `boxedBeside`); Backspace or Delete turns the block into an empty paragraph in its place, whatever follows it, aligned as it was shown (`page/align.mjs`), Backspace, ← or ↑ at the start of the text right below one puts it there (`back`, `up`), Enter or a key typed makes a paragraph after it, the arrows leave it, Esc selects the block |
| `format-bar/` | the bar over selected text (`bar.mjs`) and its keys, formatting through `marks.mjs` |
| `formula/` | the formula box: selected text made an inline formula (the format bar's √x, Ctrl+⇧+E), KaTeX loaded when the page has none (KaTeX's mhchem with it, `/__gallery/edit/mhchem.js`, where the page has no `math.js`); the formula editor (`panel.mjs`) shared with formula blocks, its symbol bar (`symbols.mjs`): six groups by name (常用 with 最近使用 first, 希腊, 运算, 结构, 关系与箭头, 化学), each a card of all its symbols above the editor in sections with small titles (twelve to a line, templates ten, no scrolling), a section's rows as high as what it holds: 26px for simple symbols, more for large operators, tall arrows and templates; 最近使用 one line of twelve columns, what was put in lately drawn as in its own section, as wide, and as high as the tallest. What every formula asks of KaTeX stands beside it in `page/tex.mjs` (`renderTex`, `unread`), read by the text, this box, a formula block and the import alike |
| `links/` | the link card (Ctrl+K, the bar's link button, resting on a link) and a page block's box, the search of the workspace's pages they and `at-menu/` share (`pages.mjs`: `readPages` lists the documents in the sidebar's order, column after column; a web link, a draft of another workspace and 写作偏好 are no page to link to, the web links' own links kept apart as `webs` and an extension's entries as `extensions`, which a click on such an address follows to the workbench: from a page alone through `leadsToPage`, in 演示视图 through `present-links.mjs`), a link's views (`views.mjs`: 链接 / 标题 / 图标 / 书签, 选择视图 after a paste; `pageChip`, a page block) and following links while editing, to a document or a block too (`shared/page-address.mjs`, the editor's `goTo` and `revealBlock`, [workbench.md](workbench.md) "A document's addresses") |
| `hints/` | hints on hover: why text stays read-only, what a link does |
| `slash-menu/` | the "/" menu |
| `at-menu/` | the "@" menu: a field drawn over what is typed, a page block in its place |
| `markdown/` | Markdown shortcuts as the reader types (`rules.mjs`: the one loop over the types' `markdown`; the inline marks, which are no block's) |
| `clipboard/` | copying, cutting and pasting blocks and text (`own/`: the clipboard's own format and what other programs get; `import/`: another program's content) |
| `resize/` | dragging the edges of cards, tables and code blocks |
| `table/` | a table's rows, columns and cells: the selection, grips and their menus, + points and the corner's + (`index.mjs`), cells and their keys, copy and paste (`cells.mjs`), the cells' bar (`cell-bar.mjs`), column widths (`widths.mjs`), a cell's pictures (`pictures.mjs`) |
| `handle/` | a block's handle (a grip, and the bar beside it that turns into a "+"), the 批注 button right of its middle line, a section's bar, which block the pointer is at |
| `block-menu/` | the menu a handle opens (the rows of a component's own come from its page module's `menuItems`: a figure's size rows and its frame (显示边框, a switch like a Sheet area's, `doc-figure--plain`: no line, ground or room inside it, `figureFrame`), a table's and a code block's size rows, a Sheet area's frame; a block that has a menu of its own, a Sheet tab, gives it as `menu`) |
| `selection/` | selected blocks (their fills: `page/fills.mjs`, the painter a 批注's blocks use too), box selection (`marquee.mjs`), deleting blocks (`delete.mjs`) |
| `drag/` | moving blocks: dragging a handle, where they go (`drop.mjs`), Ctrl+Shift+arrows |
| `fold/` | a heading's section folded ("Folding a heading's section"): which headings fold and what a fold takes (`rule.mjs`, pure: the outline's nesting, read with `outline/read.mjs`), the triangle right of a heading's words, the folds kept per report (`outline/folds.mjs`'s store, under a key of its own), what is hidden marked, the rule that opens a fold, what goes with a folded heading for the other tools (`takenWith`, `groupsOf`, `stepOver`); what the other tools ask of it, the mark, `reveal` and `foldOver` (the folded section a place is in, for the ghosts of an Agent's deleted blocks), is `page/folded.mjs` |
| `outline/` | 目录: the page's headings at its right while the reader has it on for the document (`read.mjs` reads them from the page, `tree.mjs` nests them, marks the current one and moves through them, `list.mjs` draws the rows, `strip.mjs` the narrow form and its card, `folds.mjs` keeps what is folded), see "The outline and the right side" |

Cards and panels that stand by something (the link card, the formula editor, the format bar and the cells' bar, the tooltips, the icon picker's popups) take their place from one function, `placeBox` of `page/placement.mjs` (below, above, beside, put on the other side where it does not fit, kept inside the window); the block menu (`menu.mjs`) and what follows the pointer (hints, the size label of `resize/`) place themselves. What the tools that follow the pointer look through on every move (the tables, `table/`; the cards, tables and code blocks whose edges resize them, `resize/`; the sections whose bar shows at their right, `handle/`) is found once and kept until the page's elements change (`page/kept.mjs`: a node added or taken off, or an attribute that makes a block one or decides its kind, class or whether it shows, anywhere but in the editor's own nodes); where each is gets measured on the move, and only a block within the pointer's reach is looked at further. The table tools look for a table's edge beside the pointer (five hit tests of the whole page, 0.6–2ms each on a long document) only with the pointer within reach of a table. A menu answers ↑/↓ for its callers with `arrow(event)`; the keys around that stay each caller's. The last four are the block tools: `blocks.mjs` wires them (what the page knows of a block, `block-info.mjs`; one task of the editor's frames, `frames.mjs`, that keeps the handle, the bar and the fills on their blocks; the keys while a block is selected), and `editor.mjs` talks to `blocks.mjs` only. A tool is made by `editor.mjs` or `blocks.mjs` with what it works with (`create…({ struct, say, … })`); it imports the shared files of `page/` and the components' modules, and no other tool's folder, except these (`tests/unit/boundaries.mjs` holds the list): the block menu takes the format bar's color rings and the "/" menu's rows of types, the clipboard the formula box's maker and the link card's address rule, the format bar opens the link card and the formula box, the link card's views build a bookmark as a pasted block (`clipboard/own/`), the media menu draws in the icon picker's popup layer, the table tools take the block menu's color rows, the format bar's buttons and the formula box, and the Agent's changes (`changes/`) read a block's text as 批注 do (`annotate/anchors.mjs` `readText`, the server's `treeText` on the page). What the tools share stays in the files of `page/`: the text model and what a save sends (`core.mjs`), carets and lines (`caret.mjs`), undo and redo (`history.mjs`: every change is recorded through `struct`, `recordText`, `recordStep` or `lookStep`), conversions (`convert.mjs`), where blocks are on the screen (`geometry.mjs`), inline marks (`marks.mjs`), zero-width pads that hold the caret where the browser would not (`pads.mjs`), tooltips (`tooltip.mjs`), the grid of block types (`type-grid.mjs`), the menu card (`menu.mjs`), the ways a press gesture ends (`gesture.mjs`) and the page's scrolling near the window's edge (`edge-scroll.mjs`), the frame of what is being edited (`frame.mjs`), the editor's one animation-frame loop (`frames.mjs`), what it draws over the page and the checks of it (`overlays.mjs`), the layers it draws in (`overlay-layer.mjs`), notices (`notices.mjs`) and the DOM helpers (`ui.mjs`). `editor.mjs` keeps the wiring itself: turning blocks into other types as undo steps (`convertHost`, `convertUnit`), the components' page modules, counting changes for the autosave, and saving and the workbench (`autosave.mjs`, `commit.mjs`, `conflicts.mjs`, `export-report.mjs`, `session.mjs`). The preview serves the tools' folders as it serves `page/`. A custom page's board (`board/`: render.md "A custom page", **Its cards edited**) is a tool of no document: `custom-page.mjs` starts it by the page map, with no editor; it imports the shared files of `page/` (the menu card, tooltips, notices, placement, the DOM helpers and the palette) and no other tool's folder.

### The outline and the right side

`page/margin.mjs` lays out the right side of the page: the outline (`setMargin({ outline, outlineWidth })`), a panel docked at the window's right edge, and the room of 批注's cards between it and the column (`setMargin({ notes })`, below). One rule places the body column (`marginLayout`, pure): in the middle of what the outline leaves of the page, as far from the window's left edge as from the outline (its list's left edge, 8px into its panel, where its top level's folding triangles stand, its guide a little further in; or its strip), so with the outline the rooms at the column's two sides are the same, and they stay the same as the guide is dragged (each room gives or takes half of what the list takes or gives) and as the window is resized; without it the column is centered as ever. Turning the outline on moves the column left by half the outline's room, at once (and back when it is off); its list fades in, and goes at once when switched off. Each room is the edge room at least (64px: the block handle at the left, a section's bar and the annotation markers at the right). The list keeps the width the reader dragged it to (200px at first, 160 to 360: `outlineWidth`) whatever the page's width does: a window resized or the workbench's sidebar dragged or sliding never narrows it, so its guide stays where it is. Where the page is short of room for it, the two rooms give (down to the block handle's, 52), and below that the outline is its strip (36px), the column in the middle of what the strip leaves; only then does the column narrow. The reader may also have the strip wherever the list would fit (`setMargin({ collapsed })`, the preference `outlineCollapsed`: a click on the list's guide, below), the column then in the middle of what the strip leaves at every width, the cards of 批注 on what it leaves; `listFits()` says whether the room would give the list now, the collapse aside (the whole width needed, as for any strip going up). So the default list is in full from 1008px (52 + 712 + 52 + 200 less the list's 8px inset; one dragged to 300 from 1108): 1280 with the sidebar open (270), a page of 995px, has its strip, and with the sidebar at its narrowest (216) the list; a document read full width keeps the edge room at both sides of its column and has the list while the column keeps 560px (from 856). A form laid out is kept 16px under the width it needs (a scrollbar coming or a pixel of a resize never flips it back and forth: a full-width document's column, narrower beside the list, can grow taller and bring a scrollbar), the column narrower by what is short (16px at most); going up needs the whole width. Each width is laid out at once, while it moves too (the workbench's sidebar dragged or sliding, a window's edge dragged): the list comes the moment it fits and goes the moment it does not, never at the end of a drag. The page's rule (`MARGIN_STYLE`) is one of its width, so it holds while the window is resized; its numbers are two custom properties, `--ge-outline-room` (what the outline takes of the page beside the column's rooms: the list less its inset, or the strip) and `--ge-side` (the least room either side of the column keeps), which the page's head sets before the page shows (`earlyMarginScript(slug)`, from the document's own switch, `shared/preferences.mjs` `outlineOpen`, and the preferences `outlineWidth` and `outlineCollapsed`; the edit service puts it there, naming the document), measured again at `DOMContentLoaded`, when the content and its scrollbar are in and the kit has not shown the page yet, and kept by the editor as it starts (the head's layout taken as the one given last), so the column never moves as a page opens or is swapped in. The edge room is the page's gutters too (`--ge-edge-room` = `MARGIN.edge`, the block handle's bar, grip and gap and 12px more; the kit's `--doc-page-gutter` and `--doc-wide-gutter` take it), set by the same sheet with no attribute and no medium: so with the outline off and no 批注, which leave the head's script nothing to do, the column the kit lets show (`data-doc-ready`, the frame a workbench swaps the page in on) stands where the editor keeps it as well, before the editor has started, at every width, look and 全宽. Until 2026-11-09 the handle put them in once the page edited (`data-ge-editing`), a few frames after the kit had shown it: a page 795px wide (a workbench at 1026 with its sidebar at 231) came in with the kit's 20px gutters, its column 712 wide and 42 in, and went to 667 and 64, its lines breaking anew (the user's report on 0.1.1; scenario `firstFrameNoShift`). An export has neither sheet: the kit's own gutters. The column's width is the kit's (`--doc-content-max-width`, 712px unless the document's look, 字体与布局, makes it another: its 正文宽度 alone, 648px or 776px, never its 字体大小, [render.md](render.md) "Design defaults"), read from the computed token by the head's script (the kit's stylesheet stands before it in the head, so a page opening with a wider column and the outline on has the strip from the first paint where the list would fit beside 712px: scenario `documentLook`) and by the editor (`numbers()`); a look changed on the open page (its attributes on the root: `data-doc-measure`, `data-doc-size`…) lays the room out again, as `data-doc-width` does, and the kit sends a `resize` with it, so what stands over the text is placed again. When the room moves the column the page hears a `resize`, so what stands over the text (the format bar, a selection's strips across blocks, a card) is placed again; `onMargin` tells the tools when the layout changes, `outlineMost()` how wide the list can be dragged on the page now (as wide as leaves the column its edge room at both sides).

**The room of 批注.** 批注 take what the outline leaves, never what it has: the outline's form depends on them in nothing. The rule is read on what the outline leaves of the page (the page's width less `room`). A card's room right of the column is 342: 10 to the card (`MARGIN.lane`, where a marker stands; the button beside a block shows only where no card does), 308 of card, 24 past it (`cardEnd`, clear of the outline's list: its top level's triangles, its guide and grip). So from 1396 (712 + 2 × 342) the column stays in the middle of what the outline leaves; under that it goes left by max(0, (1396 − width) / 2), its left room going from 64 down to the handle's 52 at 1106; under that the card gives what is short, down to 180 (`cardLeast`), at 978 (52 + 712 + 10 + 180 + 24); under that they fold into markers and move nothing (until 2026-11-12 the lane was 60, the least 240 and the end 20, so the cards folded from 1084, the user finding them too far right and folded too soon). A column read full width gives them their room at its right: it narrows by a card's room while it keeps 560 (`least`), the card giving what is short down to 180, its left room the side's (the page's rule: `width: calc(100% − room − side − max(side, --ge-card-room))`). On the workbench's windows, with Windows' 15px scrollbar: 1440 with the sidebar open, a page of 1155, has cards of 308 and the column 101 from the left, moved 120.5; 1280 with it open (995) has cards of 197, closed (1265) of 308; 1920 needs no move, with it open or closed. Beside the list in full (192 of room) the cards come from 1170, so 1440 with the sidebar open (1155) keeps the list and has markers, closed (1425) has both, the column 179 from the left. A form laid out is kept 16px under the width it needs, as the outline's, the card narrower by what is short. `marginLayout` says all this (`cards`, a card's width or 0; `cardRoom`; `floor`, the least room at the column's left), and the page's rule holds it as one of its width (`--ge-card-room`, `--ge-floor` beside the outline's two), so a window resized or the sidebar dragged lays it out at once. Only the page coming to have an annotation or having none left (a new one begun, the last one deleted) slides it, over 200ms: the column goes as a transition of `margin-left` (`data-ge-margin-sliding`), so frames.mjs's followers keep what stands over the text with it, from a length (the page's rule is put on first as things stand: a margin of `auto` slides nowhere) and the rule comes off again at its end when nothing else wants it. The page's head has the room from the start (`earlyMarginScript(slug, { notes })`: the edit service says whether `annotations.json` is there, which no page kept outlives, the file's coming or going changing the folder's stamp), so a page opened or swapped in with annotations has its column where it stays from the first paint.

The outline (`outline/`, started by `editor.mjs` before `enter`, unless the page is opened on its own, `?solo`; 演示视图 and exports have no editor: the units' kinds marked from the map first, `core.mjs` `markKinds`, the marks `captureLayout` makes in `enter`, so it reads the same headings; drawn while the page's layout is still clean and given a frame to show in before `enter` holds the main thread, it comes with the page, not after every block is made editable; what `enter` changes in a heading it reads again on the next frame, as any change) shows while the reader has it on for the document (更多 › 目录, `shared/preferences.mjs` `outlineOpen`: each document's own switch, off for every document until switched on there, so one document's outline on leaves the others as they are; kept in `localStorage` under `gallery:outline-open`, the slugs of the documents that have it on, the one switched on last at the end, 100 at most, the oldest let go; followed at once, as switched in the workbench or by the same document's page in another tab, another document's switch changing nothing; the old switch for every document is read no more, and goes from the preferences as one is kept): the heading blocks (`data-ge-unit-kind="heading"`, h1–h4) of the page's flows, nested by level (a level skipped does not indent twice), not the document's title, a figure's or a chart's title, a detail card's headings, or a heading in a table or a list item; a row of columns or a disclosure stands where its highest heading would and closes its own headings when it ends, so what follows goes on as before it; a tabs area lists its tabs in their row's order as pills (the shown one filled, as the page's), the hidden ones too, each with its panel's headings; a heading with no text has no row yet; an inline formula reads as text (`texReading`: x² for `x^2`, a/b for `\frac{a}{b}`, Greek letters and the usual signs as their characters). The list (`nav.ge-outline`, fixed at the window's right, at the `outline` level of `shared/layers.mjs`) is a hairline guide with the current row's segment dark on it and rows of 14px in the muted color, 8px short of the page's right edge; the guide stands close to the top level's titles (`list.mjs` `LIST`, from the list's left edge: the hairline 17px in, the top level's titles 26px in, 8px clear of it, a tab's pill 4px; the rows' fill from 20px in, 2px clear of it, but a top-level row's from 8px in, across it, so that its triangle stands in the grey with the 3px a second-level row's fill leaves left of its own (the user's of 2026-11-07, who saw the grey cut a top-level triangle in two; one rule: a fill starts at its row's left or 3px left of its triangle's column, whichever is further left, and only the top level's column stands left of its row; that part is drawn as the row's shadow, which takes no pointer, so the gutter and the grip stay what they were); the space left of the guide is the list's gutter, with nothing in it: the user found the guide far from the titles with a triangle's column between them, 2026-11-07, where it stood at the list's very edge with the titles 26px from it), rows indented 12px a level, the top level's headings bold (at the top, or right under a tab: a tab's own top level), the current row in the text color, medium only where the Chinese face has a medium, on Apple's systems (Windows draws a 500 title's Latin words in Segoe's semibold beside regular YaHei), a tab's row its name as a 14px pill (the shown one filled, the others in the secondary gray, no line: until 2026-11-17 ringed by a hairline), cut short with an ellipsis and whole in the shared tooltip, at the list's left over the gutter (`tooltip.mjs` shows nothing for a label that gives none, and takes a `gap`; `placeBox` keeps every placed box inside the window less its scrollbar). It scrolls by itself with no scrollbar (its fades say there is more), so its rows never move as one comes and goes; the wheel over it scrolls it, and at its ends the page (over the grip, the page: the grip is not the list). A parent's triangle (small and solid, on the 20-unit grid, turning down when open, 13px wide to take the pointer, its 16px glyph centered on it) at its title's left, out of its row's flow (so the titles of a level line up, a leaf's too), in its indentation 2px short of its title, at every level the same: a top-level one from 11px into the list, on the guide's hairline and its grip (from 4px left of the hairline to 5px right), a deeper one right of them (the top level's stood in the list's first 13px, left of the guide, for a few hours of the feedback round of 2026-11-07, until the user found them too far from their titles). Where a top-level triangle lies on the grip, the triangle takes its own box while it shows, being what the pointer is over on its row (the grip stands in the list's scroller before the list, placed by the frame, so it neither scrolls nor counts in what scrolls, and shares the scroller's stacking context with the triangles: the grip `z-index` 1, a triangle 2), and the grip has the rest of the guide: the rows with no triangle shown, above and below a shown one (16px of its row's 28), and every hidden one (`visibility: hidden` takes no pointer), with its tooltip, its click and its drag; a folded one, always shown, stands on the hairline while the guide shows, its glyph across it (decided for the user, no gap cut in the line for it). It folds its row (a folded one always shows it; the open ones while the pointer is on the rows or the keyboard is in the list, its focus seen: a fold by the pointer leaves the focus on its row, unseen; no grey under the pointer, only while pressed); while they show, the guide (its hairline and its segments) fades out over 120ms (at once with reduced motion, by the kit's rule for every transition), the titles never moving: the guide or the triangles. The pointer on the list but on no row (its gutter, left of the grip, crossed on the way to it from the page) shows nothing (when the top level's triangles stood there it showed them after a 150ms rest, and a top-level triangle counted as the gutter), and leaving the rows they go (and the guide comes back) only 120ms later, so the pointer going along the guide from one top-level triangle to another, across the grip between them, keeps them, and the pointer resting on the grip has them go as its darker bar comes (120ms); what is folded is kept per document (`folds.mjs`, `localStorage`), keyed by a heading's block id, else its level and text, a tab by its area's name (`data-doc-sheets`) and its key, and a fold follows its node as its key changes on the page (a heading with no id typed in, or given its id by a save: `carryFolds`). The current heading is the one furthest down, of those with a box, whose top has passed a line min(120px, 30% of the window) below the window's top, the first in order of those level with it (headings side by side in columns: `currentOf`); over the page's last window the line comes down with the scroll to the window's bottom (`spyLine`), so the last sections, too short to reach the top, are each current in turn and the last one at the very end; inside a folded row, that row. The list keeps the current row at most a third of its clear view down (the rows to come below it) and two rows from its top at least, scrolling smoothly as little as that takes, a row for each heading passed (`followTop`; not while the pointer is over it or the reader scrolled it in the last 1.5s; the pointer gone after that, a row scrolled out of sight is brought back). A click goes there: a hidden tab shown, a closed disclosure opened or a fold hiding the heading opened (not the heading's own fold: `page/folded.mjs` `reveal`) first (reading state, nothing saved), the page scrolled to put the heading 24px below the top (from more than two windows away at once to a window short of it, gliding the rest: `glideFrom`; the jump is over at its end or once the page stops moving towards it; again while what was just shown lays itself out, a scroll the page's layout moved landing again, but never once the reader has moved the page meanwhile: the wheel (over the list, once it goes on to the page), a key that scrolls, a press on the page or its scrollbar end the jump where it is), the row current until the page has moved 40px from where the jump left it (and while its heading has a box: a tab switched since hides it), the heading lit for a moment (`--ge-outline-flash`, in its block's overlay layer, owned as an overlay, let go at once when the heading goes or is hidden). A jump leaves the text: its selection goes, the format bar with it (it stood over the heading gone to). Chosen by the pointer the page is the reader's: the focus leaves the outline for the page itself (a letter types nothing; PageUp/PageDown, Space, ↑/↓, Home/End scroll the page, which the outline does itself while the last press was in the list, since the browser scrolls what was pressed last). Its guide, dragged (a resize cursor on it, a darker bar under the pointer and while dragged), makes the list wider or narrower as the pointer goes (160 to 360px, and no wider than leaves the column its edge room at both sides: `outlineMost`), the column staying in the middle of what it leaves (`margin.mjs`: each of its rooms gives or takes half); let go, the width is the preference `outlineWidth` (let go where it began, nothing is chosen: the width chosen before is laid out again), for every document (unlike its switch) and every page of the origin (another page follows at once, and a page's head takes it before it shows); Esc, the window losing the focus or the pointer taken away put it back (`gesture.mjs`). Clicked instead (pressed and let go before the pointer has gone 3px across, the workbench's sidebar edge's rule: the drag follows the pointer only past that), the guide collapses the list to its strip even where the room gives the list, faded in as any form that changes: the preference `outlineCollapsed`, for every document and every page of the origin as the width (another page follows at once, and a page's head takes it before it shows, so a reload opens on the strip with the column where it was); a press Esc ended collapses nothing, the outline's switch (更多 › 目录) stays each document's own. The strip's card has a grip on its guide too (9px about its hairline, which stands 25px into the card: its list 8px in, as its right padding, and the grip in its scroller as the list's is, under its top-level triangles while they show; the card's list follows the list's rules, its guide shown while the pointer is on the strip, with the lit row's blue segment, and faded while the pointer is on the card's rows); clicked, it takes the collapse back: the list in full where the room gives it now (`margin.mjs` `listFits`), the card gone at once and the list faded in; where the room is short it opens nothing (never forced) and lets the collapse go all the same, so the list comes once the page is wide enough (a strip the room alone gave has nothing to take back: the click changes nothing). Each guide says what a click does in the shared tooltip, over where the pointer rests on it (a tall target, as the workbench's sidebar edge): 收起目录 with 拖动调整宽度 on the list's; 展开目录 on the card's, a hand and the darker bar under the pointer where the room gives the list, else no hand, no bar and 页面不够宽，变宽后展开 under it (shown again after such a click, saying why nothing opened). Its keys are a tree's (↑/↓, Home/End, ← folds or goes to the parent, → unfolds or goes to the first child, Enter or Space goes there, the focus staying on the row; PageUp/PageDown scroll the page; Ctrl/⌘+A selects nothing there); Esc leaves it, for the caret where it was while no jump has moved the page, else for the page itself; `editor.mjs` leaves them to it (undo and redo still work there). With too little room it is its strip: a short line a row, the current one dark, 240px of lines at the most (4px apart at the least, then a long document's deepest levels left out), whose card (the list over the page at the `menu` level, its top level with the strip's lines, as wide as the list chosen and 56px more, faded at its ends as in full) shows at once when the pointer reaches the strip, faded in and sliding 8px from it (140ms). Each line is a target of its own: the one nearest the pointer turns blue (`--ge-accent`), longer and thicker (120ms), and the card lights its row (filled as under the pointer, a blue segment on its guide), scrolled into view as little as that takes; both follow the pointer up and down the strip, and the pointer over the card's rows lights their lines. A click on a line goes to its heading as a click on a row does; the card stays while the pointer is on the strip or the card and goes 280ms after it leaves both (faded out; crossing the gap between them keeps it), or at a press elsewhere; a touch's card stays until a press elsewhere. The keys: the strip is a button; Enter or Space opens the card with the focus in its list (the current row in its middle); Esc or a heading chosen by a key closes it, the focus back on the strip (where Enter or Space opens it again), and so does the focus leaving both (Tab); Esc on the strip leaves it as Esc in the list does. A heading chosen by the pointer leaves the card where it is while the pointer is on it, the focus on the page. When the form changes under the focus, the focus stays in the outline (its row in full, the strip's button). It reads the page again on a frame after a change that may touch what it lists (`touchesOutline`: words typed in other text only move the headings; a task of `frames.mjs`), not while an input method composes (read once it commits, so no pinyin shows in a row), drawing its rows again only when what they show changed, is drawn at once as the editor starts (it comes with the page); a form coming after that (the window resized across the width it needs, the reader's switch) fades in, the list sliding 8px from the right (160ms), never there all at once; switched off, it goes at once; and it lets the pointer through while a block is dragged, a box drawn or something resized.

**The room past the text** (宽表格, the document's look `tables: "wide"`; render.md "Table layout internals"). A table wider than the text in the body goes past the column as far as the page's area at full width, which the kit reads as `--doc-area-start` and `--doc-area-end` (`styles/page.css`); while the right side takes room (`data-ge-margin`), `roomCss` writes them beside its own numbers from the layout: the side's room at the left (never inside the column), at the right the outline's room and the side's or the cards' as the rule of a page read full width has them, and beside the cards of 批注 of a page read in its column the column's right edge, as the cards stand right of the column there (such a table goes past the text on the left alone). With no room taken the kit's own gutters are the edge room (64px), so the handle stands in the page left of such a table. A 批注's marker, its bar and the 批注 button of a table placed past the text stand right of the table, not over it (`annotate/` `pastText`); an Agent's change bar is beside the block's own box anyway (`fills.mjs`).

## Next for the editor

Interaction rules still to build, for the editor's own units and commands rather than a second model of the document:

- **Copying more.** Chart cards between documents (within a page they are copied as themselves; a table, a row of columns and the four media blocks carry their data and go anywhere, see "Copying blocks"); Notion's and Feishu's own clipboard formats read (their HTML is read now). Slack's emoji names (`:smile:`) are not turned into emoji: a name pasted stays the text it is.
- **Edge auto-scroll.** Today a fixed 56px zone scrolls (distance in)/3 per frame. The rule to build: a zone of max(10% of the container's height, 20px); at depth n (0-1) into it, scroll a + (3h/60 − a) · min(n², 1) per frame, with a = h/600 and h the container's height.
- **Checks against what is there**: the selection fill's 200ms fade in and out, Esc closing one layer at a time (submenu, menu, selection), and shortcuts written Ctrl+D / Ctrl+⇧+P, shown as ⌘ and ⌥ on a Mac.

## Annotation hover timing (2026-11-11)

The page/handle annotation entry now waits 400 ms on the same eligible block before attaching its button. Leaving, changing target, typing, scrolling, opening a menu or disposing cancels the wait; hidden entries cannot receive clicks. The shared annotation rail markerAt moves from 14 to 10 px, and section markers keep 2 px clear of the section strip (24 px from the column). Existing annotation markers remain available immediately.
